# Seri 3/12 - Jersey/JAX-RS Registration di Servlet Container

## Tujuan seri ini

Seri ini membahas satu titik yang sering menjadi sumber bug saat onboarding aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS:

> Bagaimana Jersey didaftarkan ke Servlet Container sehingga HTTP request bisa sampai ke `@Path` resource?

Secara praktis, seri ini membantu Anda menjawab:

```text
[ ] Jersey didaftarkan lewat web.xml, annotation, atau programmatic registration?
[ ] Servlet mapping-nya apa?
[ ] @ApplicationPath dipakai atau tidak?
[ ] ResourceConfig dipakai atau tidak?
[ ] Resource dan provider diregister manual atau package scanning?
[ ] Kalau endpoint 404, salahnya di container mapping atau JAX-RS matching?
[ ] Kalau ExceptionMapper/MessageBodyWriter tidak jalan, apakah provider-nya ter-register?
```

Baseline modern: **Jakarta RESTful Web Services 4.0** adalah bagian dari Jakarta EE 11 dan menyediakan API dasar untuk membangun RESTful web services. Jersey **4.0.x** adalah release line yang kompatibel dengan Jakarta EE 11 / Jakarta REST 4.0. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Mental Model Registration

Jersey tidak otomatis menerima HTTP request hanya karena ada class `@Path`.

Agar resource bisa dipanggil, perlu ada rantai registration:

```text
Servlet Container
  mengenal Web Application
    mengenal Jersey Servlet / Filter
      mengenal Jersey Application / ResourceConfig
        mengenal resource class
          mengenal provider / mapper / feature
```

Diagramnya:

```text
HTTP request
   ↓
Servlet Container
   ↓
Servlet mapping: /api/*
   ↓
org.glassfish.jersey.servlet.ServletContainer
   ↓
ResourceConfig / Application
   ↓
Registered resources:
   - CaseResource
   - CustomerResource
   - PaymentResource
   ↓
Registered providers:
   - GlobalExceptionMapper
   - JsonProvider
   - AuthFilter
   - ValidationFeature
   ↓
Matched method:
   @GET /v1/cases/{caseId}
```

Kalau ada salah satu registration putus, gejalanya biasanya:

```text
404 Not Found
resource tidak ketemu
provider tidak jalan
ExceptionMapper tidak dipakai
JSON serialization/deserialization error
filter/interceptor tidak aktif
startup error
```

---

# 2. Komponen Utama Registration

| Komponen | Peran | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| **Servlet Container** | Runtime web yang menerima HTTP request | Tomcat, Jetty, embedded container |
| **Jersey Servlet** | Bridge dari Servlet API ke Jersey runtime | `org.glassfish.jersey.servlet.ServletContainer` |
| **Servlet Mapping** | Menentukan URL yang masuk ke Jersey | `/api/*`, `/rest/*`, `/*` |
| **Application / ResourceConfig** | Konfigurasi JAX-RS/Jersey application | `CaseApplication extends ResourceConfig` |
| **Resource class** | Endpoint REST | `@Path("/v1/cases")` |
| **Provider** | Extension JAX-RS/Jersey | `ExceptionMapper`, `ContainerRequestFilter`, `MessageBodyReader`, `MessageBodyWriter` |
| **Feature** | Grup konfigurasi/provider | JSON feature, validation feature, tracing/custom feature |
| **Package scanning** | Auto-discover resource/provider dari package tertentu | `packages("com.company.caseapp")` |
| **Explicit registration** | Register class satu per satu | `register(CaseResource.class)` |

---

# 3. Registration Style yang Umum

## 3.1 Style A - `web.xml` + Jersey `ServletContainer`

Ini style yang sangat umum untuk aplikasi WAR di external Servlet Container.

```xml
<web-app xmlns="https://jakarta.ee/xml/ns/jakartaee"
         xmlns:xsi="http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance"
         xsi:schemaLocation="https://jakarta.ee/xml/ns/jakartaee
                             https://jakarta.ee/xml/ns/jakartaee/web-app_6_1.xsd"
         version="6.1">

    <display-name>case-service</display-name>

    <servlet>
        <servlet-name>jersey</servlet-name>
        <servlet-class>org.glassfish.jersey.servlet.ServletContainer</servlet-class>

        <init-param>
            <param-name>jakarta.ws.rs.Application</param-name>
            <param-value>com.company.caseapp.config.CaseApplication</param-value>
        </init-param>

        <load-on-startup>1</load-on-startup>
    </servlet>

    <servlet-mapping>
        <servlet-name>jersey</servlet-name>
        <url-pattern>/api/*</url-pattern>
    </servlet-mapping>

</web-app>
```

Jersey documentation menjelaskan bahwa untuk deployment servlet-style, Jersey container servlet dapat dideklarasikan secara eksplisit di `web.xml`. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest3x/deployment.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Dengan konfigurasi di atas:

```text
Context path    = /case-service
Servlet mapping = /api/*
Resource path   = /v1/cases/{caseId}

Final URL:
GET /case-service/api/v1/cases/123
```

---

## 3.2 Style B - `ResourceConfig` sebagai pusat konfigurasi

`ResourceConfig` adalah class Jersey untuk menyusun resource/provider/features. API documentation menunjukkan bahwa `ResourceConfig` bisa dibuat kosong atau diinisialisasi dengan class resource/provider tertentu. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/apidocs/3.0.0/jersey/org/glassfish/jersey/server/ResourceConfig.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh explicit registration:

```java
package com.company.caseapp.config;

import com.company.caseapp.resource.CaseResource;
import com.company.caseapp.resource.HealthResource;
import com.company.caseapp.web.GlobalExceptionMapper;
import com.company.caseapp.web.RequestCorrelationFilter;
import org.glassfish.jersey.server.ResourceConfig;

public class CaseApplication extends ResourceConfig {

    public CaseApplication() {
        register(CaseResource.class);
        register(HealthResource.class);

        register(GlobalExceptionMapper.class);
        register(RequestCorrelationFilter.class);
    }
}
```

Kelebihan:

```text
+ Sangat eksplisit
+ Mudah diaudit saat onboarding
+ Tidak bergantung package scanning
+ Cocok untuk microservice kecil/menengah
```

Kekurangan:

```text
- Harus ingat register setiap resource/provider baru
- Bisa lupa register mapper/filter/provider
```

Practical recommendation:

```text
Untuk service critical, explicit registration sering lebih defensible daripada scanning penuh.
Untuk service besar, package scanning boleh dipakai, tapi harus dibatasi package-nya.
```

---

## 3.3 Style C - Package scanning

Contoh:

```java
package com.company.caseapp.config;

import org.glassfish.jersey.server.ResourceConfig;

public class CaseApplication extends ResourceConfig {

    public CaseApplication() {
        packages(
                "com.company.caseapp.resource",
                "com.company.caseapp.provider"
        );
    }
}
```

Kelebihan:

```text
+ Resource/provider otomatis terdeteksi
+ Sedikit boilerplate
+ Cocok saat jumlah endpoint banyak
```

Risiko:

```text
- Package salah → resource tidak ter-load
- Package terlalu luas → class tidak sengaja ikut ter-register
- Sulit audit provider apa saja yang aktif
- Startup lebih sulit diprediksi
```

Anti-pattern:

```java
packages("com.company");
```

Lebih aman:

```java
packages(
        "com.company.caseapp.resource",
        "com.company.caseapp.provider"
);
```

Rule praktis:

```text
Package scanning harus spesifik, bukan root package seluruh perusahaan.
```

---

## 3.4 Style D - `@ApplicationPath`

Jakarta REST mendukung `Application` sebagai base konfigurasi aplikasi REST. `@Path` documentation juga menjelaskan bahwa untuk class yang diberi `@Path`, base URI terkait dengan application path. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/apidocs/jakarta.ws.rs/jakarta/ws/rs/path?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh:

```java
package com.company.caseapp.config;

import jakarta.ws.rs.ApplicationPath;
import org.glassfish.jersey.server.ResourceConfig;

@ApplicationPath("/api")
public class CaseApplication extends ResourceConfig {

    public CaseApplication() {
        packages("com.company.caseapp.resource");
    }
}
```

Dengan resource:

```java
package com.company.caseapp.resource;

import jakarta.ws.rs.GET;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Path;
import jakarta.ws.rs.PathParam;

@Path("/v1/cases")
public class CaseResource {

    @GET
    @Path("/{caseId}")
    public CaseResponse getCase(@PathParam("caseId") String caseId) {
        return new CaseResponse(caseId, "OPEN");
    }

    public record CaseResponse(String caseId, String status) {
    }
}
```

Final path jika context path `/case-service`:

```text
GET /case-service/api/v1/cases/123
```

Namun hati-hati jika digabung dengan `web.xml`.

---

# 4. Kombinasi `web.xml` dan `@ApplicationPath`: Sumber Bug Umum

Ada beberapa style yang sah, tetapi jangan campur tanpa sadar.

## 4.1 Pattern aman: `web.xml` menentukan `/api/*`, `ResourceConfig` tanpa `@ApplicationPath`

```xml
<servlet-mapping>
    <servlet-name>jersey</servlet-name>
    <url-pattern>/api/*</url-pattern>
</servlet-mapping>
```

```java
public class CaseApplication extends ResourceConfig {
    public CaseApplication() {
        register(CaseResource.class);
    }
}
```

Resource:

```java
@Path("/v1/cases")
public class CaseResource {
}
```

Final:

```text
/context/api/v1/cases
```

Ini eksplisit dan mudah dipahami.

---

## 4.2 Pattern aman: annotation-only dengan `@ApplicationPath`

```java
@ApplicationPath("/api")
public class CaseApplication extends ResourceConfig {
    public CaseApplication() {
        register(CaseResource.class);
    }
}
```

Final:

```text
/context/api/v1/cases
```

Ini ringkas, tetapi pastikan container dan packaging memang mendukung discovery yang diharapkan.

---

## 4.3 Pattern rawan: `web.xml` `/api/*` + `@ApplicationPath("/api")`

```xml
<url-pattern>/api/*</url-pattern>
```

```java
@ApplicationPath("/api")
public class CaseApplication extends ResourceConfig {
}
```

Risikonya:

```text
Developer mengira path final:
/context/api/v1/cases

Tetapi konfigurasi bisa menjadi membingungkan:
- apakah /api berasal dari servlet mapping?
- apakah /api berasal dari @ApplicationPath?
- apakah salah satu diabaikan oleh mode deployment tertentu?
- apakah dokumentasi internal menulis /api/api?
```

Practical rule:

```text
Pilih satu sumber utama untuk base path Jersey:
A. web.xml servlet mapping
atau
B. @ApplicationPath

Jangan keduanya menentukan base path yang sama kecuali benar-benar diverifikasi dengan test dan dokumentasi.
```

---

# 5. Recommended Structure untuk Microservice

Untuk service Jersey/JAX-RS berbasis Maven + WAR + Servlet Container, struktur yang mudah diaudit:

```text
case-service/
  pom.xml
  src/main/java/
    com/company/caseapp/
      config/
        CaseApplication.java
      resource/
        CaseResource.java
        HealthResource.java
      provider/
        GlobalExceptionMapper.java
        JsonMappingExceptionMapper.java
      filter/
        RequestCorrelationFilter.java
      service/
        CaseService.java
      repository/
        CaseMapper.java
  src/main/webapp/
    WEB-INF/
      web.xml
```

`web.xml`:

```xml
<servlet>
    <servlet-name>jersey</servlet-name>
    <servlet-class>org.glassfish.jersey.servlet.ServletContainer</servlet-class>
    <init-param>
        <param-name>jakarta.ws.rs.Application</param-name>
        <param-value>com.company.caseapp.config.CaseApplication</param-value>
    </init-param>
    <load-on-startup>1</load-on-startup>
</servlet>

<servlet-mapping>
    <servlet-name>jersey</servlet-name>
    <url-pattern>/api/*</url-pattern>
</servlet-mapping>
```

`CaseApplication.java`:

```java
package com.company.caseapp.config;

import com.company.caseapp.filter.RequestCorrelationFilter;
import com.company.caseapp.provider.GlobalExceptionMapper;
import com.company.caseapp.provider.JsonMappingExceptionMapper;
import com.company.caseapp.resource.CaseResource;
import com.company.caseapp.resource.HealthResource;
import org.glassfish.jersey.server.ResourceConfig;

public class CaseApplication extends ResourceConfig {

    public CaseApplication() {
        register(CaseResource.class);
        register(HealthResource.class);

        register(GlobalExceptionMapper.class);
        register(JsonMappingExceptionMapper.class);

        register(RequestCorrelationFilter.class);
    }
}
```

Kelebihan style ini:

```text
[+] Entry point Jersey jelas
[+] Endpoint yang aktif mudah diaudit
[+] Provider yang aktif terlihat
[+] Cocok untuk production debugging
[+] Tidak bergantung scanning magic
```

---

# 6. Maven Dependency: Hal yang Harus Dicek

Untuk Jersey 4.x / Jakarta EE 11 style, gunakan dependency yang konsisten dengan namespace `jakarta.*`. Jersey 4.0.x adalah line yang kompatibel dengan Jakarta EE 11 / Jakarta REST 4.0. ([projects.eclipse.org](https://projects.eclipse.org/projects/ee4j.jersey/releases/4.0.0-0?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh konseptual:

```xml
<dependencies>
    <!-- Provided by external Servlet Container -->
    <dependency>
        <groupId>jakarta.servlet</groupId>
        <artifactId>jakarta.servlet-api</artifactId>
        <version>${jakarta.servlet.version}</version>
        <scope>provided</scope>
    </dependency>

    <!-- Jakarta REST API -->
    <dependency>
        <groupId>jakarta.ws.rs</groupId>
        <artifactId>jakarta.ws.rs-api</artifactId>
        <version>${jakarta.ws.rs.version}</version>
    </dependency>

    <!-- Jersey Servlet integration -->
    <dependency>
        <groupId>org.glassfish.jersey.containers</groupId>
        <artifactId>jersey-container-servlet-core</artifactId>
        <version>${jersey.version}</version>
    </dependency>

    <!-- Jersey injection support, depending on chosen Jersey line/setup -->
    <dependency>
        <groupId>org.glassfish.jersey.inject</groupId>
        <artifactId>jersey-hk2</artifactId>
        <version>${jersey.version}</version>
    </dependency>

    <!-- JSON support if using Jackson -->
    <dependency>
        <groupId>org.glassfish.jersey.media</groupId>
        <artifactId>jersey-media-json-jackson</artifactId>
        <version>${jersey.version}</version>
    </dependency>
</dependencies>
```

Checklist dependency:

```text
[ ] Tidak campur javax.ws.rs.* dan jakarta.ws.rs.*
[ ] Tidak campur javax.servlet.* dan jakarta.servlet.*
[ ] Jersey version konsisten antar module
[ ] Servlet API scope = provided jika external container sudah menyediakan
[ ] JSON provider jelas: Jackson, JSON-B, atau custom
[ ] Bean validation integration jelas jika dipakai
[ ] Tidak ada duplicate Jersey major version di dependency tree
```

Command praktis:

```bash
mvn dependency:tree | grep -E "jersey|jakarta.ws.rs|servlet|javax.ws.rs|javax.servlet"
```

Yang perlu dicurigai:

```text
javax.servlet-api
javax.ws.rs-api
jersey 2.x bercampur jersey 3.x/4.x
jakarta.ws.rs-api versi tidak align dengan Jersey/container
duplicate JSON provider
```

---

# 7. Resource Registration

## 7.1 Resource class minimal

```java
package com.company.caseapp.resource;

import jakarta.ws.rs.GET;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Path;
import jakarta.ws.rs.PathParam;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Produces;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.MediaType;

@Path("/v1/cases")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class CaseResource {

    @GET
    @Path("/{caseId}")
    public CaseResponse getCase(@PathParam("caseId") String caseId) {
        return new CaseResponse(caseId, "OPEN");
    }

    public record CaseResponse(String caseId, String status) {
    }
}
```

Final path tergantung registration:

```text
context path    : /case-service
servlet mapping : /api/*
resource path   : /v1/cases
method path     : /{caseId}

Final:
GET /case-service/api/v1/cases/123
```

---

## 7.2 Explicit registration

```java
public class CaseApplication extends ResourceConfig {

    public CaseApplication() {
        register(CaseResource.class);
    }
}
```

Jika lupa register:

```text
GET /api/v1/cases/123
→ 404
```

Debug:

```text
[ ] Apakah CaseApplication benar-benar dipakai oleh web.xml?
[ ] Apakah CaseResource diregister?
[ ] Apakah class package benar?
[ ] Apakah endpoint path benar?
[ ] Apakah method HTTP benar?
```

---

## 7.3 Package scanning registration

```java
public class CaseApplication extends ResourceConfig {

    public CaseApplication() {
        packages("com.company.caseapp.resource");
    }
}
```

Jika resource tidak terdeteksi:

```text
[ ] Package string typo?
[ ] Resource class ada di package berbeda?
[ ] Class public?
[ ] Annotation @Path ada di class?
[ ] Artifact module masuk ke WAR?
[ ] Dependency module masuk WEB-INF/lib?
```

---

# 8. Provider Registration

Provider adalah bagian yang sering lupa didaftarkan. Akibatnya aplikasi “jalan”, tapi behavior production salah.

## 8.1 ExceptionMapper

```java
package com.company.caseapp.provider;

import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.ExceptionMapper;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.Provider;

@Provider
public class GlobalExceptionMapper implements ExceptionMapper<Throwable> {

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(Throwable exception) {
        ErrorResponse body = new ErrorResponse(
                "INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR",
                "Unexpected server error"
        );

        return Response.status(Response.Status.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR)
                .entity(body)
                .build();
    }

    public record ErrorResponse(String code, String message) {
    }
}
```

Register:

```java
register(GlobalExceptionMapper.class);
```

Kalau tidak ter-register:

```text
Exception bisa jatuh ke default Jersey/container behavior.
Response bisa tidak sesuai standard error schema.
```

---

## 8.2 JSON mapping error mapper

```java
package com.company.caseapp.provider;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.core.JsonProcessingException;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.ExceptionMapper;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.Provider;

@Provider
public class JsonMappingExceptionMapper
        implements ExceptionMapper<JsonProcessingException> {

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(JsonProcessingException exception) {
        return Response.status(Response.Status.BAD_REQUEST)
                .entity(new ErrorResponse("INVALID_JSON", "Malformed JSON request body"))
                .build();
    }

    public record ErrorResponse(String code, String message) {
    }
}
```

Register:

```java
register(JsonMappingExceptionMapper.class);
```

Practical invariant:

```text
Provider yang menentukan API contract harus eksplisit.
Jangan berharap @Provider selalu auto-discovered tanpa memahami scanning mode.
```

---

## 8.3 JAX-RS request filter

```java
package com.company.caseapp.provider;

import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestContext;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestFilter;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.Provider;

import java.io.IOException;

@Provider
public class RequestCorrelationJaxRsFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext context) throws IOException {
        String correlationId = context.getHeaderString("X-Correlation-Id");

        if (correlationId == null || correlationId.isBlank()) {
            correlationId = java.util.UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        }

        context.setProperty("correlationId", correlationId);
    }
}
```

Register:

```java
register(RequestCorrelationJaxRsFilter.class);
```

Catatan penting:

```text
Servlet Filter berjalan sebelum Jersey.
JAX-RS ContainerRequestFilter berjalan setelah request masuk Jersey.
```

Jadi jika butuh logic sebelum Jersey, gunakan Servlet Filter. Jika logic spesifik resource/API, gunakan JAX-RS filter.

---

# 9. `ServletContainer` sebagai Servlet vs Filter

Jersey bisa dipasang sebagai servlet atau sebagai filter pada deployment tertentu. Dokumentasi deployment Jersey membahas environment server-side dan mekanisme servlet-based deployment. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/3.0.1/deployment.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 9.1 Jersey sebagai Servlet

```xml
<servlet>
    <servlet-name>jersey</servlet-name>
    <servlet-class>org.glassfish.jersey.servlet.ServletContainer</servlet-class>
    ...
</servlet>

<servlet-mapping>
    <servlet-name>jersey</servlet-name>
    <url-pattern>/api/*</url-pattern>
</servlet-mapping>
```

Cocok untuk:

```text
REST API utama aplikasi
mapping jelas
deployment WAR tradisional
```

## 9.2 Jersey sebagai Filter

```xml
<filter>
    <filter-name>jersey-filter</filter-name>
    <filter-class>org.glassfish.jersey.servlet.ServletContainer</filter-class>
    <init-param>
        <param-name>jakarta.ws.rs.Application</param-name>
        <param-value>com.company.caseapp.config.CaseApplication</param-value>
    </init-param>
</filter>

<filter-mapping>
    <filter-name>jersey-filter</filter-name>
    <url-pattern>/api/*</url-pattern>
</filter-mapping>
```

Cocok jika:

```text
Jersey perlu berada dalam filter chain tertentu
aplikasi punya servlet lain yang tetap perlu menangani request
ada kebutuhan forwarding/fallback tertentu
```

Namun untuk microservice REST biasa, **Jersey sebagai Servlet** lebih mudah dipahami dan diaudit.

---

# 10. URL Mapping Diagnosis

Servlet mapping menentukan request yang diteruskan ke Jersey. Servlet spec mendefinisikan mapping request ke servlet; Jakarta Servlet juga menyediakan `HttpServletMapping` untuk mengetahui bagaimana servlet pada request saat ini dipanggil. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/servlet/6.1/jakarta-servlet-spec-6.1?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Tambahkan diagnostic endpoint/filter saat onboarding.

## 10.1 Diagnostic Servlet Filter

```java
package com.company.caseapp.web;

import jakarta.servlet.Filter;
import jakarta.servlet.FilterChain;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletException;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletResponse;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;

import java.io.IOException;

public class ServletPathDebugFilter implements Filter {

    @Override
    public void doFilter(
            ServletRequest request,
            ServletResponse response,
            FilterChain chain
    ) throws IOException, ServletException {

        HttpServletRequest r = (HttpServletRequest) request;

        System.out.printf(
                "method=%s uri=%s contextPath=%s servletPath=%s pathInfo=%s query=%s%n",
                r.getMethod(),
                r.getRequestURI(),
                r.getContextPath(),
                r.getServletPath(),
                r.getPathInfo(),
                r.getQueryString()
        );

        chain.doFilter(request, response);
    }
}
```

Register sementara:

```xml
<filter>
    <filter-name>servletPathDebugFilter</filter-name>
    <filter-class>com.company.caseapp.web.ServletPathDebugFilter</filter-class>
</filter>

<filter-mapping>
    <filter-name>servletPathDebugFilter</filter-name>
    <url-pattern>/*</url-pattern>
</filter-mapping>
```

Gunakan saat debugging, bukan untuk log permanen berisik.

---

## 10.2 Diagnostic JAX-RS endpoint

```java
package com.company.caseapp.resource;

import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import jakarta.ws.rs.GET;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Path;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Context;

@Path("/v1/debug/path")
public class PathDebugResource {

    @Context
    private HttpServletRequest request;

    @GET
    public PathDebugResponse debug() {
        return new PathDebugResponse(
                request.getRequestURI(),
                request.getContextPath(),
                request.getServletPath(),
                request.getPathInfo(),
                request.getQueryString()
        );
    }

    public record PathDebugResponse(
            String requestUri,
            String contextPath,
            String servletPath,
            String pathInfo,
            String queryString
    ) {
    }
}
```

Jika endpoint ini tidak bisa dipanggil, berarti masalah terjadi sebelum atau saat Jersey matching.

---

# 11. Common Failure: Endpoint 404

## 11.1 Decision tree

```text
404 dari API Gateway / Ingress?
  → request belum tentu sampai container

404 dari Servlet Container?
  → context path / servlet mapping / WAR deployment mungkin salah

404 dari Jersey?
  → request masuk Jersey, tapi resource/method tidak match
```

## 11.2 Checklist 404

```text
[ ] Apakah WAR benar-benar ter-deploy?
[ ] Apakah context path benar?
[ ] Apakah servlet mapping benar?
[ ] Apakah @ApplicationPath ikut mempengaruhi path?
[ ] Apakah ResourceConfig yang benar dipakai?
[ ] Apakah resource class ter-register?
[ ] Apakah @Path class benar?
[ ] Apakah @Path method benar?
[ ] Apakah HTTP method benar?
[ ] Apakah trailing slash diperlakukan berbeda?
[ ] Apakah ingress melakukan rewrite?
[ ] Apakah request masuk ke pod/service yang benar?
```

## 11.3 Debug sequence

```bash
# 1. Test root/context
curl -i http://localhost:8080/case-service/

# 2. Test servlet mapping
curl -i http://localhost:8080/case-service/api/

# 3. Test health resource
curl -i http://localhost:8080/case-service/api/v1/health

# 4. Test target endpoint
curl -i http://localhost:8080/case-service/api/v1/cases/123
```

Interpretasi:

| Hasil | Kemungkinan |
|---|---|
| `/case-service` 404 | context path / deployment salah |
| `/case-service/api` 404 | servlet mapping / Jersey servlet salah |
| `/case-service/api/v1/health` 404 | resource registration / Jersey config salah |
| health OK tapi target 404 | target resource/method/path salah |

---

# 12. Common Failure: Provider Tidak Jalan

## 12.1 Gejala

```text
ExceptionMapper tidak dipanggil
JSON error tidak sesuai standard
Custom request filter tidak jalan
Response filter tidak menambah header
MessageBodyWriter tidak dipakai
Validation error format berubah
```

## 12.2 Penyebab

```text
[ ] Provider class tidak diregister
[ ] @Provider ada tapi package scanning tidak mencakup package provider
[ ] ResourceConfig salah yang dipakai
[ ] Ada dua Application/ResourceConfig
[ ] Dependency provider tidak masuk WAR
[ ] Provider kalah precedence/prioritas
[ ] Exception type yang di-map tidak sesuai
```

## 12.3 Debug provider registration

Tambahkan log di constructor provider untuk sementara:

```java
@Provider
public class GlobalExceptionMapper implements ExceptionMapper<Throwable> {

    public GlobalExceptionMapper() {
        System.out.println("GlobalExceptionMapper registered");
    }

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(Throwable exception) {
        return Response.serverError()
                .entity(new ErrorResponse("INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR", "Unexpected error"))
                .build();
    }

    public record ErrorResponse(String code, String message) {
    }
}
```

Kalau log tidak muncul saat startup atau first use:

```text
Provider kemungkinan tidak ter-register.
```

---

# 13. Multiple `Application` / `ResourceConfig`: Bug yang Sering Tersembunyi

Kadang dalam repo ada lebih dari satu class:

```text
com.company.caseapp.config.CaseApplication
com.company.caseapp.config.ApiApplication
com.company.caseapp.config.TestApplication
com.company.common.RestApplication
```

Masalah:

```text
web.xml menunjuk ApiApplication,
tapi developer menambahkan resource ke CaseApplication.
```

Akibat:

```text
Code terlihat benar,
resource tetap 404.
```

Checklist:

```bash
grep -R "extends ResourceConfig" src/main/java
grep -R "extends Application" src/main/java
grep -R "jakarta.ws.rs.Application" src/main/webapp src/main/resources
grep -R "@ApplicationPath" src/main/java
```

Pastikan satu entry point production jelas.

---

# 14. Explicit vs Scanning: Rekomendasi Praktis

## 14.1 Untuk microservice kecil/critical

Gunakan explicit registration:

```java
public class CaseApplication extends ResourceConfig {

    public CaseApplication() {
        register(HealthResource.class);
        register(CaseResource.class);
        register(CaseSearchResource.class);

        register(GlobalExceptionMapper.class);
        register(ValidationExceptionMapper.class);
        register(JsonMappingExceptionMapper.class);

        register(RequestCorrelationJaxRsFilter.class);
    }
}
```

Kelebihan:

```text
mudah review
mudah audit
mudah tahu endpoint/provider aktif
minim surprise
```

## 14.2 Untuk microservice besar

Gunakan package scanning terbatas:

```java
public class CaseApplication extends ResourceConfig {

    public CaseApplication() {
        packages(
                "com.company.caseapp.resource",
                "com.company.caseapp.provider"
        );
    }
}
```

Tambahkan test untuk memastikan resource/provider penting aktif.

---

# 15. Test Registration Minimal

## 15.1 Smoke test untuk health endpoint

```java
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.net.URI;
import java.net.http.HttpClient;
import java.net.http.HttpRequest;
import java.net.http.HttpResponse;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;

class HealthEndpointSmokeTest {

    @Test
    void healthEndpointShouldBeReachable() throws Exception {
        HttpClient client = HttpClient.newHttpClient();

        HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
                .uri(URI.create("http://localhost:8080/case-service/api/v1/health"))
                .GET()
                .build();

        HttpResponse<String> response =
                client.send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());

        assertEquals(200, response.statusCode());
    }
}
```

Ini bukan pengganti integration test penuh, tetapi bagus untuk membuktikan:

```text
container hidup
context path benar
servlet mapping benar
Jersey aktif
resource ter-register
```

---

## 15.2 Test provider error contract

```java
@Test
void invalidEndpointShouldReturnJsonError() throws Exception {
    HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
            .uri(URI.create("http://localhost:8080/case-service/api/v1/cases/force-error"))
            .GET()
            .build();

    HttpResponse<String> response =
            HttpClient.newHttpClient().send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());

    assertEquals(500, response.statusCode());
    assertTrue(response.headers()
            .firstValue("Content-Type")
            .orElse("")
            .contains("application/json"));
}
```

Test ini membuktikan:

```text
ExceptionMapper aktif
error schema konsisten
response tidak jatuh ke default HTML container
```

---

# 16. Registration Checklist untuk Onboarding

Gunakan checklist ini saat membaca service baru.

## 16.1 Entry point

```text
[ ] Ada web.xml?
[ ] Ada @ApplicationPath?
[ ] Ada class extends ResourceConfig?
[ ] Ada class extends jakarta.ws.rs.core.Application?
[ ] web.xml menunjuk Application class yang mana?
[ ] Ada lebih dari satu Application/ResourceConfig?
```

## 16.2 Servlet mapping

```text
[ ] url-pattern Jersey apa?
[ ] Apakah /api/*, /rest/*, atau /*?
[ ] Apakah context path berasal dari nama WAR?
[ ] Apakah context path dioverride oleh container config?
[ ] Apakah ingress/gateway melakukan path rewrite?
```

## 16.3 Resource registration

```text
[ ] Manual register atau package scanning?
[ ] Semua resource penting ter-register?
[ ] Package scanning terlalu luas atau terlalu sempit?
[ ] Resource class public?
[ ] Resource punya @Path?
[ ] Method punya HTTP annotation: @GET, @POST, @PUT, @DELETE, etc?
```

## 16.4 Provider registration

```text
[ ] ExceptionMapper global ter-register?
[ ] Validation mapper ter-register?
[ ] JSON mapping provider jelas?
[ ] JAX-RS filters ter-register?
[ ] MessageBodyReader/Writer custom ter-register?
[ ] Feature yang dibutuhkan ter-register?
```

## 16.5 Dependency alignment

```text
[ ] Tidak ada javax.* API tersisa jika app sudah Jakarta
[ ] Jersey major version konsisten
[ ] Servlet API scope benar
[ ] JSON provider tidak double
[ ] Dependency module resource masuk WAR
```

---

# 17. Anti-Pattern

| Anti-pattern | Masalah | Alternatif |
|---|---|---|
| `packages("com.company")` | Terlalu luas, unpredictable | Scan package resource/provider spesifik |
| Ada `@ApplicationPath("/api")` dan `web.xml` `/api/*` tanpa dokumentasi | Base path membingungkan | Pilih salah satu sumber base path |
| Resource baru dibuat tapi lupa register | Endpoint 404 | Explicit checklist atau scanning terbatas |
| Provider hanya diberi `@Provider`, tapi scanning tidak aktif | Mapper/filter tidak jalan | Register provider eksplisit |
| Banyak `ResourceConfig` tanpa entry point jelas | Developer edit class yang tidak dipakai | Satu production application class jelas |
| Dependency `javax.*` dan `jakarta.*` bercampur | Runtime/classpath issue | Konsisten Jakarta untuk stack modern |
| Jersey servlet mapping `/*` tanpa sadar | Bisa mengambil semua route | Pakai `/api/*` kecuali ada alasan kuat |
| Error contract bergantung default container | Response error bisa HTML | Register `ExceptionMapper` eksplisit |

---

# 18. Practical Debug Playbook

## Case A - Resource 404

```text
1. curl endpoint lokal langsung ke container
2. cek access log container
3. log requestURI/contextPath/servletPath/pathInfo
4. cek web.xml servlet mapping
5. cek @ApplicationPath
6. cek ResourceConfig yang dipakai
7. cek resource registration
8. cek @Path class + method
9. cek HTTP method
10. cek ingress rewrite jika hanya gagal di environment tertentu
```

## Case B - ExceptionMapper tidak dipakai

```text
1. Pastikan class mapper ada di WAR
2. Pastikan mapper ter-register di ResourceConfig
3. Jika pakai scanning, pastikan package provider ikut discan
4. Pastikan generic exception type sesuai
5. Pastikan exception terjadi di dalam Jersey, bukan sebelum Jersey
6. Pastikan response belum committed
```

## Case C - JSON request gagal parse

```text
1. Cek Content-Type request
2. Cek JSON provider dependency
3. Cek model DTO constructor/record/property
4. Cek MessageBodyReader/Jackson provider aktif
5. Cek apakah Servlet Filter membaca request body lebih dulu
6. Cek mapper untuk JSON parse error
```

---

# 19. Production-Ready Template

Untuk banyak service, template registration yang defensible:

```java
package com.company.caseapp.config;

import com.company.caseapp.provider.GlobalExceptionMapper;
import com.company.caseapp.provider.JsonMappingExceptionMapper;
import com.company.caseapp.provider.ValidationExceptionMapper;
import com.company.caseapp.resource.CaseResource;
import com.company.caseapp.resource.HealthResource;
import com.company.caseapp.resource.ReadinessResource;
import com.company.caseapp.web.RequestCorrelationJaxRsFilter;
import org.glassfish.jersey.server.ResourceConfig;

public class CaseApplication extends ResourceConfig {

    public CaseApplication() {
        registerResources();
        registerProviders();
        registerFilters();
    }

    private void registerResources() {
        register(HealthResource.class);
        register(ReadinessResource.class);
        register(CaseResource.class);
    }

    private void registerProviders() {
        register(GlobalExceptionMapper.class);
        register(ValidationExceptionMapper.class);
        register(JsonMappingExceptionMapper.class);
    }

    private void registerFilters() {
        register(RequestCorrelationJaxRsFilter.class);
    }
}
```

`web.xml`:

```xml
<servlet>
    <servlet-name>jersey</servlet-name>
    <servlet-class>org.glassfish.jersey.servlet.ServletContainer</servlet-class>
    <init-param>
        <param-name>jakarta.ws.rs.Application</param-name>
        <param-value>com.company.caseapp.config.CaseApplication</param-value>
    </init-param>
    <load-on-startup>1</load-on-startup>
</servlet>

<servlet-mapping>
    <servlet-name>jersey</servlet-name>
    <url-pattern>/api/*</url-pattern>
</servlet-mapping>
```

Final invariant:

```text
Semua endpoint API production berada di bawah:
/<context-path>/api/...
```

---

# 20. Mini Exercise untuk Repo Anda

Ambil satu service Jersey/JAX-RS, lalu jawab:

```text
1. Application class production yang dipakai apa?
2. Jersey didaftarkan via web.xml, annotation, atau programmatic?
3. Servlet mapping Jersey apa?
4. Apakah @ApplicationPath dipakai?
5. Apakah ada risiko double base path?
6. Resource registration explicit atau scanning?
7. Package scanning-nya package apa?
8. Semua ExceptionMapper penting ter-register?
9. JSON provider yang aktif apa?
10. Ada berapa ResourceConfig/Application class di repo?
11. Apakah dependency tree masih mengandung javax.ws.rs/javax.servlet?
12. Endpoint health/readiness lewat Jersey atau servlet/filter terpisah?
```

Output yang Anda cari:

```text
Production REST entry point:
  com.company.caseapp.config.CaseApplication

Container registration:
  web.xml → org.glassfish.jersey.servlet.ServletContainer

Servlet mapping:
  /api/*

Context path:
  /case-service

Final base URL:
  /case-service/api

Resource strategy:
  explicit registration

Provider strategy:
  explicit registration

Risk:
  no @ApplicationPath duplication
```

---

# Ringkasan Seri 3

Inti dari Jersey/JAX-RS registration:

```text
Servlet Container tidak peduli @Path sampai Jersey Servlet terdaftar.
Jersey Servlet tidak tahu resource sampai Application/ResourceConfig benar.
ResourceConfig tidak tahu provider kecuali provider discan atau diregister.
Endpoint tidak reliable sampai path final terbukti lewat test.
```

Prinsip praktis:

```text
1. Pilih satu sumber base path: web.xml servlet mapping atau @ApplicationPath.
2. Untuk service critical, explicit registration lebih mudah diaudit.
3. Package scanning boleh, tapi harus spesifik.
4. Provider penting seperti ExceptionMapper harus terdaftar eksplisit.
5. Selalu punya smoke test untuk membuktikan container → Jersey → resource aktif.
```

---

# Seri 4/12 - URL Mapping: Context Path, Servlet Path, Path Info, Reverse Proxy Path

## Tujuan seri ini

Seri ini fokus pada satu skill debugging yang sangat penting di aplikasi **Java Jersey/JAX-RS berbasis Servlet Container**:

> Membedah URL final dari client sampai resource method, lalu menentukan layer mana yang salah saat terjadi 404, redirect salah, callback URL salah, CORS salah, atau endpoint hanya gagal di environment tertentu.

Secara runtime, URL tidak hanya dipengaruhi oleh `@Path`. Ada beberapa layer:

```text
Client URL
↓
Load Balancer / API Gateway
↓
Ingress / reverse proxy rewrite
↓
Servlet Container context path
↓
Servlet mapping Jersey
↓
@ApplicationPath, jika dipakai
↓
JAX-RS @Path class
↓
JAX-RS @Path method
```

Jakarta Servlet mendefinisikan kontrak mapping request di level servlet, sedangkan Jakarta REST/JAX-RS mendefinisikan model REST resource seperti `@Path`, HTTP method annotation, dan application path. Jakarta REST 4.0 adalah release untuk Jakarta EE 11 dan menyediakan API dasar untuk membangun RESTful web services. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/servlet/6.1/jakarta-servlet-spec-6.1?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Mental Model URL Mapping

Jangan melihat endpoint hanya sebagai:

```text
GET /v1/cases/123
```

Di production, endpoint lengkap biasanya terbentuk dari beberapa prefix:

```text
https://api.company.com/regulatory/case-service/api/v1/cases/123
```

Kemungkinan decomposition:

| Layer | Nilai contoh | Penjelasan |
|---|---:|---|
| Scheme | `https` | Bisa berasal dari LB/API Gateway, bukan langsung container |
| Host | `api.company.com` | Public host |
| External prefix | `/regulatory` | Prefix gateway/domain area |
| Service prefix | `/case-service` | Bisa jadi context path atau ingress path |
| Servlet mapping | `/api/*` | Mapping request ke Jersey servlet |
| Resource class path | `/v1/cases` | `@Path` pada resource class |
| Resource method path | `/{caseId}` | `@Path` pada method |
| Query | `?includeHistory=true` | Query string |

Jadi final endpoint bukan hanya ditentukan oleh Java code.

---

# 2. Diagram URL Flow

```text
Client
  GET https://api.company.com/regulatory/case-service/api/v1/cases/123
        │
        ▼
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ API Gateway / Load Balancer                              │
│ - TLS termination                                        │
│ - host routing                                           │
│ - auth pre-check optional                                │
│ - adds X-Forwarded-* headers                             │
└──────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────┘
                       ▼
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Kubernetes Ingress / Reverse Proxy                        │
│ External path: /regulatory/case-service/api/v1/cases/123  │
│ May rewrite to: /case-service/api/v1/cases/123            │
│ Or rewrite to: /api/v1/cases/123                          │
└──────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────┘
                       ▼
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Servlet Container                                         │
│ contextPath  = /case-service                              │
│ servletPath  = /api                                       │
│ pathInfo     = /v1/cases/123                              │
│ queryString  = includeHistory=true                        │
└──────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────┘
                       ▼
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Jersey / JAX-RS                                           │
│ Resource class: @Path("/v1/cases")                        │
│ Resource method: @GET @Path("/{caseId}")                  │
│ Path param: caseId = 123                                  │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

# 3. Core Servlet Path Fields

Di Servlet API, beberapa method `HttpServletRequest` sangat penting untuk membedah URL. Dokumentasi `HttpServletRequest#getContextPath()` menjelaskan bahwa context path adalah bagian URI yang menunjukkan context request, selalu muncul pertama di request URI, dimulai dengan `/`, tidak berakhir dengan `/`, dan untuk root context hasilnya string kosong. ([Apache Tomcat](https://tomcat.apache.org/tomcat-11.0-doc/servletapi/jakarta/servlet/http/HttpServletRequest.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

| Method | Contoh nilai | Fungsi |
|---|---:|---|
| `getRequestURI()` | `/case-service/api/v1/cases/123` | URI path yang diterima container, tanpa scheme/host/query |
| `getContextPath()` | `/case-service` | Root aplikasi web |
| `getServletPath()` | `/api` | Bagian path yang match servlet mapping |
| `getPathInfo()` | `/v1/cases/123` | Sisa path setelah servlet path |
| `getQueryString()` | `includeHistory=true` | Raw query string |
| `getRequestURL()` | `http://internal-host:8080/...` | Full URL menurut container |
| `getHeader("Host")` | `api.company.com` atau internal host | Host header |
| `getHeader("X-Forwarded-Proto")` | `https` | Scheme asli dari proxy/LB |
| `getHeader("X-Forwarded-For")` | client/proxy chain | Chain IP dari proxy |

Dokumentasi `getServletPath()` juga penting: method ini mengembalikan path servlet yang dipanggil sesuai request URL, atau string kosong jika servlet diproses menggunakan pattern `/*`. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/servlet/6.1/apidocs/jakarta.servlet/jakarta/servlet/http/httpservletrequest?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 4. Contoh Mapping End-to-End

Misal konfigurasi:

```xml
<servlet-mapping>
    <servlet-name>jersey</servlet-name>
    <url-pattern>/api/*</url-pattern>
</servlet-mapping>
```

Resource:

```java
@Path("/v1/cases")
public class CaseResource {

    @GET
    @Path("/{caseId}")
    public CaseResponse getCase(@PathParam("caseId") String caseId) {
        return new CaseResponse(caseId, "OPEN");
    }

    public record CaseResponse(String caseId, String status) {
    }
}
```

Request:

```text
GET /case-service/api/v1/cases/123?includeHistory=true
```

Maka decomposition idealnya:

| Komponen | Nilai |
|---|---:|
| Context path | `/case-service` |
| Servlet mapping | `/api/*` |
| Servlet path | `/api` |
| Path info | `/v1/cases/123` |
| JAX-RS class path | `/v1/cases` |
| JAX-RS method path | `/{caseId}` |
| Path param | `caseId = 123` |
| Query string | `includeHistory=true` |

Final formula:

```text
Final path =
  contextPath
+ servletPath / servlet mapping base
+ JAX-RS class @Path
+ JAX-RS method @Path
```

Dalam contoh:

```text
/case-service
+ /api
+ /v1/cases
+ /123

= /case-service/api/v1/cases/123
```

---

# 5. Mapping Scenario yang Paling Sering Muncul

## 5.1 Scenario A - WAR context path + servlet mapping `/api/*`

Ini pattern yang paling eksplisit.

```text
WAR name:
  case-service.war

Context path:
  /case-service

Jersey servlet mapping:
  /api/*

Resource:
  @Path("/v1/cases")

Final:
  /case-service/api/v1/cases
```

Kelebihan:

```text
Mudah dipahami.
Mudah dipisahkan antara app context dan API route.
Cocok untuk external Servlet Container.
```

Risiko:

```text
Context path bisa berubah jika nama WAR berubah.
Container bisa override context path.
Ingress bisa menambahkan/menghapus prefix.
```

---

## 5.2 Scenario B - Root context + servlet mapping `/api/*`

```text
Context path:
  ""

Jersey servlet mapping:
  /api/*

Resource:
  @Path("/v1/cases")

Final:
  /api/v1/cases
```

Biasanya dipakai di containerized microservice yang satu pod hanya berisi satu service.

Kelebihan:

```text
URL internal sederhana.
Cocok untuk Kubernetes service per microservice.
```

Risiko:

```text
Jika gateway menambahkan prefix /case-service, aplikasi mungkin tidak sadar prefix publiknya.
Absolute URL/callback bisa salah jika forwarded headers tidak dikonfigurasi.
```

---

## 5.3 Scenario C - `@ApplicationPath("/api")` tanpa `web.xml`

```java
@ApplicationPath("/api")
public class CaseApplication extends ResourceConfig {
    public CaseApplication() {
        register(CaseResource.class);
    }
}
```

Resource:

```java
@Path("/v1/cases")
public class CaseResource {
}
```

Final:

```text
/context/api/v1/cases
```

Ini valid dan ringkas, tapi saat onboarding harus dipastikan apakah `@ApplicationPath` benar-benar menjadi sumber base path utama. Jakarta REST tutorial menunjukkan pola penggunaan `@ApplicationPath` pada subclass `Application` untuk mendefinisikan base URI aplikasi REST. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/learn/docs/jakartaee-tutorial/current/websvcs/rest/rest.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

## 5.4 Scenario D - Double `/api`

Bug umum:

```xml
<url-pattern>/api/*</url-pattern>
```

dan:

```java
@ApplicationPath("/api")
public class CaseApplication extends ResourceConfig {
}
```

Lalu resource:

```java
@Path("/v1/cases")
public class CaseResource {
}
```

Developer bisa bingung apakah final-nya:

```text
/case-service/api/v1/cases
```

atau:

```text
/case-service/api/api/v1/cases
```

Practical rule:

```text
Pilih satu sumber base path:
- servlet mapping di web.xml
atau
- @ApplicationPath

Jangan keduanya sama-sama menentukan /api kecuali sudah dibuktikan lewat test.
```

---

# 6. Reverse Proxy dan Ingress Rewrite

Di Kubernetes atau environment on-prem/cloud, request biasanya tidak langsung masuk ke Servlet Container.

Contoh public route:

```text
https://api.company.com/regulatory/case-service/v1/cases/123
```

Namun aplikasi internal mungkin hanya melihat:

```text
/api/v1/cases/123
```

karena ingress melakukan rewrite.

Ingress NGINX memiliki mekanisme rewrite lewat annotation seperti `nginx.ingress.kubernetes.io/rewrite-target`, dan dokumentasinya menunjukkan contoh rewrite path yang mengubah path external sebelum diteruskan ke service backend. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.github.io/ingress-nginx/examples/rewrite/?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh konseptual:

```yaml
apiVersion: networking.k8s.io/v1
kind: Ingress
metadata:
  name: case-service-ingress
  annotations:
    nginx.ingress.kubernetes.io/rewrite-target: /api/$2
spec:
  rules:
    - host: api.company.com
      http:
        paths:
          - path: /case-service(/|$)(.*)
            pathType: ImplementationSpecific
            backend:
              service:
                name: case-service
                port:
                  number: 8080
```

Dengan contoh di atas:

```text
External:
  /case-service/v1/cases/123

Internal forwarded to app:
  /api/v1/cases/123
```

Artinya aplikasi tidak melihat `/case-service` sebagai `contextPath`. Itu hanya prefix publik di ingress.

---

# 7. Forwarded Headers

Saat TLS termination terjadi di load balancer atau gateway, Servlet Container sering menerima request sebagai HTTP internal:

```text
http://case-service:8080/api/v1/cases/123
```

Padahal client memanggil:

```text
https://api.company.com/case-service/v1/cases/123
```

Agar aplikasi tahu scheme/host/client IP asli, proxy biasanya mengirim header seperti:

```text
X-Forwarded-For
X-Forwarded-Proto
X-Forwarded-Host
X-Forwarded-Port
Forwarded
```

Tomcat menyediakan `RemoteIpValve` yang dapat mengganti apparent client remote IP/hostname berdasarkan header proxy/load balancer seperti `X-Forwarded-For`, serta mengganti scheme dan server port berdasarkan header seperti `X-Forwarded-Proto`. ([Apache Tomcat](https://tomcat.apache.org/tomcat-11.0-doc/api/org/apache/catalina/valves/RemoteIpValve.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Practical consequence:

| Tanpa proxy awareness | Gejala |
|---|---|
| `request.getScheme()` = `http` | Redirect/callback memakai `http://` padahal public HTTPS |
| `request.getServerName()` = internal host | Link absolute salah |
| `request.getRemoteAddr()` = IP proxy | Audit log client IP salah |
| secure cookie tidak benar | Auth/session behavior bermasalah |
| CORS origin/redirect mismatch | Frontend gagal |

---

# 8. Diagnostic Filter: Wajib Punya Saat Debug Mapping

Gunakan filter ini untuk membedah apa yang benar-benar diterima aplikasi.

```java
package com.company.caseapp.web;

import jakarta.servlet.Filter;
import jakarta.servlet.FilterChain;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletException;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletResponse;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;

import java.io.IOException;

public class UrlMappingDebugFilter implements Filter {

    @Override
    public void doFilter(
            ServletRequest request,
            ServletResponse response,
            FilterChain chain
    ) throws IOException, ServletException {

        HttpServletRequest r = (HttpServletRequest) request;

        System.out.printf("""
                URL_DEBUG method=%s
                  requestURI=%s
                  requestURL=%s
                  contextPath=%s
                  servletPath=%s
                  pathInfo=%s
                  queryString=%s
                  scheme=%s
                  serverName=%s
                  serverPort=%d
                  remoteAddr=%s
                  host=%s
                  xForwardedFor=%s
                  xForwardedProto=%s
                  xForwardedHost=%s
                  xForwardedPort=%s
                %n""",
                r.getMethod(),
                r.getRequestURI(),
                r.getRequestURL(),
                r.getContextPath(),
                r.getServletPath(),
                r.getPathInfo(),
                r.getQueryString(),
                r.getScheme(),
                r.getServerName(),
                r.getServerPort(),
                r.getRemoteAddr(),
                r.getHeader("Host"),
                r.getHeader("X-Forwarded-For"),
                r.getHeader("X-Forwarded-Proto"),
                r.getHeader("X-Forwarded-Host"),
                r.getHeader("X-Forwarded-Port")
        );

        chain.doFilter(request, response);
    }
}
```

Register sementara di `web.xml`:

```xml
<filter>
    <filter-name>urlMappingDebugFilter</filter-name>
    <filter-class>com.company.caseapp.web.UrlMappingDebugFilter</filter-class>
</filter>

<filter-mapping>
    <filter-name>urlMappingDebugFilter</filter-name>
    <url-pattern>/*</url-pattern>
</filter-mapping>
```

Jangan aktifkan permanen di production dengan log lengkap, karena bisa terlalu verbose dan berpotensi mengekspos metadata internal. Untuk production, log secukupnya: method, URI, status, latency, correlation id, remote IP yang sudah benar, dan selected forwarded metadata.

---

# 9. Diagnostic Endpoint di Jersey

Tambahkan endpoint internal/debug-only jika diperlukan.

```java
package com.company.caseapp.resource;

import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import jakarta.ws.rs.GET;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Path;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Context;

@Path("/v1/internal/debug/url")
public class UrlDebugResource {

    @Context
    private HttpServletRequest request;

    @GET
    public UrlDebugResponse debug() {
        return new UrlDebugResponse(
                request.getMethod(),
                request.getRequestURI(),
                request.getRequestURL().toString(),
                request.getContextPath(),
                request.getServletPath(),
                request.getPathInfo(),
                request.getQueryString(),
                request.getScheme(),
                request.getServerName(),
                request.getServerPort(),
                request.getHeader("Host"),
                request.getHeader("X-Forwarded-For"),
                request.getHeader("X-Forwarded-Proto"),
                request.getHeader("X-Forwarded-Host"),
                request.getHeader("X-Forwarded-Port")
        );
    }

    public record UrlDebugResponse(
            String method,
            String requestUri,
            String requestUrl,
            String contextPath,
            String servletPath,
            String pathInfo,
            String queryString,
            String scheme,
            String serverName,
            int serverPort,
            String host,
            String xForwardedFor,
            String xForwardedProto,
            String xForwardedHost,
            String xForwardedPort
    ) {
    }
}
```

Catatan keamanan:

```text
Endpoint seperti ini jangan dibuka publik.
Batasi hanya internal profile, admin network, atau non-production.
```

---

# 10. Practical 404 Decision Tree

## 10.1 404 sebelum container

Gejala:

```text
Tidak ada access log container.
Tidak ada log filter.
Tidak ada log Jersey.
```

Kemungkinan:

```text
DNS salah
LB route salah
API Gateway route salah
Ingress rule salah
Kubernetes Service selector salah
Pod tidak ready
Port service salah
Network policy/firewall
TLS/SNI mismatch
```

Debug:

```bash
curl -i https://api.company.com/case-service/api/v1/cases/123
kubectl get ingress
kubectl describe ingress case-service-ingress
kubectl get svc case-service
kubectl get endpoints case-service
kubectl logs deploy/case-service
```

---

## 10.2 404 di Servlet Container

Gejala:

```text
Access log container ada.
Filter untuk /* mungkin jalan.
Jersey resource tidak masuk.
```

Kemungkinan:

```text
Context path salah
WAR belum deploy
Servlet mapping salah
Jersey servlet tidak terdaftar
Request tidak match url-pattern Jersey
```

Debug:

```text
Log:
requestURI
contextPath
servletPath
pathInfo
```

Contoh:

```text
requestURI=/case-service/rest/v1/cases/123
contextPath=/case-service
servletPath=
pathInfo=null
```

Padahal Jersey mapping:

```text
/api/*
```

Maka request `/rest/...` tidak masuk Jersey.

---

## 10.3 404 di Jersey

Gejala:

```text
Request sudah masuk servlet mapping Jersey.
Tapi tidak ada resource method match.
```

Kemungkinan:

```text
@Path class salah
@Path method salah
HTTP method salah
Resource tidak register
@Consumes/@Produces mismatch
Trailing slash behavior
Path parameter regex tidak match
```

Contoh:

```java
@Path("/v1/case")
public class CaseResource {
}
```

Client memanggil:

```text
/v1/cases/123
```

Bug-nya pluralization: `case` vs `cases`.

---

# 11. Mapping Test Matrix

Saat onboarding, buat matrix sederhana.

Misal:

```text
Context path production:
  /case-service

Jersey mapping:
  /api/*

Resource:
  @Path("/v1/cases")
```

Test:

| Test URL | Expected | Diagnosis jika gagal |
|---|---:|---|
| `/case-service` | container/app root reachable atau 404 terkontrol | Deployment/context |
| `/case-service/api` | masuk Jersey, mungkin 404 Jersey | Servlet mapping |
| `/case-service/api/v1/health` | 200 | Jersey resource registration |
| `/case-service/api/v1/cases/123` | 200/expected business status | Resource method |
| `/api/v1/cases/123` | biasanya 404 jika context path tidak root | Context path confusion |
| `/case-service/v1/cases/123` | biasanya 404 jika servlet mapping `/api/*` | Missing servlet prefix |
| `/case-service/api/api/v1/cases/123` | biasanya 404 | Double `/api` confusion |

---

# 12. Common Bug: Ingress Prefix Tidak Sama dengan Context Path

## Case

Public URL:

```text
https://api.company.com/case-service/v1/cases/123
```

Aplikasi internal expected:

```text
/case-service/api/v1/cases/123
```

Ingress rewrite:

```text
/case-service/(.*) -> /$1
```

Maka container menerima:

```text
/v1/cases/123
```

Padahal Jersey mapping:

```text
/api/*
```

Akibat:

```text
404 karena /v1/cases/123 tidak match /api/*
```

Fix options:

```text
Option A:
  Ubah ingress rewrite agar menjadi /api/$1

Option B:
  Ubah Jersey servlet mapping menjadi /*

Option C:
  Ubah public route agar menyertakan /api

Option D:
  Gunakan context path root dan selaraskan semua route di gateway
```

Rekomendasi praktis:

```text
Jangan pilih fix secara lokal.
Tentukan canonical contract:
- public API path
- internal app path
- gateway rewrite rule
- servlet mapping
- OpenAPI server URL
```

---

# 13. Common Bug: Double Prefix

## Case

Gateway route:

```text
/case-service/api/*
```

Ingress tidak rewrite.

WAR context path:

```text
/case-service
```

Jersey mapping:

```text
/api/*
```

Client memanggil:

```text
/case-service/api/v1/cases/123
```

Container menerima:

```text
/case-service/api/v1/cases/123
```

Ini benar.

Tapi jika ingress menambahkan prefix lagi:

```text
/case-service/api -> /case-service/api
```

atau upstream client juga menambahkan base path dari config, bisa terjadi:

```text
/case-service/case-service/api/v1/cases/123
```

atau:

```text
/case-service/api/api/v1/cases/123
```

Debug dengan log:

```text
requestURI=/case-service/api/api/v1/cases/123
contextPath=/case-service
servletPath=/api
pathInfo=/api/v1/cases/123
```

Jika `pathInfo` masih mengandung `/api`, berarti ada double API prefix sebelum masuk JAX-RS.

---

# 14. Common Bug: Absolute URL Salah

## Case

API mengembalikan link:

```json
{
  "callbackUrl": "http://case-service:8080/api/v1/callback/abc"
}
```

Padahal seharusnya:

```json
{
  "callbackUrl": "https://api.company.com/case-service/api/v1/callback/abc"
}
```

Penyebab:

```java
String url = request.getRequestURL().toString();
```

Tanpa mempertimbangkan reverse proxy headers.

Problem:

```text
Container melihat internal URL.
Client butuh public URL.
```

Fix design:

```text
Untuk URL publik, jangan asal pakai request.getRequestURL().
Gunakan configured public base URL dari config resmi:
PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL=https://api.company.com/case-service/api

Atau pastikan forwarded headers diproses benar oleh container/framework.
```

Rekomendasi:

```text
Untuk callback/payment/webhook/external integration:
  gunakan explicit external base URL dari configuration.

Untuk internal self-link/debug:
  boleh pakai request metadata, dengan proxy awareness yang benar.
```

---

# 15. Common Bug: Client IP Salah

## Case

Audit log mencatat:

```text
remoteAddr=10.0.12.34
```

Padahal itu IP ingress/proxy, bukan client.

Jika Tomcat berada di belakang reverse proxy, dokumentasi Tomcat menjelaskan bahwa client information di access log dapat merepresentasikan reverse proxy, browser, atau kombinasi keduanya tergantung konfigurasi Tomcat dan reverse proxy. ([Apache Tomcat](https://tomcat.apache.org/tomcat-9.0-doc/config/valve.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Approach:

```text
[ ] Konfigurasi trusted proxy di infrastructure/container
[ ] Jangan langsung percaya X-Forwarded-For dari internet publik
[ ] Ambil client IP setelah proxy chain diproses oleh trusted component
[ ] Audit log harus eksplisit: clientIp, proxyIp, xForwardedFor raw jika aman
```

Anti-pattern:

```java
String clientIp = request.getHeader("X-Forwarded-For");
```

Lebih aman:

```text
Biarkan gateway/container trusted memproses forwarded headers,
lalu gunakan remoteAddr yang sudah dinormalisasi,
atau parsing X-Forwarded-For hanya jika request datang dari trusted proxy.
```

---

# 16. Servlet Mapping Pattern: `/`, `/*`, `/api/*`

## 16.1 `/api/*`

```xml
<url-pattern>/api/*</url-pattern>
```

Makna praktis:

```text
Hanya request di bawah /api yang masuk servlet.
```

Contoh:

```text
/api/v1/cases       → match
/health             → tidak match
/static/app.js      → tidak match
```

Cocok untuk REST API.

---

## 16.2 `/*`

```xml
<url-pattern>/*</url-pattern>
```

Makna praktis:

```text
Hampir semua request di context masuk servlet ini.
```

Efek:

```text
/v1/cases           → match
/health             → match
/static/app.js      → match
```

Cocok jika service murni API dan semua request ingin dikendalikan Jersey.

Risiko:

```text
Static/default servlet bisa ketutup.
Health endpoint non-Jersey bisa ikut tertangkap.
Error handling path bisa berubah.
```

---

## 16.3 `/`

```xml
<url-pattern>/</url-pattern>
```

Ini biasanya default servlet mapping semantics dan berbeda dari `/*` dalam detail matching. Untuk aplikasi Jersey microservice, lebih aman pakai `/api/*` atau `/*` secara eksplisit sesuai desain, bukan mengandalkan `/` tanpa memahami efeknya.

Practical recommendation:

```text
Untuk service API biasa:
  gunakan /api/*

Untuk service yang seluruh context adalah REST API:
  gunakan /*

Hindari / kecuali memang memahami default servlet mapping semantics.
```

---

# 17. Practical Implementation: Central Route Contract

Buat dokumen kecil di repo:

```text
docs/runtime-routing.md
```

Isi minimal:

```md
# Runtime Routing

## Public URL

https://api.company.com/case-service

## Ingress

External path:
  /case-service(/|$)(.*)

Rewrite:
  /api/$2

## Container

Context path:
  ""

Port:
  8080

## Jersey

Servlet mapping:
  /api/*

ApplicationPath:
  not used

## REST Resources

Health:
  GET /api/v1/health

Cases:
  GET /api/v1/cases/{caseId}
  POST /api/v1/cases

## Final Examples

Public:
  GET https://api.company.com/case-service/v1/cases/123

Internal to app:
  GET http://case-service:8080/api/v1/cases/123
```

Tujuannya bukan dokumentasi cantik, tapi mencegah debat saat incident:

```text
Apakah /case-service itu context path?
Apakah /api itu gateway prefix?
Apakah ingress rewrite menghapus prefix?
Apakah OpenAPI server URL benar?
```

---

# 18. Practical Implementation: Route Contract Test

Buat smoke test sederhana terhadap deployed service.

```java
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.net.URI;
import java.net.http.HttpClient;
import java.net.http.HttpRequest;
import java.net.http.HttpResponse;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;

class RouteContractIT {

    private final HttpClient client = HttpClient.newHttpClient();

    @Test
    void healthEndpointShouldBeReachableThroughCanonicalPath() throws Exception {
        HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
                .uri(URI.create("http://localhost:8080/case-service/api/v1/health"))
                .GET()
                .build();

        HttpResponse<String> response =
                client.send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());

        assertEquals(200, response.statusCode());
    }

    @Test
    void missingApiPrefixShouldNotAccidentallyWork() throws Exception {
        HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
                .uri(URI.create("http://localhost:8080/case-service/v1/health"))
                .GET()
                .build();

        HttpResponse<String> response =
                client.send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());

        assertEquals(404, response.statusCode());
    }
}
```

Kenapa test negative berguna?

```text
Agar route contract tidak berubah diam-diam.
Jika /case-service/v1/health tiba-tiba 200,
berarti ada mapping baru atau rewrite baru yang perlu diaudit.
```

---

# 19. Practical Implementation: Build URL dengan Config

Untuk external callback URL:

```java
public final class PublicUrlBuilder {

    private final String publicApiBaseUrl;

    public PublicUrlBuilder(String publicApiBaseUrl) {
        this.publicApiBaseUrl = trimTrailingSlash(publicApiBaseUrl);
    }

    public String caseCallbackUrl(String callbackId) {
        return publicApiBaseUrl + "/v1/callbacks/" + encodePathSegment(callbackId);
    }

    private static String trimTrailingSlash(String value) {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("publicApiBaseUrl is required");
        }

        String result = value;
        while (result.endsWith("/")) {
            result = result.substring(0, result.length() - 1);
        }
        return result;
    }

    private static String encodePathSegment(String value) {
        return java.net.URLEncoder.encode(value, java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8)
                .replace("+", "%20");
    }
}
```

Config:

```text
PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL=https://api.company.com/case-service/api
```

Jangan hardcode:

```java
String url = "http://localhost:8080/api/v1/callbacks/" + id;
```

Jangan juga sembarang:

```java
String url = request.getRequestURL().toString();
```

Untuk callback, webhook, payment redirect, document signing callback, atau S2S public link, **external base URL sebaiknya explicit config**.

---

# 20. Practical Troubleshooting Table

| Gejala | Layer paling mungkin | Cek cepat |
|---|---|---|
| Tidak ada access log app | Gateway/Ingress/Service routing | LB log, ingress log, service endpoint |
| Access log ada, filter tidak jalan | Filter mapping/dispatcher/path | `url-pattern`, dispatcher type |
| Filter jalan, Jersey tidak | Servlet mapping/Jersey registration | `servletPath`, `pathInfo`, `web.xml` |
| Jersey 404 | Resource path/method/registration | `@Path`, HTTP method, ResourceConfig |
| Local OK, Kubernetes 404 | Ingress rewrite/context mismatch | compare internal vs external path |
| HTTPS public jadi HTTP link | Forwarded proto tidak diproses | `X-Forwarded-Proto`, RemoteIpValve/config |
| Audit IP = proxy IP | Forwarded IP belum diproses | trusted proxy config |
| Callback URL salah host | Pakai internal request URL | public base URL config |
| `/api/api/...` muncul | double prefix | ingress rewrite + servlet mapping + `@ApplicationPath` |
| `/case-service/case-service/...` muncul | double service prefix | gateway base path + app context path |

---

# 21. Checklist Onboarding URL Mapping

Saat membuka service baru, jawab ini:

```text
[ ] Public base URL service apa?
[ ] Apakah ada API Gateway prefix?
[ ] Apakah ada Kubernetes ingress path?
[ ] Apakah ingress melakukan rewrite?
[ ] Apakah service memakai root context atau context path tertentu?
[ ] Apakah context path berasal dari nama WAR?
[ ] Apa Jersey servlet mapping?
[ ] Apakah @ApplicationPath dipakai?
[ ] Apa base path OpenAPI?
[ ] Apakah health/readiness berada di path yang sama dengan API?
[ ] Apakah absolute URL dibuat dari request atau config?
[ ] Apakah forwarded headers diproses oleh container?
[ ] Apakah access log mencatat client IP asli atau proxy IP?
[ ] Apakah route contract punya integration/smoke test?
```

Output yang ideal:

```text
Public URL:
  https://api.company.com/case-service

Ingress:
  /case-service(/|$)(.*) -> /api/$2

Container:
  contextPath = ""
  port = 8080

Jersey:
  servlet mapping = /api/*
  @ApplicationPath = not used

Example:
  Public  GET /case-service/v1/cases/123
  App     GET /api/v1/cases/123
```

---

# 22. Anti-Pattern

| Anti-pattern | Kenapa buruk | Alternatif |
|---|---|---|
| Tidak tahu context path production | Debug 404 lambat | Dokumentasikan route contract |
| `@ApplicationPath("/api")` + servlet mapping `/api/*` tanpa test | Double prefix/confusion | Pilih satu |
| Ingress rewrite tidak terdokumentasi | Local/prod path beda | Tulis external path → internal path |
| Membuat callback URL dari `request.getRequestURL()` | Bisa pakai host/scheme internal | Gunakan public base URL config |
| Langsung percaya `X-Forwarded-For` | Bisa dipalsukan jika tidak lewat trusted proxy | Trusted proxy/container config |
| Semua service pakai path berbeda tanpa standard | Gateway dan OpenAPI kacau | Tetapkan canonical route convention |
| Debug hanya dari `@Path` | Mengabaikan gateway/container mapping | Trace full path chain |
| Servlet mapping `/*` tanpa sadar | Semua route tertangkap Jersey | Gunakan `/api/*` jika butuh boundary jelas |

---

# 23. Mini Exercise

Ambil satu service internal dan isi template ini:

```text
Service name:
  ...

Public URL:
  ...

Gateway route:
  ...

Ingress path:
  ...

Ingress rewrite:
  ...

Kubernetes service port:
  ...

Container port:
  ...

Context path:
  ...

Jersey servlet mapping:
  ...

@ApplicationPath:
  ...

Resource class @Path:
  ...

Resource method @Path:
  ...

Final public endpoint example:
  ...

Final internal endpoint example:
  ...

Does app generate absolute URL?
  yes/no

If yes, source:
  request metadata / config / other

Forwarded headers processed?
  yes/no/unknown

Client IP source:
  remoteAddr / X-Forwarded-For / trusted proxy normalized
```

Kalau banyak yang `unknown`, itu red flag onboarding. Bukan karena Anda belum paham Java, tapi karena runtime route contract belum eksplisit.

---

# Ringkasan Seri 4

URL di aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS bukan hanya `@Path`.

Model yang benar:

```text
Public URL
  = gateway prefix
  + ingress rewrite behavior
  + servlet container context path
  + servlet mapping
  + optional @ApplicationPath
  + JAX-RS @Path class
  + JAX-RS @Path method
```

Debugging rule paling penting:

```text
Jangan tebak path.
Log actual requestURI, contextPath, servletPath, pathInfo, queryString,
scheme, host, forwarded headers, dan remote address.
```

Production invariant:

```text
Harus ada satu canonical route contract:
public path → internal app path → servlet mapping → JAX-RS resource path.
```
