# Seri 5/12 - Filter Chain Practical: Logging, Correlation ID, Auth, Tenant/User Context

## Tujuan seri ini

Seri ini fokus pada penggunaan **Servlet Filter** sebagai boundary praktis sebelum request masuk ke Jersey/JAX-RS.

Di aplikasi **Java Jersey/JAX-RS microservice**, Servlet Filter biasanya dipakai untuk concern yang harus terjadi sangat awal di HTTP pipeline:

```text id="whztbs"
HTTP request
  → Servlet Container
  → Servlet Filter Chain
  → Jersey Servlet
  → JAX-RS filter/interceptor
  → Resource method
  → Service layer
```

Secara resmi, `Filter` adalah object yang melakukan filtering task pada request menuju resource, response dari resource, atau keduanya. Filter bekerja lewat method `doFilter`, dan `FilterChain` dipakai untuk meneruskan request/response ke entity berikutnya di chain. ([Apache Tomcat](https://tomcat.apache.org/tomcat-11.0-doc/servletapi/jakarta/servlet/Filter.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Mental Model Filter Chain

Servlet Filter adalah **pre-Jersey HTTP boundary**.

Artinya, filter bisa berjalan bahkan sebelum Jersey melakukan:

```text id="7hub03"
resource matching
@Path matching
@Consumes / @Produces negotiation
JAX-RS ContainerRequestFilter
ExceptionMapper
MessageBodyReader / MessageBodyWriter
```

Diagram praktis:

```text id="9qnas0"
Client
  ↓
API Gateway / Ingress
  ↓
Servlet Container
  ↓
┌───────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Servlet Filter Chain                           │
│                                               │
│  1. RequestIdFilter                            │
│  2. AccessLogFilter                            │
│  3. CorsFilter                                 │
│  4. SecurityPreCheckFilter                     │
│  5. TenantContextFilter                        │
│  6. RequestSizeGuardFilter                     │
│                                               │
└───────────────────────┬───────────────────────┘
                        ↓
Jersey ServletContainer
  ↓
JAX-RS ContainerRequestFilter
  ↓
Resource method
  ↓
Service / MyBatis / Camunda / PostgreSQL
```

Rule utama:

```text id="7r0rv0"
Servlet Filter:
  concern yang harus terjadi sebelum Jersey

JAX-RS Filter:
  concern yang spesifik ke resource/API layer setelah masuk Jersey

Service layer:
  business rule, authorization business, orchestration, transaction
```

---

# 2. Decomposition Map

| No | Area | Fungsi praktis | Implementasi umum | Risiko utama |
|---:|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Filter ordering** | Menentukan urutan request diproses | `web.xml` order atau annotation/container-specific order | Auth jalan sebelum correlation id, log kehilangan trace id |
| 2 | **Correlation ID** | Trace request lintas log/service | Ambil/generate `X-Correlation-Id`, simpan di request attribute/MDC/context | Trace putus, duplicate id, tidak cleanup |
| 3 | **Access logging** | Catat method, URI, status, latency | Filter dengan response wrapper | Salah status, log secret, log terlalu noisy |
| 4 | **Security pre-check** | Reject request tanpa token/header wajib | Short-circuit 401/403 sebelum Jersey | Business auth terlalu banyak di filter |
| 5 | **Tenant context** | Menentukan tenant/user boundary | Header/claim → context object → service | Tenant leak via `ThreadLocal` |
| 6 | **User context** | Menyediakan authenticated user metadata | Principal/claim/header trusted → context | Percaya header dari client langsung |
| 7 | **CORS** | Handle browser cross-origin request | Preflight `OPTIONS`, allowed origin/header/method | Wildcard origin + credentials |
| 8 | **Request guard** | Lindungi app dari request tidak valid | size check, content-type check, method check | Membaca body terlalu awal |
| 9 | **Response headers** | Tambah security/trace headers | `X-Content-Type-Options`, `X-Correlation-Id` | Header diset setelah response committed |
| 10 | **Error boundary** | Tangani error sebelum Jersey | JSON short-circuit minimal | Error schema beda dari Jersey |
| 11 | **Dispatcher type** | Kontrol filter berjalan untuk request/error/async | `REQUEST`, `ERROR`, `ASYNC`, dll | Filter dobel jalan saat error dispatch |
| 12 | **ThreadLocal cleanup** | Hindari context bocor antar request | `try/finally clear()` | User/tenant/correlation id bocor ke request lain |

Servlet API memiliki `DispatcherType`, dan `ServletRequest#getDispatcherType()` dapat dipakai untuk mengetahui dispatcher type request saat ini; ini penting karena filter bisa ikut berjalan pada request normal, error dispatch, async dispatch, forward, atau include tergantung konfigurasi. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/servlet/5.0/apidocs/jakarta/servlet/class-use/dispatchertype?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 3. Servlet Filter vs JAX-RS Filter

JAX-RS punya `ContainerRequestFilter` dan `ContainerResponseFilter`. `ContainerRequestFilter` bisa berjalan sebagai pre-match filter untuk mengubah input ke matching algorithm, atau sebagai post-match filter setelah resource method berhasil dimatch. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/apidocs/jakarta.ws.rs/jakarta/ws/rs/container/containerrequestfilter?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Jersey juga mendokumentasikan bahwa filters dapat memodifikasi inbound/outbound request/response, sementara interceptors lebih fokus pada entity input/output stream. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest31x/filters-and-interceptors.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

| Concern | Servlet Filter | JAX-RS Filter |
|---|---|---|
| Correlation ID paling awal | Sangat cocok | Bisa, tapi setelah masuk Jersey |
| Access log raw request | Sangat cocok | Kurang lengkap untuk request yang gagal sebelum Jersey |
| CORS global | Cocok | Bisa juga, tapi preflight bisa gagal sebelum resource |
| Auth header existence check | Cocok | Cocok jika butuh resource-aware |
| Role/permission per endpoint | Kurang ideal | Lebih cocok |
| Tenant context awal | Cocok | Bisa untuk API-level context |
| ExceptionMapper integration | Tidak langsung | Lebih natural |
| Request body/entity stream transformation | Berisiko | Interceptor/JAX-RS provider lebih cocok |
| Business authorization | Jangan | Bisa sebagian, tapi sering lebih baik di service/domain layer |

Practical rule:

```text id="e5s931"
Gunakan Servlet Filter untuk HTTP boundary.
Gunakan JAX-RS Filter untuk API/resource boundary.
Gunakan service layer untuk business decision.
```

---

# 4. Struktur Filter Chain yang Direkomendasikan

Untuk aplikasi Jersey microservice, urutan yang defensible:

```text id="k1dys4"
1. RequestId / CorrelationId Filter
2. Basic Request Metadata / Access Log Filter
3. Security Headers / CORS Filter
4. Authentication Pre-Check Filter
5. Tenant/User Context Filter
6. Request Guard Filter
7. Jersey Servlet
```

Kenapa correlation id paling awal?

```text id="y68c6b"
Supaya semua log berikutnya, termasuk auth failure dan parsing failure,
punya identifier yang sama.
```

Kenapa tenant/user context setelah auth pre-check?

```text id="x3jqpy"
Karena tenant/user context idealnya berasal dari identity yang sudah dipercaya,
bukan sekadar header random dari client.
```

---

# 5. Implementasi Context Holder

Untuk contoh praktis, kita buat `RequestContext`.

```java id="o7il7s"
package com.company.caseapp.web;

public final class RequestContext {

    private static final ThreadLocal<RequestMetadata> CURRENT = new ThreadLocal<>();

    private RequestContext() {
    }

    public static void set(RequestMetadata metadata) {
        CURRENT.set(metadata);
    }

    public static RequestMetadata current() {
        RequestMetadata metadata = CURRENT.get();
        if (metadata == null) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("RequestContext is not initialized");
        }
        return metadata;
    }

    public static RequestMetadata currentOrNull() {
        return CURRENT.get();
    }

    public static void clear() {
        CURRENT.remove();
    }

    public record RequestMetadata(
            String correlationId,
            String tenantId,
            String userId,
            String clientIp,
            String method,
            String path
    ) {
    }
}
```

Critical invariant:

```text id="mh5iq2"
Set context sebelum chain.doFilter.
Clear context di finally.
Jangan pernah menyimpan mutable request object di ThreadLocal.
Jangan pernah lupa remove().
```

Kenapa harus `remove()`?

```text id="mdmr4l"
Servlet Container memakai thread pool.
Thread yang sama bisa memproses request user A lalu request user B.
Jika ThreadLocal tidak dibersihkan, context bisa bocor.
```

---

# 6. Filter 1 - Correlation ID Filter

## 6.1 Tujuan

Correlation ID filter bertugas:

```text id="gdqie8"
[ ] membaca X-Correlation-Id dari request
[ ] generate jika kosong
[ ] validasi format/panjang
[ ] pasang ke response header
[ ] simpan ke request attribute
[ ] opsional: pasang ke logging MDC
```

## 6.2 Implementasi

```java id="naw30p"
package com.company.caseapp.web;

import jakarta.servlet.Filter;
import jakarta.servlet.FilterChain;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletException;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletResponse;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;

public class CorrelationIdFilter implements Filter {

    public static final String HEADER = "X-Correlation-Id";
    public static final String ATTRIBUTE = "correlationId";

    private static final Pattern SAFE_VALUE =
            Pattern.compile("^[a-zA-Z0-9._:-]{1,128}$");

    @Override
    public void doFilter(
            ServletRequest request,
            ServletResponse response,
            FilterChain chain
    ) throws IOException, ServletException {

        HttpServletRequest httpRequest = (HttpServletRequest) request;
        HttpServletResponse httpResponse = (HttpServletResponse) response;

        String correlationId = normalizeCorrelationId(
                httpRequest.getHeader(HEADER)
        );

        httpRequest.setAttribute(ATTRIBUTE, correlationId);
        httpResponse.setHeader(HEADER, correlationId);

        chain.doFilter(request, response);
    }

    private static String normalizeCorrelationId(String incoming) {
        if (incoming == null || incoming.isBlank()) {
            return UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        }

        String value = incoming.trim();

        if (!SAFE_VALUE.matcher(value).matches()) {
            return UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        }

        return value;
    }
}
```

## 6.3 Kenapa tidak asal menerima correlation id?

Karena header berasal dari client. Minimal perlu limit panjang dan karakter.

Anti-pattern:

```java id="q7rum6"
String correlationId = request.getHeader("X-Correlation-Id");
log.info("correlationId={}", correlationId);
```

Masalah:

```text id="sgsbjk"
Header bisa kosong.
Header bisa sangat panjang.
Header bisa mengandung karakter aneh.
Header bisa mengotori log.
```

---

# 7. Filter 2 - Access Log Filter

## 7.1 Tujuan

Access log filter mencatat:

```text id="yzqh1i"
method
request URI
query, jika aman
status code
elapsed time
correlation id
client IP
user/tenant, jika sudah tersedia
```

Masalahnya: `HttpServletResponse` standar perlu dibungkus jika ingin membaca final status dengan konsisten.

## 7.2 Response wrapper

```java id="dyxdsd"
package com.company.caseapp.web;

import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponseWrapper;

import java.io.IOException;

public class StatusCaptureResponseWrapper extends HttpServletResponseWrapper {

    private int statusCode = SC_OK;

    public StatusCaptureResponseWrapper(HttpServletResponse response) {
        super(response);
    }

    @Override
    public void setStatus(int sc) {
        this.statusCode = sc;
        super.setStatus(sc);
    }

    @Override
    public void sendError(int sc) throws IOException {
        this.statusCode = sc;
        super.sendError(sc);
    }

    @Override
    public void sendError(int sc, String msg) throws IOException {
        this.statusCode = sc;
        super.sendError(sc, msg);
    }

    @Override
    public void sendRedirect(String location) throws IOException {
        this.statusCode = SC_FOUND;
        super.sendRedirect(location);
    }

    public int getStatusCode() {
        return statusCode;
    }
}
```

## 7.3 Access log implementation

```java id="ctdj4a"
package com.company.caseapp.web;

import jakarta.servlet.Filter;
import jakarta.servlet.FilterChain;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletException;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletResponse;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.time.Duration;

public class AccessLogFilter implements Filter {

    @Override
    public void doFilter(
            ServletRequest request,
            ServletResponse response,
            FilterChain chain
    ) throws IOException, ServletException {

        HttpServletRequest httpRequest = (HttpServletRequest) request;
        StatusCaptureResponseWrapper wrappedResponse =
                new StatusCaptureResponseWrapper((HttpServletResponse) response);

        long startNanos = System.nanoTime();

        try {
            chain.doFilter(request, wrappedResponse);
        } finally {
            long elapsedMs = Duration
                    .ofNanos(System.nanoTime() - startNanos)
                    .toMillis();

            String correlationId = String.valueOf(
                    httpRequest.getAttribute(CorrelationIdFilter.ATTRIBUTE)
            );

            System.out.printf(
                    "access correlationId=%s method=%s uri=%s status=%d elapsedMs=%d remoteAddr=%s%n",
                    correlationId,
                    httpRequest.getMethod(),
                    safeUri(httpRequest),
                    wrappedResponse.getStatusCode(),
                    elapsedMs,
                    httpRequest.getRemoteAddr()
            );
        }
    }

    private static String safeUri(HttpServletRequest request) {
        String uri = request.getRequestURI();
        String query = request.getQueryString();

        if (query == null || query.isBlank()) {
            return uri;
        }

        return uri + "?<redacted-query>";
    }
}
```

## 7.4 Jangan log query string sembarangan

Query string bisa berisi:

```text id="cgbyno"
token
email
NIK/PII
search keyword sensitif
signed URL
callback secret
```

Lebih aman:

```text id="jpp9kg"
Log path.
Log whitelist query param jika perlu.
Jangan log semua query mentah secara default.
```

---

# 8. Filter 3 - CORS Filter

## 8.1 Tujuan

CORS filter menangani browser cross-origin request.

Biasanya dibutuhkan jika frontend domain berbeda dari API domain:

```text id="t5uirr"
frontend:
  https://app.company.com

api:
  https://api.company.com
```

## 8.2 Implementasi sederhana

```java id="i7t8y3"
package com.company.caseapp.web;

import jakarta.servlet.Filter;
import jakarta.servlet.FilterChain;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletException;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletResponse;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.util.Set;

public class CorsFilter implements Filter {

    private static final Set<String> ALLOWED_ORIGINS = Set.of(
            "https://app.company.com",
            "https://admin.company.com"
    );

    @Override
    public void doFilter(
            ServletRequest request,
            ServletResponse response,
            FilterChain chain
    ) throws IOException, ServletException {

        HttpServletRequest httpRequest = (HttpServletRequest) request;
        HttpServletResponse httpResponse = (HttpServletResponse) response;

        String origin = httpRequest.getHeader("Origin");

        if (origin != null && ALLOWED_ORIGINS.contains(origin)) {
            httpResponse.setHeader("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", origin);
            httpResponse.setHeader("Vary", "Origin");
            httpResponse.setHeader("Access-Control-Allow-Credentials", "true");
            httpResponse.setHeader(
                    "Access-Control-Allow-Headers",
                    "Authorization, Content-Type, X-Correlation-Id, X-Tenant-Id"
            );
            httpResponse.setHeader(
                    "Access-Control-Allow-Methods",
                    "GET, POST, PUT, PATCH, DELETE, OPTIONS"
            );
        }

        if ("OPTIONS".equalsIgnoreCase(httpRequest.getMethod())) {
            httpResponse.setStatus(HttpServletResponse.SC_NO_CONTENT);
            return;
        }

        chain.doFilter(request, response);
    }
}
```

## 8.3 Anti-pattern CORS

Jangan:

```java id="gpz7ov"
response.setHeader("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", "*");
response.setHeader("Access-Control-Allow-Credentials", "true");
```

Masalah:

```text id="mudszs"
Wildcard origin tidak boleh dipakai sembarangan bersama credential.
Untuk sistem enterprise/regulatory, allowed origin harus explicit.
```

---

# 9. Filter 4 - Authentication Pre-Check Filter

## 9.1 Tujuan

Filter ini bukan untuk semua authorization business. Ia hanya melakukan pre-check yang murah dan universal:

```text id="mzr6si"
[ ] Authorization header ada?
[ ] format Bearer token valid secara kasar?
[ ] endpoint public dilewati?
[ ] request invalid bisa ditolak sebelum masuk Jersey?
```

## 9.2 Implementasi

```java id="ppj502"
package com.company.caseapp.web;

import jakarta.servlet.Filter;
import jakarta.servlet.FilterChain;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletException;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletResponse;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.util.Set;

public class AuthenticationPreCheckFilter implements Filter {

    private static final Set<String> PUBLIC_PREFIXES = Set.of(
            "/api/v1/health",
            "/api/v1/readiness",
            "/api/v1/openapi"
    );

    @Override
    public void doFilter(
            ServletRequest request,
            ServletResponse response,
            FilterChain chain
    ) throws IOException, ServletException {

        HttpServletRequest httpRequest = (HttpServletRequest) request;
        HttpServletResponse httpResponse = (HttpServletResponse) response;

        String path = httpRequest.getServletPath()
                + (httpRequest.getPathInfo() == null ? "" : httpRequest.getPathInfo());

        if (isPublicPath(path)) {
            chain.doFilter(request, response);
            return;
        }

        String authorization = httpRequest.getHeader("Authorization");

        if (authorization == null || authorization.isBlank()) {
            writeJsonError(
                    httpResponse,
                    HttpServletResponse.SC_UNAUTHORIZED,
                    "UNAUTHORIZED",
                    "Missing Authorization header"
            );
            return;
        }

        if (!authorization.startsWith("Bearer ")) {
            writeJsonError(
                    httpResponse,
                    HttpServletResponse.SC_UNAUTHORIZED,
                    "UNAUTHORIZED",
                    "Invalid Authorization header format"
            );
            return;
        }

        chain.doFilter(request, response);
    }

    private static boolean isPublicPath(String path) {
        return PUBLIC_PREFIXES.stream().anyMatch(path::startsWith);
    }

    private static void writeJsonError(
            HttpServletResponse response,
            int status,
            String code,
            String message
    ) throws IOException {

        if (response.isCommitted()) {
            return;
        }

        response.setStatus(status);
        response.setContentType("application/json");
        response.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
        response.getWriter().write("""
                {"code":"%s","message":"%s"}
                """.formatted(code, message));
    }
}
```

## 9.3 Batas tanggung jawab filter ini

Filter ini boleh:

```text id="ml1o10"
reject missing token
reject invalid auth scheme
skip public endpoints
attach basic auth metadata
```

Filter ini sebaiknya tidak:

```text id="bec3m9"
memutuskan business permission kompleks
memanggil database untuk authorization detail tanpa alasan kuat
menjalankan Camunda authorization workflow
memuat seluruh user profile
membuat transaction business
```

Business authorization lebih defensible jika tetap berada di service/domain layer:

```text id="n99kgb"
caseService.approveCase(caseId, actor)
  → check actor can approve this case
  → check case state allows approval
  → check regulatory escalation rule
```

---

# 10. Filter 5 - Tenant/User Context Filter

## 10.1 Tujuan

Tenant/user context filter mengubah metadata HTTP/auth menjadi context internal yang bisa dipakai downstream layer.

Contoh sumber tenant:

```text id="3f7386"
JWT claim
mTLS certificate attribute
gateway-authenticated header
internal SSO principal
trusted service-to-service header
```

Jangan langsung percaya `X-Tenant-Id` dari internet publik tanpa gateway/auth trust boundary.

## 10.2 Implementasi sederhana

```java id="pofgz4"
package com.company.caseapp.web;

import jakarta.servlet.Filter;
import jakarta.servlet.FilterChain;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletException;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletResponse;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;

import java.io.IOException;

public class TenantUserContextFilter implements Filter {

    @Override
    public void doFilter(
            ServletRequest request,
            ServletResponse response,
            FilterChain chain
    ) throws IOException, ServletException {

        HttpServletRequest httpRequest = (HttpServletRequest) request;

        String correlationId = String.valueOf(
                httpRequest.getAttribute(CorrelationIdFilter.ATTRIBUTE)
        );

        String tenantId = readTrustedTenantId(httpRequest);
        String userId = readTrustedUserId(httpRequest);
        String clientIp = httpRequest.getRemoteAddr();

        RequestContext.RequestMetadata metadata =
                new RequestContext.RequestMetadata(
                        correlationId,
                        tenantId,
                        userId,
                        clientIp,
                        httpRequest.getMethod(),
                        httpRequest.getRequestURI()
                );

        try {
            RequestContext.set(metadata);
            chain.doFilter(request, response);
        } finally {
            RequestContext.clear();
        }
    }

    private static String readTrustedTenantId(HttpServletRequest request) {
        String value = request.getHeader("X-Tenant-Id");
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            return "default";
        }
        return value.trim();
    }

    private static String readTrustedUserId(HttpServletRequest request) {
        String value = request.getHeader("X-User-Id");
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            return "anonymous";
        }
        return value.trim();
    }
}
```

## 10.3 Lebih aman: bedakan trusted dan untrusted header

Untuk production, bentuk yang lebih defensible:

```java id="f9sso3"
private static String readTrustedTenantId(HttpServletRequest request) {
    String source = request.getHeader("X-Identity-Source");

    if (!"trusted-gateway".equals(source)) {
        throw new SecurityException("Untrusted identity source");
    }

    String tenantId = request.getHeader("X-Tenant-Id");

    if (tenantId == null || tenantId.isBlank()) {
        throw new SecurityException("Missing tenant id");
    }

    return tenantId.trim();
}
```

Namun lebih baik lagi jika trust boundary ditangani oleh gateway/mTLS/JWT validation yang jelas.

---

# 11. Filter 6 - Request Guard Filter

## 11.1 Tujuan

Request guard menjaga agar request yang jelas buruk ditolak lebih awal.

Contoh:

```text id="p49hc9"
Content-Type tidak didukung
header terlalu panjang
method tidak diizinkan
request body terlalu besar
missing required header
unsupported API version
```

Sebagian limit sebaiknya diatur di container/gateway, tetapi filter bisa menjadi lapisan tambahan.

## 11.2 Content-Type guard

```java id="nyunpl"
package com.company.caseapp.web;

import jakarta.servlet.Filter;
import jakarta.servlet.FilterChain;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletException;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletResponse;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.util.Set;

public class ContentTypeGuardFilter implements Filter {

    private static final Set<String> METHODS_WITH_BODY = Set.of(
            "POST", "PUT", "PATCH"
    );

    @Override
    public void doFilter(
            ServletRequest request,
            ServletResponse response,
            FilterChain chain
    ) throws IOException, ServletException {

        HttpServletRequest httpRequest = (HttpServletRequest) request;
        HttpServletResponse httpResponse = (HttpServletResponse) response;

        if (METHODS_WITH_BODY.contains(httpRequest.getMethod())) {
            String contentType = httpRequest.getContentType();

            if (contentType == null ||
                    !contentType.toLowerCase().startsWith("application/json")) {

                httpResponse.setStatus(HttpServletResponse.SC_UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE);
                httpResponse.setContentType("application/json");
                httpResponse.getWriter().write("""
                        {"code":"UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE","message":"Expected application/json"}
                        """);
                return;
            }
        }

        chain.doFilter(request, response);
    }
}
```

## 11.3 Jangan baca body di filter kecuali benar-benar perlu

Anti-pattern:

```java id="px9fjd"
String body = new String(request.getInputStream().readAllBytes());
log.info("body={}", body);
chain.doFilter(request, response);
```

Masalah:

```text id="sx20qs"
Request body stream umumnya hanya bisa dibaca sekali.
Kalau filter sudah membaca stream, Jersey MessageBodyReader bisa gagal membaca body.
```

Jika harus membaca body, gunakan wrapper dengan buffering dan size limit. Tapi untuk production, lebih baik hindari logging body default.

---

# 12. Filter Registration via `web.xml`

Filter order di `web.xml` mengikuti urutan mapping yang dievaluasi container untuk chain. Tomcat mendokumentasikan bahwa filter dapat dikonfigurasi di `$CATALINA_BASE/conf/web.xml` untuk semua web app atau di `WEB-INF/web.xml` untuk web app tertentu. ([Apache Tomcat](https://tomcat.apache.org/tomcat-11.0-doc/config/filter.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh:

```xml id="yubcid"
<filter>
    <filter-name>correlationIdFilter</filter-name>
    <filter-class>com.company.caseapp.web.CorrelationIdFilter</filter-class>
</filter>

<filter-mapping>
    <filter-name>correlationIdFilter</filter-name>
    <url-pattern>/api/*</url-pattern>
    <dispatcher>REQUEST</dispatcher>
</filter-mapping>

<filter>
    <filter-name>accessLogFilter</filter-name>
    <filter-class>com.company.caseapp.web.AccessLogFilter</filter-class>
</filter>

<filter-mapping>
    <filter-name>accessLogFilter</filter-name>
    <url-pattern>/api/*</url-pattern>
    <dispatcher>REQUEST</dispatcher>
</filter-mapping>

<filter>
    <filter-name>corsFilter</filter-name>
    <filter-class>com.company.caseapp.web.CorsFilter</filter-class>
</filter>

<filter-mapping>
    <filter-name>corsFilter</filter-name>
    <url-pattern>/api/*</url-pattern>
    <dispatcher>REQUEST</dispatcher>
</filter-mapping>

<filter>
    <filter-name>authenticationPreCheckFilter</filter-name>
    <filter-class>com.company.caseapp.web.AuthenticationPreCheckFilter</filter-class>
</filter>

<filter-mapping>
    <filter-name>authenticationPreCheckFilter</filter-name>
    <url-pattern>/api/*</url-pattern>
    <dispatcher>REQUEST</dispatcher>
</filter-mapping>

<filter>
    <filter-name>tenantUserContextFilter</filter-name>
    <filter-class>com.company.caseapp.web.TenantUserContextFilter</filter-class>
</filter>

<filter-mapping>
    <filter-name>tenantUserContextFilter</filter-name>
    <url-pattern>/api/*</url-pattern>
    <dispatcher>REQUEST</dispatcher>
</filter-mapping>
```

Practical advice:

```text id="h4so31"
Untuk service critical, web.xml lebih mudah diaudit daripada annotation tersebar.
Pastikan order tertulis eksplisit.
```

---

# 13. Registration via Annotation

Contoh:

```java id="luz35y"
import jakarta.servlet.annotation.WebFilter;

@WebFilter(
        filterName = "correlationIdFilter",
        urlPatterns = "/api/*"
)
public class CorrelationIdFilter implements Filter {
}
```

Kelebihan:

```text id="d54qsz"
ringkas
dekat dengan class
```

Kekurangan:

```text id="tjt07i"
order lebih sulit diaudit
filter tersebar
risiko ada filter aktif tanpa disadari
```

Untuk onboarding, lakukan search:

```bash id="msp7h4"
grep -R "@WebFilter" src/main/java
grep -R "implements Filter" src/main/java
grep -R "<filter>" src/main/webapp src/main/resources
```

---

# 14. Dispatcher Type: REQUEST, ERROR, ASYNC

## 14.1 Masalah praktis

Filter bisa berjalan lebih dari sekali untuk request yang sama jika ikut `ERROR`, `FORWARD`, atau `ASYNC`.

Contoh gejala:

```text id="gvjiyb"
access log dobel
correlation id tergenerate ulang
auth filter jalan saat error page
tenant context hilang saat async dispatch
```

## 14.2 Filter mapping dengan dispatcher

```xml id="w90sdb"
<filter-mapping>
    <filter-name>accessLogFilter</filter-name>
    <url-pattern>/api/*</url-pattern>
    <dispatcher>REQUEST</dispatcher>
</filter-mapping>
```

Untuk error logging khusus:

```xml id="zf7s3v"
<filter-mapping>
    <filter-name>errorDispatchLoggingFilter</filter-name>
    <url-pattern>/api/*</url-pattern>
    <dispatcher>ERROR</dispatcher>
</filter-mapping>
```

Rule praktis:

```text id="u6wgcq"
Default-kan filter utama ke REQUEST.
Tambahkan ERROR/ASYNC hanya jika memang sudah didesain.
```

---

# 15. Error Handling di Servlet Filter

## 15.1 Kapan filter boleh menulis error response?

Filter boleh short-circuit untuk error yang jelas sebelum Jersey:

```text id="e45f9i"
missing Authorization
invalid origin
unsupported media type
request too large
maintenance mode
missing tenant
```

Contoh helper reusable:

```java id="u0sly7"
package com.company.caseapp.web;

import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;

import java.io.IOException;

public final class JsonErrorWriter {

    private JsonErrorWriter() {
    }

    public static void write(
            HttpServletResponse response,
            int status,
            String code,
            String message
    ) throws IOException {

        if (response.isCommitted()) {
            return;
        }

        response.setStatus(status);
        response.setContentType("application/json");
        response.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
        response.getWriter().write("""
                {"code":"%s","message":"%s"}
                """.formatted(escape(code), escape(message)));
    }

    private static String escape(String value) {
        return value == null ? "" : value.replace("\"", "\\\"");
    }
}
```

Penggunaan:

```java id="hr48pa"
JsonErrorWriter.write(
        httpResponse,
        HttpServletResponse.SC_UNAUTHORIZED,
        "UNAUTHORIZED",
        "Missing Authorization header"
);
return;
```

## 15.2 Jangan ubah semua exception menjadi response di filter

Anti-pattern:

```java id="xv8xzi"
try {
    chain.doFilter(request, response);
} catch (Exception e) {
    response.setStatus(500);
    response.getWriter().write("error");
}
```

Masalah:

```text id="ilxftc"
Jersey ExceptionMapper bisa tidak terpakai.
Error contract bisa beda.
Stacktrace/logging bisa kacau.
Response mungkin sudah committed.
```

Lebih baik:

```text id="wym3wh"
Biarkan exception dari Jersey/resource ditangani oleh JAX-RS ExceptionMapper.
Filter hanya tangani error yang terjadi sebelum request masuk Jersey,
atau error filter-specific yang memang berada di boundary tersebut.
```

---

# 16. Integrasi dengan Logging MDC

Jika memakai SLF4J/Logback/Log4j, correlation id biasanya dimasukkan ke MDC.

Contoh dengan SLF4J MDC:

```java id="ktx7rq"
import org.slf4j.MDC;

public class MdcCorrelationFilter implements Filter {

    private static final String HEADER = "X-Correlation-Id";

    @Override
    public void doFilter(
            ServletRequest request,
            ServletResponse response,
            FilterChain chain
    ) throws IOException, ServletException {

        HttpServletRequest httpRequest = (HttpServletRequest) request;
        HttpServletResponse httpResponse = (HttpServletResponse) response;

        String correlationId = httpRequest.getHeader(HEADER);
        if (correlationId == null || correlationId.isBlank()) {
            correlationId = java.util.UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        }

        try {
            MDC.put("correlationId", correlationId);
            httpRequest.setAttribute("correlationId", correlationId);
            httpResponse.setHeader(HEADER, correlationId);

            chain.doFilter(request, response);
        } finally {
            MDC.remove("correlationId");
        }
    }
}
```

Pattern log:

```text id="uwya56"
%d{yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss.SSS} %-5level [%thread] correlationId=%X{correlationId} %logger - %msg%n
```

Critical invariant:

```text id="dj1fy8"
MDC juga berbasis context per-thread.
Clear di finally wajib.
```

---

# 17. Context Propagation ke Service Layer

Ada dua style.

## 17.1 Style A - eksplisit lewat parameter

Resource:

```java id="ndczqh"
@Path("/v1/cases")
public class CaseResource {

    private final CaseService caseService;

    public CaseResource(CaseService caseService) {
        this.caseService = caseService;
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/{caseId}")
    public CaseResponse getCase(@PathParam("caseId") String caseId) {
        RequestContext.RequestMetadata metadata = RequestContext.current();

        return caseService.getCase(caseId, metadata);
    }
}
```

Service:

```java id="wwh9ub"
public class CaseService {

    public CaseResponse getCase(
            String caseId,
            RequestContext.RequestMetadata metadata
    ) {
        // use metadata.tenantId(), metadata.userId(), metadata.correlationId()
        return new CaseResponse(caseId, "OPEN");
    }
}
```

Kelebihan:

```text id="uk9y02"
jelas
mudah dites
tidak hidden dependency
```

## 17.2 Style B - service membaca RequestContext langsung

```java id="zkcynf"
public class CaseService {

    public CaseResponse getCase(String caseId) {
        String tenantId = RequestContext.current().tenantId();
        return new CaseResponse(caseId, "OPEN");
    }
}
```

Kelebihan:

```text id="z07jkn"
lebih sedikit parameter
praktis untuk legacy code
```

Kekurangan:

```text id="g85ub0"
hidden dependency
unit test perlu setup ThreadLocal
berisiko jika dipakai di async/background thread
```

Rekomendasi:

```text id="epc53w"
Untuk business service baru, prefer parameter eksplisit.
Untuk cross-cutting logging/audit legacy, ThreadLocal masih bisa dipakai dengan disiplin cleanup.
```

---

# 18. Context Propagation ke Outbound HTTP

Saat service memanggil service lain, correlation id harus diteruskan.

```java id="uz12j0"
public class ExternalCaseClient {

    private final java.net.http.HttpClient httpClient;

    public ExternalCaseClient(java.net.http.HttpClient httpClient) {
        this.httpClient = httpClient;
    }

    public String getExternalCase(String caseId) throws Exception {
        RequestContext.RequestMetadata metadata = RequestContext.current();

        java.net.http.HttpRequest request = java.net.http.HttpRequest.newBuilder()
                .uri(java.net.URI.create("https://external.company.com/cases/" + caseId))
                .header("X-Correlation-Id", metadata.correlationId())
                .header("X-Tenant-Id", metadata.tenantId())
                .GET()
                .build();

        return httpClient.send(
                request,
                java.net.http.HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString()
        ).body();
    }
}
```

Rule:

```text id="r3dxxl"
Correlation id boleh diteruskan ke downstream.
Tenant/user header hanya diteruskan jika downstream berada dalam trust boundary yang jelas.
Authorization token forwarding harus sangat hati-hati.
```

---

# 19. Async dan Multi-thread Warning

Servlet request thread context tidak otomatis pindah ke thread lain.

Berbahaya:

```java id="hbh1al"
executor.submit(() -> {
    String tenantId = RequestContext.current().tenantId();
    process(tenantId);
});
```

Bisa gagal karena thread executor berbeda dan `ThreadLocal` tidak ada.

Lebih aman:

```java id="zir3xz"
RequestContext.RequestMetadata metadata = RequestContext.current();

executor.submit(() -> {
    process(metadata.tenantId(), metadata.correlationId());
});
```

Atau wrapper:

```java id="g7qw0f"
public final class ContextAwareRunnable implements Runnable {

    private final RequestContext.RequestMetadata metadata;
    private final Runnable delegate;

    public ContextAwareRunnable(
            RequestContext.RequestMetadata metadata,
            Runnable delegate
    ) {
        this.metadata = metadata;
        this.delegate = delegate;
    }

    @Override
    public void run() {
        try {
            RequestContext.set(metadata);
            delegate.run();
        } finally {
            RequestContext.clear();
        }
    }
}
```

Penggunaan:

```java id="w1u65o"
RequestContext.RequestMetadata metadata = RequestContext.current();

executor.submit(new ContextAwareRunnable(metadata, () -> {
    processSomething();
}));
```

Prinsip:

```text id="pky9vh"
ThreadLocal bukan context propagation otomatis.
Untuk async, capture context secara eksplisit.
Clear tetap wajib di thread worker.
```

---

# 20. Filter untuk Feature Flag / Maintenance Mode

Filter bisa dipakai untuk maintenance mode global.

```java id="z01c67"
public class MaintenanceModeFilter implements Filter {

    @Override
    public void doFilter(
            ServletRequest request,
            ServletResponse response,
            FilterChain chain
    ) throws IOException, ServletException {

        HttpServletRequest httpRequest = (HttpServletRequest) request;
        HttpServletResponse httpResponse = (HttpServletResponse) response;

        if (isMaintenanceEnabled() && !isHealthPath(httpRequest)) {
            JsonErrorWriter.write(
                    httpResponse,
                    HttpServletResponse.SC_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
                    "SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE",
                    "Service is temporarily unavailable"
            );
            return;
        }

        chain.doFilter(request, response);
    }

    private boolean isMaintenanceEnabled() {
        return Boolean.parseBoolean(System.getenv("MAINTENANCE_MODE"));
    }

    private boolean isHealthPath(HttpServletRequest request) {
        String path = request.getServletPath()
                + (request.getPathInfo() == null ? "" : request.getPathInfo());

        return path.startsWith("/api/v1/health")
                || path.startsWith("/api/v1/readiness");
    }
}
```

Catatan:

```text id="2afqzq"
Jangan block readiness/liveness tanpa sengaja.
Kalau maintenance mode membuat pod tidak ready,
deployment/orchestrator bisa menganggap service rusak total.
```

---

    </filter>

        <dispatcher>REQUEST</dispatcher>
    </filter-mapping>

    <filter>
        <filter-name>corsFilter</filter-name>

    </filter>
    <filter-mapping>
        <filter-name>corsFilter</filter-name>

        <dispatcher>REQUEST</dispatcher>
    </filter-mapping>

    <filter>
        <filter-name>authenticationPreCheckFilter</filter-name>

    </filter>

    <filter>

        <init-param>

            <param-value>com.company.caseapp.config.CaseApplication</param-value>
        </init-param>
        <load-on-startup>1</load-on-startup>
    </servlet>

    <servlet-mapping>
        <servlet-name>jersey</servlet-name>
        <url-pattern>/api/*</url-pattern>
    </servlet-mapping>

</web-app>

# 22. Testing Filter

## 22.1 Test correlation id generated

```java id="lx6e0c"
@Test
void shouldGenerateCorrelationIdWhenMissing() throws Exception {
    HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
            .uri(URI.create("http://localhost:8080/case-service/api/v1/health"))
            .GET()
            .build();

    HttpResponse<String> response = HttpClient.newHttpClient()
            .send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());

    assertEquals(200, response.statusCode());
    assertTrue(response.headers().firstValue("X-Correlation-Id").isPresent());
}
```

## 22.2 Test correlation id preserved

```java id="p9gdef"
@Test
void shouldPreserveIncomingCorrelationId() throws Exception {
    String correlationId = "test-correlation-123";

    HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
            .uri(URI.create("http://localhost:8080/case-service/api/v1/health"))
            .header("X-Correlation-Id", correlationId)
            .GET()
            .build();

    HttpResponse<String> response = HttpClient.newHttpClient()
            .send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());

    assertEquals(correlationId, response.headers()
            .firstValue("X-Correlation-Id")
            .orElseThrow());
}
```

## 22.3 Test auth short-circuit

```java id="adk7dp"
@Test
void protectedEndpointShouldRejectMissingAuthorization() throws Exception {
    HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
            .uri(URI.create("http://localhost:8080/case-service/api/v1/cases/123"))
            .GET()
            .build();

    HttpResponse<String> response = HttpClient.newHttpClient()
            .send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());

    assertEquals(401, response.statusCode());
    assertTrue(response.body().contains("UNAUTHORIZED"));
}
```

## 22.4 Test public endpoint skip auth

```java id="qvj40y"
@Test
void healthEndpointShouldNotRequireAuthorization() throws Exception {
    HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
            .uri(URI.create("http://localhost:8080/case-service/api/v1/health"))
            .GET()
            .build();

    HttpResponse<String> response = HttpClient.newHttpClient()
            .send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());

    assertEquals(200, response.statusCode());
}
```

---

# 23. Code Review Checklist

## 23.1 Filter design

```text id="c2b0yw"
[ ] Filter punya satu tanggung jawab jelas.
[ ] Tidak ada business workflow kompleks di filter.
[ ] Tidak ada DB call berat tanpa timeout.
[ ] Tidak membaca request body tanpa wrapper dan size limit.
[ ] Tidak log Authorization/Cookie/token/body sensitif.
```

## 23.2 Chain safety

```text id="i2wk3b"
[ ] chain.doFilter dipanggil tepat sekali untuk path normal.
[ ] Short-circuit return disengaja dan jelas.
[ ] try/finally dipakai untuk cleanup.
[ ] Response belum committed sebelum menulis error.
[ ] Error response minimal dan konsisten JSON.
```

## 23.3 Context safety

```text id="ztkxs8"
[ ] ThreadLocal selalu remove di finally.
[ ] MDC selalu remove/clear di finally.
[ ] Tenant/user context berasal dari trusted source.
[ ] Async task tidak membaca ThreadLocal request thread secara buta.
[ ] Context tidak menyimpan HttpServletRequest mentah.
```

## 23.4 Registration

```text id="ajq5wu"
[ ] Filter order eksplisit.
[ ] Mapping tidak terlalu luas tanpa alasan.
[ ] Dispatcher type jelas.
[ ] Public endpoint skip auth terdokumentasi.
[ ] CORS origin explicit.
```

---

# 24. Anti-Pattern Penting

| Anti-pattern | Kenapa bahaya | Alternatif |
|---|---|---|
| Semua logic auth di Servlet Filter | Tidak resource-aware, sulit test business rule | Pre-check di filter, business auth di service |
| `ThreadLocal` tanpa `finally remove()` | Context bocor antar request | `try/finally clear()` |
| Membaca request body untuk logging | Jersey tidak bisa parse body | Log metadata, bukan body |
| Log `Authorization` dan `Cookie` | Secret leak | Log presence, bukan value |
| CORS `*` untuk enterprise app | Origin terlalu permisif | Allowlist origin |
| Filter menangkap semua exception | Mengganggu `ExceptionMapper` | Biarkan Jersey handle exception resource |
| Banyak filter annotation tersebar | Order sulit diaudit | Centralize di `web.xml` atau config |
| Percaya `X-Tenant-Id` mentah | Tenant spoofing | Ambil dari trusted identity source |
| Filter berjalan di `ERROR` tanpa sadar | Log/context dobel | Explicit dispatcher `REQUEST` |
| Context disimpan di `ServletContext` | Global state lintas request | Request attribute / metadata object |

---

# 25. Mini Exercise untuk Repo Anda

Cari semua filter:

```bash id="o70e52"
grep -R "implements Filter" src/main/java
grep -R "@WebFilter" src/main/java
grep -R "<filter>" src/main/webapp src/main/resources
```

Isi matrix:

```text id="oz8a34"
Filter name:
  ...

Responsibility:
  ...

Order:
  ...

URL pattern:
  ...

Dispatcher:
  REQUEST / ERROR / ASYNC / etc

Short-circuit?
  yes/no

Writes response body?
  yes/no

Reads request body?
  yes/no

Uses ThreadLocal/MDC?
  yes/no

Cleanup in finally?
  yes/no

Calls downstream system?
  yes/no

Security-sensitive headers?
  ...

Risk:
  low / medium / high
```

Prioritas review tertinggi:

```text id="mxpdfr"
1. Filter yang membaca request body
2. Filter yang menggunakan ThreadLocal/MDC
3. Filter auth/tenant/user context
4. Filter yang menulis response body
5. Filter dengan mapping /*
6. Filter yang berjalan untuk ERROR/ASYNC
7. Filter yang memanggil DB/downstream
```

---

# Ringkasan Seri 5

Servlet Filter adalah boundary paling awal di dalam aplikasi web Java setelah request masuk Servlet Container. Filter cocok untuk:

```text id="dns91e"
correlation id
access log
CORS
security pre-check
tenant/user context setup
request guard
response security header
maintenance mode
```

Tetapi filter tidak cocok untuk:

```text id="xm3l8i"
business workflow
complex authorization domain
DB transaction orchestration
body transformation sembarangan
catch-all exception handling
```

Invariants production:

```text id="t5w0kg"
Correlation id harus dibuat paling awal.
Secret tidak boleh masuk log.
ThreadLocal/MDC wajib cleanup di finally.
chain.doFilter harus jelas.
Short-circuit harus disengaja.
Tenant/user context harus berasal dari trusted source.
Request body jangan dibaca di filter tanpa wrapper.
Dispatcher type harus eksplisit.
```
