## Response:
7/15/2026, 6:59:00 AM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 6/12**. Fokusnya: kenapa satu request bisa melewati filter lebih dari sekali, bagaimana `ERROR`/`ASYNC` dispatch bekerja, dan cara menjaga error response tetap konsisten antara Servlet Container dan Jersey.

# Seri 6/12 - Dispatcher Types & Error Dispatch

## Tujuan seri ini

Seri ini membahas kenapa satu request di Servlet Container tidak selalu hanya berjalan sebagai request normal. Dalam Servlet API, request bisa diproses sebagai beberapa **dispatcher type**:

```text
REQUEST
FORWARD
INCLUDE
ERROR
ASYNC
```

Skill praktis yang ingin dicapai:

```text
[ ] Bisa membedakan request normal, forward, include, error dispatch, dan async dispatch.
[ ] Bisa mencegah filter jalan dua kali tanpa sadar.
[ ] Bisa membedakan error yang ditangani Jersey ExceptionMapper vs error dispatch dari Servlet Container.
[ ] Bisa membuat error response API tetap JSON, bukan kadang JSON kadang HTML.
[ ] Bisa men-debug kenapa access log/correlation id/auth filter jalan dobel.
```

Dalam Servlet API, dispatcher type digunakan container untuk memilih filter yang akan diterapkan; hanya filter dengan dispatcher type dan URL pattern yang cocok yang akan dijalankan. Initial dispatcher type sebuah request adalah `REQUEST`, forward menjadi `FORWARD`, include menjadi `INCLUDE`, dan async dispatch menjadi `ASYNC`. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/servlet/6.1/apidocs/jakarta.servlet/jakarta/servlet/servletrequest?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Mental Model

Jangan anggap alur selalu begini:

```text
Client
  -> Filter
  -> Jersey
  -> Resource
  -> Response
```

Kadang alurnya begini:

```text
Client
  -> Filter dispatcher=REQUEST
  -> Jersey
  -> Exception terjadi
  -> Container melakukan ERROR dispatch
  -> Filter dispatcher=ERROR bisa jalan lagi
  -> Error handler / error page
  -> Response
```

Atau async:

```text
Client
  -> Filter dispatcher=REQUEST
  -> Servlet/Jersey mulai async
  -> Request thread dilepas
  -> Async work selesai
  -> AsyncContext.dispatch()
  -> Filter dispatcher=ASYNC bisa jalan lagi
  -> Response selesai
```

Jadi pertanyaan praktis saat melihat filter adalah:

```text
Filter ini harus berjalan untuk dispatcher type apa saja?
REQUEST saja?
ERROR juga?
ASYNC juga?
FORWARD/INCLUDE perlu?
```

Default yang aman untuk kebanyakan microservice API:

```text
Filter utama:
  REQUEST only

Filter khusus error:
  ERROR only, jika memang dibutuhkan

Filter async-aware:
  REQUEST + ASYNC, hanya jika sudah didesain eksplisit
```

---

# 2. Diagram Dispatcher Types

```text
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Client HTTP request                                          │
└──────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────┘
                           ▼
                    DispatcherType.REQUEST
                           │
                           ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Servlet Filter Chain                                         │
│ correlation, access log, auth, tenant context                │
└──────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────┘
                           ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Jersey ServletContainer                                      │
│ JAX-RS matching, provider, resource method                   │
└──────────────┬───────────────────────┬──────────────────────┘
               │                       │
               │ normal response        │ exception before/after handling
               ▼                       ▼
        Response committed       Container/Jersey error path
                                       │
                                       ▼
                              DispatcherType.ERROR
                                       │
                                       ▼
                         error handler / error endpoint / page

Optional paths:

FORWARD:
  Servlet/handler forwards request to another server-side resource.

INCLUDE:
  Servlet/handler includes another resource output.

ASYNC:
  Request starts async mode, later dispatched again by AsyncContext.
```

---

# 3. Decomposition Map

| No | Area | Fungsi | Relevansi praktis di Jersey/JAX-RS | Risiko umum |
|---:|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `REQUEST` | Request normal pertama dari client | Hampir semua filter utama berjalan di sini | Aman jika filter memang hanya untuk request awal |
| 2 | `FORWARD` | Server-side forward ke resource lain | Jarang di pure REST microservice, tapi bisa muncul di error handler/legacy servlet | Filter jalan ulang dengan path berbeda |
| 3 | `INCLUDE` | Server-side include content dari resource lain | Jarang di REST API, lebih sering di JSP/legacy web | Header/status tidak bisa diubah oleh included resource |
| 4 | `ERROR` | Dispatch khusus saat error handling container | Penting untuk menjaga error JSON jika error terjadi sebelum Jersey | Filter/log/auth bisa jalan dobel |
| 5 | `ASYNC` | Dispatch lanjutan setelah async processing | Relevan untuk long polling, streaming, async servlet/Jersey | Context hilang, wrapper dilepas terlalu cepat, log dobel |
| 6 | Filter dispatcher mapping | Menentukan kapan filter berjalan | Harus eksplisit di `web.xml` | Default/annotation bisa membuat behavior tidak jelas |
| 7 | Error dispatch attributes | Metadata error dari container | Bisa dibaca untuk membuat JSON error response | Bocor stack trace/path internal |
| 8 | Jersey `ExceptionMapper` | Mapping exception di dalam JAX-RS runtime | Standardisasi error dari resource/provider | Tidak menangani error sebelum masuk Jersey |
| 9 | `ContainerRequestContext.abortWith` | Short-circuit di JAX-RS filter | Alternatif untuk auth/resource-level reject | Berbeda dari Servlet Filter short-circuit |
| 10 | Async lifecycle | `startAsync`, `AsyncContext`, listener, timeout | Berguna tapi harus dikontrol | Timeout/error dispatch tidak ditangani |
| 11 | Response committed | Status/header sudah terkirim | Forward/error mapper bisa gagal mengubah response | Partial response / connection reset |
| 12 | Logging idempotency | Filter mungkin jalan lebih dari sekali | Log harus tahu dispatcher type | Access log dobel atau salah latency |

---

# 4. Dispatcher Type: `REQUEST`

## 4.1 Makna

`REQUEST` adalah dispatcher type awal untuk request normal dari client. Ini adalah mode paling umum dan paling aman untuk filter seperti:

```text
correlation id
access log
CORS
auth pre-check
tenant/user context
request guard
security headers
```

## 4.2 Mapping yang direkomendasikan

```xml
<filter-mapping>
    <filter-name>correlationIdFilter</filter-name>
    <url-pattern>/api/*</url-pattern>
    <dispatcher>REQUEST</dispatcher>
</filter-mapping>
```

Rule praktis:

```text
Kalau belum yakin filter perlu ERROR/ASYNC/FORWARD/INCLUDE,
pakai REQUEST saja.
```

## 4.3 Kenapa?

Karena jika filter ikut `ERROR` atau `ASYNC`, ia bisa berjalan lagi untuk request yang sama. Itu bisa menyebabkan:

```text
correlation id regenerate
access log dobel
auth filter memblok error handler
tenant context overwrite
response header ditulis setelah committed
```

---

# 5. Dispatcher Type: `FORWARD`

## 5.1 Makna

`FORWARD` terjadi saat server-side code meneruskan request ke resource lain menggunakan `RequestDispatcher.forward(...)`. API `RequestDispatcher.forward` meneruskan request dari satu servlet ke resource lain di server, dan harus dipanggil sebelum response committed; jika response sudah committed, method ini melempar `IllegalStateException`. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/servlet/6.1/apidocs/jakarta.servlet/jakarta/servlet/requestdispatcher?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh:

```java
RequestDispatcher dispatcher =
        request.getRequestDispatcher("/api/v1/internal/error");

dispatcher.forward(request, response);
```

## 5.2 Apakah sering dipakai di Jersey microservice?

Biasanya tidak sering, karena REST API modern lebih sering langsung mengembalikan JSON response lewat Jersey `Response` atau `ExceptionMapper`.

Tapi `FORWARD` bisa muncul jika:

```text
ada legacy servlet
ada custom error servlet
ada static/error page forwarding
ada hybrid app: servlet + Jersey + JSP/static
```

## 5.3 Risiko praktis

Jika filter ikut `FORWARD`, maka filter bisa berjalan lagi setelah forward.

Contoh:

```text
REQUEST /api/v1/cases/123
  -> AuthFilter
  -> Jersey
  -> forward ke /api/v1/internal/error
  -> AuthFilter jalan lagi sebagai FORWARD
```

Jika error/internal path tidak punya auth header/context yang sama, filter bisa memblok request internal.

Rule:

```text
Jangan aktifkan FORWARD di filter API utama kecuali punya use case jelas.
```

---

# 6. Dispatcher Type: `INCLUDE`

## 6.1 Makna

`INCLUDE` terjadi saat server-side code menyisipkan output resource lain ke response menggunakan `RequestDispatcher.include(...)`. Dokumentasi API menyebut included resource tidak bisa mengubah status code atau set header; attempt untuk mengubahnya diabaikan. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/servlet/6.1/apidocs/jakarta.servlet/jakarta/servlet/requestdispatcher?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh:

```java
RequestDispatcher dispatcher =
        request.getRequestDispatcher("/fragment/footer");

dispatcher.include(request, response);
```

## 6.2 Relevansi di microservice API

Untuk pure Jersey/JAX-RS microservice, `INCLUDE` biasanya hampir tidak dipakai.

Lebih relevan di:

```text
JSP
server-side rendered web app
legacy servlet application
hybrid app dengan HTML page
```

Rule:

```text
Untuk REST microservice, filter biasanya tidak perlu dispatcher INCLUDE.
```

---

# 7. Dispatcher Type: `ERROR`

## 7.1 Makna

`ERROR` terjadi ketika Servlet Container melakukan dispatch ke error handling path setelah terjadi exception atau status error tertentu.

Contoh sumber error:

```text
Servlet Filter throw exception sebelum Jersey
Jersey Servlet gagal init
Resource tidak ditemukan oleh container
sendError dipanggil
Exception tidak tertangani
Async error
```

Dalam Servlet error dispatch, container dapat meneruskan request ke error handler/page yang dikonfigurasi.

## 7.2 Kenapa penting?

Karena error bisa terjadi **sebelum request masuk Jersey**.

Jika error terjadi sebelum Jersey, maka ini tidak bisa ditangani oleh Jersey `ExceptionMapper`.

Contoh:

```text
Client
  -> CorrelationIdFilter
  -> AuthFilter throws RuntimeException
  -> Jersey belum dipanggil
  -> GlobalExceptionMapper milik Jersey tidak tahu error ini
  -> Container melakukan ERROR dispatch
  -> Default error page bisa HTML
```

JAX-RS `ExceptionMapper` adalah provider yang memetakan Java exception menjadi `Response`, tetapi provider ini harus berada di runtime JAX-RS, terdaftar programmatically atau ditemukan via `@Provider`; artinya ia tidak otomatis menangani exception yang terjadi di luar JAX-RS runtime. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/apidocs/jakarta.ws.rs/jakarta/ws/rs/ext/exceptionmapper?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 8. Servlet Error vs Jersey ExceptionMapper

## 8.1 Perbedaan boundary

| Error terjadi di mana | Handler utama | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| Sebelum Jersey Servlet | Servlet Container error dispatch | Filter throw exception, context init failure |
| Saat Jersey matching/provider/resource | Jersey `ExceptionMapper` | Resource throw `BusinessException` |
| Saat response sudah committed | Sulit distandarkan | Streaming error, output already flushed |
| Saat async work error | `AsyncListener` / async error dispatch / container | Exception di async task |

## 8.2 Diagram

```text
Case A - Error sebelum Jersey

Filter
  throws RuntimeException
    ↓
Jersey belum masuk
    ↓
ExceptionMapper tidak jalan
    ↓
Container ERROR dispatch
    ↓
error-page / default error response

Case B - Error di resource Jersey

Jersey Resource
  throws BusinessException
    ↓
Jersey runtime menangkap
    ↓
ExceptionMapper<BusinessException>
    ↓
JSON error response

Case C - Error setelah response committed

Jersey/Servlet sudah flush body
    ↓
Exception terjadi
    ↓
Status/header tidak bisa diganti dengan aman
    ↓
partial response / connection reset / container-specific behavior
```

Practical invariant:

```text
Error API contract harus punya dua lapisan:
1. Jersey ExceptionMapper untuk error di dalam JAX-RS.
2. Servlet/container error handling untuk error sebelum/di luar Jersey.
```

---

# 9. Dispatcher Type Debug Filter

Tambahkan filter debug saat investigasi:

```java
package com.company.caseapp.web;

import jakarta.servlet.Filter;
import jakarta.servlet.FilterChain;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletException;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletResponse;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;

import java.io.IOException;

public class DispatcherDebugFilter implements Filter {

    @Override
    public void doFilter(
            ServletRequest request,
            ServletResponse response,
            FilterChain chain
    ) throws IOException, ServletException {

        HttpServletRequest r = (HttpServletRequest) request;

        System.out.printf(
                "DISPATCH_DEBUG dispatcher=%s method=%s uri=%s servletPath=%s pathInfo=%s%n",
                r.getDispatcherType(),
                r.getMethod(),
                r.getRequestURI(),
                r.getServletPath(),
                r.getPathInfo()
        );

        chain.doFilter(request, response);
    }
}
```

Mapping sementara:

```xml
<filter>
    <filter-name>dispatcherDebugFilter</filter-name>
    <filter-class>com.company.caseapp.web.DispatcherDebugFilter</filter-class>
</filter>

<filter-mapping>
    <filter-name>dispatcherDebugFilter</filter-name>
    <url-pattern>/*</url-pattern>
    <dispatcher>REQUEST</dispatcher>
    <dispatcher>ERROR</dispatcher>
    <dispatcher>ASYNC</dispatcher>
    <dispatcher>FORWARD</dispatcher>
    <dispatcher>INCLUDE</dispatcher>
</filter-mapping>
```

Gunakan untuk observasi. Jangan jadikan log permanen penuh di production.

---

# 10. Error Dispatch Attributes

Saat error dispatch, container menyediakan attributes tertentu di request. Nama konstanta tersedia di `RequestDispatcher`, misalnya untuk status code, exception, request URI, servlet name, dan message.

Contoh error handler servlet/resource bisa membaca:

```java
Object statusCode = request.getAttribute(RequestDispatcher.ERROR_STATUS_CODE);
Object exception = request.getAttribute(RequestDispatcher.ERROR_EXCEPTION);
Object requestUri = request.getAttribute(RequestDispatcher.ERROR_REQUEST_URI);
Object message = request.getAttribute(RequestDispatcher.ERROR_MESSAGE);
Object servletName = request.getAttribute(RequestDispatcher.ERROR_SERVLET_NAME);
```

Practical usage:

```text
Gunakan attributes ini untuk logging dan membangun error response aman.
Jangan expose raw exception message/stack trace ke client.
```

---

# 11. Membuat Servlet Error JSON Handler

## 11.1 Tujuan

Agar error sebelum Jersey tetap JSON, bukan HTML default container.

Contoh handler:

```java
package com.company.caseapp.web;

import jakarta.servlet.RequestDispatcher;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletException;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServlet;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;

import java.io.IOException;

public class JsonErrorServlet extends HttpServlet {

    @Override
    protected void service(
            HttpServletRequest request,
            HttpServletResponse response
    ) throws ServletException, IOException {

        int status = extractStatus(request);
        String correlationId = extractCorrelationId(request);

        response.setStatus(status);
        response.setContentType("application/json");
        response.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");

        response.getWriter().write("""
                {
                  "code": "%s",
                  "message": "%s",
                  "correlationId": "%s"
                }
                """.formatted(
                codeFor(status),
                messageFor(status),
                escape(correlationId)
        ));
    }

    private static int extractStatus(HttpServletRequest request) {
        Object value = request.getAttribute(RequestDispatcher.ERROR_STATUS_CODE);

        if (value instanceof Integer status) {
            return status;
        }

        return HttpServletResponse.SC_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR;
    }

    private static String extractCorrelationId(HttpServletRequest request) {
        Object value = request.getAttribute("correlationId");
        return value == null ? "" : String.valueOf(value);
    }

    private static String codeFor(int status) {
        return switch (status) {
            case 400 -> "BAD_REQUEST";
            case 401 -> "UNAUTHORIZED";
            case 403 -> "FORBIDDEN";
            case 404 -> "NOT_FOUND";
            case 405 -> "METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED";
            case 415 -> "UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE";
            default -> status >= 500 ? "INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR" : "HTTP_ERROR";
        };
    }

    private static String messageFor(int status) {
        return switch (status) {
            case 400 -> "Bad request";
            case 401 -> "Unauthorized";
            case 403 -> "Forbidden";
            case 404 -> "Resource not found";
            case 405 -> "Method not allowed";
            case 415 -> "Unsupported media type";
            default -> status >= 500
                    ? "Unexpected server error"
                    : "Request could not be processed";
        };
    }

    private static String escape(String value) {
        return value == null ? "" : value.replace("\"", "\\\"");
    }
}
```

## 11.2 Register di `web.xml`

```xml
<servlet>
    <servlet-name>jsonErrorServlet</servlet-name>
    <servlet-class>com.company.caseapp.web.JsonErrorServlet</servlet-class>
</servlet>

<servlet-mapping>
    <servlet-name>jsonErrorServlet</servlet-name>
    <url-pattern>/internal/error</url-pattern>
</servlet-mapping>

<error-page>
    <error-code>400</error-code>
    <location>/internal/error</location>
</error-page>

<error-page>
    <error-code>401</error-code>
    <location>/internal/error</location>
</error-page>

<error-page>
    <error-code>403</error-code>
    <location>/internal/error</location>
</error-page>

<error-page>
    <error-code>404</error-code>
    <location>/internal/error</location>
</error-page>

<error-page>
    <error-code>500</error-code>
    <location>/internal/error</location>
</error-page>

<error-page>
    <exception-type>java.lang.Throwable</exception-type>
    <location>/internal/error</location>
</error-page>
```

Practical note:

```text
Pastikan auth filter tidak memblok /internal/error.
Pastikan access log tidak mencatat dua kali secara membingungkan.
Pastikan error handler tidak throw exception lagi.
```

---

# 12. Filter Mapping untuk Error Handler

Jika filter utama hanya `REQUEST`, error handler bisa berjalan tanpa auth/correlation/user filter saat `ERROR`.

Masalahnya: correlation id bisa hilang jika belum disimpan sebagai request attribute.

Rekomendasi:

```text
CorrelationIdFilter:
  REQUEST saja biasanya cukup, karena attribute tetap ada pada request yang sama.

AccessLogFilter:
  REQUEST saja untuk satu access log utama.
  Atau desain khusus jika ingin log ERROR dispatch.

AuthFilter:
  REQUEST saja.
  Jangan jalankan auth filter untuk ERROR kecuali benar-benar perlu.

JsonErrorServlet:
  harus bisa berjalan tanpa auth context.
```

Contoh mapping aman:

```xml
<filter-mapping>
    <filter-name>correlationIdFilter</filter-name>
    <url-pattern>/api/*</url-pattern>
    <dispatcher>REQUEST</dispatcher>
</filter-mapping>

<filter-mapping>
    <filter-name>accessLogFilter</filter-name>
    <url-pattern>/api/*</url-pattern>
    <dispatcher>REQUEST</dispatcher>
</filter-mapping>

<filter-mapping>
    <filter-name>authenticationPreCheckFilter</filter-name>
    <url-pattern>/api/*</url-pattern>
    <dispatcher>REQUEST</dispatcher>
</filter-mapping>
```

Jika error bisa terjadi pada URL di luar `/api/*`, correlation id filter tidak akan berjalan. Untuk API microservice, ini biasanya acceptable. Jika ingin semua request punya correlation id:

```xml
<filter-mapping>
    <filter-name>correlationIdFilter</filter-name>
    <url-pattern>/*</url-pattern>
    <dispatcher>REQUEST</dispatcher>
</filter-mapping>
```

---

# 13. Mencegah Filter Jalan Dobel

## 13.1 Pattern idempotent marker

Kadang filter memang harus support beberapa dispatcher type. Gunakan request attribute marker.

```java
package com.company.caseapp.web;

import jakarta.servlet.Filter;
import jakarta.servlet.FilterChain;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletException;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletResponse;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;

import java.io.IOException;

public class OncePerRequestFilter implements Filter {

    private static final String ALREADY_FILTERED =
            OncePerRequestFilter.class.getName() + ".FILTERED";

    @Override
    public void doFilter(
            ServletRequest request,
            ServletResponse response,
            FilterChain chain
    ) throws IOException, ServletException {

        HttpServletRequest httpRequest = (HttpServletRequest) request;

        if (httpRequest.getAttribute(ALREADY_FILTERED) != null) {
            chain.doFilter(request, response);
            return;
        }

        httpRequest.setAttribute(ALREADY_FILTERED, Boolean.TRUE);

        try {
            doFilterOnce(httpRequest, response, chain);
        } finally {
            // Jangan remove jika ingin marker tetap ada sepanjang lifecycle request,
            // termasuk ERROR dispatch.
        }
    }

    private void doFilterOnce(
            HttpServletRequest request,
            ServletResponse response,
            FilterChain chain
    ) throws IOException, ServletException {

        System.out.printf(
                "once dispatcher=%s uri=%s%n",
                request.getDispatcherType(),
                request.getRequestURI()
        );

        chain.doFilter(request, response);
    }
}
```

## 13.2 Kapan marker dipakai?

Pakai jika:

```text
Filter perlu mapping REQUEST + ERROR.
Filter harus hanya inisialisasi context sekali.
Filter tidak boleh regenerate correlation id.
Filter tidak boleh mencatat access log dobel.
```

Jangan pakai marker membabi buta jika memang filter harus memproses setiap dispatch secara berbeda.

---

# 14. Access Log: REQUEST vs ERROR

## 14.1 Problem

Jika `AccessLogFilter` aktif untuk `REQUEST` dan `ERROR`, log bisa seperti ini:

```text
access dispatcher=REQUEST uri=/api/v1/cases/123 status=500 elapsedMs=120
access dispatcher=ERROR uri=/internal/error status=500 elapsedMs=2
```

Ini tidak selalu salah, tapi bisa membingungkan.

## 14.2 Rekomendasi

Untuk microservice API:

```text
Satu access log utama per client request:
  dispatcher REQUEST saja.

Tambahan error dispatch log:
  optional, hanya untuk debugging, bedakan field dispatcher=ERROR.
```

Access log filter:

```java
if (httpRequest.getDispatcherType() != DispatcherType.REQUEST) {
    chain.doFilter(request, response);
    return;
}
```

---

# 15. Jersey ExceptionMapper untuk Error API

## 15.1 Contoh domain exception

```java
package com.company.caseapp.error;

public class CaseNotFoundException extends RuntimeException {

    private final String caseId;

    public CaseNotFoundException(String caseId) {
        super("Case not found");
        this.caseId = caseId;
    }

    public String caseId() {
        return caseId;
    }
}
```

## 15.2 ExceptionMapper

```java
package com.company.caseapp.provider;

import com.company.caseapp.error.CaseNotFoundException;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.ExceptionMapper;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.Provider;

@Provider
public class CaseNotFoundExceptionMapper
        implements ExceptionMapper<CaseNotFoundException> {

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(CaseNotFoundException exception) {
        return Response.status(Response.Status.NOT_FOUND)
                .entity(new ErrorResponse(
                        "CASE_NOT_FOUND",
                        "Case not found"
                ))
                .build();
    }

    public record ErrorResponse(String code, String message) {
    }
}
```

Register:

```java
public class CaseApplication extends ResourceConfig {

    public CaseApplication() {
        register(CaseNotFoundExceptionMapper.class);
        register(GlobalExceptionMapper.class);
    }
}
```

`ExceptionMapper` contract memetakan exception menjadi `Response`; jika `toResponse` mengembalikan `null`, hasilnya `204 No Content`, dan jika `toResponse` melempar runtime exception, hasilnya `500 Internal Server Error`. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/apidocs/jakarta.ws.rs/jakarta/ws/rs/ext/exceptionmapper?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 16. JAX-RS `abortWith` vs Servlet Filter Short-Circuit

## 16.1 Servlet Filter short-circuit

```java
response.setStatus(HttpServletResponse.SC_UNAUTHORIZED);
response.setContentType("application/json");
response.getWriter().write("""
        {"code":"UNAUTHORIZED","message":"Missing token"}
        """);
return;
```

Ini terjadi sebelum Jersey.

## 16.2 JAX-RS Filter `abortWith`

```java
package com.company.caseapp.provider;

import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestContext;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestFilter;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.Provider;

import java.io.IOException;

@Provider
public class ResourceAuthFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext context) throws IOException {
        boolean allowed = checkAllowed(context);

        if (!allowed) {
            context.abortWith(
                    Response.status(Response.Status.FORBIDDEN)
                            .entity(new ErrorResponse(
                                    "FORBIDDEN",
                                    "Access denied"
                            ))
                            .build()
            );
        }
    }

    private boolean checkAllowed(ContainerRequestContext context) {
        return false;
    }

    public record ErrorResponse(String code, String message) {
    }
}
```

`ContainerRequestFilter` berjalan sebelum request dikirim ke resource, dan `ContainerRequestContext.abortWith(Response)` menghentikan request filter chain lalu mengembalikan response ke client melalui response filter chain yang berlaku. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/apidocs/jakarta.ws.rs/jakarta/ws/rs/container/containerrequestfilter?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Practical difference:

| Aspek | Servlet Filter short-circuit | JAX-RS `abortWith` |
|---|---|---|
| Layer | Sebelum Jersey | Di dalam JAX-RS runtime |
| Cocok untuk | Missing header, CORS, raw request guard | Resource/API-level auth |
| Akses JAX-RS metadata | Tidak | Ya |
| Response filter JAX-RS jalan? | Tidak | Ya, sesuai chain |
| ExceptionMapper terlibat? | Tidak langsung | Tidak perlu, response sudah dibuat |

---

# 17. Async Dispatch

## 17.1 Makna

Servlet async dimulai dengan `ServletRequest.startAsync()`. `AsyncContext` merepresentasikan execution context untuk operasi asynchronous yang dimulai dari request, dan dapat melakukan `dispatch`, `complete`, register `AsyncListener`, serta set timeout. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/servlet/6.1/apidocs/jakarta.servlet/jakarta/servlet/asynccontext?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh sederhana:

```java
AsyncContext asyncContext = request.startAsync();
asyncContext.setTimeout(5_000);

asyncContext.start(() -> {
    try {
        HttpServletResponse response =
                (HttpServletResponse) asyncContext.getResponse();

        response.setContentType("application/json");
        response.getWriter().write("""
                {"status":"done"}
                """);
    } catch (Exception e) {
        // log
    } finally {
        asyncContext.complete();
    }
});
```

## 17.2 Async dispatch dan dispatcher type

Saat `AsyncContext.dispatch()` dipanggil, request akan masuk lagi ke container sebagai dispatcher type `ASYNC`; dokumentasi `AsyncContext.dispatch()` menyebut dispatcher type request diset ke `DispatcherType.ASYNC`, dan berbeda dengan forward, response buffer/header tidak di-reset serta dispatch legal walaupun response sudah committed. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/servlet/6.1/apidocs/jakarta.servlet/jakarta/servlet/asynccontext?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh:

```java
AsyncContext asyncContext = request.startAsync();
asyncContext.dispatch("/api/v1/async/result");
```

Filter dengan dispatcher `ASYNC` bisa berjalan lagi.

## 17.3 Risiko async

```text
ThreadLocal request context tidak otomatis pindah.
MDC tidak otomatis pindah.
Response wrapper dari filter bisa tidak valid jika dilepas terlalu awal.
Access log bisa mencatat selesai sebelum async benar-benar selesai.
Timeout async bisa menjadi ERROR dispatch.
```

Rule praktis:

```text
Jangan gunakan Servlet async hanya karena ingin “lebih cepat”.
Gunakan jika benar-benar butuh melepas request thread saat menunggu operasi lama.
Untuk REST biasa dengan blocking DB/HTTP call, lebih sering cukup tuning timeout/thread pool dan downstream client.
```

---

# 18. Async Listener untuk Timeout/Error

Contoh listener:

```java
package com.company.caseapp.web;

import jakarta.servlet.AsyncEvent;
import jakarta.servlet.AsyncListener;

import java.io.IOException;

public class LoggingAsyncListener implements AsyncListener {

    @Override
    public void onComplete(AsyncEvent event) throws IOException {
        System.out.println("async complete");
    }

    @Override
    public void onTimeout(AsyncEvent event) throws IOException {
        System.out.println("async timeout");
    }

    @Override
    public void onError(AsyncEvent event) throws IOException {
        Throwable throwable = event.getThrowable();
        System.out.println("async error: " + throwable);
    }

    @Override
    public void onStartAsync(AsyncEvent event) throws IOException {
        System.out.println("async start");
    }
}
```

Register:

```java
AsyncContext asyncContext = request.startAsync();
asyncContext.setTimeout(5_000);
asyncContext.addListener(new LoggingAsyncListener());
```

Use case:

```text
log timeout
cleanup resource
complete response safely
dispatch ke error handler
```

---

# 19. Async Context Propagation

Berbahaya:

```java
asyncContext.start(() -> {
    String tenantId = RequestContext.current().tenantId();
    process(tenantId);
});
```

Karena `RequestContext` berbasis `ThreadLocal`, thread async belum tentu punya value.

Lebih aman:

```java
RequestContext.RequestMetadata metadata = RequestContext.current();

asyncContext.start(() -> {
    try {
        RequestContext.set(metadata);
        process(metadata.tenantId(), metadata.correlationId());
    } finally {
        RequestContext.clear();
        asyncContext.complete();
    }
});
```

Tetap hati-hati:

```text
Jangan simpan HttpServletRequest mentah ke background task jangka panjang.
Jangan akses request/response setelah async complete.
Pastikan timeout dan error path jelas.
```

---

# 20. Error Response Consistency Strategy

Agar error response konsisten, buat dua layer.

## 20.1 Layer 1 - Jersey ExceptionMapper

Untuk error yang terjadi di resource/provider/service:

```text
BusinessException
ValidationException
NotFoundException
JsonMappingException
Throwable fallback
```

Output:

```json
{
  "code": "CASE_NOT_FOUND",
  "message": "Case not found",
  "correlationId": "..."
}
```

## 20.2 Layer 2 - Servlet Error Handler

Untuk error yang terjadi sebelum/di luar Jersey:

```text
filter exception
servlet mapping error
container error
unhandled throwable
error dispatch
```

Output harus seformat mungkin:

```json
{
  "code": "INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR",
  "message": "Unexpected server error",
  "correlationId": "..."
}
```

## 20.3 Jangan expose detail internal

Jangan return:

```json
{
  "exception": "org.postgresql.util.PSQLException",
  "message": "relation customer_secret_table does not exist",
  "stackTrace": "..."
}
```

Return:

```json
{
  "code": "INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR",
  "message": "Unexpected server error",
  "correlationId": "abc-123"
}
```

Log internal detail server-side, bukan ke client.

---

# 21. Practical `web.xml` Setup

Contoh defensible untuk API microservice:

```xml
<web-app xmlns="https://jakarta.ee/xml/ns/jakartaee"
         xmlns:xsi="http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance"
         xsi:schemaLocation="https://jakarta.ee/xml/ns/jakartaee
                             https://jakarta.ee/xml/ns/jakartaee/web-app_6_1.xsd"
         version="6.1">

    <display-name>case-service</display-name>

    <!-- Correlation first -->
    <filter>
        <filter-name>correlationIdFilter</filter-name>
        <filter-class>com.company.caseapp.web.CorrelationIdFilter</filter-class>
    </filter>

    <filter-mapping>
        <filter-name>correlationIdFilter</filter-name>
        <url-pattern>/api/*</url-pattern>
        <dispatcher>REQUEST</dispatcher>
    </filter-mapping>

    <!-- Access log once per client request -->
    <filter>
        <filter-name>accessLogFilter</filter-name>
        <filter-class>com.company.caseapp.web.AccessLogFilter</filter-class>
    </filter>

    <filter-mapping>
        <filter-name>accessLogFilter</filter-name>
        <url-pattern>/api/*</url-pattern>
        <dispatcher>REQUEST</dispatcher>
    </filter-mapping>

    <!-- Auth only for normal API request -->
    <filter>
        <filter-name>authenticationPreCheckFilter</filter-name>
        <filter-class>com.company.caseapp.web.AuthenticationPreCheckFilter</filter-class>
    </filter>

    <filter-mapping>
        <filter-name>authenticationPreCheckFilter</filter-name>
        <url-pattern>/api/*</url-pattern>
        <dispatcher>REQUEST</dispatcher>
    </filter-mapping>

    <!-- Error servlet should not be under /api/* auth mapping -->
    <servlet>
        <servlet-name>jsonErrorServlet</servlet-name>
        <servlet-class>com.company.caseapp.web.JsonErrorServlet</servlet-class>
    </servlet>

    <servlet-mapping>
        <servlet-name>jsonErrorServlet</servlet-name>
        <url-pattern>/internal/error</url-pattern>
    </servlet-mapping>

    <error-page>
        <error-code>404</error-code>
        <location>/internal/error</location>
    </error-page>

    <error-page>
        <error-code>500</error-code>
        <location>/internal/error</location>
    </error-page>

    <error-page>
        <exception-type>java.lang.Throwable</exception-type>
        <location>/internal/error</location>
    </error-page>

    <!-- Jersey -->
    <servlet>
        <servlet-name>jersey</servlet-name>
        <servlet-class>org.glassfish.jersey.servlet.ServletContainer</servlet-class>
        <init-param>
            <param-name>jakarta.ws.rs.Application</param-name>
            <param-value>com.company.caseapp.config.CaseApplication</param-value>
        </init-param>
        <load-on-startup>1</load-on-startup>
    </servlet>

    <servlet-mapping>
        <servlet-name>jersey</servlet-name>
        <url-pattern>/api/*</url-pattern>
    </servlet-mapping>

</web-app>
```

---

# 22. Testing Error Dispatch

## 22.1 Test error dari Jersey

Resource:

```java
@Path("/v1/debug")
public class DebugResource {

    @GET
    @Path("/jersey-error")
    public String jerseyError() {
        throw new IllegalStateException("debug jersey error");
    }
}
```

Test:

```java
@Test
void jerseyErrorShouldReturnJson() throws Exception {
    HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
            .uri(URI.create("http://localhost:8080/case-service/api/v1/debug/jersey-error"))
            .GET()
            .build();

    HttpResponse<String> response = HttpClient.newHttpClient()
            .send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());

    assertEquals(500, response.statusCode());
    assertTrue(response.body().contains("INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR"));
    assertTrue(response.headers()
            .firstValue("Content-Type")
            .orElse("")
            .contains("application/json"));
}
```

## 22.2 Test error sebelum Jersey

Buat filter khusus profile test:

```java
public class ThrowBeforeJerseyFilter implements Filter {

    @Override
    public void doFilter(
            ServletRequest request,
            ServletResponse response,
            FilterChain chain
    ) throws IOException, ServletException {

        HttpServletRequest r = (HttpServletRequest) request;

        if ("/api/v1/debug/before-jersey-error".equals(
                r.getServletPath() + r.getPathInfo()
        )) {
            throw new RuntimeException("before jersey error");
        }

        chain.doFilter(request, response);
    }
}
```

Test:

```java
@Test
void beforeJerseyErrorShouldStillReturnJson() throws Exception {
    HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
            .uri(URI.create("http://localhost:8080/case-service/api/v1/debug/before-jersey-error"))
            .GET()
            .build();

    HttpResponse<String> response = HttpClient.newHttpClient()
            .send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());

    assertEquals(500, response.statusCode());
    assertTrue(response.body().contains("INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR"));
    assertTrue(response.headers()
            .firstValue("Content-Type")
            .orElse("")
            .contains("application/json"));
}
```

## 22.3 Test 404

```java
@Test
void unknownApiPathShouldReturnJson404() throws Exception {
    HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
            .uri(URI.create("http://localhost:8080/case-service/api/v1/not-exist"))
            .GET()
            .build();

    HttpResponse<String> response = HttpClient.newHttpClient()
            .send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());

    assertEquals(404, response.statusCode());
    assertTrue(response.headers()
            .firstValue("Content-Type")
            .orElse("")
            .contains("application/json"));
}
```

Catatan: 404 yang terjadi di Jersey dan 404 yang terjadi di container bisa punya jalur berbeda. Yang penting kontrak akhirnya konsisten.

---

# 23. Production Debug Playbook

## Case A - Access log dobel

Cek:

```text
[ ] Apakah AccessLogFilter mapping punya dispatcher ERROR?
[ ] Apakah request masuk async dan dispatch lagi?
[ ] Apakah ada forward ke error servlet?
[ ] Apakah container access log + custom access log sama-sama aktif?
[ ] Apakah filter annotation dan web.xml sama-sama mendaftarkan filter?
```

Tambahkan log:

```java
System.out.printf(
        "dispatcher=%s uri=%s%n",
        request.getDispatcherType(),
        ((HttpServletRequest) request).getRequestURI()
);
```

---

## Case B - Error response kadang HTML

Cek:

```text
[ ] Error terjadi sebelum Jersey?
[ ] Ada <error-page> untuk status/Throwable?
[ ] Error handler mengembalikan JSON?
[ ] Auth filter memblok /internal/error?
[ ] Response sudah committed?
[ ] ExceptionMapper global ter-register?
```

Diagnosis:

```text
JSON dari Jersey:
  berarti ExceptionMapper bekerja.

HTML dari container:
  berarti error jatuh ke container default error handling.

Connection reset / partial body:
  mungkin response sudah committed atau streaming gagal.
```

---

## Case C - Correlation ID hilang di error response

Cek:

```text
[ ] CorrelationIdFilter jalan sebelum error?
[ ] Correlation id disimpan sebagai request attribute?
[ ] Error handler membaca attribute yang sama?
[ ] Error terjadi sebelum CorrelationIdFilter?
[ ] Error terjadi di path yang tidak match filter mapping?
```

Fix:

```text
Mapping CorrelationIdFilter ke /* REQUEST jika ingin semua request punya id.
Atau pastikan semua API path berada di /api/* dan error berasal dari sana.
```

---

## Case D - Auth filter memblok error handler

Cek:

```text
[ ] AuthFilter mapping terlalu luas /*?
[ ] AuthFilter berjalan untuk dispatcher ERROR?
[ ] /internal/error dianggap protected endpoint?
```

Fix:

```text
AuthFilter:
  /api/* + REQUEST only

Error handler:
  /internal/error, tidak perlu auth filter
```

---

## Case E - Async timeout tidak jelas

Cek:

```text
[ ] Async timeout diset eksplisit?
[ ] Ada AsyncListener?
[ ] onTimeout melakukan complete atau dispatch?
[ ] Access log mengukur sampai request awal saja atau async selesai?
[ ] ThreadLocal context dipropagasi?
```

---

# 24. Code Review Checklist

## 24.1 Dispatcher mapping

```text
[ ] Semua filter punya dispatcher type eksplisit.
[ ] Filter utama hanya REQUEST kecuali ada alasan kuat.
[ ] ERROR dispatcher hanya untuk filter/error handler yang siap.
[ ] ASYNC dispatcher hanya untuk flow async yang sudah dites.
[ ] FORWARD/INCLUDE tidak aktif tanpa use case.
```

## 24.2 Error consistency

```text
[ ] Jersey punya GlobalExceptionMapper.
[ ] Domain exception punya mapper khusus.
[ ] Servlet container punya JSON error handler.
[ ] <error-page> mencakup 404, 500, dan Throwable.
[ ] Error response tidak expose stack trace.
[ ] Error response menyertakan correlationId jika tersedia.
```

## 24.3 Filter safety

```text
[ ] Access log tidak dobel.
[ ] Correlation id tidak regenerate saat ERROR/ASYNC.
[ ] Auth filter tidak memblok error handler.
[ ] ThreadLocal clear tetap berjalan.
[ ] Response committed dicek sebelum menulis error.
```

## 24.4 Async safety

```text
[ ] isAsyncSupported dipahami.
[ ] Async timeout eksplisit.
[ ] AsyncListener ada jika flow penting.
[ ] Context propagation eksplisit.
[ ] asyncContext.complete() selalu terpanggil.
[ ] Error async tidak hilang tanpa log.
```

---

# 25. Anti-Pattern

| Anti-pattern | Kenapa berbahaya | Alternatif |
|---|---|---|
| Filter mapping tanpa dispatcher eksplisit | Bisa jalan di dispatcher yang tidak diinginkan | Set `REQUEST` eksplisit |
| Auth filter aktif untuk `ERROR` | Error handler bisa ikut diblok | Auth hanya `REQUEST` |
| Access log aktif untuk semua dispatcher | Log dobel dan latency misleading | Satu access log utama untuk `REQUEST` |
| Mengandalkan Jersey `ExceptionMapper` untuk semua error | Error sebelum Jersey tidak tertangani | Tambahkan Servlet error handler |
| Error handler expose exception message | Bocor detail internal | Generic message + correlation id |
| Async tanpa timeout | Request menggantung | `asyncContext.setTimeout(...)` |
| Async tanpa `complete()` | Resource leak / request menggantung | `finally complete()` |
| ThreadLocal dianggap ikut async otomatis | Context hilang atau bocor | Capture metadata eksplisit |
| Forward setelah response committed | `IllegalStateException` | Forward sebelum commit atau gunakan response langsung |
| Error handler di bawah `/api/*` lalu kena auth | Error path gagal | Gunakan `/internal/error` di luar auth path |

---

# 26. Mini Exercise untuk Repo Anda

Cari semua filter dan error config:

```bash
grep -R "<filter-mapping>" src/main/webapp src/main/resources
grep -R "<dispatcher>" src/main/webapp src/main/resources
grep -R "<error-page>" src/main/webapp src/main/resources
grep -R "implements ExceptionMapper" src/main/java
grep -R "startAsync" src/main/java
grep -R "RequestDispatcher" src/main/java
```

Isi matrix:

```text
Filter:
  name:
  url-pattern:
  dispatcher:
  should run on REQUEST:
  should run on ERROR:
  should run on ASYNC:
  risk of double logging:
  uses ThreadLocal/MDC:
  safe cleanup:

Error handling:
  Jersey GlobalExceptionMapper:
  domain ExceptionMapper:
  JSON servlet error handler:
  404 mapped:
  500 mapped:
  Throwable mapped:
  stack trace hidden:

Async:
  used:
  timeout:
  listener:
  complete guaranteed:
  context propagation:
```

Red flag terbesar:

```text
[ ] Tidak ada dispatcher eksplisit.
[ ] Tidak ada Servlet error handler JSON.
[ ] Semua exception hanya mengandalkan Jersey ExceptionMapper.
[ ] Auth filter mapping /*
[ ] Access log aktif untuk ERROR tanpa desain.
[ ] Ada startAsync tanpa timeout/listener/complete.
```

---

# Ringkasan Seri 6

Dispatcher type menentukan **kenapa filter bisa berjalan lebih dari sekali** dan **kenapa error tidak selalu masuk ke Jersey ExceptionMapper**.

Model yang harus diingat:

```text
REQUEST:
  request normal dari client

FORWARD:
  server-side forward ke resource lain

INCLUDE:
  server-side include content resource lain

ERROR:
  container error dispatch

ASYNC:
  dispatch lanjutan setelah async processing
```

Production invariants:

```text
Filter utama sebaiknya REQUEST only.
ERROR dispatch perlu handler JSON sendiri.
Jersey ExceptionMapper hanya mencakup error di dalam JAX-RS runtime.
Auth filter jangan memblok error handler.
Access log harus idempotent.
Async harus punya timeout, listener, complete, dan context propagation eksplisit.
```

---

# Rekomendasi materi selanjutnya

Lanjut ke **Seri 7/12 - Thread Pool, Blocking IO, Async Servlet, Timeout**.

Fokus berikutnya: bagaimana Servlet Container memakai request thread pool, kenapa blocking DB/HTTP call bisa membuat service collapse walau CPU rendah, bagaimana membaca gejala thread starvation, kapan async berguna, dan bagaimana menyusun timeout dari gateway → container → Jersey → service → DB/downstream.

**Seri 6/12 selesai.**

---

**Sources:**

- [ServletRequest (Jakarta Servlet API documentation)](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/servlet/6.1/apidocs/jakarta.servlet/jakarta/servlet/servletrequest?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

## Response:
7/15/2026, 7:02:07 AM

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 7/12**. Fokusnya: hubungan request thread pool, blocking IO, async servlet, timeout chain, dan cara membaca gejala production seperti CPU rendah tapi latency tinggi atau request timeout beruntun.

# Seri 7/12 - Thread Pool, Blocking IO, Async Servlet, Timeout

## Tujuan seri ini

Seri ini membahas satu area yang sangat sering menjadi akar incident production:

> Service lambat atau timeout, CPU rendah, memory belum penuh, tapi request mulai menumpuk dan akhirnya 5xx/timeout.

Di aplikasi **Java Jersey/JAX-RS di atas Servlet Container**, penyebabnya sering bukan “Java lambat”, tetapi:

```text id="k60o1g"
request thread pool habis
blocking DB call terlalu lama
blocking HTTP downstream call tanpa timeout
Camunda/external workflow call menggantung
connection pool DB habis
gateway timeout lebih pendek dari app timeout
async dipakai tanpa lifecycle yang benar
```

Tomcat, misalnya, menjelaskan bahwa setiap incoming **non-asynchronous request** membutuhkan thread selama request tersebut diproses; jika request lebih banyak daripada thread yang tersedia, thread dibuat sampai `maxThreads`, lalu koneksi masih bisa diterima sampai batas koneksi/queue tertentu. Ini penting karena request yang sedang blocking tetap memegang thread container. ([Apache Tomcat](https://tomcat.apache.org/tomcat-10.1-doc/config/http.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Mental Model: Request Thread Itu Resource Mahal

Pada model Servlet blocking biasa:

```text id="a479mh"
1 request masuk
  ↓
container mengambil 1 request thread
  ↓
filter chain
  ↓
Jersey resource
  ↓
service layer
  ↓
blocking DB / HTTP / Camunda / file IO
  ↓
response selesai
  ↓
thread dikembalikan ke pool
```

Artinya:

```text id="hdm4og"
Selama resource method menunggu DB/downstream,
thread container ikut menunggu.
```

Kalau ada 200 request thread dan 200 request semuanya stuck menunggu downstream 30 detik:

```text id="v06ads"
CPU bisa rendah.
Memory bisa normal.
Tapi service tidak bisa melayani request baru.
```

Inilah gejala klasik **thread starvation karena blocking IO**.

---

# 2. Diagram Runtime

```text id="umhxqq"
Client
  ↓
Gateway / Ingress timeout
  ↓
Servlet Container connector
  ↓
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Request Thread Pool                                           │
│                                                              │
│  thread-1   → request A → DB query 3s                         │
│  thread-2   → request B → downstream HTTP 10s                 │
│  thread-3   → request C → Camunda call 5s                     │
│  ...                                                         │
│  thread-200 → request Z → waiting for DB connection           │
│                                                              │
│  New request arrives:                                         │
│    wait / queue / reject / timeout                            │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
  ↓
Jersey Resource
  ↓
Service Layer
  ↓
MyBatis / PostgreSQL / Camunda / External HTTP
```

Key point:

```text id="zs5xak"
Throughput bukan hanya soal CPU.
Throughput = seberapa cepat thread kembali tersedia.
```

---

# 3. Decomposition Map

| No | Area | Yang perlu dipahami | Fokus praktis | Failure mode umum |
|---:|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **Request thread pool** | Container memakai thread untuk memproses request blocking | Pahami `maxThreads`, executor, queue, busy thread | CPU rendah tapi request timeout |
| 2 | **Connector config** | Connector mengatur port, connection timeout, keep-alive, max connections | Baca config Tomcat/Jetty aktual | Koneksi menumpuk sebelum masuk app |
| 3 | **Blocking IO** | DB, HTTP client, file, Camunda call bisa memblok thread | Semua outbound call wajib timeout | Thread pool habis |
| 4 | **DB pool** | Request thread bisa menunggu connection pool | Cocokkan max request thread vs max DB pool | Thread stuck waiting connection |
| 5 | **Downstream timeout** | Timeout harus lebih pendek dari caller timeout | Set connect/read/request timeout | Gateway timeout duluan |
| 6 | **Gateway timeout** | Client-facing timeout biasanya di gateway/ingress | Selaraskan SLA app dengan timeout gateway | App terus kerja setelah client timeout |
| 7 | **Async Servlet** | Bisa melepas request thread saat menunggu operasi async | Gunakan untuk long wait/event-driven case | Async lifecycle leak |
| 8 | **JAX-RS async** | `AsyncResponse` / `CompletionStage` untuk response async | Cocok untuk long-running non-blocking/queued work | Tidak resume, client menggantung |
| 9 | **Timeout chain** | Timeout harus berurutan dari luar ke dalam | Design budget per hop | Timeout acak/tidak deterministik |
| 10 | **Backpressure** | Sistem harus menolak lebih awal saat overload | Limit, queue, 429/503, circuit breaker | Semua request masuk lalu collapse |
| 11 | **Observability** | Perlu metrik busy thread, queue, latency, pool usage | Access log + JMX/metrics + DB pool metrics | Salah diagnosis |
| 12 | **Thread dump** | Snapshot thread menunjukkan stuck point | Ambil thread dump saat incident | Hanya lihat log, akar masalah tidak terlihat |

---

# 4. Connector dan Thread Pool

## 4.1 Tomcat HTTP Connector

Di Tomcat, HTTP Connector adalah komponen yang listen pada port tertentu dan meneruskan request ke engine untuk diproses. Dokumentasi Tomcat 11 menjelaskan atribut seperti `connectionTimeout`, `keepAliveTimeout`, `maxConnections`, dan penggunaan executor/thread pool pada connector. ([Apache Tomcat](https://tomcat.apache.org/tomcat-11.0-doc/config/http.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh konfigurasi konseptual:

```xml id="dtix2v"
<Connector
    port="8080"
    protocol="HTTP/1.1"
    connectionTimeout="20000"
    keepAliveTimeout="15000"
    maxThreads="200"
    acceptCount="100"
    maxConnections="8192" />
```

Makna praktis:

| Setting | Fungsi | Risiko jika salah |
|---|---|---|
| `maxThreads` | Batas thread request aktif | Terlalu kecil → queue cepat penuh; terlalu besar → DB/downstream overload |
| `acceptCount` | Queue koneksi saat semua thread sibuk | Terlalu besar → latency menumpuk; terlalu kecil → reject cepat |
| `maxConnections` | Batas koneksi yang diterima/diproses | Terlalu besar → resource pressure |
| `connectionTimeout` | Waktu tunggu request line/body awal | Terlalu besar → koneksi idle makan resource |
| `keepAliveTimeout` | Waktu tunggu request berikutnya pada koneksi keep-alive | Terlalu besar → koneksi idle menumpuk |

Prinsip penting:

```text id="0vpejf"
maxThreads bukan angka yang makin besar makin bagus.
maxThreads harus selaras dengan DB pool, downstream capacity, CPU, dan latency target.
```

---

# 5. Blocking IO: Akar Banyak Masalah

## 5.1 Blocking DB call

Contoh biasa:

```java id="kwvx0m"
@GET
@Path("/{caseId}")
public CaseResponse getCase(@PathParam("caseId") String caseId) {
    return caseService.getCase(caseId);
}
```

Service:

```java id="ufs8bu"
public CaseResponse getCase(String caseId) {
    CaseEntity entity = caseMapper.findById(caseId); // blocking DB call
    return mapper.toResponse(entity);
}
```

Selama `caseMapper.findById` menunggu PostgreSQL:

```text id="z81gzi"
request thread tetap occupied.
```

Jika DB lambat dan semua request menunggu DB, service bisa collapse walau CPU rendah.

## 5.2 Blocking HTTP downstream call

```java id="v74sxr"
HttpResponse<String> response = httpClient.send(
        request,
        HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString()
);
```

Ini blocking. Wajib ada timeout.

```java id="pyzv45"
HttpClient client = HttpClient.newBuilder()
        .connectTimeout(Duration.ofSeconds(2))
        .build();

HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
        .uri(URI.create("https://downstream.company.com/api/v1/check"))
        .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(3))
        .GET()
        .build();

HttpResponse<String> response =
        client.send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());
```

Rule:

```text id="5gg1oo"
Set timeout di setiap outbound call.
Connect timeout saja tidak cukup.
Request/read timeout juga perlu.
```

---

# 6. Little’s Law untuk Intuisi Thread

Rumus kasar:

```text id="fa3pc9"
Concurrency ≈ Throughput × Latency
```

Misal:

```text id="tb7x8u"
Target throughput = 100 request/second
Average latency   = 500 ms = 0.5 second

Concurrency needed ≈ 100 × 0.5 = 50 active requests
```

Jika latency naik:

```text id="ouoihm"
Target throughput = 100 request/second
Average latency   = 5 seconds

Concurrency needed ≈ 100 × 5 = 500 active requests
```

Artinya:

```text id="kixz0p"
Saat downstream lambat 10x,
kebutuhan thread bisa naik 10x untuk throughput yang sama.
```

Karena itu, menaikkan `maxThreads` hanya “menunda” collapse jika akar masalahnya latency downstream.

---

# 7. Gejala Production dan Diagnosis

| Gejala | Kemungkinan | Cek |
|---|---|---|
| CPU rendah, latency tinggi | Thread menunggu IO | Thread dump, busy threads, DB/downstream latency |
| Banyak timeout gateway | App lebih lambat dari gateway timeout | Access log duration vs gateway timeout |
| 5xx naik saat downstream lambat | No timeout/circuit breaker | Outbound client metrics |
| DB pool usage 100% | Request menunggu connection | Hikari/pool metrics, PostgreSQL active sessions |
| Thread count penuh | Thread starvation | JMX/thread dump |
| Queue naik, RPS turun | Backpressure kurang | Connector queue, LB retry |
| Latency p95/p99 melonjak | Tail latency downstream | Distributed tracing / per dependency timing |
| Request tetap jalan setelah client timeout | App timeout lebih panjang dari gateway | Timeout chain |

---

# 8. Timeout Chain

Timeout harus didesain sebagai rantai.

Contoh chain yang buruk:

```text id="hdbkoz"
Client timeout        10s
API Gateway timeout   30s
Servlet app timeout   none
DB query timeout      none
HTTP downstream       none
```

Akibat:

```text id="zc7x7u"
Client sudah pergi setelah 10s.
App masih kerja.
DB/downstream masih dipanggil.
Thread tetap occupied.
Load makin parah.
```

Contoh chain yang lebih sehat:

```text id="eylm9o"
Client timeout        15s
API Gateway timeout   12s
Servlet request SLA   10s
Service budget         8s
DB query timeout       3s
Downstream timeout     2s
Retries total budget   <= 8s
```

Prinsip:

```text id="qrlhcx"
Timeout terdalam harus lebih pendek dari timeout caller.
Total retry + backoff tidak boleh melebihi request budget.
```

---

# 9. Timeout Budget per Endpoint

Tidak semua endpoint sama.

| Endpoint type | Typical behavior | Timeout strategy |
|---|---|---|
| Health | Sangat cepat | 100-500 ms internal budget |
| Read simple | DB lookup/cache | 1-3s |
| Search/report | Query lebih berat | 3-10s, pagination wajib |
| Command/write | DB + workflow | 3-10s, idempotency penting |
| External verification | HTTP downstream | 2-5s + fallback/circuit |
| Long-running process | Tidak cocok sync | 202 Accepted + polling/event |
| File/media processing | Tidak cocok sync | async job |

Rule penting:

```text id="rqysmh"
Jangan paksa long-running workflow menjadi synchronous HTTP request.
```

Untuk proses panjang seperti OCR, Camunda workflow, settlement, reconciliation, atau document processing:

```text id="p00ao6"
POST /jobs
→ 202 Accepted
→ Location: /jobs/{jobId}

GET /jobs/{jobId}
→ status/result
```

---

# 10. DB Pool vs Request Threads

Misal:

```text id="xwaijy"
Tomcat maxThreads = 200
DB pool max       = 20
```

Jika 200 request masuk dan semuanya butuh DB:

```text id="2g7fvq"
20 request pakai DB connection
180 request menunggu connection
200 request thread occupied
service tidak bisa melayani request lain
```

Ini bukan selalu salah, tetapi harus disengaja.

## 10.1 Rule of thumb

```text id="n91bjq"
DB pool kecil melindungi database,
tapi request thread bisa habis menunggu pool.

DB pool besar bisa menaikkan concurrency DB,
tapi database bisa overload.
```

Solusi bukan sekadar menaikkan pool.

Solusi yang lebih matang:

```text id="5i0vnt"
[ ] query lebih cepat
[ ] pagination
[ ] index
[ ] timeout query
[ ] fail fast saat pool exhausted
[ ] bulkhead per dependency
[ ] cache untuk read-heavy
[ ] async job untuk heavy query/report
[ ] backpressure 429/503
```

---

# 11. MyBatis/PostgreSQL Practical Timeout

Untuk stack MyBatis + PostgreSQL, timeout harus dipikirkan di beberapa tempat:

```text id="5layfa"
JDBC driver connection timeout
DB pool connection timeout
statement/query timeout
transaction timeout, jika ada
PostgreSQL statement_timeout
lock_timeout
idle_in_transaction_session_timeout
```

Contoh SQL-level PostgreSQL per session/transaction:

```sql id="phejz5"
SET LOCAL statement_timeout = '3s';
SET LOCAL lock_timeout = '1s';
```

Contoh MyBatis mapper query harus punya desain defensif:

```sql id="abnj70"
SELECT
    case_id,
    status,
    created_at
FROM regulatory_case
WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
  AND case_id = #{caseId}
LIMIT 1;
```

Untuk search endpoint:

```sql id="otem44"
SELECT
    case_id,
    status,
    created_at
FROM regulatory_case
WHERE tenant_id = #{tenantId}
  AND status = #{status}
ORDER BY created_at DESC
LIMIT #{limit}
OFFSET #{offset};
```

Anti-pattern:

```sql id="p90m02"
SELECT *
FROM regulatory_case
WHERE LOWER(description) LIKE '%' || LOWER(#{keyword}) || '%';
```

Risiko:

```text id="55e22q"
query lambat
sequential scan
thread menunggu DB
pool habis
request timeout
```

---

# 12. Async Servlet

## 12.1 Kapan async berguna?

Jakarta EE tutorial menjelaskan bahwa asynchronous processing memungkinkan servlet/filter yang mencapai operasi potensial blocking untuk menyerahkan operasi ke asynchronous execution context dan mengembalikan thread request ke container tanpa langsung membuat response. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/learn/docs/jakartaee-tutorial/current/web/servlets/servlets.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Async berguna untuk:

```text id="nqzwn6"
long polling
server-sent events
menunggu event eksternal
queued/background result
fan-out async yang benar-benar non-blocking
streaming tertentu
```

Async **tidak otomatis membuat operasi blocking menjadi murah**.

Jika async hanya melakukan ini:

```text id="2v8m17"
request thread melepas kerja
  → worker thread blocking DB 30s
```

Maka Anda hanya memindahkan blocking dari request thread ke worker thread. Itu bisa berguna untuk melindungi container thread, tetapi tetap harus ada worker pool limit, timeout, dan backpressure.

---

## 12.2 Servlet async minimal

```java id="sab5q4"
import jakarta.servlet.AsyncContext;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletException;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServlet;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;

public class AsyncJobServlet extends HttpServlet {

    private final ExecutorService executor = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(16);

    @Override
    protected void doGet(
            HttpServletRequest request,
            HttpServletResponse response
    ) throws ServletException, IOException {

        AsyncContext asyncContext = request.startAsync();
        asyncContext.setTimeout(5_000);

        executor.submit(() -> {
            try {
                HttpServletResponse asyncResponse =
                        (HttpServletResponse) asyncContext.getResponse();

                String result = doSlowWork();

                asyncResponse.setStatus(HttpServletResponse.SC_OK);
                asyncResponse.setContentType("application/json");
                asyncResponse.getWriter().write("""
                        {"result":"%s"}
                        """.formatted(result));
            } catch (Exception e) {
                try {
                    HttpServletResponse asyncResponse =
                            (HttpServletResponse) asyncContext.getResponse();

                    if (!asyncResponse.isCommitted()) {
                        asyncResponse.setStatus(HttpServletResponse.SC_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR);
                        asyncResponse.setContentType("application/json");
                        asyncResponse.getWriter().write("""
                                {"code":"INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR","message":"Unexpected error"}
                                """);
                    }
                } catch (IOException ignored) {
                    // log in real implementation
                }
            } finally {
                asyncContext.complete();
            }
        });
    }

    private String doSlowWork() {
        return "done";
    }
}
```

Wajib:

```text id="836kc5"
setTimeout()
complete() di finally
bounded executor
error handling
shutdown executor saat app stop
```

---

# 13. JAX-RS Async: `AsyncResponse` dan `CompletionStage`

Jersey mendukung asynchronous server API. Dokumentasi Jersey menjelaskan bahwa pada asynchronous resource, response tidak dikembalikan langsung dari resource method; request processing disuspend, thread container dikembalikan ke pool, lalu response di-resume kemudian melalui `AsyncResponse`. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/3.0.6/user-guide.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Jakarta REST 4.0 juga mendukung pendekatan `CompletionStage<T>`: method resource dapat mengembalikan `CompletionStage<T>` sebagai indikasi bahwa asynchronous processing aktif, dan future diselesaikan setelah long-running operation selesai. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/jakarta-restful-ws-spec-4.0.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 13.1 `AsyncResponse` example

```java id="rbz6x5"
import jakarta.ws.rs.GET;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Path;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.AsyncResponse;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.Suspended;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;

import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;

@Path("/v1/async")
public class AsyncResource {

    private final ExecutorService executor;

    public AsyncResource(ExecutorService executor) {
        this.executor = executor;
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/cases")
    public void getCasesAsync(@Suspended AsyncResponse asyncResponse) {
        asyncResponse.setTimeout(3, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
        asyncResponse.setTimeoutHandler(ar -> ar.resume(
                Response.status(Response.Status.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE)
                        .entity(new ErrorResponse(
                                "TIMEOUT",
                                "Request processing timed out"
                        ))
                        .build()
        ));

        executor.submit(() -> {
            try {
                CaseResponse result = doWork();
                asyncResponse.resume(result);
            } catch (Exception e) {
                asyncResponse.resume(e);
            }
        });
    }

    private CaseResponse doWork() {
        return new CaseResponse("123", "OPEN");
    }

    public record CaseResponse(String caseId, String status) {
    }

    public record ErrorResponse(String code, String message) {
    }
}
```

Jika `asyncResponse.resume(e)` dipanggil dengan exception, Jersey dapat memprosesnya melalui exception mapper yang sesuai.

## 13.2 `CompletionStage` example

```java id="besuui"
import jakarta.ws.rs.GET;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Path;

import java.util.concurrent.CompletableFuture;
import java.util.concurrent.CompletionStage;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;

@Path("/v1/async")
public class CompletionStageResource {

    private final ExecutorService executor;

    public CompletionStageResource(ExecutorService executor) {
        this.executor = executor;
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/summary")
    public CompletionStage<SummaryResponse> summary() {
        return CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(this::loadSummary, executor);
    }

    private SummaryResponse loadSummary() {
        return new SummaryResponse(42, 7);
    }

    public record SummaryResponse(int openCases, int escalatedCases) {
    }
}
```

Practical warning:

```text id="nxakcr"
CompletionStage tidak otomatis membuat blocking call menjadi non-blocking.
Kalau supplyAsync berisi blocking DB/HTTP call, worker thread tetap blocking.
```

---

# 14. Async Anti-Pattern

| Anti-pattern | Kenapa salah | Alternatif |
|---|---|---|
| Async tanpa timeout | Client bisa menunggu terlalu lama | `setTimeout` + timeout handler |
| Async tanpa `complete()` / `resume()` | Request menggantung | `finally complete()` atau always resume |
| Unbounded executor | Bisa membuat thread meledak | Bounded pool + queue limit |
| Blocking call dipindahkan ke executor besar | Downstream overload | Bulkhead + timeout + circuit breaker |
| Tidak propagate context | Correlation/tenant hilang | Capture metadata eksplisit |
| Tidak cleanup ThreadLocal di worker | Context leak worker thread | `try/finally clear()` |
| Async untuk semua endpoint | Complexity naik | Pakai hanya untuk use case tepat |
| Tidak handle client disconnect | Resource tetap kerja | callbacks/listener/cancel strategy |
| Timeout gateway < async timeout | Client putus sebelum app selesai | Selaraskan timeout chain |

---

# 15. Context Propagation pada Async

Filter request thread:

```java id="boc8n9"
RequestContext.RequestMetadata metadata = RequestContext.current();
```

Submit async:

```java id="qaz61g"
executor.submit(() -> {
    try {
        RequestContext.set(metadata);
        doWork(metadata);
    } finally {
        RequestContext.clear();
    }
});
```

Jangan:

```java id="j8sq0z"
executor.submit(() -> {
    String tenantId = RequestContext.current().tenantId();
});
```

Karena worker thread berbeda dari request thread.

---

# 16. Timeout Implementation Pattern

## 16.1 HTTP downstream client

```java id="kjby7i"
public class DownstreamClient {

    private final HttpClient client = HttpClient.newBuilder()
            .connectTimeout(Duration.ofSeconds(2))
            .build();

    public String check(String caseId) throws IOException, InterruptedException {
        HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
                .uri(URI.create("https://risk.company.com/api/v1/cases/" + caseId))
                .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(3))
                .GET()
                .build();

        HttpResponse<String> response =
                client.send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());

        if (response.statusCode() >= 500) {
            throw new DownstreamUnavailableException();
        }

        return response.body();
    }
}
```

## 16.2 CompletableFuture timeout

```java id="qgt6d9"
CompletableFuture<CaseRisk> future =
        CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> riskClient.getRisk(caseId), executor)
                .orTimeout(3, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
                .exceptionally(ex -> CaseRisk.unknown());
```

Hati-hati:

```text id="f8vcsm"
Timeout di CompletableFuture tidak selalu membatalkan blocking IO yang sedang berjalan.
Pastikan client/downstream call juga punya timeout sendiri.
```

---

# 17. Bulkhead: Pisahkan Pool per Dependency

Anti-pattern:

```text id="vu0l4h"
Semua async work memakai satu executor besar:
- DB work
- external risk API
- Camunda call
- report generation
```

Jika risk API lambat, semua worker bisa habis dan DB work ikut terdampak.

Lebih baik:

```text id="wu5rcn"
riskApiExecutor       max 20
camundaExecutor       max 10
reportExecutor        max 5
notificationExecutor  max 10
```

Contoh:

```java id="heawch"
public final class ExecutorsRegistry {

    private final ExecutorService riskExecutor =
            Executors.newFixedThreadPool(20);

    private final ExecutorService camundaExecutor =
            Executors.newFixedThreadPool(10);

    private final ExecutorService reportExecutor =
            Executors.newFixedThreadPool(5);

    public ExecutorService riskExecutor() {
        return riskExecutor;
    }

    public ExecutorService camundaExecutor() {
        return camundaExecutor;
    }

    public ExecutorService reportExecutor() {
        return reportExecutor;
    }

    public void shutdown() {
        riskExecutor.shutdown();
        camundaExecutor.shutdown();
        reportExecutor.shutdown();
    }
}
```

Prinsip:

```text id="xtvqgs"
Satu dependency lambat tidak boleh menghabiskan semua execution capacity service.
```

---

# 18. Backpressure: Tolak Lebih Awal

Jika sistem overload, pilihan buruk:

```text id="l9u0d6"
Terima semua request
biarkan queue panjang
semua latency naik
client timeout
retry storm
service makin collapse
```

Pilihan lebih sehat:

```text id="sm5ov4"
Batasi concurrency
Jika penuh, return 429/503 cepat
Client retry dengan backoff
Service tetap stabil
```

Contoh simple semaphore guard:

```java id="3fb047"
public class ConcurrencyLimitFilter implements Filter {

    private final Semaphore semaphore = new Semaphore(100);

    @Override
    public void doFilter(
            ServletRequest request,
            ServletResponse response,
            FilterChain chain
    ) throws IOException, ServletException {

        HttpServletResponse httpResponse = (HttpServletResponse) response;

        if (!semaphore.tryAcquire()) {
            httpResponse.setStatus(HttpServletResponse.SC_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE);
            httpResponse.setContentType("application/json");
            httpResponse.getWriter().write("""
                    {"code":"OVERLOADED","message":"Service is busy"}
                    """);
            return;
        }

        try {
            chain.doFilter(request, response);
        } finally {
            semaphore.release();
        }
    }
}
```

Catatan:

```text id="t8e8xw"
Ini contoh sederhana.
Production perlu metrik, tuning, endpoint-specific limits, dan retry-after policy.
```

---

# 19. Thread Dump: Alat Diagnosis Cepat

Saat incident:

```bash id="aj8ixa"
jcmd <pid> Thread.print > thread-dump.txt
```

Cari pola:

```text id="oxjnfs"
http-nio-8080-exec-*
waiting on DB connection
java.net.SocketInputStream.socketRead
org.postgresql
java.net.http
Camunda client
BLOCKED on lock
WAITING on Future.get
```

Contoh interpretasi:

| Thread dump terlihat | Makna |
|---|---|
| Banyak thread di PostgreSQL driver read | DB query lambat |
| Banyak thread menunggu Hikari connection | DB pool habis |
| Banyak thread di HTTP client socket read | Downstream lambat |
| Banyak thread `WAITING Future.get` | Async tapi tetap blocking wait |
| Banyak `BLOCKED` pada lock sama | Lock contention |
| Banyak request thread idle tapi queue naik | Masalah sebelum app/container |

Practical rule:

```text id="25g6jv"
Saat CPU rendah + latency tinggi, thread dump sering lebih berguna daripada CPU profiler.
```

---

# 20. Observability yang Harus Ada

Minimal metrik:

```text id="avvd7m"
HTTP request count
HTTP latency p50/p95/p99
HTTP status code count
container busy threads
container max threads
connection count
DB pool active/idle/pending
DB query latency
downstream HTTP latency/status/timeout
executor queue size
executor active threads
timeout count
rejected request count
```

Access log format minimal:

```text id="jtpt22"
timestamp
correlationId
method
path
status
elapsedMs
remoteIp/clientIp
user/tenant if safe
bytesSent optional
```

Jangan hanya punya log error. Untuk thread starvation, banyak request “tidak error” sampai akhirnya timeout di gateway.

---

# 21. Practical Timeout Table

| Layer | Contoh setting | Prinsip |
|---|---|---|
| Client/mobile/frontend | request timeout | Harus realistis dengan UX |
| API Gateway/Ingress | upstream timeout | Lebih pendek dari client timeout |
| Servlet connector | connection/read timeout | Lindungi koneksi idle/lambat |
| Jersey/resource | request SLA | Jangan biarkan long work sync |
| DB pool | connection acquisition timeout | Jangan tunggu pool selamanya |
| DB statement | statement timeout | Query lambat harus berhenti |
| HTTP client | connect + request/read timeout | Semua downstream wajib timeout |
| Executor | queue timeout/rejection | Jangan queue tidak terbatas |
| AsyncResponse | async timeout handler | Resume/return timeout JSON |

---

# 22. Contoh End-to-End Budget

Endpoint:

```text id="9noffp"
POST /api/v1/cases/{caseId}/approve
```

Dependency:

```text id="dtcup0"
DB read case
DB update state
Camunda signal/message
Audit insert
Notification async fire-and-forget
```

Budget:

```text id="9loheg"
Gateway timeout:          10s
Application target:        8s

DB read:                   1s
DB update:                 1s
Camunda call:              3s
Audit insert:              1s
Buffer/serialization:      1s
```

Jika notification tidak wajib untuk response:

```text id="z71ee8"
Jangan block HTTP response untuk notification.
Publish async/outbox.
```

Jika Camunda call bisa lama:

```text id="gtblwe"
Pertimbangkan command accepted:
POST approve → 202 Accepted
background process handles Camunda
GET operation status
```

# 23. Jersey Resource Pattern: Synchronous dengan Timeout

```java id="58pg4x"
@Path("/v1/cases")
public class CaseApprovalResource {

    private final CaseApprovalService service;

    public CaseApprovalResource(CaseApprovalService service) {
        this.service = service;
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{caseId}/approve")
    public Response approve(@PathParam("caseId") String caseId) {
        ApprovalResult result = service.approve(caseId);

        return Response.ok(result).build();
    }
}
```

Service harus punya timeout di dependency:

```java id="d84hja"
public class CaseApprovalService {

    private final CaseRepository repository;
    private final CamundaClient camundaClient;

    public ApprovalResult approve(String caseId) {
        CaseEntity caseEntity = repository.findByIdOrThrow(caseId);

        validateApproval(caseEntity);

        repository.markApproved(caseId);

        camundaClient.signalApproval(caseId); // timeout wajib di client

        return new ApprovalResult(caseId, "APPROVED");
    }
}
```

Jika `camundaClient.signalApproval` tidak punya timeout, request thread bisa menggantung lama.

---

# 24. Jersey Resource Pattern: Accepted Async Job

Untuk long-running workflow:

```java id="x3u5ch"
@POST
@Path("/{caseId}/recalculate-risk")
public Response recalculateRisk(@PathParam("caseId") String caseId) {
    String jobId = jobService.submitRiskRecalculation(caseId);

    return Response.accepted(new JobAcceptedResponse(jobId))
            .header("Location", "/api/v1/jobs/" + jobId)
            .build();
}

public record JobAcceptedResponse(String jobId) {
}
```

Status endpoint:

```java id="j0n59v"
@GET
@Path("/jobs/{jobId}")
public JobStatusResponse getJob(@PathParam("jobId") String jobId) {
    return jobService.getStatus(jobId);
}
```

Ini sering lebih baik daripada mempertahankan HTTP request terbuka selama puluhan detik.

---

# 25. Container Shutdown dan In-flight Request

Jika deployment rolling update:

```text id="3qgly0"
Pod menerima SIGTERM
container mulai shutdown
request in-flight masih berjalan
gateway/ingress mungkin masih mengirim traffic sementara
```

Yang harus dipikirkan:

```text id="xnkh0a"
readiness harus turun dulu
stop menerima traffic baru
beri waktu in-flight request selesai
shutdown executor dengan timeout
close DB pool/HTTP client
```

Tomcat 11 connector juga punya setting terkait penghentian executor internal, misalnya `executorTerminationTimeoutMillis`, yaitu waktu tunggu private executor untuk request processing threads terminate sebelum stop connector berlanjut. ([Apache Tomcat](https://tomcat.apache.org/tomcat-11.0-doc/config/http.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Ini akan dibahas lebih dalam di seri production hardening, tapi sudah relevan karena thread yang menggantung memperburuk shutdown.

---

# 26. Code Review Checklist

## 26.1 Request thread safety

```text id="zjh87d"
[ ] Semua endpoint blocking punya timeout di dependency.
[ ] Tidak ada Future.get() tanpa timeout.
[ ] Tidak ada CountDownLatch.await() tanpa timeout.
[ ] Tidak ada Thread.sleep() di request path.
[ ] Tidak ada synchronized lock besar di hot path.
```

## 26.2 DB

```text id="2gpi9g"
[ ] DB pool max diketahui.
[ ] DB pool acquisition timeout diketahui.
[ ] Query berat punya limit/pagination.
[ ] Query penting punya index.
[ ] Statement timeout/lock timeout dipertimbangkan.
[ ] Tidak ada transaction idle lama.
```

## 26.3 HTTP downstream

```text id="tbc1bm"
[ ] Connect timeout.
[ ] Request/read timeout.
[ ] Retry count terbatas.
[ ] Retry total budget tidak melebihi endpoint SLA.
[ ] Circuit breaker/bulkhead untuk dependency kritis.
[ ] Correlation id diteruskan.
```

## 26.4 Async

```text id="gr6cli"
[ ] Async hanya dipakai untuk use case tepat.
[ ] Executor bounded.
[ ] Queue bounded.
[ ] Timeout handler ada.
[ ] Resume/complete selalu terjadi.
[ ] Context propagation eksplisit.
[ ] Executor shutdown saat app stop.
```

## 26.5 Container

```text id="y1jwmv"
[ ] maxThreads diketahui.
[ ] acceptCount diketahui.
[ ] maxConnections diketahui.
[ ] connectionTimeout diketahui.
[ ] keepAliveTimeout diketahui.
[ ] busy thread metrics tersedia.
```

---

# 27. Anti-Pattern

| Anti-pattern | Dampak | Alternatif |
|---|---|---|
| Outbound HTTP tanpa timeout | Thread menggantung | connect + request timeout |
| DB query tanpa limit | Query lambat, memory tinggi | pagination/limit/index |
| `Future.get()` tanpa timeout | Request thread stuck | `get(timeout)` / `orTimeout` |
| Menaikkan `maxThreads` terus | Downstream makin overload | Fix latency, timeout, bulkhead |
| Executor unbounded | Thread explosion | bounded pool + rejection |
| Queue besar tanpa backpressure | Latency makin tinggi | fail fast 429/503 |
| Async untuk semua endpoint | Complexity tanpa manfaat | Async hanya use case tepat |
| Long workflow sync HTTP | Gateway timeout, duplicate retry | 202 + job status |
| Timeout gateway < downstream total retry | Client timeout duluan | timeout budget |
| Tidak ada thread dump saat incident | Diagnosis menebak | ambil thread dump/JMX |

---

Endpoint:

```text id="mfy1eq"
[ ] Tidak tahu timeout gateway.
[ ] HTTP client tanpa timeout.
[ ] Query/report endpoint tanpa limit.
[ ] Future.get tanpa timeout.
[ ] Executor unbounded.
[ ] AsyncResponse tanpa timeout handler.
[ ] DB pool pending tinggi tapi tidak dimonitor.
[ ] maxThreads dinaikkan tanpa analisis DB/downstream.
```
