# Ringkasan Seri 7

Servlet Container request thread adalah resource terbatas. Pada request blocking biasa, thread tetap occupied selama resource method menunggu DB, HTTP downstream, Camunda, file IO, atau lock. Karena itu, service bisa collapse walau CPU rendah.

Model yang harus diingat:

```text id="vztui1"
Latency naik
  → active request naik
  → thread pool penuh
  → queue naik
  → timeout naik
  → retry naik
  → load makin parah
```

Production invariants:

```text id="bg8w2i"
Semua blocking dependency wajib punya timeout.
maxThreads harus selaras dengan DB pool dan downstream capacity.
Queue besar bukan solusi jika latency tinggi.
Async melepas request thread, tapi tidak menghapus biaya blocking.
Async wajib punya timeout, complete/resume, bounded executor, dan context propagation.
Long-running workflow lebih baik 202 Accepted + job status daripada sync HTTP panjang.
```

---

## Response:
7/15/2026, 7:03:59 AM

# Seri 8/12 - Request/Response Handling: Body, Header, Encoding, Compression, Commit

## Tujuan seri ini

Seri ini membahas bagaimana **Servlet Container + Jersey/JAX-RS** menangani request dan response secara praktis:

```text id="90bjm4"
request body
request header
Content-Type
Accept
encoding / charset
JSON/XML mapping
request body stream
multipart / large payload
response status/header/body
response buffering
compression
chunking
response committed
```

Masalah production yang sering muncul di area ini:

```text id="ea2x77"
request body sudah dibaca di Servlet Filter lalu Jersey gagal parse
JSON error berubah jadi HTML/default container
response header gagal diset karena response sudah committed
Content-Type salah sehingga MessageBodyReader tidak aktif
encoding salah sehingga karakter rusak
large payload menyebabkan memory pressure
compression aktif untuk payload yang tidak seharusnya
client timeout saat response streaming/chunking
```

Dalam Servlet API, `ServletRequest#getReader()` membaca body sebagai character data dan **hanya salah satu** dari `getReader()` atau `getInputStream()` yang boleh dipakai untuk membaca body request. Ini menjadi dasar kenapa membaca request body di filter bisa merusak parsing Jersey jika tidak memakai wrapper yang benar. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/servlet/6.1/apidocs/jakarta.servlet/jakarta/servlet/servletrequest?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Mental Model Request Handling

Alur umum request body di aplikasi Jersey:

```text id="uxs0d0"
Client
  ↓
HTTP request body + headers
  ↓
Servlet Container
  ↓
HttpServletRequest
  ↓
Servlet Filter Chain
  ↓
Jersey ServletContainer
  ↓
JAX-RS MessageBodyReader
  ↓
DTO / resource method parameter
  ↓
Service layer
```

Contoh endpoint:

```java id="a6dq00"
@POST
@Path("/v1/cases")
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public CaseResponse createCase(CreateCaseRequest request) {
    return caseService.create(request);
}
```

Yang sebenarnya terjadi:

```text id="4qmwgr"
1. Client mengirim Content-Type: application/json
2. Servlet Container menyediakan request input stream
3. Filter chain berjalan
4. Jersey membaca body melalui provider
5. MessageBodyReader mengubah JSON menjadi CreateCaseRequest
6. Resource method dipanggil
```

Jakarta REST menjelaskan bahwa entity provider terdiri dari `MessageBodyReader` dan `MessageBodyWriter`; `MessageBodyReader` memetakan HTTP request entity body ke method parameter, sedangkan `MessageBodyWriter` memetakan return value menjadi HTTP response entity body. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/learn/docs/jakartaee-tutorial/current/websvcs/rest/rest.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 2. Mental Model Response Handling

Alur response:

```text id="lq4v2i"
Resource method returns object / Response
  ↓
Jersey chooses MessageBodyWriter
  ↓
Object serialized to JSON/XML/etc
  ↓
HttpServletResponse status/header/body
  ↓
Servlet Filter after-section
  ↓
Servlet Container buffer/compression/chunking
  ↓
Client
```

Response bisa menjadi “committed” ketika status/header/body sudah mulai dikirim ke client. Setelah committed, status dan header tidak bisa lagi diubah secara bebas. Servlet API juga menjelaskan bahwa buffer size mempengaruhi kapan response body mulai dikirim; buffer yang lebih besar memberi servlet lebih banyak waktu untuk menetapkan status code dan header sebelum content dikirim. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/servlet/6.1/apidocs/jakarta.servlet/jakarta/servlet/servletresponse?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 3. Diagram

```text id="a3zgr5"
REQUEST SIDE
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Client
  POST /api/v1/cases
  Content-Type: application/json
  Accept: application/json
  Body: {"caseType":"AML","subjectId":"C-123"}

        │
        ▼

Servlet Container
  creates HttpServletRequest
  exposes:
    - headers
    - query
    - input stream
    - character encoding
    - content length/type

        │
        ▼

Servlet Filter Chain
  good:
    - read metadata/header
    - validate Content-Type
    - set correlation id
    - do not consume body

  risky:
    - read getInputStream()
    - read getReader()
    - log body
    - wrap incorrectly

        │
        ▼

Jersey
  MessageBodyReader
    JSON → DTO

        │
        ▼

Resource Method
  CreateCaseRequest dto

RESPONSE SIDE
────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Resource Method
  returns CaseResponse

        │
        ▼

Jersey
  MessageBodyWriter
    DTO → JSON

        │
        ▼

HttpServletResponse
  status
  headers
  content type
  charset
  body buffer

        │
        ▼

Servlet Container
  commit
  compression
  chunking/content-length
  socket write

        │
        ▼

Client
```

---

# 4. Decomposition Map

| No | Area | Fungsi praktis | Yang harus dikuasai | Failure mode umum |
|---:|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Request headers | Metadata request | `Content-Type`, `Accept`, auth, correlation, tenant | Header sensitif ter-log |
| 2 | Request body stream | Payload mentah request | `getInputStream()` vs `getReader()` | Body terbaca dua kali |
| 3 | Content-Type | Menentukan parser request body | `@Consumes`, JSON/XML/form/multipart | 415 Unsupported Media Type |
| 4 | Accept | Menentukan format response | `@Produces`, content negotiation | 406 Not Acceptable / response salah format |
| 5 | Character encoding | Decode text body | UTF-8, `setCharacterEncoding` timing | Karakter rusak |
| 6 | MessageBodyReader | Deserialize body ke object | JSON/XML provider, DTO shape | JSON parse error |
| 7 | MessageBodyWriter | Serialize object ke response | Jackson/JSON-B/XML writer | Serialization error setelah response mulai |
| 8 | Request wrapper | Membaca body ulang dengan aman | bounded buffering | OOM / body hilang |
| 9 | Large payload | File/JSON besar/multipart | limit, streaming, temp file, async job | Memory pressure |
| 10 | Response status/header | Set sebelum committed | `Response.status`, servlet response header | Header/status tidak berubah |
| 11 | Response buffer | Menunda commit body | buffer size, flush behavior | Partial response |
| 12 | Compression | GZIP/text compression | container compression, proxy compression | CPU overhead / double compression |
| 13 | Chunking | Streaming tanpa content-length | streaming response | Error di tengah stream |
| 14 | Error response | JSON error contract | Jersey mapper + servlet error handler | Kadang JSON, kadang HTML |
| 15 | Security | Jangan expose body/header sensitif | redaction, allowlist logging | Token/PII leak |

---

# 5. Request Body: Stream Hanya Bisa Dibaca Sekali

## 5.1 Problem utama

Ini salah satu bug paling sering:

```java id="y9jwjj"
public class BodyLoggingFilter implements Filter {

    @Override
    public void doFilter(
            ServletRequest request,
            ServletResponse response,
            FilterChain chain
    ) throws IOException, ServletException {

        String body = new String(request.getInputStream().readAllBytes());

        System.out.println("body=" + body);

        chain.doFilter(request, response);
    }
}
```

Setelah filter membaca `getInputStream()`, Jersey biasanya tidak bisa membaca body lagi untuk deserialize JSON ke DTO.

Servlet API menyatakan bahwa untuk membaca body, hanya salah satu dari `getReader()` atau `getInputStream()` yang boleh dipanggil, bukan keduanya. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/servlet/6.1/apidocs/jakarta.servlet/jakarta/servlet/servletrequest?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 5.2 Rule praktis

```text id="dkxcgb"
Servlet Filter boleh membaca:
  method
  path
  headers
  query metadata
  content length
  content type

Servlet Filter sebaiknya tidak membaca:
  body stream
  reader
  multipart content
```

Kecuali Anda benar-benar membuat wrapper yang aman.

---

# 6. Request Body Logging: Jangan Default

Request body bisa berisi:

```text id="t9z2t2"
password
token
PII
NIK/KTP/passport
financial data
case evidence
regulatory confidential info
signed payload
medical/legal data
```

Anti-pattern:

```java id="qk1fq1"
log.info("request body={}", body);
```

Lebih aman:

```java id="m8rsv6"
log.info(
    "request method={} uri={} contentType={} contentLength={}",
    request.getMethod(),
    request.getRequestURI(),
    request.getContentType(),
    request.getContentLengthLong()
);
```

Untuk debugging body di non-production, gunakan:

```text id="g6ktex"
feature flag
sampling
max length
redaction
allowlist endpoint
strict access control
auto-expiry
```

---

# 7. Bounded Cached Body Wrapper

Kadang body memang perlu dibaca di filter, misalnya untuk:

```text id="wrcxel"
HMAC signature verification
audit khusus endpoint tertentu
debugging sementara
idempotency key derived from payload
security validation tertentu
```

Gunakan wrapper dengan size limit.

## 7.1 Wrapper

```java id="uuok8t"
package com.company.caseapp.web;

import jakarta.servlet.ReadListener;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletInputStream;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequestWrapper;

import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.ByteArrayInputStream;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStreamReader;
import java.nio.charset.Charset;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;

public class CachedBodyHttpServletRequest extends HttpServletRequestWrapper {

    private final byte[] cachedBody;

    public CachedBodyHttpServletRequest(
            HttpServletRequest request,
            int maxBytes
    ) throws IOException {
        super(request);

        byte[] body = request.getInputStream().readNBytes(maxBytes + 1);

        if (body.length > maxBytes) {
            throw new PayloadTooLargeException(maxBytes);
        }

        this.cachedBody = body;
    }

    @Override
    public ServletInputStream getInputStream() {
        ByteArrayInputStream input = new ByteArrayInputStream(cachedBody);

        return new ServletInputStream() {
            @Override
            public boolean isFinished() {
                return input.available() == 0;
            }

            @Override
            public boolean isReady() {
                return true;
            }

            @Override
            public void setReadListener(ReadListener readListener) {
                throw new UnsupportedOperationException(
                        "Async read listener is not supported by this wrapper"
                );
            }

            @Override
            public int read() {
                return input.read();
            }
        };
    }

    @Override
    public BufferedReader getReader() {
        Charset charset = resolveCharset();
        return new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(getInputStream(), charset));
    }

    public byte[] cachedBody() {
        return cachedBody.clone();
    }

    private Charset resolveCharset() {
        String encoding = getCharacterEncoding();

        if (encoding == null || encoding.isBlank()) {
            return StandardCharsets.UTF_8;
        }

        return Charset.forName(encoding);
    }
}
```

Exception:

```java id="z9ceai"
package com.company.caseapp.web;

public class PayloadTooLargeException extends RuntimeException {

    public PayloadTooLargeException(int maxBytes) {
        super("Payload exceeds max allowed size: " + maxBytes + " bytes");
    }
}
```

## 7.2 Filter penggunaan

```java id="5uyjoc"
package com.company.caseapp.web;

import jakarta.servlet.Filter;
import jakarta.servlet.FilterChain;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletException;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletResponse;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;

public class SafeBodyDebugFilter implements Filter {

    private static final int MAX_CAPTURE_BYTES = 16 * 1024;

    @Override
    public void doFilter(
            ServletRequest request,
            ServletResponse response,
            FilterChain chain
    ) throws IOException, ServletException {

        HttpServletRequest httpRequest = (HttpServletRequest) request;
        HttpServletResponse httpResponse = (HttpServletResponse) response;

        if (!shouldCapture(httpRequest)) {
            chain.doFilter(request, response);
            return;
        }

        try {
            CachedBodyHttpServletRequest wrapped =
                    new CachedBodyHttpServletRequest(httpRequest, MAX_CAPTURE_BYTES);

            String body = new String(
                    wrapped.cachedBody(),
                    StandardCharsets.UTF_8
            );

            System.out.printf(
                    "debug body length=%d preview=%s%n",
                    wrapped.cachedBody().length,
                    redact(body)
            );

            chain.doFilter(wrapped, response);
        } catch (PayloadTooLargeException e) {
            httpResponse.setStatus(HttpServletResponse.SC_REQUEST_ENTITY_TOO_LARGE);
            httpResponse.setContentType("application/json");
            httpResponse.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
            httpResponse.getWriter().write("""
                    {"code":"PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE","message":"Request body is too large"}
                    """);
        }
    }

    private boolean shouldCapture(HttpServletRequest request) {
        return "true".equalsIgnoreCase(System.getenv("DEBUG_CAPTURE_BODY"))
                && request.getRequestURI().startsWith("/api/v1/debug/");
    }

    private String redact(String body) {
        return body
                .replaceAll("(?i)\"password\"\\s*:\\s*\"[^\"]*\"", "\"password\":\"<redacted>\"")
                .replaceAll("(?i)\"token\"\\s*:\\s*\"[^\"]*\"", "\"token\":\"<redacted>\"");
    }
}
```

Rule:

```text id="2ptub8"
Wrapper harus meneruskan wrapped request ke chain.
Jangan baca original request lalu tetap meneruskan original request.
```

---

# 8. Content-Type dan `@Consumes`

## 8.1 Apa fungsi `Content-Type`?

`Content-Type` memberi tahu server format request body.

Contoh:

```http id="eu2xpv"
POST /api/v1/cases HTTP/1.1
Content-Type: application/json
Accept: application/json

{"caseType":"AML"}
```

Resource:

```java id="ercr4z"
@POST
@Path("/v1/cases")
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public CaseResponse create(CreateCaseRequest request) {
    return caseService.create(request);
}
```

Jika client mengirim:

```http id="ejs43z"
Content-Type: text/plain
```

Maka Jersey bisa tidak menemukan `MessageBodyReader` yang cocok dan menghasilkan error seperti `415 Unsupported Media Type`.

## 8.2 Content-Type guard di Servlet Filter

```java id="07c3fi"
public class JsonContentTypeGuardFilter implements Filter {

    @Override
    public void doFilter(
            ServletRequest request,
            ServletResponse response,
            FilterChain chain
    ) throws IOException, ServletException {

        HttpServletRequest httpRequest = (HttpServletRequest) request;
        HttpServletResponse httpResponse = (HttpServletResponse) response;

        boolean methodMayHaveBody = switch (httpRequest.getMethod()) {
            case "POST", "PUT", "PATCH" -> true;
            default -> false;
        };

        if (methodMayHaveBody) {
            String contentType = httpRequest.getContentType();

            if (contentType == null ||
                    !contentType.toLowerCase().startsWith("application/json")) {
                httpResponse.setStatus(HttpServletResponse.SC_UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE);
                httpResponse.setContentType("application/json");
                httpResponse.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
                httpResponse.getWriter().write("""
                        {"code":"UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE","message":"Expected application/json"}
                        """);
                return;
            }
        }

        chain.doFilter(request, response);
    }
}
```

Catatan:

```text id="469h55"
Filter ini cocok jika seluruh API body harus JSON.
Jika ada multipart/form-data atau XML endpoint, buat allowlist per path.
```

---

# 9. Accept dan `@Produces`

## 9.1 Apa fungsi `Accept`?

`Accept` memberi tahu server format response yang diinginkan client.

```http id="n2hajm"
Accept: application/json
```

Resource:

```java id="0rvq68"
@GET
@Path("/v1/cases/{caseId}")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public CaseResponse getCase(@PathParam("caseId") String caseId) {
    return caseService.getCase(caseId);
}
```

Jika client meminta:

```http id="s7vma9"
Accept: application/xml
```

sementara endpoint hanya produce JSON, bisa terjadi negotiation failure.

## 9.2 Rekomendasi praktis

Untuk microservice internal/enterprise, buat default sederhana:

```text id="y79rmb"
Request:
  Content-Type: application/json untuk body JSON

Response:
  Produces application/json

Error:
  selalu application/json
```

Jangan biarkan endpoint sama kadang JSON kadang XML kecuali memang API contract-nya membutuhkan itu.

---

# 10. Character Encoding

## 10.1 Request encoding

Text request body perlu charset. Untuk JSON, praktik modernnya gunakan UTF-8.

Servlet request menyediakan character encoding untuk body; `getReader()` memakai encoding tersebut saat menerjemahkan bytes ke characters. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/servlet/6.1/apidocs/jakarta.servlet/jakarta/servlet/servletrequest?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Filter encoding:

```java id="5d1gz6"
public class Utf8EncodingFilter implements Filter {

    @Override
    public void doFilter(
            ServletRequest request,
            ServletResponse response,
            FilterChain chain
    ) throws IOException, ServletException {

        request.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
        response.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");

        chain.doFilter(request, response);
    }
}
```

Penting:

```text id="71ol8k"
setCharacterEncoding harus dilakukan sebelum body/parameter dibaca.
Jika body sudah dibaca, perubahan encoding bisa terlambat.
```

## 10.2 Response encoding

Untuk JSON response:

```java id="avh9z4"
return Response.ok(response)
        .type(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE.withCharset("UTF-8"))
        .build();
```

Atau di servlet error writer:

```java id="6ur0u1"
response.setContentType("application/json");
response.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
```

---

# 11. DTO Shape dan JSON Mapping

## 11.1 Java record untuk request DTO

```java id="0lngsb"
public record CreateCaseRequest(
        String caseType,
        String subjectId,
        String description
) {
}
```

Resource:

```java id="1bubmy"
@POST
@Path("/v1/cases")
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public CaseResponse createCase(CreateCaseRequest request) {
    return caseService.create(request);
}
```

## 11.2 Error umum

| Gejala | Kemungkinan |
|---|---|
| 400 invalid JSON | JSON malformed |
| 415 unsupported media type | `Content-Type` salah |
| 500 serialization error | response DTO tidak bisa diserialize |
| field null | nama property mismatch |
| date/time salah | format date/time tidak jelas |
| number precision salah | pakai floating untuk uang/amount |
| unknown property gagal | mapper dikonfigurasi strict |

## 11.3 Praktik DTO

```text id="cxwq9r"
Gunakan DTO terpisah dari entity DB.
Jangan expose MyBatis entity langsung.
Gunakan tipe eksplisit untuk date/time: Instant, LocalDate, OffsetDateTime sesuai kebutuhan.
Gunakan BigDecimal untuk amount.
Validasi field wajib dengan Bean Validation.
Jangan taruh business behavior berat di DTO.
```

---

# 12. MessageBodyReader / MessageBodyWriter

Dalam Jakarta REST, entity provider adalah mekanisme mapping request/response body. `MessageBodyReader` membaca body request menjadi Java object, dan `MessageBodyWriter` menulis Java object menjadi response entity. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/restful-ws/4.0/jakarta-restful-ws-spec-4.0.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 12.1 Custom reader/writer kapan diperlukan?

Biasanya tidak perlu untuk JSON biasa karena Jackson/JSON-B sudah cukup.

Custom provider berguna untuk:

```text id="gyct35"
custom media type
encrypted payload
legacy format
CSV import/export
binary protocol tertentu
audit envelope format
```

## 12.2 Risiko custom provider

```text id="dr4k6i"
salah media type matching
provider priority konflik
stream tidak ditutup/terbaca salah
error tidak masuk mapper yang sesuai
memory tinggi karena readAllBytes untuk file besar
```

Recommendation:

```text id="jfouhq"
Untuk JSON/XML biasa, gunakan provider standard.
Custom MessageBodyReader/Writer harus punya test integrasi.
```

---

# 13. Large JSON Payload

Large JSON payload sering muncul untuk:

```text id="7kayru"
bulk import
case migration
rules upload
evidence metadata
batch approval
report criteria besar
```

Risiko:

```text id="eig56q"
memory pressure
slow parsing
request thread occupied lama
timeout
partial failure sulit di-retry
audit sulit
```

Praktik lebih baik:

```text id="o0rihf"
batasi size payload
batasi jumlah item batch
gunakan pagination/chunk upload
gunakan async job untuk bulk besar
gunakan idempotency key
validasi per item dengan result detail
```

Contoh guard:

```java id="b2k73d"
public class ContentLengthGuardFilter implements Filter {

    private static final long MAX_BODY_BYTES = 2 * 1024 * 1024; // 2 MB

    @Override
    public void doFilter(
            ServletRequest request,
            ServletResponse response,
            FilterChain chain
    ) throws IOException, ServletException {

        HttpServletRequest httpRequest = (HttpServletRequest) request;
        HttpServletResponse httpResponse = (HttpServletResponse) response;

        long contentLength = httpRequest.getContentLengthLong();

        if (contentLength > MAX_BODY_BYTES) {
            httpResponse.setStatus(HttpServletResponse.SC_REQUEST_ENTITY_TOO_LARGE);
            httpResponse.setContentType("application/json");
            httpResponse.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
            httpResponse.getWriter().write("""
                    {"code":"PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE","message":"Request body is too large"}
                    """);
            return;
        }

        chain.doFilter(request, response);
    }
}
```

Catatan:

```text id="6dy77m"
Content-Length bisa tidak ada pada chunked request.
Limit yang kuat sebaiknya juga ada di gateway/container.
```

---

# 14. Multipart / File Upload

Multipart biasanya untuk:

```text id="xz9bcm"
document evidence upload
CSV import
image/media upload
OCR input
case attachment
```

Praktik penting:

```text id="vyxl4n"
jangan load file besar sepenuhnya ke memory
gunakan size limit
validasi content type dan extension
scan malware jika perlu
store ke object storage / file service
jangan simpan file besar di DB utama kecuali memang desainnya begitu
gunakan async processing untuk OCR/media
```

Pattern API:

```text id="ttrt62"
POST /api/v1/cases/{caseId}/attachments
Content-Type: multipart/form-data

Response:
  202 Accepted atau 201 Created
```

Untuk file besar:

```text id="p67fim"
Option A:
  upload langsung ke object storage dengan pre-signed URL

Option B:
  upload ke file service dedicated

Option C:
  multipart endpoint dengan strict max size + streaming
```

Jangan campurkan upload file besar dengan transaksi DB panjang.

---

# 15. Response Status, Header, Body

## 15.1 Gunakan `Response` untuk kontrol eksplisit

```java id="4ta1wu"
@POST
@Path("/v1/cases")
public Response createCase(CreateCaseRequest request) {
    CaseResponse created = caseService.create(request);

    return Response.status(Response.Status.CREATED)
            .entity(created)
            .header("Location", "/api/v1/cases/" + created.caseId())
            .build();
}
```

## 15.2 Common status code

| Status | Kapan |
|---:|---|
| 200 | Read/update success dengan body |
| 201 | Resource created |
| 202 | Long-running job accepted |
| 204 | Success tanpa body |
| 400 | Invalid request |
| 401 | Unauthenticated |
| 403 | Authenticated tapi tidak authorized |
| 404 | Resource tidak ditemukan |
| 409 | Conflict/state transition invalid |
| 415 | Unsupported media type |
| 422 | Semantic validation error, jika API contract memakai |
| 429 | Rate/concurrency limit |
| 500 | Unexpected server error |
| 503 | Dependency/service unavailable |

---

# 16. Response Committed

## 16.1 Apa itu committed?

Response committed berarti status dan header sudah dikirim ke client atau setidaknya sudah tidak bisa diubah secara aman.

Servlet API menyediakan method seperti `isCommitted()`, `flushBuffer()`, dan buffer handling pada `ServletResponse`; buffer yang lebih besar memberi lebih banyak ruang sebelum content dikirim ke client. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/servlet/6.1/apidocs/jakarta.servlet/jakarta/servlet/servletresponse?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 16.2 Bug umum

```java id="xy3bci"
response.getWriter().write("partial response");
response.flushBuffer();

response.setStatus(500); // terlambat
```

Atau:

```java id="o979q7"
StreamingOutput stream = output -> {
    output.write(firstChunk);
    riskyOperation(); // throws exception
    output.write(secondChunk);
};
```

Jika error terjadi setelah chunk pertama terkirim, `ExceptionMapper` tidak bisa mengubah response menjadi JSON error normal.

## 16.3 Rule

```text id="mb6hpl"
Untuk response JSON biasa:
  jangan flush manual
  biarkan Jersey/container mengatur buffer

Untuk streaming:
  sadar bahwa error setelah commit tidak bisa menjadi error JSON normal
```

---

# 17. Response Wrapper untuk Status dan Body

## 17.1 Status capture

Sudah dipakai di seri sebelumnya:

```java id="t084bq"
public class StatusCaptureResponseWrapper extends HttpServletResponseWrapper {

    private int statusCode = SC_OK;

    public StatusCaptureResponseWrapper(HttpServletResponse response) {
        super(response);
    }

    @Override
    public void setStatus(int sc) {
        this.statusCode = sc;
        super.setStatus(sc);
    }

    @Override
    public void sendError(int sc) throws IOException {
        this.statusCode = sc;
        super.sendError(sc);
    }

    @Override
    public void sendRedirect(String location) throws IOException {
        this.statusCode = SC_FOUND;
        super.sendRedirect(location);
    }

    public int getStatusCode() {
        return statusCode;
    }
}
```

## 17.2 Body capture hati-hati

Response body capture berisiko:

```text id="o11xna"
memory pressure
sensitive data leak
streaming rusak
compression/chunking berubah
latency naik
```

Gunakan hanya untuk debugging terbatas atau endpoint kecil.

---

# 18. Compression

Compression bisa dilakukan oleh:

```text id="doamdx"
API Gateway
Ingress
Servlet Container
application filter
```

Jangan aktifkan di banyak layer tanpa desain karena bisa terjadi double work atau behavior tidak konsisten.

Tomcat HTTP Connector mendukung parameter `compression` untuk GZIP HTTP/1.1, dengan opsi seperti `off`, `on`, `force`, atau angka minimum size untuk compression. ([Apache Tomcat](https://tomcat.apache.org/tomcat-9.0-doc/config/http.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh Tomcat conceptual config:

```xml id="lxp84l"
<Connector
    port="8080"
    protocol="HTTP/1.1"
    compression="on"
    compressionMinSize="2048"
    compressibleMimeType="text/html,text/xml,text/plain,text/css,text/javascript,application/javascript,application/json" />
```

Practical rules:

```text id="aj66av"
Compress text/json besar.
Jangan compress payload kecil secara agresif.
Jangan compress file yang sudah compressed: zip, jpg, png, pdf tertentu.
Ukur CPU overhead.
Pastikan gateway/container tidak double-compress.
```

---

# 19. Chunked Response dan Streaming

Jika response size tidak diketahui, server bisa memakai chunked transfer.

Use case:

```text id="0slb7b"
large export
streaming report
server-sent events
download file
```

Risiko:

```text id="4t9xgt"
error setelah chunk pertama tidak bisa diubah menjadi normal JSON error
client disconnect
long-held connection
proxy timeout
backpressure ke writer
```

Pattern untuk export besar:

```text id="tc5uhw"
Lebih aman:
  POST /exports → 202 Accepted
  background generate file
  GET /exports/{id}/download

Daripada:
  GET /huge-report langsung streaming 5 menit
```

Untuk streaming, definisikan:

```text id="35n1o5"
timeout
max duration
client disconnect behavior
audit status
partial failure semantics
retry strategy
```

---

# 20. Error Handling saat Body Parsing Gagal

JSON parse error terjadi sebelum resource method dipanggil, tapi masih di dalam Jersey request processing.

Mapper khusus:

```java id="q8f0nm"
package com.company.caseapp.provider;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.core.JsonProcessingException;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.ExceptionMapper;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.Provider;

@Provider
public class JsonProcessingExceptionMapper
        implements ExceptionMapper<JsonProcessingException> {

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(JsonProcessingException exception) {
        return Response.status(Response.Status.BAD_REQUEST)
                .entity(new ErrorResponse(
                        "INVALID_JSON",
                        "Malformed JSON request body"
                ))
                .build();
    }

    public record ErrorResponse(String code, String message) {
    }
}
```

Register:

```java id="c580m2"
public class CaseApplication extends ResourceConfig {

    public CaseApplication() {
        register(JsonProcessingExceptionMapper.class);
        register(GlobalExceptionMapper.class);
    }
}
```

Catatan:

```text id="kc76su"
Exception type spesifik bergantung JSON provider yang dipakai.
Jika memakai JSON-B, exception type bisa berbeda dari Jackson.
```

---

# 21. Validasi Body

Gunakan Bean Validation untuk validasi DTO.

```java id="v8fbsd"
public record CreateCaseRequest(
        @NotBlank String caseType,
        @NotBlank String subjectId,
        @Size(max = 2000) String description
) {
}
```

Resource:

```java id="7bso44"
@POST
@Path("/v1/cases")
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public CaseResponse createCase(@Valid CreateCaseRequest request) {
    return caseService.create(request);
}
```

Error mapper validation:

```java id="ny735n"
@Provider
public class ConstraintViolationExceptionMapper
        implements ExceptionMapper<jakarta.validation.ConstraintViolationException> {

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(jakarta.validation.ConstraintViolationException exception) {
        var errors = exception.getConstraintViolations()
                .stream()
                .map(v -> new FieldError(
                        v.getPropertyPath().toString(),
                        v.getMessage()
                ))
                .toList();

        return Response.status(Response.Status.BAD_REQUEST)
                .entity(new ValidationErrorResponse("VALIDATION_ERROR", errors))
                .build();
    }

    public record ValidationErrorResponse(String code, java.util.List<FieldError> errors) {
    }

    public record FieldError(String field, String message) {
    }
}
```

---

# 22. Response Error Contract

Buat satu shape standar:

```java id="zfecx7"
public record ErrorResponse(
        String code,
        String message,
        String correlationId
) {
}
```

Jersey mapper:

```java id="6p9bw0"
@Provider
public class GlobalExceptionMapper implements ExceptionMapper<Throwable> {

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(Throwable exception) {
        String correlationId = RequestContext.currentOrNull() == null
                ? ""
                : RequestContext.currentOrNull().correlationId();

        ErrorResponse body = new ErrorResponse(
                "INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR",
                "Unexpected server error",
                correlationId
        );

        return Response.status(Response.Status.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR)
                .type(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE)
                .entity(body)
                .build();
    }
}
```

Servlet error handler juga harus mengeluarkan shape yang mirip.

Prinsip:

```text id="l3hy5h"
Jangan expose stack trace.
Jangan expose SQL/internal exception detail.
Selalu berikan correlationId jika tersedia.
```

---

# 23. Header Security

Response security header umum:

```java id="rffndb"
public class SecurityHeadersFilter implements Filter {

    @Override
    public void doFilter(
            ServletRequest request,
            ServletResponse response,
            FilterChain chain
    ) throws IOException, ServletException {

        HttpServletResponse httpResponse = (HttpServletResponse) response;

        httpResponse.setHeader("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff");
        httpResponse.setHeader("X-Frame-Options", "DENY");
        httpResponse.setHeader("Referrer-Policy", "no-referrer");
        httpResponse.setHeader("Cache-Control", "no-store");

        chain.doFilter(request, response);
    }
}
```

Catatan:

```text id="pnc5yi"
Cache-Control: no-store cocok untuk API yang mengembalikan data sensitif.
Untuk public/static response, policy bisa berbeda.
```

---

# 24. Cache-Control untuk API

Untuk data regulatory/case management, default aman:

```http id="0dm7tu"
Cache-Control: no-store
Pragma: no-cache
```

Untuk endpoint static/reference non-sensitive:

```http id="734f7y"
Cache-Control: public, max-age=3600
```

Untuk API internal yang sensitif:

```text id="bgh7xg"
Prefer no-store kecuali ada desain caching eksplisit.
```

---

# 25. Practical Implementation: Request/Response Guard Filter

```java id="3c7m1b"
public class ApiHttpGuardFilter implements Filter {

    private static final long MAX_JSON_BODY_BYTES = 2 * 1024 * 1024;

    @Override
    public void doFilter(
            ServletRequest request,
            ServletResponse response,
            FilterChain chain
    ) throws IOException, ServletException {

        HttpServletRequest httpRequest = (HttpServletRequest) request;
        HttpServletResponse httpResponse = (HttpServletResponse) response;

        setDefaultResponseHeaders(httpResponse);

        if (hasBody(httpRequest)) {
            if (!isJson(httpRequest)) {
                writeError(
                        httpResponse,
                        HttpServletResponse.SC_UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE,
                        "UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE",
                        "Expected application/json"
                );
                return;
            }

            long contentLength = httpRequest.getContentLengthLong();

            if (contentLength > MAX_JSON_BODY_BYTES) {
                writeError(
                        httpResponse,
                        HttpServletResponse.SC_REQUEST_ENTITY_TOO_LARGE,
                        "PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE",
                        "Request body is too large"
                );
                return;
            }
        }

        chain.doFilter(request, response);
    }

    private static boolean hasBody(HttpServletRequest request) {
        return switch (request.getMethod()) {
            case "POST", "PUT", "PATCH" -> true;
            default -> false;
        };
    }

    private static boolean isJson(HttpServletRequest request) {
        String contentType = request.getContentType();
        return contentType != null
                && contentType.toLowerCase().startsWith("application/json");
    }

    private static void setDefaultResponseHeaders(HttpServletResponse response) {
        response.setHeader("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff");
        response.setHeader("Cache-Control", "no-store");
    }

    private static void writeError(
            HttpServletResponse response,
            int status,
            String code,
            String message
    ) throws IOException {

        if (response.isCommitted()) {
            return;
        }

        response.setStatus(status);
        response.setContentType("application/json");
        response.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
        response.getWriter().write("""
                {"code":"%s","message":"%s"}
                """.formatted(code, message));
    }
}
```

Catatan:

```text id="7rhjvw"
Filter ini cocok jika API Anda murni JSON.
Jika ada multipart/upload/XML endpoint, buat pengecualian berbasis path atau pisahkan filter per route.
```

---

# 26. Testing Request/Response Handling

## 26.1 Valid JSON

```java id="69e2bg"
@Test
void createCaseShouldAcceptJson() throws Exception {
    HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
            .uri(URI.create("http://localhost:8080/case-service/api/v1/cases"))
            .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
            .header("Accept", "application/json")
            .POST(HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofString("""
                    {"caseType":"AML","subjectId":"C-123"}
                    """))
            .build();

    HttpResponse<String> response = HttpClient.newHttpClient()
            .send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());

    assertEquals(201, response.statusCode());
    assertTrue(response.headers()
            .firstValue("Content-Type")
            .orElse("")
            .contains("application/json"));
}
```

## 26.2 Invalid Content-Type

```java id="vug8d8"
@Test
void createCaseShouldRejectPlainText() throws Exception {
    HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
            .uri(URI.create("http://localhost:8080/case-service/api/v1/cases"))
            .header("Content-Type", "text/plain")
            .POST(HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofString("hello"))
            .build();

    HttpResponse<String> response = HttpClient.newHttpClient()
            .send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());

    assertEquals(415, response.statusCode());
    assertTrue(response.body().contains("UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE"));
}
```

## 26.3 Malformed JSON

```java id="t1jj0w"
@Test
void createCaseShouldRejectMalformedJson() throws Exception {
    HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
            .uri(URI.create("http://localhost:8080/case-service/api/v1/cases"))
            .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
            .POST(HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofString("""
                    {"caseType":
                    """))
            .build();

    HttpResponse<String> response = HttpClient.newHttpClient()
            .send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());

    assertEquals(400, response.statusCode());
    assertTrue(response.body().contains("INVALID_JSON"));
}
```

## 26.4 Large payload

```java id="yrsh61"
@Test
void createCaseShouldRejectLargePayload() throws Exception {
    String largeDescription = "x".repeat(3 * 1024 * 1024);

    HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
            .uri(URI.create("http://localhost:8080/case-service/api/v1/cases"))
            .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
            .POST(HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofString("""
                    {"caseType":"AML","subjectId":"C-123","description":"%s"}
                    """.formatted(largeDescription)))
            .build();

    HttpResponse<String> response = HttpClient.newHttpClient()
            .send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());

    assertEquals(413, response.statusCode());
}
```

## 26.5 Response security headers

```java id="30k9p1"
@Test
void responseShouldContainSecurityHeaders() throws Exception {
    HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
            .uri(URI.create("http://localhost:8080/case-service/api/v1/health"))
            .GET()
            .build();

    HttpResponse<String> response = HttpClient.newHttpClient()
            .send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());

    assertEquals("nosniff", response.headers()
            .firstValue("X-Content-Type-Options")
            .orElseThrow());
}
```

---

# 27. Production Debug Playbook

## Case A - Jersey DTO selalu null / parse gagal

Cek:

```text id="6szgd3"
[ ] Content-Type benar?
[ ] Body valid JSON?
[ ] DTO punya shape yang cocok?
[ ] JSON provider aktif?
[ ] Filter membaca body duluan?
[ ] getReader/getInputStream dipanggil di filter?
[ ] Charset/encoding aneh?
```

## Case B - `IllegalStateException: getInputStream() has already been called`

Cek:

```text id="j2drb7"
[ ] Ada filter yang memanggil getReader?
[ ] Ada library security/logging yang membaca body?
[ ] Ada multipart parser custom?
[ ] Ada wrapper yang salah implementasi?
```

## Case C - Error response HTML

Cek:

```text id="2nmeud"
[ ] Error terjadi sebelum Jersey?
[ ] Servlet error handler JSON ada?
[ ] ExceptionMapper aktif?
[ ] Content-Type error response diset?
[ ] Response sudah committed?
```

## Case D - Header tidak muncul di response

Cek:

```text id="k1w3cx"
[ ] Header diset sebelum response committed?
[ ] Ada proxy/gateway yang menghapus header?
[ ] Filter mapping benar?
[ ] Response short-circuit melewati filter tertentu?
[ ] CORS preflight path berbeda?
```

## Case E - Karakter rusak

Cek:

```text id="wtx8gi"
[ ] Request charset?
[ ] Response charset?
[ ] JSON provider config?
[ ] Database encoding?
[ ] File/source encoding?
[ ] setCharacterEncoding dipanggil terlambat?
```

## Case F - Memory naik saat request besar

Cek:

```text id="t3nr04"
[ ] Ada readAllBytes?
[ ] Ada body logging?
[ ] Multipart load ke memory?
[ ] JSON array terlalu besar?
[ ] Response body dicapture wrapper?
[ ] Compression buffering?
```

---

# 28. Code Review Checklist

## 28.1 Request body

```text id="ivuxlc"
[ ] Tidak ada body logging default.
[ ] Tidak ada getInputStream/getReader di filter tanpa wrapper.
[ ] Jika wrapper ada, punya max size.
[ ] Large payload punya limit.
[ ] Multipart endpoint punya size limit.
[ ] Content-Type divalidasi.
```

## 28.2 Serialization

```text id="bdmvc5"
[ ] JSON provider jelas.
[ ] DTO tidak expose entity internal.
[ ] Date/time format jelas.
[ ] BigDecimal untuk amount.
[ ] Unknown field policy jelas.
[ ] Validation error dimap ke response standar.
```

## 28.3 Response

```text id="6ksv2e"
[ ] Status code eksplisit untuk create/update/delete.
[ ] Content-Type JSON konsisten.
[ ] Error response shape konsisten.
[ ] Header security diset.
[ ] Tidak flush manual untuk JSON biasa.
[ ] Streaming endpoint punya failure semantics.
```

## 28.4 Security/privacy

```text id="79ygkb"
[ ] Authorization/Cookie/token tidak ter-log.
[ ] Query/body sensitif tidak ter-log.
[ ] Response error tidak expose stack trace.
[ ] Cache-Control aman untuk data sensitif.
[ ] File upload divalidasi.
```

## 28.5 Compression/large response

```text id="789plm"
[ ] Compression layer jelas: gateway atau container.
[ ] Tidak double compression.
[ ] Large export tidak sync panjang tanpa alasan.
[ ] Streaming response punya timeout dan client disconnect strategy.
```

---

# 29. Anti-Pattern

| Anti-pattern | Dampak | Alternatif |
|---|---|---|
| Membaca body di filter lalu meneruskan original request | Jersey gagal parse | Gunakan wrapper bounded |
| Logging raw body | PII/secret leak | Log metadata + redaction |
| Tidak validasi Content-Type | Parser error tidak jelas | Guard + `@Consumes` |
| Error response tanpa Content-Type JSON | Client gagal parse | Set JSON content type |
| Flush response terlalu awal | Status/header tidak bisa diubah | Biarkan Jersey/container buffer |
| Streaming untuk proses panjang tanpa desain | Partial response, timeout | 202 job + polling/download |
| Large JSON array tanpa limit | Memory/latency tinggi | Batch limit + async job |
| Multipart file load ke memory | OOM | Streaming/temp file/object storage |
| Compression di app dan gateway | CPU/double work | Satu layer compression jelas |
| Expose stack trace di JSON error | Security leak | Generic message + correlationId |

---

# 30. Mini Exercise untuk Repo Anda

Cari kode berisiko:

```bash id="m2hiy0"
grep -R "getInputStream" src/main/java
grep -R "getReader" src/main/java
grep -R "readAllBytes" src/main/java
grep -R "getWriter" src/main/java
grep -R "flushBuffer" src/main/java
grep -R "setContentType" src/main/java
grep -R "setCharacterEncoding" src/main/java
grep -R "@Consumes" src/main/java
grep -R "@Produces" src/main/java
grep -R "StreamingOutput" src/main/java
grep -R "multipart" src/main/java
```

Isi matrix:

```text id="qxm4dj"
Endpoint:
  ...

Request body type:
  JSON / XML / multipart / binary / none

Content-Type required:
  ...

Max body size:
  ...

Reads body in filter:
  yes/no

Wrapper used:
  yes/no

JSON provider:
  Jackson / JSON-B / other

Validation:
  yes/no

Error mapper:
  ...

Response type:
  JSON / file / stream

Compression:
  gateway / container / app / unknown

Risk:
  low / medium / high
```

Red flag terbesar:

```text id="pxtws0"
[ ] readAllBytes di request path.
[ ] Body logging aktif default.
[ ] Multipart tanpa size limit.
[ ] StreamingOutput tanpa error/timeout strategy.
[ ] Error response HTML untuk API.
[ ] Response flush manual sebelum semua validation selesai.
[ ] Large export sync lewat HTTP.
```

---

# Ringkasan Seri 8

Request/response handling adalah area boundary yang sangat rawan karena Servlet Container, Filter, Jersey provider, JSON/XML mapper, dan client contract bertemu di sini.

Invariants penting:

```text id="n91nln"
Request body stream hanya boleh dibaca dengan disiplin.
Filter jangan membaca body kecuali memakai bounded wrapper.
Content-Type dan Accept harus jelas.
JSON provider dan DTO shape harus eksplisit.
Response status/header harus diset sebelum committed.
Error response API harus selalu JSON.
Large payload perlu limit dan strategi async/streaming.
Compression harus jelas layer-nya.
Secret/PII tidak boleh masuk log.
```

Model yang harus diingat:

```text id="i1ipjv"
Request body:
  client bytes → servlet stream → filter → Jersey MessageBodyReader → DTO

Response body:
  object/Response → Jersey MessageBodyWriter → servlet response buffer → commit → client
```

---

**Seri 8/12 selesai.**
