# Seri 9/12 - Session, Cookie, Security Constraint, TLS, Forwarded Headers

## Tujuan seri ini

Seri ini membahas boundary keamanan HTTP yang disediakan atau dipengaruhi oleh **Servlet Container**:

```text id="7u4x70"
HttpSession
Cookie
JSESSIONID
Secure / HttpOnly / SameSite
security-constraint di web.xml
container-managed auth
TLS termination
reverse proxy / ingress
X-Forwarded-* / Forwarded headers
client IP audit
redirect / callback URL
```

Target praktisnya: Anda bisa membedakan mana yang sebaiknya ditangani oleh **Servlet Container**, mana yang sebaiknya ditangani oleh **API Gateway/Ingress**, dan mana yang seharusnya tetap berada di **application/Jersey/service layer**.

Dalam Jakarta Servlet, `HttpSession` merepresentasikan mekanisme server-side untuk mengidentifikasi user lintas lebih dari satu request; session bisa dipertahankan dengan cookie atau URL rewriting. Ini penting karena begitu session dipakai, aplikasi yang awalnya stateless bisa berubah menjadi stateful. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/servlet/5.0/apidocs/jakarta/servlet/http/httpsession?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Mental Model

Dalam microservice Jersey/JAX-RS, ada dua model besar:

```text id="rtm7ve"
Stateless API model:
  request membawa token/context
  server tidak menyimpan user session per client
  scaling lebih mudah
  cocok untuk Kubernetes/microservice

Stateful servlet session model:
  server membuat HttpSession
  client membawa JSESSIONID cookie
  server perlu menyimpan state session
  scaling butuh sticky session / shared session / replication
```

Untuk microservice API modern, default yang lebih aman biasanya:

```text id="sxq6sm"
Avoid HttpSession unless explicitly required.
Use explicit token/context.
Keep service stateless where possible.
```

Kenapa? Karena `HttpSession` bisa membuat dependency tersembunyi terhadap memory lokal container. Di Kubernetes, request berikutnya bisa masuk pod berbeda, kecuali ada sticky session atau session replication.

---

# 2. Diagram Security Boundary

```text id="xczetw"
Client / Browser / Service Client
  │
  │ HTTPS public
  │ Authorization: Bearer ...
  │ Cookie: JSESSIONID=...
  │ X-Correlation-Id: ...
  ▼

API Gateway / Load Balancer
  - TLS termination
  - WAF / rate limit
  - auth pre-check optional
  - adds X-Forwarded-For
  - adds X-Forwarded-Proto
  - adds X-Forwarded-Host
  │
  │ HTTP or HTTPS internal
  ▼

Ingress / Reverse Proxy
  - path routing
  - rewrite
  - trusted proxy boundary
  │
  ▼

Servlet Container
  - sees request scheme/host/remoteAddr
  - may create HttpSession
  - may set cookies
  - may enforce web.xml security-constraint
  - may normalize forwarded headers if configured
  │
  ▼

Servlet Filter Chain
  - correlation id
  - auth pre-check
  - tenant/user context
  - security headers
  │
  ▼

Jersey / JAX-RS
  - resource-level auth
  - exception mapper
  - DTO validation
  │
  ▼

Application Service
  - business authorization
  - audit trail
  - DB/Camunda/external integration
```

Critical boundary:

```text id="bapzw9"
Header seperti X-Forwarded-For, X-Forwarded-Proto, X-User-Id, X-Tenant-Id
hanya boleh dipercaya jika berasal dari proxy/gateway yang trusted.
```

---

# 3. Decomposition Map

| No | Area | Fungsi praktis | Yang harus dikuasai | Failure mode umum |
|---:|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `HttpSession` | Server-side state antar request | Kapan session dibuat, timeout, invalidate | Microservice tidak sengaja stateful |
| 2 | `JSESSIONID` | Cookie default session servlet | Secure, HttpOnly, path, domain | Session fixation/leak |
| 3 | Cookie attributes | Kontrol browser behavior | Secure, HttpOnly, SameSite, Max-Age, Path, Domain | Cookie dikirim ke scope terlalu luas |
| 4 | Stateless API | Token/context per request | Bearer token, mTLS, gateway identity | Token forwarding sembarangan |
| 5 | `security-constraint` | Container-level access restriction | URL pattern, auth-constraint, transport-guarantee | Salah path, endpoint terbuka/tertutup |
| 6 | Container-managed auth | Basic/form/client-cert/realm | Cocok atau tidak untuk microservice | Duplikasi dengan gateway/app auth |
| 7 | TLS termination | HTTPS berhenti di LB/gateway/container | Public HTTPS vs internal HTTP | Redirect jadi `http://` |
| 8 | Forwarded headers | Preserve original scheme/host/IP | `X-Forwarded-*`, `Forwarded`, trusted proxy | Audit IP salah, spoofing |
| 9 | Remote IP normalization | Container membaca client IP asli | Tomcat `RemoteIpValve` / equivalent | Semua IP terlihat sebagai ingress |
| 10 | Secure redirect/callback | Generate public URL benar | Public base URL vs request URL | Callback/payment URL salah |
| 11 | Security headers | Response hardening | HSTS, nosniff, frame options, cache control | Browser behavior tidak aman |
| 12 | Audit identity | User/tenant/client IP/correlation ID | Source of truth identity | Audit trail tidak defensible |
| 13 | CSRF | Risiko browser cookie auth | Token/header/origin strategy | Cookie session disalahgunakan |
| 14 | Session cleanup | Logout/invalidate/timeout | `invalidate()`, max inactive interval | Session zombie |
| 15 | Kubernetes scaling | Session/pod affinity | sticky session vs stateless | Random logout/session missing |

---

# 4. `HttpSession`: Jangan Dipakai Tanpa Sadar

## 4.1 Apa itu `HttpSession`?

`HttpSession` memungkinkan servlet container mengasosiasikan request dengan session user/client tertentu. Session dapat dipertahankan lewat cookie atau URL rewriting. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/servlet/5.0/apidocs/jakarta/servlet/http/httpsession?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh:

```java id="1ztrle"
HttpSession session = request.getSession();
session.setAttribute("userId", "u-123");
```

Masalah: `request.getSession()` tanpa argumen akan **membuat session baru** jika belum ada.

Lebih aman untuk mengecek tanpa membuat:

```java id="syrgfy"
HttpSession session = request.getSession(false);

if (session != null) {
    Object userId = session.getAttribute("userId");
}
```

## 4.2 Red flag di microservice

Cari:

```bash id="f5u4s8"
grep -R "getSession()" src/main/java
grep -R "getSession(true)" src/main/java
grep -R "setAttribute" src/main/java | grep session
grep -R "JSESSIONID" src/main/java src/main/resources src/main/webapp
```

Jika service API seharusnya stateless, penggunaan berikut perlu review:

```java id="vuxqv5"
request.getSession().setAttribute("tenantId", tenantId);
```

Karena ini membuat tenant/user context bergantung pada server-side session.

---

# 5. Stateless vs Stateful: Pilihan Arsitektural

| Aspek | Stateless API | Stateful HttpSession |
|---|---|---|
| Scaling Kubernetes | Mudah | Butuh sticky/replication/shared store |
| Debugging | Context di request/token | Context tersebar di server session |
| Failure pod | Tidak kehilangan session | Session bisa hilang jika lokal |
| Browser login app | Bisa tetap pakai token/cookie auth | Cocok untuk web app tradisional |
| Service-to-service | Cocok | Tidak cocok |
| Regulatory audit | Lebih eksplisit per request | Perlu korelasi session kuat |
| Risiko | Token leak | Session fixation/hijacking/state leak |

Rekomendasi untuk Jersey microservice:

```text id="cnlb0b"
Service-to-service API:
  stateless

Browser-facing backend:
  boleh cookie/session, tapi harus sadar CSRF, SameSite, Secure, HttpOnly

Regulatory/case-management API:
  prefer explicit authenticated principal + tenant per request,
  bukan implicit mutable session state.
```

---

# 6. Cookie Basics

Jakarta Servlet `Cookie` API menyediakan atribut seperti `HttpOnly`; dokumentasinya menjelaskan bahwa `setHttpOnly(true)` menambahkan atribut `HttpOnly`, sehingga cookie tidak seharusnya terekspos ke client-side scripting dan dapat membantu mengurangi beberapa risiko XSS. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/servlet/6.1/apidocs/jakarta.servlet/jakarta/servlet/http/cookie?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh membuat cookie:

```java id="qx11yg"
Cookie cookie = new Cookie("SESSION_HINT", "abc123");
cookie.setHttpOnly(true);
cookie.setSecure(true);
cookie.setPath("/case-service");
cookie.setMaxAge(3600);

response.addCookie(cookie);
```

Namun Servlet `Cookie` API tradisional tidak selalu nyaman untuk semua atribut modern seperti `SameSite`, tergantung container/API version dan cara set header. Untuk SameSite, banyak aplikasi mengatur lewat container config atau manual `Set-Cookie` header dengan hati-hati.

---

# 7. Cookie Attributes yang Wajib Dipahami

| Attribute | Fungsi | Rekomendasi praktis |
|---|---|---|
| `Secure` | Cookie hanya dikirim lewat HTTPS | Wajib untuk auth/session cookie |
| `HttpOnly` | Tidak bisa dibaca JS via `document.cookie` | Wajib untuk session cookie |
| `SameSite` | Kontrol cross-site cookie sending | `Lax` atau `Strict`; `None` hanya jika perlu dan harus `Secure` |
| `Path` | Scope path cookie | Buat sesempit mungkin |
| `Domain` | Scope domain/subdomain | Hindari domain terlalu luas |
| `Max-Age` | Lifetime cookie | Session cookie atau durasi terbatas |
| `Expires` | Expiry lama-style | Konsisten dengan Max-Age |
| `Name` | Nama cookie | Hindari nama framework default jika bisa dan perlu hardening |

OWASP Session Management Cheat Sheet juga menekankan `HttpOnly` untuk mencegah akses cookie session lewat DOM scripting, meskipun request browser tetap akan membawa cookie tersebut sehingga CSRF tetap perlu dipikirkan. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Session_Management_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 8. `JSESSIONID`

Jika Servlet Container membuat `HttpSession`, biasanya client akan menerima cookie session seperti:

```http id="7q3zpk"
Set-Cookie: JSESSIONID=ABCDEF123456; Path=/case-service; HttpOnly
```

Risiko:

```text id="92witp"
[ ] Session cookie tidak Secure di production HTTPS
[ ] Path terlalu luas, misalnya /
[ ] Domain terlalu luas, misalnya .company.com
[ ] SameSite tidak jelas untuk browser-facing app
[ ] Session dibuat tidak sengaja oleh filter/resource
[ ] Session tidak invalidated saat logout
[ ] Session timeout terlalu panjang
```

Practical check dengan curl:

```bash id="sx45ej"
curl -i https://api.company.com/case-service/api/v1/health
```

Cari:

```text id="fqrcu0"
Set-Cookie: JSESSIONID=...
```

Jika health endpoint stateless mengeluarkan `JSESSIONID`, itu red flag. Artinya ada layer yang membuat session padahal mungkin tidak perlu.

---

# 9. Mencegah Session Tidak Sengaja

## 9.1 Filter audit session creation

```java id="r83cst"
package com.company.caseapp.web;

import jakarta.servlet.Filter;
import jakarta.servlet.FilterChain;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletException;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletResponse;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;

import java.io.IOException;

public class SessionAuditFilter implements Filter {

    @Override
    public void doFilter(
            ServletRequest request,
            ServletResponse response,
            FilterChain chain
    ) throws IOException, ServletException {

        HttpServletRequest httpRequest = (HttpServletRequest) request;

        boolean hadSessionBefore = httpRequest.getSession(false) != null;

        chain.doFilter(request, response);

        boolean hasSessionAfter = httpRequest.getSession(false) != null;

        if (!hadSessionBefore && hasSessionAfter) {
            System.out.printf(
                    "SESSION_CREATED method=%s uri=%s%n",
                    httpRequest.getMethod(),
                    httpRequest.getRequestURI()
            );
        }
    }
}
```

Gunakan di non-prod/staging untuk mencari endpoint/filter yang membuat session.

## 9.2 Jangan begini

```java id="humrzb"
HttpSession session = request.getSession();
```

Jika hanya mau cek session:

```java id="0xol2h"
HttpSession session = request.getSession(false);
```

---

# 10. Session Fixation dan Login

Jika aplikasi memang memakai session untuk login browser, setelah login sukses sebaiknya session ID diganti agar session fixation tidak terjadi.

Pattern:

```java id="bo7wrn"
HttpSession oldSession = request.getSession(false);
if (oldSession != null) {
    oldSession.invalidate();
}

HttpSession newSession = request.getSession(true);
newSession.setAttribute("userId", authenticatedUser.userId());
```

Servlet API juga punya mekanisme `changeSessionId()` di `HttpServletRequest` pada versi modern, yang dapat dipakai setelah autentikasi sukses:

```java id="rbopcx"
request.changeSessionId();
request.getSession().setAttribute("userId", authenticatedUser.userId());
```

Practical rule:

```text id="77vq6i"
Jika session dipakai untuk auth browser:
  rotate session id setelah login
  invalidate saat logout
  set timeout
  set cookie Secure + HttpOnly + SameSite
```

---

# 11. Security Constraint di `web.xml`

Servlet Container bisa menerapkan declarative security melalui `web.xml`.

Jakarta EE tutorial menjelaskan bahwa Servlet specification mendefinisikan mekanisme Basic dan Form authentication serta security constraints di `web.xml` untuk menentukan resource yang diproteksi. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/learn/docs/jakartaee-tutorial/current/security/security.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Contoh:

```xml id="sgh8pf"
<security-constraint>
    <web-resource-collection>
        <web-resource-name>Protected API</web-resource-name>
        <url-pattern>/api/*</url-pattern>
        <http-method>GET</http-method>
        <http-method>POST</http-method>
        <http-method>PUT</http-method>
        <http-method>DELETE</http-method>
    </web-resource-collection>

    <auth-constraint>
        <role-name>api-user</role-name>
    </auth-constraint>

    <user-data-constraint>
        <transport-guarantee>CONFIDENTIAL</transport-guarantee>
    </user-data-constraint>
</security-constraint>

<login-config>
    <auth-method>BASIC</auth-method>
    <realm-name>CaseServiceRealm</realm-name>
</login-config>

<security-role>
    <role-name>api-user</role-name>
</security-role>
```

Makna:

```text id="wsa75y"
URL /api/* diproteksi
hanya role api-user boleh akses
transport harus confidential
auth method BASIC
```

---

# 12. Apakah Security Constraint Cocok untuk Microservice?

Jawaban praktis: **tergantung arsitektur auth perusahaan**.

Cocok jika:

```text id="e7t7y7"
container/app server memang jadi security enforcement point
realm/user/role dikelola container
aplikasi legacy
environment on-prem dengan standard container security
```

Kurang cocok jika:

```text id="qomnxp"
auth dilakukan API Gateway
token JWT/mTLS diproses filter/app
authorization berbasis domain/state kompleks
role harus dicek terhadap case/workflow/tenant
```

Untuk regulatory case system, authorization sering bergantung pada:

```text id="ua03cd"
tenant
case ownership
case state
escalation level
workflow task assignee
role + permission
delegation
jurisdiction
business calendar/time window
```

Itu biasanya tidak cukup hanya dengan `web.xml security-constraint`.

---

# 13. Layering Auth yang Direkomendasikan

```text id="rqdso7"
API Gateway:
  coarse auth, TLS, WAF, rate limit, token validation optional

Servlet Filter:
  correlation id
  auth pre-check
  trusted identity extraction
  tenant/user context setup

JAX-RS Filter:
  resource/API-level checks
  annotation-based permission if used

Service Layer:
  business authorization
  state transition authorization
  audit decision
```

Contoh:

```java id="9gn9xs"
public ApprovalResult approveCase(String caseId, Actor actor) {
    CaseEntity caseEntity = caseRepository.findById(caseId);

    authorizationPolicy.assertCanApprove(actor, caseEntity);
    stateMachine.assertTransitionAllowed(caseEntity.status(), "APPROVE");

    caseRepository.markApproved(caseId, actor.userId());
    auditService.recordApproval(caseId, actor);

    return new ApprovalResult(caseId, "APPROVED");
}
```

Rule:

```text id="vfpk9b"
Jangan letakkan business authorization hanya di Servlet Filter.
Filter tidak cukup memahami state/domain.
```

---

# 14. TLS Termination

Ada beberapa model:

```text id="520db4"
Model A:
  Client HTTPS → Gateway HTTPS termination → App HTTP internal

Model B:
  Client HTTPS → Gateway HTTPS → App HTTPS internal

Model C:
  Client HTTPS langsung ke Servlet Container

Model D:
  mTLS antar service
```

Jakarta Servlet specification menyatakan semua servlet container harus mendukung HTTP dan HTTPS sebagai protokol request/response, dengan HTTP/1.1 dan HTTP/2 sebagai versi HTTP yang diperlukan; support HTTP/2 juga membawa requirement terkait ALPN. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/servlet/6.1/jakarta-servlet-spec-6.1?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Dalam Kubernetes/cloud, model yang umum:

```text id="41rx0f"
Public TLS terminates at Load Balancer / Ingress.
Internal traffic may be HTTP or HTTPS depending security policy.
```

Masalahnya: app bisa melihat request sebagai HTTP internal walaupun client memakai HTTPS.

---

# 15. Forwarded Headers

Header umum:

```text id="m9ddxl"
X-Forwarded-For
X-Forwarded-Proto
X-Forwarded-Host
X-Forwarded-Port
Forwarded
```

Tomcat `RemoteIpValve` dapat mengganti apparent client remote IP/hostname berdasarkan header proxy/load balancer seperti `X-Forwarded-For`, dan juga mengganti scheme serta server port berdasarkan header seperti `X-Forwarded-Proto`. ([Apache Tomcat](https://tomcat.apache.org/tomcat-11.0-doc/api/org/apache/catalina/valves/RemoteIpValve.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Tanpa konfigurasi forwarded headers:

| Method | Bisa terlihat sebagai |
|---|---|
| `request.getScheme()` | `http` |
| `request.isSecure()` | `false` |
| `request.getServerName()` | internal service name |
| `request.getServerPort()` | `8080` |
| `request.getRemoteAddr()` | IP ingress/proxy |

Padahal public request:

```text id="h43mly"
https://api.company.com/case-service/...
```

---

# 16. Risiko Forwarded Header Spoofing

Jangan langsung percaya:

```java id="947cu9"
String clientIp = request.getHeader("X-Forwarded-For");
```

Karena client bisa mengirim header palsu jika tidak dibersihkan/di-set ulang oleh gateway.

Approach yang benar:

```text id="f87ta2"
1. Gateway/Ingress harus overwrite forwarded headers.
2. App/container hanya trust forwarded headers dari proxy IP yang dipercaya.
3. Container normalize scheme/host/remoteAddr.
4. Application memakai value yang sudah dinormalisasi.
```

Untuk Tomcat, konfigurasi RemoteIpValve harus mempertimbangkan internal/trusted proxies, bukan sekadar membaca header mentah.

---

# 17. Tomcat RemoteIpValve Contoh

Contoh konseptual di `server.xml`:

```xml id="4fkkl2"
<Valve className="org.apache.catalina.valves.RemoteIpValve"
       internalProxies="10\.\d+\.\d+\.\d+|192\.168\.\d+\.\d+|172\.(1[6-9]|2\d|3[0-1])\.\d+\.\d+"
       remoteIpHeader="x-forwarded-for"
       protocolHeader="x-forwarded-proto"
       protocolHeaderHttpsValue="https"
       portHeader="x-forwarded-port" />
```

Setelah benar:

```java id="8rkxtt"
request.getRemoteAddr(); // client IP normalized
request.getScheme();     // https
request.isSecure();      // true
request.getServerPort(); // 443
```

Sebelum benar:

```java id="ogd2a2"
request.getRemoteAddr(); // ingress IP
request.getScheme();     // http
request.isSecure();      // false
request.getServerPort(); // 8080
```

---

# 18. Redirect dan Callback URL

Bug umum:

```java id="4ev3bp"
String callbackUrl = request.getRequestURL().toString();
```

Di balik proxy, hasilnya bisa:

```text id="sh5k8k"
http://case-service:8080/api/v1/callback
```

Padahal public URL:

```text id="msgatq"
https://api.company.com/case-service/api/v1/callback
```

Untuk URL yang dikirim ke pihak eksternal, payment provider, document signing, OAuth callback, webhook registration, atau email link:

```text id="kgf0v7"
Gunakan explicit public base URL dari config.
Jangan bergantung penuh pada request.getRequestURL().
```

Contoh:

```java id="jccsqc"
public final class PublicUrlFactory {

    private final URI publicApiBaseUri;

    public PublicUrlFactory(String publicApiBaseUrl) {
        this.publicApiBaseUri = URI.create(stripTrailingSlash(publicApiBaseUrl));
    }

    public URI callbackUrl(String callbackId) {
        return publicApiBaseUri.resolve(
                "/case-service/api/v1/callbacks/" + encode(callbackId)
        );
    }

    private static String stripTrailingSlash(String value) {
        while (value.endsWith("/")) {
            value = value.substring(0, value.length() - 1);
        }
        return value;
    }

    private static String encode(String value) {
        return java.net.URLEncoder.encode(
                value,
                java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8
        ).replace("+", "%20");
    }
}
```

Config:

```text id="1zjqhu"
PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL=https://api.company.com/case-service/api
```

---

# 19. Security Headers

Untuk API response, filter sederhana:

```java id="mutdp9"
package com.company.caseapp.web;

import jakarta.servlet.Filter;
import jakarta.servlet.FilterChain;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletException;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletResponse;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;

import java.io.IOException;

public class SecurityHeadersFilter implements Filter {

    @Override
    public void doFilter(
            ServletRequest request,
            ServletResponse response,
            FilterChain chain
    ) throws IOException, ServletException {

        HttpServletResponse httpResponse = (HttpServletResponse) response;

        httpResponse.setHeader("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff");
        httpResponse.setHeader("X-Frame-Options", "DENY");
        httpResponse.setHeader("Referrer-Policy", "no-referrer");
        httpResponse.setHeader("Cache-Control", "no-store");

        chain.doFilter(request, response);
    }
}
```

Untuk browser-facing HTTPS app, pertimbangkan HSTS:

```http id="mxbtv2"
Strict-Transport-Security: max-age=31536000; includeSubDomains
```

Hati-hati: HSTS adalah kebijakan browser yang kuat. Jangan aktifkan `includeSubDomains` tanpa memastikan seluruh subdomain memang HTTPS-ready.

---

# 20. CSRF: Penting Jika Pakai Cookie Auth

Jika browser otomatis mengirim cookie auth/session, maka CSRF perlu dipikirkan.

OWASP mencatat bahwa walaupun `HttpOnly` melindungi cookie dari akses JavaScript, browser tetap mengirim cookie dalam request, sehingga kombinasi XSS/CSRF tetap perlu dipertimbangkan. ([OWASP Cheat Sheet Series](https://cheatsheetseries.owasp.org/cheatsheets/Session_Management_Cheat_Sheet.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Jika API auth memakai:

```text id="e0ecgv"
Authorization: Bearer <token>
```

dan token tidak otomatis dikirim browser, risiko CSRF berbeda.

Jika auth memakai:

```text id="xk28yr"
Cookie: JSESSIONID=...
```

maka browser otomatis mengirim cookie sesuai policy, sehingga perlu strategi:

```text id="pwbzev"
SameSite=Lax/Strict jika memungkinkan
CSRF token untuk state-changing request
Origin/Referer validation
custom header requirement
CORS allowlist ketat
```

---

# 21. Cookie Setting Praktis

## 21.1 Manual cookie

```java id="1dc3l2"
Cookie cookie = new Cookie("SESSION_HINT", value);
cookie.setHttpOnly(true);
cookie.setSecure(true);
cookie.setPath("/case-service");
cookie.setMaxAge(3600);

response.addCookie(cookie);
```

## 21.2 SameSite manual via header

Jika perlu manual:

```java id="yx2h9h"
response.addHeader(
        "Set-Cookie",
        "SESSION_HINT=%s; Path=/case-service; Max-Age=3600; Secure; HttpOnly; SameSite=Lax"
                .formatted(encodeCookieValue(value))
);
```

Helper sederhana:

```java id="sb2yjd"
private static String encodeCookieValue(String value) {
    return java.net.URLEncoder.encode(
            value,
            java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8
    );
}
```

Hati-hati:

```text id="fux8xk"
Manual Set-Cookie harus benar-benar hati-hati:
- jangan ada CRLF injection
- value harus aman
- path/domain tepat
- tidak dobel dengan container session cookie
```

---

# 22. Audit Identity

Untuk regulatory/case management system, audit trail harus defensible.

Minimal audit context per request:

```text id="bvfcgo"
correlationId
requestId
userId / serviceAccountId
tenantId
clientIp normalized
auth mechanism
source system
user agent, jika relevan
operation
resource id
decision
timestamp
```

Jangan hanya simpan:

```text id="w7k2fx"
remoteAddr = 10.0.0.12
```

Kalau itu IP ingress, audit tidak cukup.

Audit filter:

```java id="7bjh8v"
public class AuditContextFilter implements Filter {

    @Override
    public void doFilter(
            ServletRequest request,
            ServletResponse response,
            FilterChain chain
    ) throws IOException, ServletException {

        HttpServletRequest r = (HttpServletRequest) request;

        String clientIp = r.getRemoteAddr(); // assumed normalized by trusted proxy/container
        String correlationId = String.valueOf(r.getAttribute("correlationId"));
        String userId = r.getHeader("X-User-Id");
        String tenantId = r.getHeader("X-Tenant-Id");

        AuditContext context = new AuditContext(
                correlationId,
                userId,
                tenantId,
                clientIp,
                r.getHeader("User-Agent")
        );

        try {
            AuditContextHolder.set(context);
            chain.doFilter(request, response);
        } finally {
            AuditContextHolder.clear();
        }
    }
}
```

Critical note:

```text id="f8eue7"
X-User-Id dan X-Tenant-Id hanya boleh dipercaya jika gateway sudah mengautentikasi
dan app hanya menerima traffic dari gateway terpercaya.
```

---

# 23. Container-managed Auth vs Application Auth

## 23.1 Container-managed auth

Contoh:

```xml id="8i3pey"
<login-config>
    <auth-method>BASIC</auth-method>
    <realm-name>CaseServiceRealm</realm-name>
</login-config>
```

Resource bisa membaca:

```java id="qj5e3z"
Principal principal = request.getUserPrincipal();
boolean admin = request.isUserInRole("admin");
```

Cocok untuk:

```text id="yzka6t"
simple internal tools
legacy app
container-standard environment
basic/form/client-cert auth
```

## 23.2 Application/gateway auth

Filter/JAX-RS/service membaca token/identity:

```java id="j3zpnt"
String authorization = request.getHeader("Authorization");
```

Cocok untuk:

```text id="2m7gts"
JWT/OIDC
API Gateway auth
service-to-service token
tenant-aware authorization
domain-specific permission
```

Practical recommendation:

```text id="puk6xq"
Jangan campur dua model auth tanpa diagram jelas.
Kalau gateway sudah enforce auth, app tetap perlu validate trust boundary dan business authorization.
```

---

# 24. Kubernetes / Cloud Practical Issues

## 24.1 Sticky session

Jika app memakai `HttpSession`, di Kubernetes perlu salah satu:

```text id="9n9pw8"
sticky session di ingress/load balancer
session replication
external session store
atau redesign stateless
```

Tanpa itu:

```text id="701i79"
Request 1 → pod A → session created
Request 2 → pod B → session missing
```

## 24.2 Pod restart

Jika session local memory:

```text id="kdbedw"
pod restart → session hilang
```

Untuk API critical, ini sering tidak acceptable.

## 24.3 Readiness dan auth

Health/readiness endpoint biasanya tidak boleh tergantung user session/auth browser.

Pattern:

```text id="fxwepk"
GET /api/v1/health
GET /api/v1/readiness
```

Filter auth harus skip endpoint tersebut, atau endpoint health berada di path yang tidak masuk auth filter.

---

# 25. Practical `web.xml` Snippets

## 25.1 Session timeout

```xml id="n9317x"
<session-config>
    <session-timeout>30</session-timeout>
    <cookie-config>
        <http-only>true</http-only>
        <secure>true</secure>
        <name>CASESESSIONID</name>
        <path>/case-service</path>
    </cookie-config>
</session-config>
```

Catatan:

```text id="lq900l"
secure=true butuh HTTPS dari perspektif container/proxy.
Jika TLS terminate di proxy dan app melihat http,
pastikan forwarded headers/secure handling benar.
```

## 25.2 Security constraint transport guarantee

```xml id="3w8xra"
<security-constraint>
    <web-resource-collection>
        <web-resource-name>All API</web-resource-name>
        <url-pattern>/api/*</url-pattern>
    </web-resource-collection>
    <user-data-constraint>
        <transport-guarantee>CONFIDENTIAL</transport-guarantee>
    </user-data-constraint>
</security-constraint>
```

Hati-hati di balik TLS-terminating proxy: jika container melihat request sebagai HTTP, constraint bisa menyebabkan redirect atau blocking yang salah kecuali proxy/connector dikonfigurasi benar.

---

# 26. Practical Filters

## 26.1 Reject accidental session creation for stateless API

Untuk non-prod atau hardening mode:

```java id="fe0p16"
public class StatelessSessionGuardFilter implements Filter {

    @Override
    public void doFilter(
            ServletRequest request,
            ServletResponse response,
            FilterChain chain
    ) throws IOException, ServletException {

        HttpServletRequest httpRequest = (HttpServletRequest) request;
        HttpServletResponse httpResponse = (HttpServletResponse) response;

        chain.doFilter(request, response);

        if (httpRequest.getSession(false) != null) {
            httpResponse.setHeader("X-Session-Warning", "HttpSession-created");
        }
    }
}
```

Untuk enforcement keras, lebih sulit karena session bisa dibuat downstream setelah response hampir selesai. Biasanya lebih baik audit + code review daripada membatalkan response.

## 26.2 Forwarded header diagnostic filter

```java id="i22n9y"
public class ForwardedHeaderDebugFilter implements Filter {

    @Override
    public void doFilter(
            ServletRequest request,
            ServletResponse response,
            FilterChain chain
    ) throws IOException, ServletException {

        HttpServletRequest r = (HttpServletRequest) request;

        System.out.printf("""
                FORWARDED_DEBUG
                  scheme=%s
                  secure=%s
                  serverName=%s
                  serverPort=%d
                  remoteAddr=%s
                  host=%s
                  xForwardedFor=%s
                  xForwardedProto=%s
                  xForwardedHost=%s
                  xForwardedPort=%s
                %n""",
                r.getScheme(),
                r.isSecure(),
                r.getServerName(),
                r.getServerPort(),
                r.getRemoteAddr(),
                r.getHeader("Host"),
                r.getHeader("X-Forwarded-For"),
                r.getHeader("X-Forwarded-Proto"),
                r.getHeader("X-Forwarded-Host"),
                r.getHeader("X-Forwarded-Port")
        );

        chain.doFilter(request, response);
    }
}
```

Gunakan sementara di staging/prod incident, jangan permanen dengan full header log.

---

# 27. Testing

## 27.1 Pastikan health tidak membuat session

```java id="vxq4dj"
@Test
void healthShouldNotCreateSessionCookie() throws Exception {
    HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
            .uri(URI.create("https://api.company.com/case-service/api/v1/health"))
            .GET()
            .build();

    HttpResponse<String> response = HttpClient.newHttpClient()
            .send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());

    boolean hasSessionCookie = response.headers()
            .allValues("Set-Cookie")
            .stream()
            .anyMatch(value -> value.contains("JSESSIONID"));

    assertFalse(hasSessionCookie);
}
```

## 27.2 Cookie security attributes

```java id="xgqyzm"
@Test
void sessionCookieShouldHaveSecureAndHttpOnly() throws Exception {
    HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
            .uri(URI.create("https://api.company.com/case-service/login"))
            .POST(HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofString("{}"))
            .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
            .build();

    HttpResponse<String> response = HttpClient.newHttpClient()
            .send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());

    String setCookie = response.headers()
            .allValues("Set-Cookie")
            .stream()
            .filter(value -> value.contains("JSESSIONID") || value.contains("CASESESSIONID"))
            .findFirst()
            .orElseThrow();

    assertTrue(setCookie.contains("Secure"));
    assertTrue(setCookie.contains("HttpOnly"));
    assertTrue(setCookie.contains("SameSite"));
}
```

## 27.3 Forwarded proto

```java id="d83jxo"
@Test
void appShouldSeeHttpsBehindProxy() throws Exception {
    HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
            .uri(URI.create("http://localhost:8080/case-service/api/v1/internal/debug/url"))
            .header("X-Forwarded-Proto", "https")
            .header("X-Forwarded-Host", "api.company.com")
            .header("X-Forwarded-Port", "443")
            .GET()
            .build();

    HttpResponse<String> response = HttpClient.newHttpClient()
            .send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());

    assertEquals(200, response.statusCode());
    assertTrue(response.body().contains("\"scheme\":\"https\""));
}
```

Catatan: test ini valid hanya jika container/proxy normalization di test environment memang disiapkan.

---

# 28. Production Debug Playbook

## Case A - Muncul `Set-Cookie: JSESSIONID` padahal API stateless

Cek:

```text id="p2jsna"
[ ] Ada request.getSession()?
[ ] Ada framework/security library membuat session?
[ ] Ada JSP/default servlet behavior?
[ ] Ada login/form auth container?
[ ] Ada code menyimpan user/tenant di session?
```

Fix:

```text id="mud28x"
Ganti getSession() menjadi getSession(false) jika hanya cek.
Pindahkan context ke request/token.
Disable/session policy sesuai framework/container jika tersedia.
```

---

## Case B - Redirect menjadi HTTP, bukan HTTPS

Cek:

```text id="w9a1co"
[ ] TLS terminate di gateway?
[ ] X-Forwarded-Proto dikirim?
[ ] Container memproses forwarded proto?
[ ] request.isSecure() true?
[ ] request.getScheme() https?
[ ] Redirect dibangun dari request.getRequestURL?
```

Fix:

```text id="zh9jst"
Konfigurasi RemoteIpValve/equivalent.
Atau gunakan PUBLIC_BASE_URL untuk redirect/callback.
```

---

## Case C - Audit IP semua sama

Cek:

```text id="7migzl"
[ ] remoteAddr menunjukkan ingress IP?
[ ] X-Forwarded-For ada?
[ ] Gateway overwrite X-Forwarded-For?
[ ] Trusted proxy config benar?
[ ] App langsung percaya header mentah?
```

Fix:

```text id="24ja3i"
Normalize di gateway/container.
Audit client IP setelah trust boundary.
Simpan proxy chain jika perlu dan aman.
```

---

## Case D - Session hilang random di Kubernetes

Cek:

```text id="f8sgwd"
[ ] App memakai HttpSession?
[ ] LB/Ingress sticky session aktif?
[ ] Session replication ada?
[ ] Pod restart?
[ ] Multiple replicas?
```

Fix options:

```text id="6ij10o"
Prefer stateless.
Atau sticky session.
Atau external session store.
Atau redesign auth/session boundary.
```

---

## Case E - CSRF risk pada browser API

Cek:

```text id="ruwgk8"
[ ] Auth pakai cookie?
[ ] State-changing method POST/PUT/PATCH/DELETE?
[ ] SameSite cookie apa?
[ ] CSRF token ada?
[ ] Origin/Referer validation ada?
[ ] CORS allow credentials?
```

---

# 29. Code Review Checklist

## 29.1 Session

```text id="cpkc11"
[ ] Tidak ada getSession() tanpa alasan.
[ ] Stateless API tidak mengeluarkan JSESSIONID.
[ ] Session timeout jelas jika session dipakai.
[ ] Logout invalidate session.
[ ] Session ID rotate setelah login.
[ ] Session data tidak menyimpan object besar/sensitif berlebihan.
```

## 29.2 Cookie

```text id="6k3n8i"
[ ] Auth/session cookie Secure.
[ ] Auth/session cookie HttpOnly.
[ ] SameSite policy jelas.
[ ] Path sesempit mungkin.
[ ] Domain tidak terlalu luas.
[ ] Max-Age/Expires sesuai risiko.
[ ] Tidak ada sensitive data di cookie value.
```

## 29.3 Forwarded headers

```text id="eed0xp"
[ ] Gateway overwrite X-Forwarded-*.
[ ] Container trust hanya proxy yang benar.
[ ] request.getScheme/isSecure benar di balik proxy.
[ ] remoteAddr normalized untuk audit.
[ ] App tidak langsung percaya header mentah dari internet.
```

## 29.4 TLS / redirect / callback

```text id="xlm69v"
[ ] Public base URL explicit untuk callback eksternal.
[ ] HSTS dipakai hanya jika seluruh domain siap.
[ ] transport-guarantee tidak konflik dengan TLS termination.
[ ] Secure cookie tetap bekerja di balik proxy.
```

## 29.5 Authorization

```text id="qjsj1y"
[ ] Security constraint tidak menggantikan business authorization.
[ ] Gateway auth, filter auth, JAX-RS auth, service auth punya boundary jelas.
[ ] Tenant/user identity berasal dari trusted source.
[ ] Audit trail menyimpan identity decision yang cukup.
```

---

# 30. Anti-Pattern

| Anti-pattern | Dampak | Alternatif |
|---|---|---|
| `request.getSession()` di API stateless | Membuat session tidak sengaja | `getSession(false)` atau jangan pakai session |
| Menyimpan tenant/user di session untuk microservice | Stateful scaling issue | Context per request/token |
| Cookie auth tanpa Secure/HttpOnly/SameSite | Session leak/CSRF risk | Set attributes jelas |
| Percaya `X-Forwarded-For` mentah | IP spoofing | Trusted proxy normalization |
| Membuat callback dari `request.getRequestURL()` | URL internal/scheme salah | Public base URL config |
| Business auth hanya di filter | Tidak domain-aware | Service-layer policy |
| `security-constraint` dianggap cukup untuk workflow permission | Authorization terlalu kasar | Domain authorization |
| HSTS includeSubDomains tanpa readiness | Bisa memutus subdomain non-HTTPS | Rollout terkontrol |
| Sticky session diam-diam | Scaling/HA buruk | Stateless atau documented session strategy |
| Health endpoint butuh session/auth | Probe gagal | Health/readiness bebas session |

---

# 31. Mini Exercise untuk Repo Anda

Jalankan pencarian:

```bash id="yy173k"
grep -R "getSession" src/main/java
grep -R "JSESSIONID" src/main/java src/main/resources src/main/webapp
grep -R "Set-Cookie" src/main/java
grep -R "security-constraint" src/main/webapp src/main/resources
grep -R "login-config" src/main/webapp src/main/resources
grep -R "X-Forwarded" src/main/java src/main/resources
grep -R "getRemoteAddr" src/main/java
grep -R "getRequestURL" src/main/java
grep -R "isSecure" src/main/java
```

Isi matrix:

```text id="q4weaj"
Session:
  used:
  where:
  why:
  timeout:
  replication/sticky:
  logout invalidation:
  session id rotation:

Cookie:
  names:
  Secure:
  HttpOnly:
  SameSite:
  Path:
  Domain:
  Max-Age:

TLS/proxy:
  TLS termination layer:
  internal protocol:
  forwarded headers sent:
  container normalization:
  trusted proxies:

Audit:
  client IP source:
  user identity source:
  tenant identity source:
  correlation id:
  source system:

Auth:
  gateway auth:
  servlet filter auth:
  container security-constraint:
  JAX-RS filter:
  service-layer policy:
```

Red flag terbesar:

```text id="13ydl5"
[ ] Stateless API mengeluarkan JSESSIONID.
[ ] Auth/session cookie tanpa Secure/HttpOnly.
[ ] Client IP dari X-Forwarded-For mentah.
[ ] Callback URL dibuat dari internal request URL.
[ ] Tidak tahu TLS terminate di mana.
[ ] Business authorization hanya di filter/security-constraint.
[ ] Session dipakai di multi-replica tanpa sticky/replication.
```

---

# Ringkasan Seri 9

Servlet Container menyediakan session, cookie, security constraint, dan integrasi HTTP/TLS/proxy, tetapi dalam microservice modern semua fitur ini harus dipakai dengan sadar.

Invariants penting:

```text id="0h3mxk"
Stateless API jangan membuat HttpSession tanpa alasan.
JSESSIONID di endpoint stateless adalah red flag.
Cookie auth/session wajib Secure + HttpOnly + SameSite policy.
Forwarded headers hanya boleh dipercaya dari proxy/gateway trusted.
request.getScheme(), isSecure(), remoteAddr harus benar di balik TLS termination.
Callback/public URL sebaiknya dari explicit config.
Container security constraint tidak cukup untuk business authorization.
Audit trail harus punya client IP dan identity yang defensible.
```

Model yang perlu diingat:

```text id="jfhvnl"
Gateway handles edge security.
Servlet Container handles HTTP/session/cookie/proxy normalization.
Filter/JAX-RS handles request identity boundary.
Service layer handles domain authorization.
```

---

Saya lanjut ke **Seri 10/12**. Fokusnya: cara aplikasi benar-benar dipaketkan dan dijalankan—WAR external container vs embedded app—serta bagaimana Maven scope dan classloader bisa menyebabkan bug yang tampak “misterius”.

# Seri 10/12 - Deployment Model: WAR, Embedded Container, Maven Scope, Classloader

## Tujuan seri ini

Seri ini membahas bagaimana aplikasi **Java Jersey/JAX-RS berbasis Servlet Container** benar-benar dipaketkan, dijalankan, dan di-load oleh runtime.

Fokus praktis:

```text id="dj7dcp"
WAR deployment ke external Servlet Container
embedded container / executable application
struktur WEB-INF/classes dan WEB-INF/lib
Maven dependency scope
provided vs compile vs runtime
classloader hierarchy
dependency conflict
jakarta.* vs javax.* mismatch
Docker image layout
debugging NoClassDefFoundError / ClassCastException / LinkageError
```

Ini penting karena banyak error production terlihat seperti error application, padahal akar masalahnya adalah:

```text id="d6jg6i"
dependency salah scope
JAR tidak masuk WAR
API JAR dobel antara container dan aplikasi
container menyediakan versi library berbeda
javax.* dan jakarta.* tercampur
class yang diedit bukan class yang ter-deploy
WAR context path berubah karena nama artifact
Docker image membawa artifact lama
```

Dalam Servlet specification, web application class loader harus memuat class dari `WEB-INF/classes` lalu JAR dari `WEB-INF/lib`; resource di `WEB-INF/` juga tidak boleh langsung diakses client dan harus menghasilkan 404, kecuali kasus resource statis yang memang dipaketkan lewat JAR sesuai aturan spec. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/servlet/6.1/jakarta-servlet-spec-6.1?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Mental Model Deployment

Ada dua model besar:

```text id="rfdahm"
Model A - External Servlet Container

  build:
    Maven → case-service.war

  deploy:
    copy WAR ke Tomcat/Jetty/app server

  runtime:
    container start
    container deploy WAR
    container membuat webapp classloader
    Jersey ServletContainer aktif

Model B - Embedded Container / Executable App

  build:
    Maven → executable jar / fat jar / custom distribution

  run:
    java -jar case-service.jar

  runtime:
    main() start embedded server
    app mendaftarkan Jersey/servlet/filter programmatically
```

Untuk onboarding, pertanyaan pertama:

```text id="sadxpo"
Service ini dijalankan sebagai WAR di container eksternal,
atau executable app dengan embedded container?
```

Karena jawaban ini menentukan:

```text id="3jlr3q"
dependency scope
Dockerfile
entrypoint
classloader behavior
context path
servlet registration
where config lives
how to debug deployment
```

---

# 2. Diagram Deployment Model

```text id="g4np8w"
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Model A: WAR + External Servlet Container                     │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

Maven build
  ↓
target/case-service.war
  ↓
WAR structure:
  WEB-INF/classes/
  WEB-INF/lib/
  WEB-INF/web.xml
  ↓
External Container:
  Tomcat / Jetty / application server
  ↓
Container classloader
  ↓
Webapp classloader
  ↓
Jersey ServletContainer
  ↓
JAX-RS resources

┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Model B: Embedded Container / Executable App                  │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

Maven build
  ↓
target/case-service.jar
  ↓
java -jar case-service.jar
  ↓
main()
  ↓
start embedded server
  ↓
register servlet/filter/Jersey programmatically
  ↓
Jersey resources
```

# 3. Decomposition Map

| No | Area | Fungsi praktis | Yang harus dikuasai | Failure mode umum |
|---:|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | WAR deployment | Deploy artifact ke external container | Struktur WAR, `WEB-INF`, `web.xml`, context path | JAR hilang, context path salah |
| 2 | Embedded deployment | App menjalankan server sendiri | `main()`, embedded Tomcat/Jetty/Grizzly/Undertow-like setup | Local-prod beda runtime |
| 3 | `WEB-INF/classes` | Output compiled class aplikasi | Resource/class aplikasi berada di sini | Class lama/beda module |
| 4 | `WEB-INF/lib` | Dependency JAR webapp | JAR library aplikasi | Duplicate API/container jar |
| 5 | Maven scope | Menentukan dependency masuk classpath/artifact | `compile`, `provided`, `runtime`, `test` | Dependency tidak ikut WAR atau dobel |
| 6 | Provided dependency | Ada saat compile, disediakan runtime/container | `jakarta.servlet-api` untuk external container | NoClassDef kalau ternyata runtime tidak menyediakan |
| 7 | Compile dependency | Dibutuhkan compile dan packaged | Jersey servlet, app libs, MyBatis | Conflict transitive |
| 8 | Runtime dependency | Dibutuhkan saat runtime, tidak compile langsung | JDBC driver, logging impl | ClassNotFound saat runtime |
| 9 | Classloader hierarchy | Isolasi container vs webapp | Parent/common/webapp classloader | ClassCastException / LinkageError |
| 10 | Jakarta vs javax | Namespace modern vs lama | `jakarta.*` untuk Servlet 5+ / EE 9+ | Type mismatch total |
| 11 | Container-provided API | Servlet API disediakan container | Scope `provided` pada WAR external | API jar dobel |
| 12 | Fat JAR risk | Semua dependency dibundle | shading/relocation, service loader | Duplicate resource/provider |
| 13 | Docker image layout | Artifact masuk image | COPY target artifact, entrypoint, env | Image membawa artifact stale |
| 14 | Context path | Nama WAR/config menentukan base path | ROOT.war, case-service.war, server config | Endpoint 404 |
| 15 | Dependency debugging | Cari dependency final | `dependency:tree`, inspect WAR/JAR | Salah diagnosis application bug |
| 16 | Version alignment | Container, Servlet, Jersey, Jakarta REST | Match major version | Runtime incompatible |

---

# 4. WAR Deployment: External Servlet Container

## 4.1 Struktur WAR

Contoh:

```text id="pu0tp2"
case-service.war
├── META-INF/
├── WEB-INF/
│   ├── web.xml
│   ├── classes/
│   │   └── com/company/caseapp/...
│   └── lib/
│       ├── jersey-container-servlet-core-...
│       ├── jersey-hk2-...
│       ├── mybatis-...
│       ├── postgresql-...
│       └── ...
└── static resources, if any
```

Servlet specification mensyaratkan classloader web application memuat class dari `WEB-INF/classes` terlebih dahulu, lalu JAR di `WEB-INF/lib`. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/servlet/6.1/jakarta-servlet-spec-6.1?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Tomcat juga menjelaskan bahwa untuk setiap web application, class dan resource dari `/WEB-INF/classes` serta JAR di `/WEB-INF/lib` terlihat untuk webapp tersebut, tetapi tidak untuk webapp lain. ([Apache Tomcat](https://tomcat.apache.org/tomcat-9.0-doc/class-loader-howto.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Practical implication:

```text id="ygaym7"
Class aplikasi Anda biasanya ada di WEB-INF/classes.
Dependency aplikasi ada di WEB-INF/lib.
Container-level library tidak otomatis sama dengan webapp library.
```

## 4.2 `web.xml`

Untuk deployment servlet-style, Jersey dapat dideklarasikan sebagai container servlet di `web.xml`; dokumentasi Jersey deployment menjelaskan model ini untuk servlet-based deployment. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest3x/deployment.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

```xml id="zyoo2h"
<servlet>
    <servlet-name>jersey</servlet-name>
    <servlet-class>org.glassfish.jersey.servlet.ServletContainer</servlet-class>
    <init-param>
        <param-name>jakarta.ws.rs.Application</param-name>
        <param-value>com.company.caseapp.config.CaseApplication</param-value>
    </init-param>
    <load-on-startup>1</load-on-startup>
</servlet>

<servlet-mapping>
    <servlet-name>jersey</servlet-name>
    <url-pattern>/api/*</url-pattern>
</servlet-mapping>
```

## 4.3 Context path dari WAR name

Umumnya:

```text id="c29u55"
case-service.war → /case-service
ROOT.war         → /
```

Tetapi context path bisa dioverride lewat container config, deployment descriptor, atau platform deployment. Jadi jangan hanya menebak dari nama WAR.

Checklist:

```text id="wvbm95"
[ ] Artifact final benar-benar .war?
[ ] Nama WAR menentukan context path atau dioverride?
[ ] WAR masuk folder deploy yang benar?
[ ] web.xml ada di WEB-INF/web.xml?
[ ] Jersey servlet mapping benar?
[ ] WEB-INF/lib berisi dependency yang dibutuhkan?
```

---

# 5. Embedded Container / Executable App

Embedded model berarti aplikasi sendiri yang memulai server.

Contoh konseptual:

```java id="t1kxkd"
public class Main {

    public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {
        // start embedded servlet container
        // register Jersey ServletContainer
        // register filters/listeners
        // bind to port
    }
}
```

Model ini umum pada framework modern, custom microservice launcher, atau service yang tidak ingin bergantung pada external Tomcat/Jetty installation.

Kelebihan:

```text id="7i9hqg"
runtime lebih self-contained
Docker image lebih sederhana
local-prod parity bisa lebih baik
container version dikontrol aplikasi
tidak perlu deploy WAR ke server bersama
```

Kekurangan:

```text id="ke3hde"
artifact lebih besar
semua dependency runtime harus dibundle
perlu desain startup/shutdown sendiri
classloader isolation berbeda dari WAR external
operational knobs pindah ke app config
```

Rule praktis:

```text id="h478zd"
External WAR:
  container menyediakan Servlet runtime

Embedded app:
  aplikasi membawa container runtime sendiri
```

Implikasi Maven scope-nya berbeda.

---

# 6. Maven Scope: Core Practical

Apache Maven menjelaskan bahwa dependency scope digunakan untuk membatasi transitivity dependency dan menentukan kapan dependency masuk classpath; scope umum meliputi `compile`, `provided`, `runtime`, `test`, `system`, dan `import`. ([Apache Maven](https://maven.apache.org/guides/introduction/introduction-to-dependency-mechanism.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 6.1 Scope yang paling penting

| Scope | Compile classpath | Runtime packaged | Contoh praktis |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| `compile` | Ya | Ya | app libs, Jersey libs untuk WAR jika tidak disediakan container |
| `provided` | Ya | Tidak | `jakarta.servlet-api` pada WAR external |
| `runtime` | Tidak diperlukan saat compile langsung | Ya | JDBC driver, logging runtime impl |
| `test` | Test only | Tidak | JUnit, Mockito, testcontainers |
| `import` | BOM dependency management | Tidak langsung | Jakarta/Jersey BOM |

## 6.2 `provided`

Untuk WAR di external Servlet Container:

```xml id="3be7ia"
<dependency>
    <groupId>jakarta.servlet</groupId>
    <artifactId>jakarta.servlet-api</artifactId>
    <version>${jakarta.servlet.version}</version>
    <scope>provided</scope>
</dependency>
```

Maknanya:

```text id="sjzygg"
Saat compile:
  class tersedia

Saat package WAR:
  jar tidak dimasukkan ke WEB-INF/lib

Saat runtime:
  container harus menyediakan API tersebut
```

Kalau runtime ternyata tidak menyediakan versi kompatibel:

```text id="iqa7lj"
NoClassDefFoundError
NoSuchMethodError
ClassNotFoundException
runtime incompatibility
```

## 6.3 `compile`

Untuk library yang harus ikut aplikasi:

```xml id="26bcer"
<dependency>
    <groupId>org.glassfish.jersey.containers</groupId>
    <artifactId>jersey-container-servlet-core</artifactId>
    <version>${jersey.version}</version>
</dependency>
```

Jika tidak disediakan container/app server, dependency ini harus masuk `WEB-INF/lib`.

## 6.4 `runtime`

Contoh JDBC driver:

```xml id="lwfybw"
<dependency>
    <groupId>org.postgresql</groupId>
    <artifactId>postgresql</artifactId>
    <version>${postgresql.version}</version>
    <scope>runtime</scope>
</dependency>
```

Makna:

```text id="sqo8q6"
Code Anda mungkin hanya compile terhadap JDBC API,
tapi saat runtime butuh PostgreSQL driver.
```

---

# 7. Dependency Scope: WAR External vs Embedded

## 7.1 External WAR

```text id="q51dsz"
jakarta.servlet-api:
  provided

container runtime:
  not packaged in WAR

Jersey:
  packaged unless app server already provides matching Jersey

PostgreSQL driver:
  packaged or container datasource-level, tergantung desain

MyBatis:
  packaged

Application code:
  WEB-INF/classes
```

## 7.2 Embedded JAR

```text id="ksazbx"
jakarta.servlet-api:
  usually compile/runtime via embedded container dependencies

embedded Tomcat/Jetty/Grizzly:
  packaged

Jersey:
  packaged

PostgreSQL driver:
  packaged

MyBatis:
  packaged

Application code:
  inside executable jar / classes
```

Practical rule:

```text id="l45nsh"
Dependency yang provided di WAR external bisa menjadi compile/runtime dependency di embedded model.
```

Itulah kenapa pindah dari WAR ke embedded app bukan sekadar ganti packaging.

---

# 8. Jakarta vs javax Mismatch

Ini salah satu sumber error paling mahal.

## 8.1 Gejala

```text id="ozhmbz"
ClassNotFoundException: javax.servlet.Filter
ClassNotFoundException: jakarta.servlet.Filter
NoSuchMethodError
ClassCastException
resource tidak register
filter tidak dipanggil
Jersey startup gagal
```

## 8.2 Penyebab

```text id="lsswud"
Container berbasis jakarta.*, app masih javax.*
Container lama javax.*, app sudah jakarta.*
Jersey 2.x bercampur Jersey 3.x/4.x
jakarta.ws.rs-api dan javax.ws.rs-api sama-sama ada
servlet-api dobel di WEB-INF/lib dan container lib
```

Jakarta EE 9+ memindahkan namespace Java EE lama dari `javax.*` ke `jakarta.*`; untuk konteks Anda yang memakai modern Java/Jakarta stack, konsistensi namespace harus dicek ketat.

Search:

```bash id="u34snt"
grep -R "javax.servlet" src/main/java
grep -R "javax.ws.rs" src/main/java
grep -R "jakarta.servlet" src/main/java
grep -R "jakarta.ws.rs" src/main/java
mvn dependency:tree | grep -E "javax.servlet|javax.ws.rs|jakarta.servlet|jakarta.ws.rs|jersey"
```

Red flag:

```text id="pnh5yn"
javax.servlet-api
javax.ws.rs-api
jersey 2.x
jersey 3.x
jersey 4.x
```

dalam dependency tree yang sama tanpa alasan jelas.

---

# 9. Classloader Hierarchy

Tomcat memasang beberapa classloader agar container dan webapp punya visibility berbeda; dokumentasi Tomcat menjelaskan bahwa classloader ini digunakan untuk menyediakan fungsi sesuai Servlet Specification dan memisahkan bagian container dari web application. ([Apache Tomcat](https://tomcat.apache.org/tomcat-11.0-doc/class-loader-howto.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Simplified model:

```text id="ynmiwr"
Bootstrap classloader
  ↓
Java platform classes
  ↓
Tomcat common classloader
  ↓
Webapp classloader for app A
  ↓
Webapp classloader for app B
```

Implikasi:

```text id="eymk01"
Class di webapp A tidak terlihat oleh webapp B.
Class di container common lib bisa terlihat ke webapp.
Class yang sama dari classloader berbeda bisa dianggap tipe berbeda.
```

## 9.1 ClassCastException misterius

Contoh gejala:

```text id="a6rs8q"
java.lang.ClassCastException:
  com.company.Foo cannot be cast to com.company.Foo
```

Ini bisa terjadi jika:

```text id="nfm3vl"
class dengan nama sama dimuat oleh dua classloader berbeda.
```

## 9.2 NoSuchMethodError

Gejala:

```text id="47g1g2"
java.lang.NoSuchMethodError:
  SomeLibrary.method(...)
```

Penyebab umum:

```text id="vz9s8h"
Compile memakai versi library A.
Runtime memuat versi library B.
```

Ini sering terjadi jika library ada di container lib dan juga di `WEB-INF/lib`, atau dependency transitive membawa versi lama.

---

# 10. Container Lib vs WEB-INF/lib

## 10.1 Jangan sembarang taruh JAR di container lib

Menaruh JAR di `$TOMCAT_HOME/lib` atau common lib membuat library itu visible untuk banyak webapp. Tomcat classloader docs menunjukkan ada common/shared model untuk class visibility container/webapp, dan webapp classloader punya area sendiri untuk `WEB-INF/classes` dan `WEB-INF/lib`. ([Apache Tomcat](https://tomcat.apache.org/tomcat-11.0-doc/class-loader-howto.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

Cocok untuk:

```text id="mndv35"
JDBC driver jika datasource dikelola container
container extension
shared library yang memang distandarkan platform
```

Tidak cocok untuk:

```text id="lv8p1z"
business library service tertentu
Jersey version app-specific
logging config app-specific
library yang sering beda versi antar service
```

## 10.2 Rule

```text id="hc0kel"
Jika library hanya untuk satu aplikasi:
  letakkan di WEB-INF/lib.

Jika library memang bagian platform/container:
  letakkan di container lib, tapi versioning harus dikontrol platform.
```

---

# 11. Inspect Artifact

## 11.1 Inspect WAR

```bash id="arm5yq"
jar tf target/case-service.war | head -100
jar tf target/case-service.war | grep WEB-INF/lib
jar tf target/case-service.war | grep WEB-INF/classes
jar tf target/case-service.war | grep web.xml
```

Cari Jersey:

```bash id="fpxw5o"
jar tf target/case-service.war | grep -i jersey
```

Cari API mismatch:

```bash id="r3d7ik"
jar tf target/case-service.war | grep -E "javax|jakarta|servlet|ws.rs"
```

Yang perlu dicurigai:

```text id="4i77c1"
WEB-INF/lib/javax.servlet-api-*.jar
WEB-INF/lib/jakarta.servlet-api-*.jar
WEB-INF/lib/javax.ws.rs-api-*.jar
WEB-INF/lib/jakarta.ws.rs-api-*.jar
multiple jersey major versions
```

Untuk external container, `jakarta.servlet-api` biasanya tidak perlu ikut WAR karena container menyediakan Servlet API. Jika ikut WAR, bisa terjadi conflict tergantung container behavior.

## 11.2 Inspect executable JAR

```bash id="gfq3py"
jar tf target/case-service.jar | head -100
jar tf target/case-service.jar | grep -i tomcat
jar tf target/case-service.jar | grep -i jersey
```

Kalau executable/fat JAR, dependency harus ada di dalam artifact atau disediakan via lib directory/image.

# 12. Maven Dependency Debugging

## 12.1 Dependency tree

```bash id="omsj4f"
mvn dependency:tree
```

Filter:

```bash id="08i0ic"
mvn dependency:tree | grep -E "jersey|servlet|ws.rs|jakarta|javax|jackson|mybatis|postgresql"
```

Cari konflik:

```bash id="ch7tpx"
mvn dependency:tree -Dverbose
```

## 12.2 Effective POM

```bash id="u8mh2f"
mvn help:effective-pom > effective-pom.xml
```

Gunakan untuk melihat:

```text id="f97ie6"
dependencyManagement final
plugin version final
profile aktif
packaging final
war plugin config
shade plugin config
```

## 12.3 Dependency analyze

```bash id="dzdaxd"
mvn dependency:analyze
```

Useful untuk:

```text id="0n660e"
used undeclared dependencies
declared unused dependencies
```

Tetapi jangan blindly remove dependency hanya karena tool bilang unused; reflection, service loader, Jersey provider scanning, JDBC driver, dan runtime-only dependency bisa membuat analisis statis tidak lengkap.

# 13. Maven WAR Plugin dan Packaging

Contoh `pom.xml` untuk WAR:

```xml id="wzv65x"
<project>
    <packaging>war</packaging>

    <dependencies>
        <dependency>
            <groupId>jakarta.servlet</groupId>
            <artifactId>jakarta.servlet-api</artifactId>
            <version>${jakarta.servlet.version}</version>
            <scope>provided</scope>
        </dependency>

        <dependency>
            <groupId>org.glassfish.jersey.containers</groupId>
            <artifactId>jersey-container-servlet-core</artifactId>
            <version>${jersey.version}</version>
        </dependency>

        <dependency>
            <groupId>org.glassfish.jersey.inject</groupId>
            <artifactId>jersey-hk2</artifactId>
            <version>${jersey.version}</version>
        </dependency>

        <dependency>
            <groupId>org.glassfish.jersey.media</groupId>
            <artifactId>jersey-media-json-jackson</artifactId>
            <version>${jersey.version}</version>
        </dependency>

        <dependency>
            <groupId>org.mybatis</groupId>
            <artifactId>mybatis</artifactId>
            <version>${mybatis.version}</version>
        </dependency>

        <dependency>
            <groupId>org.postgresql</groupId>
            <artifactId>postgresql</artifactId>
            <version>${postgresql.version}</version>
            <scope>runtime</scope>
        </dependency>
    </dependencies>
</project>
```

Build:

```bash id="tqghzv"
mvn clean package
```

Output:

```text id="x0fr0a"
target/case-service.war
```

# 14. BOM / Dependency Management

Gunakan BOM untuk menjaga versi konsisten.

Contoh konseptual:

```xml id="8v78mg"
<dependencyManagement>
    <dependencies>
        <dependency>
            <groupId>org.glassfish.jersey</groupId>
            <artifactId>jersey-bom</artifactId>
            <version>${jersey.version}</version>
            <type>pom</type>
            <scope>import</scope>
        </dependency>
    </dependencies>
</dependencyManagement>
```

Lalu dependency tanpa version individual:

```xml id="v0b5nc"
<dependency>
    <groupId>org.glassfish.jersey.containers</groupId>
    <artifactId>jersey-container-servlet-core</artifactId>
</dependency>
```

Keuntungan:

```text id="k4utuw"
mengurangi mismatch antar jersey modules
dependency version lebih mudah diaudit
upgrade lebih terpusat
```

---

# 15. Exclusions

Jika dependency transitive membawa library yang salah:

```xml id="s8s6p1"
<dependency>
    <groupId>com.company</groupId>
    <artifactId>internal-lib</artifactId>
    <version>1.2.3</version>
    <exclusions>
        <exclusion>
            <groupId>javax.ws.rs</groupId>
            <artifactId>javax.ws.rs-api</artifactId>
        </exclusion>
    </exclusions>
</dependency>
```

Gunakan exclusion dengan alasan jelas.

Checklist:

```text id="k47s38"
[ ] Kenapa exclusion diperlukan?
[ ] Library pengganti disediakan di mana?
[ ] Ada test runtime?
[ ] Apakah exclusion memecah library lain?
```

---

# 16. ServiceLoader dan Provider Discovery

Beberapa library Java memakai `ServiceLoader` dengan file:

```text id="onzugr"
META-INF/services/...
```

Fat JAR/shading bisa merusak service files jika tidak merge dengan benar.

Gejala:

```text id="b723ob"
provider tidak ditemukan
JSON mapper tidak aktif
JDBC driver tidak register
JAX-RS feature tidak jalan
```

WAR model biasanya lebih natural karena JAR tetap terpisah di `WEB-INF/lib`. Fat JAR butuh plugin packaging yang benar agar service metadata tidak hilang.

Practical check:

```bash id="bckvkj"
jar tf target/case-service.jar | grep META-INF/services
```

---

# 17. Docker Image Layout

## 17.1 External Tomcat WAR image

Contoh:

```dockerfile id="z1e16o"
FROM tomcat:11-jdk17

RUN rm -rf /usr/local/tomcat/webapps/*

COPY target/case-service.war /usr/local/tomcat/webapps/ROOT.war

EXPOSE 8080

CMD ["catalina.sh", "run"]
```

Implication:

```text id="q79q90"
Context path = /
API path = /api/...
```

Jika copy sebagai:

```dockerfile id="ggelgt"
COPY target/case-service.war /usr/local/tomcat/webapps/case-service.war
```

Maka context path biasanya:

```text id="yc4sel"
/case-service
```

## 17.2 Embedded JAR image

```dockerfile id="vuntq6"
FROM eclipse-temurin:17-jre

WORKDIR /app

COPY target/case-service.jar /app/case-service.jar

EXPOSE 8080

ENTRYPOINT ["java", "-jar", "/app/case-service.jar"]
```

Checklist Docker:

```text id="k8rxb4"
[ ] Artifact yang dicopy benar?
[ ] Build cache tidak memakai artifact lama?
[ ] Context path sesuai copy target?
[ ] Port expose sesuai app?
[ ] JVM options disediakan?
[ ] Config/env/secret tersedia?
[ ] User non-root jika policy mewajibkan?
[ ] Logs ke stdout/stderr?
```

---

# 18. Local vs Prod Runtime Drift

Masalah umum:

```text id="vpj651"
Local pakai embedded Grizzly/Jetty.
Prod pakai Tomcat external.
Test pakai JerseyTest in-memory.
```

Akibat:

```text id="5is58u"
filter behavior berbeda
servlet mapping berbeda
classloader berbeda
multipart behavior berbeda
error dispatch berbeda
compression berbeda
forwarded header handling berbeda
```

Rule:

```text id="4dd8xa"
Unit test boleh in-memory.
Integration/smoke test harus menjalankan runtime yang mendekati production.
```

Jika production Tomcat + WAR, buat minimal test yang deploy WAR ke Tomcat container atau Docker image yang sama.

---

# 19. Practical WAR Smoke Test

## 19.1 Build and inspect

```bash id="5guz10"
mvn clean package
jar tf target/case-service.war | grep WEB-INF/web.xml
jar tf target/case-service.war | grep "WEB-INF/lib/jersey"
```

## 19.2 Run Tomcat Docker

```bash id="8vvzr5"
docker build -t case-service:local .
docker run --rm -p 8080:8080 case-service:local
```

## 19.3 Hit endpoint

```bash id="m4h7jx"
curl -i http://localhost:8080/api/v1/health
```

Jika WAR dicopy sebagai `case-service.war`:

```bash id="q3t0s5"
curl -i http://localhost:8080/case-service/api/v1/health
```

Interpretasi:

```text id="cqrh6v"
404 root:
  context path salah atau WAR tidak deploy

404 /api:
  servlet mapping/Jersey registration salah

500 startup:
  dependency/classloader/config issue

No response:
  container port/entrypoint issue
```

---

# 20. Common Runtime Errors

## 20.1 `ClassNotFoundException`

Contoh:

```text id="fys5h0"
java.lang.ClassNotFoundException:
  org.glassfish.jersey.servlet.ServletContainer
```

Kemungkinan:

```text id="wfc2qc"
jersey-container-servlet-core tidak masuk WEB-INF/lib
dependency scope provided padahal container tidak menyediakan Jersey
WAR packaging exclude dependency
artifact yang dideploy bukan build terbaru
```

Cek:

```bash id="l3ff4g"
jar tf target/case-service.war | grep jersey-container-servlet
mvn dependency:tree | grep jersey-container
```

## 20.2 `NoClassDefFoundError`

```text id="5ququi"
java.lang.NoClassDefFoundError: jakarta/ws/rs/core/Application
```

Kemungkinan:

```text id="9b8qty"
jakarta.ws.rs-api tidak tersedia saat runtime
scope salah
container tidak menyediakan Jakarta REST API
Jersey dependency incomplete
```

## 20.3 `NoSuchMethodError`

```text id="09mce5"
java.lang.NoSuchMethodError: com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper...
```

Kemungkinan:

```text id="r19x6t"
compile dengan Jackson versi baru
runtime memuat Jackson versi lama
dependency transitive conflict
container common lib punya versi berbeda
```

Cek:

```bash id="5bikne"
mvn dependency:tree | grep jackson
jar tf target/case-service.war | grep jackson
```

## 20.4 `ClassCastException`

```text id="h2xgwb"
com.company.Foo cannot be cast to com.company.Foo
```

Kemungkinan:

```text id="n8h68u"
class yang sama dimuat oleh dua classloader berbeda
shared lib di container dan WEB-INF/lib
static singleton lintas webapp/redeploy
```

## 20.5 `LinkageError`

```text id="kw5yp9"
loader constraint violation
```

Kemungkinan:

```text id="j1ru26"
dua versi class/API berbeda dipakai bersamaan
javax/jakarta mismatch
container lib conflict
```

---

# 21. Redeploy dan Memory Leak

External Servlet Container bisa redeploy WAR tanpa restart process. Risiko:

```text id="p91ktd"
thread background tidak mati
executor tidak shutdown
JDBC driver leak
ThreadLocal leak
static singleton memegang classloader lama
timer/scheduler tetap jalan
logging appender tidak close
```

Saat redeploy, classloader webapp lama seharusnya bisa di-GC. Tetapi jika ada thread/static reference yang masih memegang classloader lama, memory leak terjadi.

Checklist listener shutdown:

```java id="09j07v"
public class ApplicationLifecycleListener implements ServletContextListener {

    @Override
    public void contextInitialized(ServletContextEvent event) {
        AppRuntime.start();
    }

    @Override
    public void contextDestroyed(ServletContextEvent event) {
        AppRuntime.stop();
    }
}
```

Shutdown:

```java id="gp03jh"
public final class AppRuntime {

    private static ExecutorService executor;

    public static void start() {
        executor = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(16);
    }

    public static void stop() {
        if (executor != null) {
            executor.shutdown();
            try {
                if (!executor.awaitTermination(10, TimeUnit.SECONDS)) {
                    executor.shutdownNow();
                }
            } catch (InterruptedException e) {
                Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
                executor.shutdownNow();
            }
        }
    }
}
```

Rule:

```text id="vec47f"
Apa pun yang dibuat saat startup harus punya shutdown path.
```

---

# 22. Classpath Resource Loading

Resource bisa dibaca dari classpath:

```java id="b84mlx"
try (InputStream input = Thread.currentThread()
        .getContextClassLoader()
        .getResourceAsStream("application.properties")) {

    if (input == null) {
        throw new IllegalStateException("application.properties not found");
    }
}
```

Dalam WAR:

```text id="li6trm"
src/main/resources/application.properties
→ WEB-INF/classes/application.properties
```

Risiko:

```text id="r7kujz"
resource dengan nama sama di banyak JAR
config lama ikut artifact
classpath order membuat resource yang salah dibaca
secret tidak seharusnya dibundle ke WAR
```

Practical rule:

```text id="3x8pv3"
Classpath resource cocok untuk default config/static metadata.
Secret dan environment-specific config sebaiknya dari external config/secret source.
```

---

# 23. Multi-module Maven

Struktur umum:

```text id="opv4ny"
case-platform/
├── pom.xml parent
├── case-api/
├── case-domain/
├── case-persistence/
├── case-web/
│   └── packaging war
└── case-integration/
```

Common issue:

```text id="go3e8h"
Resource class ada di module case-api,
tapi module case-web tidak depend ke case-api.

Atau:
MyBatis mapper XML ada di module lain,
tapi tidak ikut masuk artifact.
```

Checklist:

```text id="h1ln8v"
[ ] Module WAR depend ke semua module runtime?
[ ] Mapper XML masuk classpath?
[ ] ResourceConfig scan package module yang benar?
[ ] Dependency scope antar module tidak salah?
[ ] Parent dependencyManagement konsisten?
```

Inspect WAR:

```bash id="x4iklu"
jar tf case-web/target/case-service.war | grep "com/company/caseapp/resource"
jar tf case-web/target/case-service.war | grep "mapper"
```

---

# 24. MyBatis Resource Packaging

MyBatis sering butuh XML mapper:

```text id="bjs6jp"
src/main/resources/mappers/CaseMapper.xml
```

Dalam artifact:

```text id="vlgdqa"
WEB-INF/classes/mappers/CaseMapper.xml
```

Cek:

```bash id="rdar5q"
jar tf target/case-service.war | grep CaseMapper.xml
```

Jika mapper tidak ada:

```text id="lg6sib"
binding exception
statement not found
mapper XML not found
```

Pastikan Maven resources tidak exclude file XML:

```xml id="7mejy6"
<build>
    <resources>
        <resource>
            <directory>src/main/resources</directory>
        </resource>
    </resources>
</build>
```

---

# 25. Logging Dependency Conflict

Logging sering rawan:

```text id="8ulqpe"
slf4j-api
logback-classic
log4j-api
log4j-core
jul-to-slf4j
jcl-over-slf4j
multiple bindings
```

Gejala:

```text id="rup718"
multiple SLF4J bindings warning
log tidak muncul
format log berbeda di prod
correlation id MDC tidak muncul
container log vs app log terpisah
```

Checklist:

```bash id="5pvy78"
mvn dependency:tree | grep -E "slf4j|logback|log4j|jul|commons-logging"
jar tf target/case-service.war | grep -E "slf4j|logback|log4j"
```

Rule:

```text id="znv5bt"
Pilih satu logging implementation.
Jangan biarkan transitive dependency membawa binding lain tanpa sadar.
```

# 26. Practical Production Readiness Checklist

## 26.1 Artifact

```text id="asgvrc"
[ ] Artifact type jelas: WAR atau executable JAR.
[ ] Artifact name dan context path terdokumentasi.
[ ] Artifact yang di-Docker-copy adalah hasil build terbaru.
[ ] `WEB-INF/web.xml` ada jika deployment bergantung padanya.
[ ] `WEB-INF/lib` berisi dependency yang diperlukan.
[ ] Tidak ada API jar dobel yang seharusnya provided.
```

## 26.2 Dependency

```text id="n3ry74"
[ ] `mvn dependency:tree` bersih dari javax/jakarta mismatch.
[ ] Jersey modules satu major/minor line.
[ ] Servlet API scope sesuai deployment model.
[ ] JDBC driver tersedia di tempat yang benar.
[ ] MyBatis mapper XML ikut artifact.
[ ] JSON provider jelas dan tidak dobel.
```

## 26.3 Runtime

```text id="gbhv9q"
[ ] Container version kompatibel dengan Servlet/Jakarta namespace.
[ ] Java version sesuai.
[ ] Startup log menunjukkan Jersey application class benar.
[ ] Health endpoint berhasil lewat final route.
[ ] Access log container aktif.
[ ] Shutdown listener menutup resource.
```

## 26.4 Docker/Kubernetes

```text id="v1szdh"
[ ] Dockerfile copy artifact benar.
[ ] Port sesuai.
[ ] Context path sesuai route.
[ ] Env/config/secret mount tersedia.
[ ] Image tag immutable.
[ ] Readiness/liveness hit path yang benar.
[ ] Rolling update tidak memakai artifact lama.
```

# 27. Debug Playbook

## Case A - Local jalan, prod `ClassNotFoundException`

```text id="9oazwf"
1. Compare Maven profile local vs prod.
2. Inspect WAR/JAR final.
3. Cek dependency scope.
4. Cek Dockerfile COPY target.
5. Cek container lib.
6. Cek artifact yang benar-benar deployed.
```

Commands:

```bash id="iptlur"
mvn clean package -Pprod
jar tf target/*.war | grep MissingClass
mvn dependency:tree | grep missing-artifact
```

---

## Case B - Endpoint 404 setelah deploy

```text id="cdydp3"
1. Cek nama WAR/context path.
2. Cek web.xml ada.
3. Cek Jersey ServletContainer class tersedia.
4. Cek ResourceConfig class benar.
5. Cek resource class masuk artifact.
6. Cek ingress path/context path.
```

Commands:

```bash id="fn0l3j"
jar tf target/*.war | grep WEB-INF/web.xml
jar tf target/*.war | grep CaseApplication
jar tf target/*.war | grep CaseResource
```

---

## Case C - `NoSuchMethodError` setelah upgrade

```text id="4mdxgt"
1. Cek dependency tree versi compile.
2. Cek JAR final di WEB-INF/lib.
3. Cek container common lib.
4. Cek transitive dependency dari internal library.
5. Cek BOM/dependencyManagement.
```

Commands:

```bash id="ace1xj"
mvn dependency:tree -Dverbose | grep target-library
jar tf target/*.war | grep target-library
```

---

## Case D - `javax.*` / `jakarta.*` problem

```text id="eyqkz4"
1. Search source import.
2. Search dependency tree.
3. Inspect WAR.
4. Cek container version.
5. Align Jersey major version.
```

Commands:

```bash id="xih5p1"
grep -R "javax.ws.rs\|javax.servlet" src/main/java
mvn dependency:tree | grep -E "javax.ws.rs|javax.servlet|jakarta.ws.rs|jakarta.servlet"
jar tf target/*.war | grep -E "javax|jakarta"
```

---

## Case E - MyBatis mapper not found

```text id="a76729"
1. Cek mapper XML di src/main/resources.
2. Cek mapper XML di artifact.
3. Cek MyBatis config path.
4. Cek module dependency.
5. Cek resource filtering/exclusion.
```

Commands:

```bash id="jua56m"
jar tf target/*.war | grep Mapper.xml
```

# 28. Code Review Checklist

## 28.1 POM

```text id="o15qg5"
[ ] Packaging benar: war/jar.
[ ] Dependency scope benar untuk deployment model.
[ ] BOM digunakan untuk dependency besar seperti Jersey/Jackson jika perlu.
[ ] Tidak ada duplicate major version.
[ ] Tidak ada javax/jakarta campur.
[ ] Internal module dependency lengkap.
[ ] Runtime-only dependency tidak hilang.
```

## 28.2 WAR

```text id="fhp5gr"
[ ] web.xml benar.
[ ] WEB-INF/classes berisi app classes/resources.
[ ] WEB-INF/lib berisi app dependencies.
[ ] Servlet API tidak dibundle jika external container menyediakan.
[ ] Context path diketahui.
```

## 28.3 Embedded

```text id="7e1yqw"
[ ] main class jelas.
[ ] Embedded container dependency dibundle.
[ ] Programmatic servlet/filter registration jelas.
[ ] Shutdown hook/lifecycle clean.
[ ] Port/config/env jelas.
```

## 28.4 Classloader

```text id="j1p5d7"
[ ] Tidak ada business library di container common lib tanpa alasan.
[ ] Tidak ada library app-specific di shared container.
[ ] Tidak ada duplicate API JAR.
[ ] Logging binding tidak dobel.
[ ] ServiceLoader files tidak rusak oleh shading/fat jar.
```

## 28.5 Deployment

```text id="fnsyim"
[ ] Dockerfile copy artifact hasil build.
[ ] Artifact version/tag traceable.
[ ] Health endpoint diuji setelah image build.
[ ] Container startup log diperiksa.
[ ] Rollback artifact tersedia.
```

---

# 29. Anti-Pattern

| Anti-pattern | Dampak | Alternatif |
|---|---|---|
| Menaruh semua dependency ke container lib | Conflict antar aplikasi | App-specific dependency di `WEB-INF/lib` |
| `jakarta.servlet-api` ikut WAR external tanpa sadar | API conflict | Scope `provided` |
| Dependency penting dibuat `provided` padahal runtime tidak menyediakan | `ClassNotFoundException` | `compile/runtime` sesuai model |
| WAR lokal beda dengan image deploy | Debug misleading | Inspect artifact di image |
| Nama WAR menentukan context path tanpa dokumentasi | 404 route confusion | Route contract eksplisit |
| Jersey 2.x + Jakarta Servlet modern | Namespace mismatch | Align Jersey/Jakarta version |
| Fat JAR tanpa merge service files | Provider tidak ditemukan | Konfigurasi shade/assembly benar |
| Banyak logging binding | Log tidak konsisten | Satu implementation |
| Redeploy tanpa shutdown executor | Memory leak/classloader leak | `ServletContextListener` cleanup |
| Multi-module resource tidak ikut WAR | Runtime mapper/config not found | Inspect artifact |

---

```bash id="h3qcew"
mvn help:effective-pom > effective-pom.xml

jar tf target/*.war | sort > artifact-files.txt
```

Lalu isi matrix:

```text id="g69l4i"
Deployment model:

Container:

  Java version:

Artifact:
  file:

Maven:

Artifact inspection:

Docker:
  copied artifact:
  runtime command:
  exposed port:

```

Red flag terbesar:

```text id="ftlv82"
[ ] Tidak tahu WAR atau embedded.
[ ] Tidak tahu container version.
[ ] `javax.*` dan `jakarta.*` campur.
[ ] Servlet API salah scope.
[ ] Jersey dependency tidak masuk artifact.
[ ] Mapper XML tidak masuk artifact.
[ ] Docker image copy artifact salah.
[ ] Context path berubah karena nama WAR.
[ ] Library app-specific ditaruh di container lib.
```

---

# Ringkasan Seri 10

Deployment model menentukan bagaimana aplikasi Jersey/JAX-RS dipaketkan, dependency apa yang ikut artifact, classloader mana yang memuat class, dan bagaimana runtime menemukan servlet/filter/resource/provider.

Model utama:

```text id="gdx864"
WAR external:
  container menyediakan Servlet runtime
  app classes di WEB-INF/classes
  app dependencies di WEB-INF/lib
  servlet-api biasanya provided

Embedded:
  app membawa container runtime
  dependency harus ikut executable artifact
  registration sering programmatic
```

Invariants penting:

```text id="o958x6"
Inspect artifact, jangan hanya percaya pom.xml.
Maven scope harus sesuai deployment model.
Jangan campur javax.* dan jakarta.*.
Jangan taruh library app-specific di container common lib tanpa alasan kuat.
Context path harus terdokumentasi.
Dependency tree harus dicek sebelum menyalahkan business code.
Apa pun yang dibuat saat startup harus ditutup saat shutdown.
```

---

Lanjut ke **Seri 11/12 - Observability & Production Debugging: Access Log, Metrics, JMX, Trace ID**.

Fokus berikutnya: bagaimana membaca sinyal dari Servlet Container dan aplikasi saat production issue: access log, request latency, status code, busy thread, connection pool, JMX/MBeans, correlation id, structured log, thread dump, heap symptom, dan playbook debugging dari gateway sampai database.

**Seri 10/12 selesai.**
