7/15/2026, 7:33:50 AM

# Seri 11/12 - Observability & Production Debugging: Access Log, Metrics, JMX, Trace ID

## Tujuan seri ini

Seri ini fokus pada cara membaca sinyal production dari aplikasi **Java Jersey/JAX-RS di atas Servlet Container**.

Masalah yang ingin dijawab:

```text id="f5yaah"
Request lambat di mana?
Request gagal sebelum Jersey atau setelah masuk resource?
Thread pool container penuh atau DB pool penuh?
Gateway timeout duluan atau aplikasi yang timeout?
Correlation ID hilang di layer mana?
Error 500 berasal dari filter, Jersey, DB, Camunda, atau downstream?
CPU rendah tapi latency tinggi, harus cek apa?
```

Servlet Container seperti Tomcat menyediakan access log sebagai fitur terpisah dari logging aplikasi. Tomcat Access Log Valve membuat log dengan format seperti web server standar, dapat dianalisis oleh log tools, dan dirancang untuk menulis aliran data access log besar dengan overhead rendah. ([Apache Tomcat](https://tomcat.apache.org/tomcat-11.0-doc/config/valve.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Tomcat juga memiliki JMX Proxy Servlet untuk membaca dan mengubah internal MBeans Tomcat, tetapi dokumentasinya mengingatkan bahwa interface ini bersifat low-level dan sangat powerful sehingga harus hati-hati saat mengaktifkan role `manager-jmx`. ([Apache Tomcat](https://tomcat.apache.org/tomcat-11.0-doc/manager-howto.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

---

# 1. Mental Model Observability

Untuk production debugging, jangan mulai dari “kemungkinan bug di service layer”. Mulai dari pertanyaan:

```text id="812w15"
Request sudah sampai layer mana?
```

Layer observability:

```text id="zj3dlq"
Client / caller
  ↓
API Gateway / Load Balancer
  ↓
Ingress / reverse proxy
  ↓
Servlet Container access log
  ↓
Servlet Filter log
  ↓
Jersey request/resource log
  ↓
Service log
  ↓
MyBatis/PostgreSQL log/metrics
  ↓
Camunda/downstream/external service log
```

Jika tidak ada access log container, kemungkinan request belum sampai aplikasi. Jika access log ada tetapi filter log tidak ada, kemungkinan mapping/filter/dispatcher issue. Jika filter log ada tetapi resource log tidak ada, kemungkinan Jersey mapping/provider issue. Jika resource log ada tetapi DB log lambat, kemungkinan service/dependency issue.

---

# 2. Diagram Debugging Signal

```text id="lyr8xt"
Client
  │
  │ client timeout / status / latency
  ▼
API Gateway / LB
  │
  │ gateway access log
  │ upstream status
  │ upstream latency
  │ retry count
  ▼
Ingress / Reverse Proxy
  │
  │ route match
  │ rewrite path
  │ upstream service/pod
  │ 499/502/503/504
  ▼
Servlet Container
  │
  │ access log
  │ connector metrics
  │ busy threads
  │ current thread count
  │ request count/error count
  ▼
Servlet Filter Chain
  │
  │ correlation id
  │ auth decision
  │ tenant/user context
  │ elapsed time
  ▼
Jersey / JAX-RS
  │
  │ resource matched
  │ exception mapper
  │ message body reader/writer
  ▼
Application Service
  │
  │ business operation
  │ state transition
  │ external call timing
  ▼
Persistence / Workflow / Integration
  │
  │ DB pool active/pending
  │ SQL latency
  │ Camunda latency
  │ downstream HTTP latency
```

---

# 3. Decomposition Map

| No | Area | Fungsi praktis | Yang harus dikuasai | Failure mode umum |
|---:|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Access log container | Bukti request sampai container | method, URI, status, bytes, duration, remote IP | Tidak aktif / format tidak cukup |
| 2 | Application structured log | Bukti request masuk filter/resource/service | correlation id, tenant, user, operation | Log tidak bisa dikorelasikan |
| 3 | Correlation ID | Menghubungkan log lintas layer/service | generate, propagate, return response header | Trace putus |
| 4 | Request timing | Memecah latency per layer | total elapsed, DB elapsed, downstream elapsed | Hanya tahu total, tidak tahu penyebab |
| 5 | Status code analysis | Membedakan 4xx/5xx/gateway error | rate by endpoint/status | Semua error dianggap sama |
| 6 | Servlet thread metrics | Mengetahui thread starvation | busy threads, max threads, queue symptom | CPU rendah tapi request timeout |
| 7 | JMX/MBeans | Introspeksi JVM/container | Tomcat/JVM MBeans, custom MBeans | JMX dibuka tidak aman |
| 8 | DB pool metrics | Melihat pool saturation | active/idle/pending/acquire timeout | Thread menunggu connection |
| 9 | PostgreSQL metrics/log | Melihat query lambat/lock | slow query, lock wait, active session | Semua blamed ke app |
| 10 | Downstream metrics | Melihat dependency lambat/gagal | latency, timeout, retry, circuit state | Retry storm |
| 11 | Thread dump | Snapshot blocking/waiting | `jcmd Thread.print` | Tidak diambil saat incident |
| 12 | Heap/memory symptom | OOM/leak/large payload | heap usage, GC pause, dump | Salah tuning thread padahal memory |
| 13 | Kubernetes signals | Pod restart/readiness/liveness | restart count, events, probes, logs | App dianggap error padahal probe salah |
| 14 | Error contract | Konsistensi error response | Jersey mapper + servlet error handler | Kadang JSON kadang HTML |
| 15 | Runbook | Langkah debugging repeatable | query log, metrics, commands | Incident selalu ad-hoc |

---

# 4. Access Log: Sinyal Pertama di Aplikasi

Access log menjawab:

```text id="4697xe"
Apakah request sampai ke container?
Status akhirnya apa?
Berapa lama di sisi container?
Path yang diterima container apa?
Remote IP yang terlihat apa?
Bytes response berapa?
```

Tomcat membedakan access logging dari logging internal aplikasi; dokumentasi logging Tomcat menyebut access logging adalah fitur terkait tetapi berbeda, diimplementasikan sebagai Valve dengan logika self-contained. ([Apache Tomcat](https://tomcat.apache.org/tomcat-11.0-doc/logging.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 4.1 Contoh Tomcat AccessLogValve

```xml id="yk47bi"
<Valve className="org.apache.catalina.valves.AccessLogValve"
       directory="logs"
       prefix="access_log"
       suffix=".txt"
       pattern="%h %l %u %t &quot;%r&quot; %s %b %D" />
```

Makna umum:

| Token | Makna |
|---|---|
| `%h` | remote host/IP yang terlihat container |
| `%t` | timestamp |
| `%r` | request line |
| `%s` | status code |
| `%b` | bytes sent |
| `%D` | request duration dalam microseconds pada Tomcat pattern |

Format ideal untuk microservice sebaiknya memasukkan request id/correlation id jika tersedia sebagai header.

Contoh konseptual:

```xml id="psn61k"
<Valve className="org.apache.catalina.valves.AccessLogValve"
       directory="logs"
       prefix="access"
       suffix=".log"
       pattern="%h %t &quot;%r&quot; %s %b %D %{X-Correlation-Id}i %{User-Agent}i" />
```

---

# 5. Custom Access Log Filter

Access log container bagus, tetapi kadang Anda butuh log aplikasi yang lebih kaya:

```text id="luz87f"
correlationId
tenantId
userId/serviceAccountId
operation name
Jersey matched resource, jika tersedia
business outcome
dependency timing summary
```

Contoh filter:

```java id="0wz2ai"
package com.company.caseapp.web;

import jakarta.servlet.Filter;
import jakarta.servlet.FilterChain;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletException;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletResponse;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.time.Duration;

public class ApiAccessLogFilter implements Filter {

    @Override
    public void doFilter(
            ServletRequest request,
            ServletResponse response,
            FilterChain chain
    ) throws IOException, ServletException {

        HttpServletRequest httpRequest = (HttpServletRequest) request;
        StatusCaptureResponseWrapper wrappedResponse =
                new StatusCaptureResponseWrapper((HttpServletResponse) response);

        long startNanos = System.nanoTime();

        try {
            chain.doFilter(request, wrappedResponse);
        } finally {
            long elapsedMs = Duration.ofNanos(System.nanoTime() - startNanos).toMillis();

            RequestContext.RequestMetadata metadata = RequestContext.currentOrNull();

            String correlationId = metadata == null ? "" : metadata.correlationId();
            String tenantId = metadata == null ? "" : metadata.tenantId();
            String userId = metadata == null ? "" : metadata.userId();

            System.out.printf(
                    "api_access correlationId=%s tenantId=%s userId=%s method=%s uri=%s status=%d elapsedMs=%d remoteAddr=%s%n",
                    safe(correlationId),
                    safe(tenantId),
                    safe(userId),
                    httpRequest.getMethod(),
                    safePath(httpRequest),
                    wrappedResponse.getStatusCode(),
                    elapsedMs,
                    httpRequest.getRemoteAddr()
            );
        }
    }

    private static String safePath(HttpServletRequest request) {
        return request.getRequestURI();
    }

    private static String safe(String value) {
        return value == null ? "" : value.replaceAll("[\\r\\n\\t]", "_");
    }
}
```

Catatan penting:

```text id="ldpd4n"
Jangan log Authorization, Cookie, raw body, atau query string sensitif.
Log correlation id dan path sudah cukup untuk banyak kasus.
```

---

# 6. Structured Logging

Untuk production, log bentuk bebas sulit dianalisis. Lebih baik structured JSON log.

Contoh log JSON konseptual:

```json id="x5rwz7"
{
  "event": "api_access",
  "correlationId": "8b6a2c4e-83af-4d5e-87d8-10a2a8e3e43f",
  "tenantId": "tenant-a",
  "userId": "user-123",
  "method": "GET",
  "path": "/api/v1/cases/CASE-001",
  "status": 200,
  "elapsedMs": 84,
  "remoteAddr": "203.0.113.10"
}
```

Minimal fields:

```text id="03kp84"
timestamp
level
service
environment
pod/host
correlationId
event
method
path
status
elapsedMs
tenantId, jika aman
userId/serviceAccountId, jika aman
errorCode, jika ada
exceptionClass, server-side only
```

---

# 7. Correlation ID

Correlation ID harus ada sejak awal request.

## 7.1 Filter

```java id="85zpkp"
public class CorrelationIdFilter implements Filter {

    public static final String HEADER = "X-Correlation-Id";
    public static final String ATTRIBUTE = "correlationId";

    @Override
    public void doFilter(
            ServletRequest request,
            ServletResponse response,
            FilterChain chain
    ) throws IOException, ServletException {

        HttpServletRequest httpRequest = (HttpServletRequest) request;
        HttpServletResponse httpResponse = (HttpServletResponse) response;

        String correlationId = httpRequest.getHeader(HEADER);

        if (correlationId == null || correlationId.isBlank()) {
            correlationId = java.util.UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        }

        try {
            org.slf4j.MDC.put("correlationId", correlationId);
            httpRequest.setAttribute(ATTRIBUTE, correlationId);
            httpResponse.setHeader(HEADER, correlationId);

            chain.doFilter(request, response);
        } finally {
            org.slf4j.MDC.remove("correlationId");
        }
    }
}
```

## 7.2 Propagate ke downstream HTTP

```java id="y70au5"
HttpRequest outbound = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
        .uri(URI.create("https://risk-service/api/v1/check"))
        .header("X-Correlation-Id", RequestContext.current().correlationId())
        .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(3))
        .GET()
        .build();
```

Rule:

```text id="4l4z48"
Correlation ID boleh diteruskan lintas service.
User/tenant/token hanya diteruskan jika trust boundary jelas.
```

---

# 8. Metrics: Apa yang Wajib Ada

## 8.1 HTTP metrics

```text id="p2ypd6"
http.server.requests.count
http.server.requests.duration
http.server.requests.errors
status_code count
endpoint/path template
method
p50/p95/p99 latency
request size
response size
```

Hindari label cardinality tinggi:

```text id="u8i19i"
Jangan label metric dengan raw caseId/userId/correlationId.
Gunakan path template:
/api/v1/cases/{caseId}
bukan:
/api/v1/cases/CASE-001
```

## 8.2 Servlet Container metrics

```text id="269oe0"
busy request threads
current thread count
max threads
connection count
request count
error count
processing time
bytes sent/received
```

Tomcat Manager JMX Proxy dapat membaca MBeans Tomcat, tetapi harus dilindungi ketat karena dokumentasi Tomcat menyatakan interface ini adalah low-level administrative interface yang bisa melakukan banyak hal jika user tahu command-nya. ([Apache Tomcat](https://tomcat.apache.org/tomcat-11.0-doc/manager-howto.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 8.3 DB pool metrics

```text id="zlbd0p"
active connections
idle connections
pending/waiting threads
connection acquisition time
connection timeout count
max pool size
query latency
transaction duration
```

## 8.4 Downstream metrics

```text id="pl44ih"
outbound request count
outbound latency
outbound status code
timeout count
retry count
circuit breaker state
bulkhead queue/rejection
```

---

# 9. JMX / MBeans

JMX berguna untuk observability JVM dan container. Tomcat exposes banyak informasi via JMX; presentasi resmi Tomcat tentang JMX menjelaskan bahwa JMX menyediakan protocol/API untuk monitoring dan management, akses data lewat MBeans, membaca/menulis attributes, invoke operations, dan menerima notifications. ([Apache Tomcat](https://tomcat.apache.org/presentations/2015-04-15-acna-Monitoring%20Apache%20Tomcat%20with%20JMX.pdf?utm_source=chatgpt.com)) Jetty 12 juga memiliki dokumentasi JMX monitoring/management dan remote JMX access, termasuk catatan bahwa kontrol authentication/authorization untuk JMX harus diperhatikan. ([Jetty](https://jetty.org/docs/jetty/12.1/operations-guide/jmx/index.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

## 9.1 Yang bisa diamati via JMX

```text id="n2ka0r"
JVM memory
GC
thread count
class loading
Tomcat connector/thread pool
Tomcat request processor
session manager
datasource, jika exposed
custom app MBeans
```

## 9.2 Security JMX

Jangan buka JMX remote sembarangan.

Checklist:

```text id="vtubui"
[ ] JMX remote hanya di network trusted.
[ ] Auth aktif.
[ ] TLS/SSL dipertimbangkan.
[ ] Tidak diekspos publik.
[ ] Role manager-jmx Tomcat dibatasi.
[ ] Prefer scrape melalui agent internal jika memungkinkan.
```

---

# 10. Custom MBean untuk App Metrics

Jika belum ada metrics framework, MBean sederhana bisa membantu.

Interface:

```java id="8sq3gg"
package com.company.caseapp.observability;

public interface CaseServiceStatsMBean {

    long getTotalRequests();

    long getFailedRequests();

    long getCurrentInFlightRequests();
}
```

Implementation:

```java id="9xsy0o"
package com.company.caseapp.observability;

import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong;

public class CaseServiceStats implements CaseServiceStatsMBean {

    private final AtomicLong totalRequests = new AtomicLong();
    private final AtomicLong failedRequests = new AtomicLong();
    private final AtomicLong inFlightRequests = new AtomicLong();

    public void requestStarted() {
        totalRequests.incrementAndGet();
        inFlightRequests.incrementAndGet();
    }

    public void requestFinished(boolean failed) {
        if (failed) {
            failedRequests.incrementAndGet();
        }
        inFlightRequests.decrementAndGet();
    }

    @Override
    public long getTotalRequests() {
        return totalRequests.get();
    }

    @Override
    public long getFailedRequests() {
        return failedRequests.get();
    }

    @Override
    public long getCurrentInFlightRequests() {
        return inFlightRequests.get();
    }
}
```

Register:

```java id="g0f9dr"
package com.company.caseapp.observability;

import javax.management.MBeanServer;
import javax.management.ObjectName;
import java.lang.management.ManagementFactory;

public final class AppMBeans {

    private AppMBeans() {
    }

    public static void registerCaseServiceStats(CaseServiceStats stats) {
        try {
            MBeanServer server = ManagementFactory.getPlatformMBeanServer();
            ObjectName name = new ObjectName("com.company.caseapp:type=CaseServiceStats");

            if (!server.isRegistered(name)) {
                server.registerMBean(stats, name);
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Failed to register MBean", e);
        }
    }
}
```

Catatan:

```text id="ywqwve"
Untuk aplikasi modern, metrics framework seperti Micrometer/Prometheus biasanya lebih nyaman.
MBean tetap berguna untuk lingkungan on-prem atau ketika JMX sudah menjadi standard operasi.
```

---

# 11. Thread Dump

Thread dump adalah alat paling cepat untuk kasus:

```text id="4rk62h"
CPU rendah tapi latency tinggi
request timeout
thread pool penuh
deadlock
DB/downstream wait
lock contention
```

Command:

```bash id="b1y0gp"
jcmd <pid> Thread.print > thread-dump.txt
```

Dalam container:

```bash id="qkxz6p"
kubectl exec -it <pod> -- jcmd 1 Thread.print
```

Cari pola:

```text id="bvbpdw"
http-nio-8080-exec-* waiting on PostgreSQL
http-nio-8080-exec-* waiting for DB connection
http-nio-8080-exec-* socketRead external HTTP
BLOCKED on synchronized lock
WAITING on Future.get
ForkJoinPool/commonPool used by request path
```

Interpretasi:

| Thread dump | Makna praktis |
|---|---|
| Banyak thread di PostgreSQL driver | Query lambat / DB overload |
| Banyak thread menunggu pool connection | DB pool habis |
| Banyak thread di HTTP socket read | Downstream lambat/tanpa timeout |
| Banyak `BLOCKED` pada lock sama | Lock contention |
| Banyak `Future.get()` | Async tapi tetap blocking |
| Banyak request thread idle | Masalah mungkin sebelum container |

Rule:

```text id="znnasb"
Saat incident latency, ambil 3 thread dump berjarak 10-30 detik.
Satu dump snapshot, tiga dump menunjukkan apakah thread stuck di tempat yang sama.
```

---

# 12. Heap / Memory Symptom

Gejala memory issue:

```text id="iaizzn"
latency naik bersamaan GC pause
OOMKilled di Kubernetes
OutOfMemoryError
heap usage naik terus
large request/response payload
multipart upload memory spike
body logging/readAllBytes
```

Command dasar:

```bash id="p4gxva"
jcmd <pid> GC.heap_info
jcmd <pid> VM.native_memory summary
jcmd <pid> GC.class_histogram
```

Heap dump saat perlu:

```bash id="3rkjcy"
jcmd <pid> GC.heap_dump /tmp/heap.hprof
```

Hati-hati:

```text id="2jk1dc"
Heap dump bisa berisi data sensitif.
Perlakukan sebagai secret artifact.
Jangan upload sembarangan.
```

---

# 13. Kubernetes Signals

Untuk deployment Docker/Kubernetes, observability bukan hanya aplikasi.

Cek:

```bash id="m9xszj"
kubectl get pods
kubectl describe pod <pod>
kubectl logs <pod>
kubectl logs <pod> --previous
kubectl get events --sort-by=.lastTimestamp
kubectl top pod <pod>
```

Sinyal penting:

| Sinyal | Makna |
|---|---|
| Restart count naik | crash/OOM/liveness failure |
| OOMKilled | memory limit |
| Readiness false | pod tidak menerima traffic |
| Liveness failure | app dianggap mati dan direstart |
| ImagePullBackOff | deployment issue |
| 503 dari ingress | no ready endpoints / upstream fail |
| 504 dari gateway | upstream timeout |
| 499, jika NGINX | client menutup koneksi sebelum response |

Debug route:

```bash id="74fxmg"
kubectl get ingress
kubectl describe ingress <name>
kubectl get svc <service>
kubectl get endpoints <service>
```

---

# 14. Status Code Triage

| Status | Kemungkinan layer | Debug awal |
|---:|---|---|
| 400 | Jersey validation/body parsing/filter guard | request body/header, mapper |
| 401 | auth pre-check/gateway/container security | auth filter/gateway logs |
| 403 | authorization/domain policy | user role/tenant/resource state |
| 404 | gateway route/container mapping/Jersey path | access log + path decomposition |
| 409 | business state conflict | state machine/domain rule |
| 413 | gateway/container/body guard | payload size |
| 415 | content type / `@Consumes` | `Content-Type` |
| 429 | rate/concurrency limit | limiter metrics |
| 499 | client closed request | client/gateway timeout |
| 500 | app exception | exception mapper/log |
| 502 | gateway upstream bad response | ingress/gateway/upstream |
| 503 | no ready upstream/dependency unavailable | readiness/service health |
| 504 | upstream timeout | gateway timeout vs app latency |

---

# 15. Latency Breakdown

Jangan hanya log total latency. Pecah latency per dependency.

Contoh service timing:

```java id="6or0n4"
public CaseResponse getCase(String caseId) {
    long start = System.nanoTime();

    CaseEntity entity;
    long dbStart = System.nanoTime();
    try {
        entity = caseMapper.findById(caseId);
    } finally {
        logTiming("db.findCase", dbStart);
    }

    long riskStart = System.nanoTime();
    try {
        riskClient.enrich(entity);
    } finally {
        logTiming("downstream.risk", riskStart);
    }

    logTiming("service.getCase.total", start);

    return mapper.toResponse(entity);
}

private void logTiming(String operation, long startNanos) {
    long elapsedMs = java.time.Duration
            .ofNanos(System.nanoTime() - startNanos)
            .toMillis();

    System.out.printf(
            "timing correlationId=%s operation=%s elapsedMs=%d%n",
            RequestContext.current().correlationId(),
            operation,
            elapsedMs
    );
}
```

Lebih baik lagi jika metrics dikirim ke monitoring system, bukan hanya log.

---

# 16. DB Observability

Untuk MyBatis/PostgreSQL, sinyal minimal:

```text id="e3k7hp"
query latency
slow query log
DB pool active/idle/pending
connection acquisition timeout
transaction duration
lock wait
deadlock
rows scanned/returned
```

Anti-pattern:

```text id="a29t5a"
Setiap latency dianggap masalah Java.
Padahal request thread sering hanya menunggu DB.
```

Dalam log aplikasi, jangan log SQL parameter sensitif sembarangan. Untuk debugging, gunakan controlled slow query logging dan redaction.

---

# 17. Downstream Observability

Untuk setiap HTTP/gRPC/external call:

```text id="vdl66k"
dependency name
method/path template
status
elapsedMs
timeout/retry count
correlationId
error type
```

Contoh wrapper:

```java id="hsd2di"
public String callRiskService(String caseId) throws Exception {
    long start = System.nanoTime();

    try {
        HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder()
                .uri(URI.create("https://risk-service/api/v1/cases/" + caseId))
                .header("X-Correlation-Id", RequestContext.current().correlationId())
                .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(3))
                .GET()
                .build();

        HttpResponse<String> response =
                httpClient.send(request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofString());

        logDependency("risk-service", response.statusCode(), start, null);

        return response.body();
    } catch (Exception e) {
        logDependency("risk-service", -1, start, e);
        throw e;
    }
}

private void logDependency(
        String dependency,
        int status,
        long startNanos,
        Exception exception
) {
    long elapsedMs = Duration.ofNanos(System.nanoTime() - startNanos).toMillis();

    System.out.printf(
            "dependency correlationId=%s dependency=%s status=%d elapsedMs=%d error=%s%n",
            RequestContext.current().correlationId(),
            dependency,
            status,
            elapsedMs,
            exception == null ? "" : exception.getClass().getSimpleName()
    );
}
```

---

# 18. Error Logging: Jangan Duplicate Berlebihan

Pattern buruk:

```text id="4towla"
Filter log stacktrace
Jersey mapper log stacktrace
Service log stacktrace
Container log stacktrace
```

Hasilnya noisy dan sulit dipakai.

Pattern lebih baik:

```text id="g1oytk"
Service/domain:
  log business-important failure jika ada decision/event

ExceptionMapper:
  log unexpected exception sekali dengan correlationId

Access log:
  log status + elapsed

Client response:
  generic error + correlationId
```

Contoh mapper:

```java id="z7x2bx"
@Provider
public class GlobalExceptionMapper implements ExceptionMapper<Throwable> {

    private static final org.slf4j.Logger log =
            org.slf4j.LoggerFactory.getLogger(GlobalExceptionMapper.class);

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(Throwable exception) {
        String correlationId = RequestContext.currentOrNull() == null
                ? ""
                : RequestContext.currentOrNull().correlationId();

        log.error(
                "unexpected_error correlationId={} exceptionClass={}",
                correlationId,
                exception.getClass().getName(),
                exception
        );

        return Response.status(Response.Status.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR)
                .entity(new ErrorResponse(
                        "INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR",
                        "Unexpected server error",
                        correlationId
                ))
                .build();
    }

    public record ErrorResponse(String code, String message, String correlationId) {
    }
}
```

---

# 19. Request Journey Debug Playbook

## 19.1 404

```text id="2lurdn"
1. Cek gateway/ingress log: route match?
2. Cek container access log: request sampai?
3. Log requestURI/contextPath/servletPath/pathInfo.
4. Cek servlet mapping Jersey.
5. Cek ResourceConfig/resource registration.
6. Cek @Path class/method dan HTTP method.
```

## 19.2 504 gateway timeout

```text id="fs7o09"
1. Cek gateway upstream latency.
2. Cek container access log duration.
3. Cek app access log elapsedMs.
4. Cek busy threads.
5. Cek DB pool pending.
6. Cek downstream timeout/retry.
7. Ambil thread dump saat sedang terjadi.
```

## 19.3 CPU rendah + latency tinggi

```text id="htvmj4"
1. Cek request thread busy/max.
2. Cek thread dump.
3. Cek DB pool active/pending.
4. Cek downstream socket read/timeouts.
5. Cek lock contention.
6. Cek queue/retry storm.
```

## 19.4 500 spike

```text id="ljjp01"
1. Group by endpoint.
2. Group by exception class/error code.
3. Cek deploy/version change.
4. Cek dependency health.
5. Cek DB migration/schema.
6. Cek error mapper vs servlet error handler.
```

## 19.5 Random 401/403

```text id="0hz0vd"
1. Cek gateway auth logs.
2. Cek forwarded/trusted identity headers.
3. Cek tenant/user context filter.
4. Cek token expiry/clock skew.
5. Cek service-layer authorization decision log.
6. Cek session/cookie jika browser-facing.
```

---

# 20. Minimal Dashboard untuk Servlet/Jersey Microservice

Dashboard minimum:

```text id="v1db3l"
HTTP
  RPS
  error rate
  p50/p95/p99 latency
  status code by endpoint
  top slow endpoints

Servlet Container
  busy threads / max threads
  current thread count
  connection count
  request count/error count

JVM
  heap used/max
  GC pause/time
  thread count
  class loading
  CPU process

DB
  pool active/idle/pending
  connection acquisition latency
  query latency
  slow query count

Downstream
  latency/error/timeout per dependency
  retry count
  circuit breaker open count

Kubernetes
  pod restarts
  readiness/liveness failures
  CPU/memory limit usage
```

---

# 21. SLO / Alert yang Masuk Akal

Alert jangan hanya CPU.

Contoh alert yang lebih berguna:

```text id="747ln3"
p95 latency > threshold selama 5 menit
5xx rate > threshold
gateway 504 naik
busy threads > 80% max selama 5 menit
DB pool pending > 0 sustained
DB connection acquisition timeout > 0
downstream timeout rate naik
pod restart count naik
readiness failures naik
heap usage high + GC pause high
```

Anti-pattern alert:

```text id="axamg7"
CPU > 80% saja.
```

CPU rendah bisa tetap incident jika semua thread menunggu IO.

---

# 22. Safe Debug Endpoint

Untuk internal only, endpoint debug bisa membantu.

```java id="yc65ey"
@Path("/v1/internal/debug/runtime")
public class RuntimeDebugResource {

    @GET
    public RuntimeDebugResponse runtime() {
        Runtime runtime = Runtime.getRuntime();

        return new RuntimeDebugResponse(
                ManagementFactory.getRuntimeMXBean().getName(),
                ManagementFactory.getThreadMXBean().getThreadCount(),
                runtime.totalMemory(),
                runtime.freeMemory(),
                runtime.maxMemory()
        );
    }

    public record RuntimeDebugResponse(
            String runtimeName,
            int threadCount,
            long heapTotal,
            long heapFree,
            long heapMax
    ) {
    }
}
```

Wajib:

```text id="oy0xm5"
internal only
protected
no secret
no raw env dump
no full system properties dump
no stack trace dump to public
```

Jangan buat endpoint:

```text id="qkth7t"
GET /debug/env
GET /debug/system-properties
GET /debug/threads
```

yang terbuka publik.

---

# 23. Production Incident Workflow

Gunakan workflow ini saat issue terjadi.

```text id="hukivy"
1. Define symptom
   - status code?
   - latency?
   - endpoints?
   - started when?
   - all users or subset?

2. Confirm request path
   - gateway log
   - ingress log
   - container access log
   - app access log

3. Split by layer
   - before container
   - container/filter
   - Jersey/resource
   - service/dependency

4. Check saturation
   - busy threads
   - DB pool
   - executor queues
   - CPU/memory/GC
   - pod restarts

5. Take snapshots
   - thread dump
   - recent logs by correlationId
   - metrics time window
   - deployment version

6. Identify dominant failure mode
   - mapping
   - auth
   - timeout
   - DB
   - downstream
   - code exception
   - config/deployment

7. Mitigate
   - rollback
   - reduce traffic
   - disable feature flag
   - increase timeout only if safe
   - circuit-break dependency
   - scale only if bottleneck supports it

8. Document
   - root cause
   - detection gap
   - missing metric/log
   - prevention item
```

---

# 24. Correlation ID Investigation Example

Given error response:

```json id="v157bp"
{
  "code": "INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR",
  "message": "Unexpected server error",
  "correlationId": "2f2f5f81-9e16-4fb6-9d8c-f172c0f5d5b4"
}
```

Search logs:

```bash id="pqerki"
grep "2f2f5f81-9e16-4fb6-9d8c-f172c0f5d5b4" app.log
grep "2f2f5f81-9e16-4fb6-9d8c-f172c0f5d5b4" access.log
```

Expected chain:

```text id="19lzyb"
access log:
  method/path/status/elapsed

filter log:
  auth/tenant/context

resource log:
  operation start/end

service log:
  business operation

dependency log:
  DB/downstream timing

exception mapper:
  exception class/stacktrace
```

Jika correlation id hanya ada di response tapi tidak di service/downstream logs, propagation belum benar.

---

# 25. Practical Log Fields by Layer

| Layer | Fields |
|---|---|
| Gateway | request id, upstream status, upstream latency, route, client IP |
| Container access | method, URI, status, duration, remote IP, correlation header |
| Filter | correlationId, tenant/user, auth decision, dispatcher type |
| Jersey | resource/method matched, mapper exception class |
| Service | operation, resource id, state transition, decision |
| DB | query name, elapsed, rows, timeout |
| Downstream | dependency, endpoint template, status, elapsed, timeout/retry |
| Kubernetes | pod, node, container, restart, reason |

---

# 26. Code Review Checklist

## 26.1 Logs

```text id="rx79n1"
[ ] Access log container aktif.
[ ] App access log punya correlationId.
[ ] Error log punya exception class dan correlationId.
[ ] Tidak log token/cookie/body/PII raw.
[ ] Query string sensitif tidak dilog raw.
[ ] Log structured atau setidaknya key=value konsisten.
```

## 26.2 Metrics

```text id="h7zetm"
[ ] HTTP latency p95/p99 tersedia.
[ ] Status code per endpoint tersedia.
[ ] Busy threads/max threads tersedia.
[ ] DB pool active/idle/pending tersedia.
[ ] Downstream latency/error/timeout tersedia.
[ ] JVM heap/GC/thread tersedia.
[ ] Kubernetes restart/probe tersedia.
```

## 26.3 Tracing/context

```text id="h0kf7w"
[ ] Correlation ID digenerate paling awal.
[ ] Correlation ID dikembalikan ke response.
[ ] Correlation ID diteruskan ke downstream.
[ ] MDC cleanup di finally.
[ ] Async context propagation ada.
[ ] Tenant/user context trusted dan audit-safe.
```

## 26.4 Debuggability

```text id="5jn3lu"
[ ] Ada runbook 404.
[ ] Ada runbook 504.
[ ] Ada runbook CPU rendah latency tinggi.
[ ] Thread dump command terdokumentasi.
[ ] Artifact version terlihat di log startup.
[ ] Build/version/git commit terlihat di health/info internal.
```

---

# 27. Anti-Pattern

| Anti-pattern | Dampak | Alternatif |
|---|---|---|
| Tidak ada access log container | Tidak tahu request sampai app atau tidak | Aktifkan AccessLogValve/equivalent |
| Semua log tanpa correlation id | Investigasi lambat | CorrelationIdFilter + MDC |
| Log raw body/token/cookie | Secret/PII leak | Redaction + metadata only |
| Metric label raw user/caseId | Cardinality explosion | Path template/status/method |
| Hanya monitor CPU | Thread starvation tidak terlihat | Busy threads, DB pool, latency |
| Tidak ambil thread dump saat incident | Akar masalah hilang | Runbook jcmd/kubectl exec |
| JMX terbuka publik | Risiko admin takeover | Network/auth/TLS/role restriction |
| Error response tanpa correlationId | User report sulit dilacak | Include correlationId |
| Retry tanpa metric | Retry storm tersembunyi | Log/metric retry count |
| Debug endpoint expose env/secrets | Security incident | Internal-only, minimal safe info |

---

# 28. Mini Exercise untuk Repo Anda

Jalankan audit:

```bash id="9kyp2n"
grep -R "X-Correlation-Id" src/main/java
grep -R "MDC" src/main/java
grep -R "printStackTrace" src/main/java
grep -R "System.out" src/main/java
grep -R "getRequestURI" src/main/java
grep -R "getRemoteAddr" src/main/java
grep -R "ExceptionMapper" src/main/java
grep -R "Thread.print" .
grep -R "AccessLogValve" .
```

Isi matrix:

```text id="qq4hfu"
Access log:
  container access log enabled:
  fields:
  correlation id included:
  duration included:
  remote IP normalized:

Application log:
  structured:
  correlation id:
  tenant/user:
  exception class:
  stacktrace policy:
  sensitive redaction:

Metrics:
  HTTP latency:
  status code:
  busy threads:
  DB pool:
  downstream:
  JVM:
  Kubernetes:

Debugging:
  thread dump command:
  heap dump policy:
  internal debug endpoint:
  runbook:
  dashboard:
  alerts:

Risk:
  low / medium / high
```

Red flag terbesar:

```text id="qx6qun"
[ ] Tidak ada access log.
[ ] Tidak ada correlation id.
[ ] Tidak ada busy thread metric.
[ ] Tidak ada DB pool metric.
[ ] Tidak ada downstream timeout metric.
[ ] Error response tidak punya correlationId.
[ ] Debug endpoint expose env/system properties.
[ ] Log berisi Authorization/Cookie/raw body.
```

---

# Ringkasan Seri 11

Observability untuk Servlet/Jersey microservice harus menjawab tiga hal:

```text id="h8r1h1"
1. Request sampai layer mana?
2. Di layer mana latency/error terjadi?
3. Apakah ada saturation di thread, DB pool, executor, memory, atau dependency?
```

Invariants penting:

```text id="b588ua"
Access log container wajib ada.
Correlation ID harus dibuat paling awal dan dipropagasi.
Log harus structured atau minimal key=value konsisten.
Jangan log secret/PII.
Monitor busy threads, bukan CPU saja.
Monitor DB pool pending, bukan DB latency saja.
Ambil thread dump saat latency incident.
JMX berguna tapi harus diamankan.
Error response harus menyertakan correlationId.
Runbook harus repeatable, bukan ad-hoc.
```

Model debugging:

```text id="inrx23"
Gateway log
  → container access log
  → filter log
  → Jersey/resource log
  → service/dependency log
  → DB/downstream metrics
  → thread dump if saturation suspected
```

# Seri 12/12 - Production Hardening: Graceful Shutdown, Kubernetes Probes, Limits, Safe Defaults

## Tujuan seri ini

Seri terakhir ini fokus pada **hardening Servlet Container runtime** untuk production.

Target praktis:

```text id="fah72x"
[ ] Service tidak menerima traffic sebelum siap.
[ ] Service bisa berhenti tanpa memutus request penting secara brutal.
[ ] Timeout, body limit, header limit, thread limit, dan connection limit jelas.
[ ] Probe Kubernetes tidak menyebabkan restart palsu.
[ ] Request besar, slow client, downstream lambat, dan thread starvation punya guardrail.
[ ] Default security header, error response, logging, dan shutdown behavior aman.
[ ] Ada checklist final sebelum service dianggap production-ready.
```

Di Kubernetes, **readiness probe** menentukan apakah Pod siap menerima traffic; jika readiness gagal, Pod tidak menerima traffic melalui Kubernetes Service. **Startup probe** memberi waktu khusus untuk aplikasi yang lambat start sebelum liveness/readiness berjalan, sedangkan **liveness probe** dipakai untuk menentukan kapan container perlu direstart. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/tasks/configure-pod-container/configure-liveness-readiness-startup-probes/))

---

# 1. Mental Model Production Hardening

Hardening bukan hanya “setting lebih besar”.

Hardening adalah membuat boundary yang jelas:

```text id="dq8lwu"
Traffic boundary:
  readiness, ingress, gateway, draining

Runtime boundary:
  thread pool, connection limit, timeout, shutdown

Payload boundary:
  header size, body size, multipart size, content type

Failure boundary:
  error JSON, fallback, reject early, no infinite wait

Security boundary:
  TLS/proxy, forwarded headers, cookie/session, security headers

Operational boundary:
  logs, metrics, probes, alerts, runbook
```

Model ideal:

```text id="hr9q74"
Start:
  app initializes
  config validated
  DB pool ready
  Jersey ready
  readiness = true

Run:
  traffic accepted
  limits enforced
  timeout enforced
  metrics/logs emitted

Overload:
  reject early
  degrade safely
  avoid total thread starvation

Shutdown:
  readiness = false
  stop accepting new traffic
  finish in-flight request
  close executors/DB/HTTP clients
  exit before Kubernetes SIGKILL
```

---

# 2. Diagram Production Lifecycle

```text id="rdn2e1"
┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ STARTUP                                                       │
│                                                               │
│ JVM starts                                                    │
│ Servlet Container starts                                      │
│ web.xml/listeners loaded                                      │
│ config validated                                              │
│ DB pool initialized                                           │
│ Jersey ResourceConfig initialized                             │
│ health endpoints available                                    │
│ readiness becomes true                                        │
└──────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────┘
                               │
                               ▼
┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ SERVING TRAFFIC                                                │
│                                                               │
│ Gateway / Ingress                                              │
│   → Servlet connector                                          │
│   → request thread pool                                        │
│   → filter chain                                               │
│   → Jersey                                                     │
│   → service / DB / Camunda / downstream                        │
│                                                               │
│ Guardrails:                                                    │
│   timeout, header/body limits, concurrency limits,             │
│   access log, metrics, error contract                          │
└──────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────┘
                               │
                               ▼
┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ TERMINATION                                                    │
│                                                               │
│ Kubernetes marks Pod terminating                               │
│ readiness should become false                                  │
│ Endpoint removed / traffic drained                             │
│ SIGTERM sent to process                                        │
│ container shuts down                                           │
│ in-flight request completes or times out                       │
│ resources closed                                               │
│ process exits before grace period                              │
└───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

Kubernetes Pod termination is designed to give processes a grace period before forceful termination; the kubelet typically sends `TERM` first, then `KILL` after the grace period expires. The default grace period is commonly 30 seconds if not specified. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/workloads/pods/pod-lifecycle/))

---

# 3. Decomposition Map

| No | Area | Fungsi praktis | Yang harus dikuasai | Failure mode umum |
|---:|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Startup probe | Memberi waktu app selesai init | Bedakan startup vs readiness vs liveness | App direstart sebelum selesai startup |
| 2 | Readiness probe | Kontrol apakah Pod menerima traffic | DB/config/Jersey readiness | Traffic masuk saat app belum siap |
| 3 | Liveness probe | Restart container yang stuck | Deadlock/hang detection | Restart palsu saat dependency lambat |
| 4 | Graceful shutdown | Stop tanpa memutus request sembarangan | SIGTERM, readiness false, drain, cleanup | Rolling update memutus transaksi |
| 5 | PreStop hook | Delay/drain sebelum SIGTERM/selama termination flow | Sleep kecil atau shutdown endpoint jika perlu | Hook lebih lama dari grace period |
| 6 | Termination grace | Batas waktu shutdown | `terminationGracePeriodSeconds` | SIGKILL sebelum cleanup selesai |
| 7 | Thread limit | Batasi request concurrency | `maxThreads`, executor, queue | Thread starvation |
| 8 | Connection limit | Batasi koneksi masuk/idle | `maxConnections`, keep-alive | Slow/idle connection makan resource |
| 9 | Timeout | Cegah request menggantung | gateway/app/DB/downstream timeout chain | Gateway timeout duluan |
| 10 | Header/body limit | Cegah abuse payload | max header, max body, multipart config | OOM/413 tidak jelas |
| 11 | Multipart limit | File upload aman | `maxFileSize`, `maxRequestSize`, temp dir | File besar masuk memory/disk penuh |
| 12 | Error hardening | Response aman dan konsisten | Jersey mapper + Servlet error handler | Stack trace/HTML bocor |
| 13 | Security headers | Browser/client hardening | nosniff, cache-control, HSTS jika tepat | Data sensitif cache |
| 14 | Proxy hardening | Skema/host/IP benar | trusted forwarded headers | IP spoofing/callback URL salah |
| 15 | Observability | Debuggability saat incident | access log, metrics, thread dump | Incident ad-hoc |
| 16 | Safe defaults | Default production baseline | checklist config | Bergantung default container yang belum diaudit |

---

# 4. Kubernetes Probes: Startup, Readiness, Liveness

## 4.1 Startup probe

Startup probe cocok jika aplikasi butuh waktu lama untuk:

```text id="un1wch"
load config
initialize Jersey/resources
warm up cache
connect DB
load rule engine
initialize Camunda client
run lightweight validation
```

Selama startup probe belum sukses, Kubernetes tidak menjalankan liveness/readiness probe. Kubernetes documentation menjelaskan bahwa startup probe memberi aplikasi waktu untuk menyelesaikan initialization; jika startup probe gagal terus sampai threshold, container akan dibunuh sesuai restart policy. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/tasks/configure-pod-container/configure-liveness-readiness-startup-probes/))

Contoh:

```yaml id="zfhnpo"
startupProbe:
  httpGet:
    path: /api/v1/health/startup
    port: http
  periodSeconds: 10
  failureThreshold: 30
  timeoutSeconds: 2
```

Makna:

```text id="cushfw"
Maksimum waktu startup = 30 * 10s = 300s.
```

## 4.2 Readiness probe

Readiness menjawab:

```text id="ks3vqc"
Apakah service ini boleh menerima traffic sekarang?
```

Jika readiness gagal, Kubernetes akan menandai Pod tidak ready dan Pod tidak menerima traffic melalui Service; dalam Pod lifecycle docs, ketika readiness probe gagal, EndpointSlice controller menghapus IP Pod dari EndpointSlices Service yang match. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/workloads/pods/pod-lifecycle/))

Contoh:

```yaml id="kyyhzo"
readinessProbe:
  httpGet:
    path: /api/v1/health/readiness
    port: http
  periodSeconds: 5
  failureThreshold: 2
  timeoutSeconds: 2
```

Readiness boleh gagal saat:

```text id="6vkyd8"
DB pool belum siap
config critical belum tersedia
service sedang draining shutdown
dependency critical unavailable, jika tanpa dependency itu service memang tidak bisa melayani
thread pool/executor overload berat
```

Tapi hati-hati: jangan membuat readiness terlalu sensitif terhadap dependency minor. Kalau readiness gagal karena dependency optional, seluruh Pod bisa dikeluarkan dari traffic walaupun sebagian endpoint masih bisa melayani.

## 4.3 Liveness probe

Liveness menjawab:

```text id="fex7tp"
Apakah container ini stuck dan perlu direstart?
```

Liveness sebaiknya **tidak** mengecek dependency eksternal seperti DB/downstream secara ketat. Jika DB lambat lalu liveness gagal, Kubernetes akan merestart semua Pod, memperparah outage.

Contoh:

```yaml id="6x1mwd"
livenessProbe:
  httpGet:
    path: /api/v1/health/liveness
    port: http
  periodSeconds: 10
  failureThreshold: 3
  timeoutSeconds: 2
```

Liveness idealnya mengecek:

```text id="owrsll"
JVM process hidup
HTTP server masih bisa merespons
event loop/request handling tidak deadlock total
internal fatal flag tidak aktif
```

---

# 5. Health Endpoint Design

Buat tiga endpoint berbeda:

```text id="u2mnzk"
GET /api/v1/health/startup
GET /api/v1/health/readiness
GET /api/v1/health/liveness
```

## 5.1 Resource

```java id="8zrhfq"
package com.company.caseapp.resource;

import jakarta.ws.rs.GET;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Path;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;

@Path("/v1/health")
public class HealthResource {

    private final HealthService healthService;

    public HealthResource(HealthService healthService) {
        this.healthService = healthService;
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/startup")
    public Response startup() {
        if (healthService.isStartupComplete()) {
            return Response.ok(new HealthResponse("UP")).build();
        }

        return Response.status(Response.Status.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE)
                .entity(new HealthResponse("STARTING"))
                .build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/readiness")
    public Response readiness() {
        if (healthService.isReady()) {
            return Response.ok(new HealthResponse("READY")).build();
        }

        return Response.status(Response.Status.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE)
                .entity(new HealthResponse("NOT_READY"))
                .build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/liveness")
    public Response liveness() {
        if (healthService.isLive()) {
            return Response.ok(new HealthResponse("UP")).build();
        }

        return Response.status(Response.Status.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE)
                .entity(new HealthResponse("DOWN"))
                .build();
    }

    public record HealthResponse(String status) {
    }
}
```

## 5.2 Health service

```java id="llv67w"
package com.company.caseapp.health;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean;

public class HealthService {

    private final AtomicBoolean startupComplete = new AtomicBoolean(false);
    private final AtomicBoolean draining = new AtomicBoolean(false);
    private final DbHealthCheck dbHealthCheck;

    public HealthService(DbHealthCheck dbHealthCheck) {
        this.dbHealthCheck = dbHealthCheck;
    }

    public boolean isStartupComplete() {
        return startupComplete.get();
    }

    public void markStartupComplete() {
        startupComplete.set(true);
    }

    public void startDraining() {
        draining.set(true);
    }

    public boolean isReady() {
        if (!startupComplete.get()) {
            return false;
        }

        if (draining.get()) {
            return false;
        }

        return dbHealthCheck.isHealthy(Duration.ofMillis(500));
    }

    public boolean isLive() {
        return true;
    }
}
```

Practical rule:

```text id="b9z07c"
startup:
  "aplikasi sudah selesai boot?"

readiness:
  "boleh menerima traffic sekarang?"

liveness:
  "perlu direstart atau tidak?"
```

---

# 6. Readiness Jangan Terlalu Mahal

Readiness berjalan periodik sepanjang lifecycle container. Kubernetes docs juga memperingatkan bahwa implementasi readiness probe yang salah dapat menyebabkan proses bertambah terus dan resource starvation jika tidak dikontrol. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/workloads/pods/probes/))

Anti-pattern:

```java id="d48jy2"
public boolean isReady() {
    runFullDatabaseMigrationCheck();
    callFiveDownstreamServices();
    runExpensiveQuery();
    validateExternalSftp();
    return true;
}
```

Lebih baik:

```text id="3njhl7"
DB ping ringan dengan timeout kecil.
Cek internal fatal/draining flag.
Cek config critical sudah loaded.
Cek dependency critical saja.
Cache hasil health check singkat jika perlu.
```

Contoh DB health dengan timeout kecil:

```java id="hp7a0u"
public class DbHealthCheck {

    private final javax.sql.DataSource dataSource;

    public DbHealthCheck(javax.sql.DataSource dataSource) {
        this.dataSource = dataSource;
    }

    public boolean isHealthy(Duration timeout) {
        long deadlineNanos = System.nanoTime() + timeout.toNanos();

        try (var connection = dataSource.getConnection();
             var statement = connection.createStatement()) {

            int remainingMs = (int) Math.max(
                    1,
                    (deadlineNanos - System.nanoTime()) / 1_000_000
            );

            statement.setQueryTimeout(Math.max(1, remainingMs / 1000));
            try (var rs = statement.executeQuery("SELECT 1")) {
                return rs.next();
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            return false;
        }
    }
}
```

Catatan: `Statement#setQueryTimeout` memakai detik, sehingga timeout sub-detik tidak selalu presisi. Untuk PostgreSQL, pertimbangkan `statement_timeout` di level connection/session atau pool config.

---

# 7. Graceful Shutdown

## 7.1 Apa yang harus terjadi saat shutdown?

Saat rolling update atau scale down:

```text id="e5gj4p"
1. Pod mulai terminating.
2. Pod harus berhenti menerima traffic baru.
3. In-flight request diberi kesempatan selesai.
4. Background worker/executor berhenti menerima task baru.
5. DB pool/HTTP client/Camunda client ditutup.
6. Process exit sebelum termination grace habis.
```

Kubernetes termination flow melibatkan grace period, preStop hook jika ada, pengiriman TERM ke process, dan akhirnya SIGKILL jika grace period habis. Terminating Pods juga tidak seharusnya menerima traffic reguler; endpoint terminating memiliki ready status false untuk kompatibilitas. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/workloads/pods/pod-lifecycle/))

## 7.2 Listener shutdown

```java id="8ecjkx"
package com.company.caseapp.lifecycle;

import jakarta.servlet.ServletContextEvent;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletContextListener;

public class AppLifecycleListener implements ServletContextListener {

    @Override
    public void contextInitialized(ServletContextEvent event) {
        AppRuntime.start();
        event.getServletContext().log("Application runtime started");
    }

    @Override
    public void contextDestroyed(ServletContextEvent event) {
        event.getServletContext().log("Application runtime stopping");
        AppRuntime.stop();
        event.getServletContext().log("Application runtime stopped");
    }
}
```

Runtime:

```java id="h0ijcf"
package com.company.caseapp.lifecycle;

import com.company.caseapp.health.HealthRegistry;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;

public final class AppRuntime {

    private static volatile ExecutorService workerExecutor;
    private static volatile AutoCloseable dbPool;
    private static volatile AutoCloseable camundaClient;
    private static volatile AutoCloseable externalHttpClient;

    private AppRuntime() {
    }

    public static void start() {
        // initialize resources
        HealthRegistry.markStartupComplete();
    }

    public static void stop() {
        HealthRegistry.markDraining();

        shutdownExecutor(workerExecutor, Duration.ofSeconds(10));

        closeQuietly(camundaClient);
        closeQuietly(externalHttpClient);
        closeQuietly(dbPool);
    }

    private static void shutdownExecutor(
            ExecutorService executor,
            Duration timeout
    ) {
        if (executor == null) {
            return;
        }

        executor.shutdown();

        try {
            boolean completed = executor.awaitTermination(
                    timeout.toMillis(),
                    TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS
            );

            if (!completed) {
                executor.shutdownNow();
            }
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            executor.shutdownNow();
        }
    }

    private static void closeQuietly(AutoCloseable closeable) {
        if (closeable == null) {
            return;
        }

        try {
            closeable.close();
        } catch (Exception ignored) {
            // log in real implementation
        }
    }
}
```

## 7.3 Draining flag

```java id="ebt7py"
public final class HealthRegistry {

    private static final java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean STARTUP_COMPLETE =
            new java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean(false);

    private static final java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean DRAINING =
            new java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean(false);

    private HealthRegistry() {
    }

    public static void markStartupComplete() {
        STARTUP_COMPLETE.set(true);
    }

    public static void markDraining() {
        DRAINING.set(true);
    }

    public static boolean isStartupComplete() {
        return STARTUP_COMPLETE.get();
    }

    public static boolean isDraining() {
        return DRAINING.get();
    }
}
```

Readiness:

```java id="zihz6j"
public boolean isReady() {
    return HealthRegistry.isStartupComplete()
            && !HealthRegistry.isDraining()
            && dbHealthCheck.isHealthy(Duration.ofMillis(500));
}
```

---

# 8. Kubernetes Deployment Template

Contoh baseline:

```yaml id="ud5o75"
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: case-service
spec:
  replicas: 3
  strategy:
    type: RollingUpdate
    rollingUpdate:
      maxUnavailable: 0
      maxSurge: 1
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app: case-service
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app: case-service
    spec:
      terminationGracePeriodSeconds: 45
      containers:
        - name: case-service
          image: registry.company.com/case-service:1.0.0
          ports:
            - name: http
              containerPort: 8080

          startupProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /api/v1/health/startup
              port: http
            periodSeconds: 10
            failureThreshold: 30
            timeoutSeconds: 2

          readinessProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /api/v1/health/readiness
              port: http
            periodSeconds: 5
            failureThreshold: 2
            timeoutSeconds: 2

          livenessProbe:
            httpGet:
              path: /api/v1/health/liveness
              port: http
            periodSeconds: 10
            failureThreshold: 3
            timeoutSeconds: 2

          lifecycle:
            preStop:
              exec:
                command: ["/bin/sh", "-c", "sleep 10"]

          resources:
            requests:
              cpu: "500m"
              memory: "512Mi"
            limits:
              cpu: "2"
              memory: "1Gi"

          env:
            - name: JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS
              value: "-XX:MaxRAMPercentage=75.0"
```

Catatan praktis:

```text id="uxqgje"
preStop sleep kecil bisa membantu memberi waktu endpoint/service controller/load balancer berhenti mengirim traffic.
Namun jangan mengandalkan sleep saja; readiness dan graceful shutdown app tetap harus benar.
```

Jika `preStop` perlu lebih lama daripada default, `terminationGracePeriodSeconds` harus disesuaikan. Kubernetes docs mencatat bahwa jika `preStop` masih berjalan setelah grace period habis, kubelet dapat meminta extension kecil, tetapi secara umum grace period harus diset sesuai kebutuhan workload. ([Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/concepts/workloads/pods/pod-lifecycle/))

---

# 9. Servlet Container Connector Hardening

Untuk Tomcat-style connector, parameter penting:

```xml id="8aruv8"
<Connector
    port="8080"
    protocol="HTTP/1.1"
    connectionTimeout="10000"
    keepAliveTimeout="15000"
    maxThreads="200"
    acceptCount="100"
    maxConnections="8192"
    maxHttpRequestHeaderSize="16384"
    maxPostSize="2097152"
    compression="on"
    compressionMinSize="2048"
    compressibleMimeType="text/plain,text/html,text/xml,text/css,application/json,application/javascript" />
```

Tomcat HTTP Connector adalah komponen yang listen pada port tertentu dan meneruskan request ke Engine untuk diproses. Tomcat docs juga menekankan bahwa setiap incoming **non-asynchronous request** membutuhkan thread selama request itu diproses; jika request simultan melebihi thread yang tersedia, Tomcat membuat thread sampai `maxThreads`, lalu menerima koneksi sampai `maxConnections`. ([Apache Tomcat](https://tomcat.apache.org/tomcat-9.0-doc/config/http.html))

## 9.1 Parameter praktis

| Setting | Tujuan | Catatan |
|---|---|---|
| `maxThreads` | Batas thread request aktif | Selaraskan dengan DB pool/downstream |
| `acceptCount` | Backlog saat thread penuh | Terlalu besar = latency queue panjang |
| `maxConnections` | Batas koneksi | Lindungi resource container |
| `connectionTimeout` | Timeout koneksi/request awal | Lindungi slow/idle connection |
| `keepAliveTimeout` | Timeout koneksi keep-alive idle | Jangan terlalu tinggi tanpa alasan |
| `maxHttpRequestHeaderSize` | Batas header request | Lindungi dari header abuse |
| `maxPostSize` | Batas POST body tertentu di container | Tidak selalu cukup untuk semua body/multipart; tetap butuh app/gateway limit |
| `compression` | Compress response tertentu | Pastikan tidak double dengan gateway |

Prinsip:

```text id="xl6vz2"
Limit harus ada di beberapa layer:
gateway/ingress
servlet container
application filter/resource
business validation
```

---

# 10. Request Size, Header Size, Multipart Limit

## 10.1 Header size

Header terlalu besar bisa berasal dari:

```text id="9g34fl"
JWT terlalu besar
cookie terlalu banyak
client abuse
proxy chain header panjang
trace baggage berlebihan
```

Guardrail:

```text id="x0tm9l"
gateway header limit
container max header size
application reject untuk header yang tidak perlu
```

## 10.2 Body size

Untuk JSON API:

```text id="v6f8pj"
gateway max body size
container request size config
filter Content-Length guard
Jersey/provider parsing guard
DTO validation
```

Filter:

```java id="6vs0lz"
public class RequestSizeLimitFilter implements Filter {

    private static final long MAX_JSON_BODY_BYTES = 2 * 1024 * 1024;

    @Override
    public void doFilter(
            ServletRequest request,
            ServletResponse response,
            FilterChain chain
    ) throws IOException, ServletException {

        HttpServletRequest httpRequest = (HttpServletRequest) request;
        HttpServletResponse httpResponse = (HttpServletResponse) response;

        long contentLength = httpRequest.getContentLengthLong();

        if (contentLength > MAX_JSON_BODY_BYTES) {
            httpResponse.setStatus(HttpServletResponse.SC_REQUEST_ENTITY_TOO_LARGE);
            httpResponse.setContentType("application/json");
            httpResponse.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
            httpResponse.getWriter().write("""
                    {"code":"PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE","message":"Request body is too large"}
                    """);
            return;
        }

        chain.doFilter(request, response);
    }
}
```

## 10.3 Multipart

Servlet `@MultipartConfig` mendukung `fileSizeThreshold`, `location`, `maxFileSize`, dan `maxRequestSize`; Tomcat 11 Servlet 6.1 API docs menyebut `maxFileSize` sebagai maksimum ukuran upload file dan `maxRequestSize` sebagai maksimum ukuran request `multipart/form-data`. Default `-1L` berarti unlimited. ([Apache Tomcat](https://tomcat.apache.org/tomcat-11.0-doc/servletapi/jakarta/servlet/annotation/MultipartConfig.html))

Contoh:

```java id="3bckh6"
@WebServlet("/upload")
@MultipartConfig(
        location = "/tmp/case-service-upload",
        fileSizeThreshold = 1024 * 1024,
        maxFileSize = 10L * 1024 * 1024,
        maxRequestSize = 12L * 1024 * 1024
)
public class UploadServlet extends HttpServlet {
}
```

Untuk Jersey multipart, gunakan konfigurasi/library multipart yang sesuai, tapi prinsipnya sama:

```text id="4i77sh"
max file size
max request size
temp directory
streaming
malware scan, jika diperlukan
async post-processing
cleanup temp file
```

---

# 11. Timeout Chain Hardening

Production timeout harus dibuat sebagai budget berlapis.

Contoh:

```text id="txvwlz"
Client timeout:            15s
API Gateway timeout:       12s
Ingress upstream timeout:  11s
Application target:        10s
DB query timeout:           3s
Downstream HTTP timeout:    2s
Executor queue timeout:     1s
```

Anti-pattern:

```text id="td5qko"
Gateway timeout 10s
App request bisa jalan 60s
DB query tanpa timeout
HTTP downstream tanpa timeout
```

Dampaknya:

```text id="6okzxu"
client sudah timeout
gateway sudah tutup koneksi
app thread masih kerja
DB/downstream masih terbebani
retry dari client/gateway membuat load makin tinggi
```

Rule:

```text id="dlhc9q"
Inner timeout harus lebih pendek dari caller timeout.
Total retry + backoff harus masuk budget.
Long-running workflow jangan dipaksa sync HTTP.
```

---

# 12. Concurrency Limit / Backpressure

Jika semua request diterima, service bisa collapse.

Gunakan guard:

```java id="j4qkew"
public class ConcurrencyLimitFilter implements Filter {

    private final Semaphore semaphore = new Semaphore(100);

    @Override
    public void doFilter(
            ServletRequest request,
            ServletResponse response,
            FilterChain chain
    ) throws IOException, ServletException {

        HttpServletResponse httpResponse = (HttpServletResponse) response;

        if (!semaphore.tryAcquire()) {
            httpResponse.setStatus(HttpServletResponse.SC_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE);
            httpResponse.setHeader("Retry-After", "5");
            httpResponse.setContentType("application/json");
            httpResponse.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
            httpResponse.getWriter().write("""
                    {"code":"OVERLOADED","message":"Service is temporarily overloaded"}
                    """);
            return;
        }

        try {
            chain.doFilter(request, response);
        } finally {
            semaphore.release();
        }
    }
}
```

Better production design:

```text id="1wyac7"
global concurrency limit
endpoint-specific limit
downstream-specific bulkhead
queue limit
timeout
metrics for rejection
clear 429/503 semantics
```

Status code choice:

```text id="fzvgnq"
429:
  rate limit / client-specific throttling

503:
  service overloaded / unavailable

Retry-After:
  useful if client should retry later
```

---

# 13. Error Response Hardening

Production API harus menghindari:

```text id="y5vccg"
HTML default error page
stack trace ke client
SQL error detail
container version banner
internal path/classname bocor
```

Jersey mapper:

```java id="zpyg63"
@Provider
public class GlobalExceptionMapper implements ExceptionMapper<Throwable> {

    @Override
    public Response toResponse(Throwable exception) {
        String correlationId = RequestContext.currentOrNull() == null
                ? ""
                : RequestContext.currentOrNull().correlationId();

        return Response.status(Response.Status.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR)
                .type(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON_TYPE)
                .entity(new ErrorResponse(
                        "INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR",
                        "Unexpected server error",
                        correlationId
                ))
                .build();
    }

    public record ErrorResponse(
            String code,
            String message,
            String correlationId
    ) {
    }
}
```

Servlet error handler:

```java id="3ioqqd"
public class JsonErrorServlet extends HttpServlet {

    @Override
    protected void service(
            HttpServletRequest request,
            HttpServletResponse response
    ) throws IOException {

        int status = extractStatus(request);

        response.setStatus(status);
        response.setContentType("application/json");
        response.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");

        response.getWriter().write("""
                {"code":"%s","message":"%s","correlationId":"%s"}
                """.formatted(
                codeFor(status),
                messageFor(status),
                correlationId(request)
        ));
    }

    private int extractStatus(HttpServletRequest request) {
        Object value = request.getAttribute(RequestDispatcher.ERROR_STATUS_CODE);
        return value instanceof Integer status
                ? status
                : HttpServletResponse.SC_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR;
    }

    private String correlationId(HttpServletRequest request) {
        Object value = request.getAttribute("correlationId");
        return value == null ? "" : String.valueOf(value);
    }

    private String codeFor(int status) {
        return switch (status) {
            case 400 -> "BAD_REQUEST";
            case 401 -> "UNAUTHORIZED";
            case 403 -> "FORBIDDEN";
            case 404 -> "NOT_FOUND";
            case 413 -> "PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE";
            case 415 -> "UNSUPPORTED_MEDIA_TYPE";
            case 429 -> "TOO_MANY_REQUESTS";
            case 503 -> "SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE";
            default -> status >= 500 ? "INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR" : "HTTP_ERROR";
        };
    }

    private String messageFor(int status) {
        return switch (status) {
            case 404 -> "Resource not found";
            case 413 -> "Request body is too large";
            case 415 -> "Unsupported media type";
            case 429 -> "Too many requests";
            case 503 -> "Service temporarily unavailable";
            default -> status >= 500
                    ? "Unexpected server error"
                    : "Request could not be processed";
        };
    }
}
```

---

# 14. Security Headers Safe Defaults

Filter:

```java id="o3xbdv"
public class ProductionSecurityHeadersFilter implements Filter {

    @Override
    public void doFilter(
            ServletRequest request,
            ServletResponse response,
            FilterChain chain
    ) throws IOException, ServletException {

        HttpServletResponse httpResponse = (HttpServletResponse) response;

        httpResponse.setHeader("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff");
        httpResponse.setHeader("X-Frame-Options", "DENY");
        httpResponse.setHeader("Referrer-Policy", "no-referrer");
        httpResponse.setHeader("Cache-Control", "no-store");

        chain.doFilter(request, response);
    }
}
```

Untuk browser-facing HTTPS app, HSTS bisa dipertimbangkan:

```http id="yacdxj"
Strict-Transport-Security: max-age=31536000; includeSubDomains
```

Tapi jangan aktifkan `includeSubDomains` tanpa memastikan seluruh subdomain memang siap HTTPS.

Default untuk regulatory/case API:

```text id="c4pkcu"
Cache-Control: no-store
X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff
X-Frame-Options: DENY
Referrer-Policy: no-referrer
```

---

# 15. Forwarded Header / Proxy Safe Defaults

Production di balik gateway/ingress harus jelas:

```text id="1hf32v"
TLS terminate di mana?
Apakah app melihat HTTP atau HTTPS?
Apakah X-Forwarded-Proto diproses?
Apakah client IP sudah dinormalisasi?
Apakah Host publik diketahui?
Apakah app membuat absolute URL?
```

Safe rule:

```text id="8netj6"
Jangan percaya X-Forwarded-* mentah dari client.
Gateway harus overwrite header.
Container/app hanya trust proxy internal.
Untuk callback eksternal, gunakan PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL dari config.
```

Diagnostic:

```java id="upz3db"
public class ProxyDebugFilter implements Filter {

    @Override
    public void doFilter(
            ServletRequest request,
            ServletResponse response,
            FilterChain chain
    ) throws IOException, ServletException {

        HttpServletRequest r = (HttpServletRequest) request;

        System.out.printf(
                "proxy_debug scheme=%s secure=%s serverName=%s serverPort=%d remoteAddr=%s host=%s xfp=%s xfh=%s xff=%s%n",
                r.getScheme(),
                r.isSecure(),
                r.getServerName(),
                r.getServerPort(),
                r.getRemoteAddr(),
                r.getHeader("Host"),
                r.getHeader("X-Forwarded-Proto"),
                r.getHeader("X-Forwarded-Host"),
                r.getHeader("X-Forwarded-For")
        );

        chain.doFilter(request, response);
    }
}
```

Gunakan sementara, jangan permanen dengan full metadata di production.

---

# 16. Session Hardening

Untuk stateless API:

```text id="zgen16"
Jangan membuat HttpSession.
Jangan mengeluarkan JSESSIONID.
Jangan menyimpan user/tenant di session.
```

Audit filter:

```java id="zjd62a"
public class SessionCreationAuditFilter implements Filter {

    @Override
    public void doFilter(
            ServletRequest request,
            ServletResponse response,
            FilterChain chain
    ) throws IOException, ServletException {

        HttpServletRequest r = (HttpServletRequest) request;

        boolean before = r.getSession(false) != null;

        chain.doFilter(request, response);

        boolean after = r.getSession(false) != null;

        if (!before && after) {
            System.out.printf(
                    "session_created method=%s uri=%s%n",
                    r.getMethod(),
                    r.getRequestURI()
            );
        }
    }
}
```

Untuk app yang memang memakai session:

```xml id="d54p4d"
<session-config>
    <session-timeout>30</session-timeout>
    <cookie-config>
        <http-only>true</http-only>
        <secure>true</secure>
        <name>CASESESSIONID</name>
        <path>/case-service</path>
    </cookie-config>
</session-config>
```

# 17. Docker Runtime Hardening

Dockerfile baseline untuk WAR external Tomcat:

```dockerfile id="4l18es"
FROM tomcat:11-jdk17

RUN rm -rf /usr/local/tomcat/webapps/*

COPY target/case-service.war /usr/local/tomcat/webapps/ROOT.war

EXPOSE 8080

CMD ["catalina.sh", "run"]
```

Checklist:

```text id="k097s6"
[ ] Image memakai Java version sesuai.
[ ] Artifact yang dicopy benar.
[ ] Tidak copy secret ke image.
[ ] App log ke stdout/stderr.
[ ] Port benar.
[ ] Context path sesuai route contract.
[ ] JVM memory mengikuti container limit.
[ ] Image tag immutable.
[ ] Non-root user jika required policy.
```

JVM option minimal:

```text id="i4vi50"
JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS=-XX:MaxRAMPercentage=75.0 -XX:+ExitOnOutOfMemoryError
```

Catatan:

```text id="lut2a0"
ExitOnOutOfMemoryError membuat container mati saat OOM Java,
sehingga orchestrator bisa restart daripada proses hidup dalam kondisi rusak.
```

---

# 18. Safe Default Config Matrix

| Area | Default awal yang defensible | Catatan |
|---|---|---|
| Startup probe | max 3-5 menit | Sesuaikan startup realistis |
| Readiness | DB/config critical + draining flag | Jangan terlalu mahal |
| Liveness | process/app alive only | Jangan cek DB/downstream berat |
| Termination grace | 30-60s | Sesuaikan max request time |
| PreStop | 5-10s sleep/drain | Jangan melebihi grace |
| Max request body | 1-5MB JSON | Endpoint-specific |
| Multipart max file | sesuai business | Jangan unlimited |
| Header size | explicit container/gateway | Watch large JWT/cookies |
| Request timeout | explicit per dependency | No infinite wait |
| Thread max | sesuai DB/downstream | Jangan asal naik |
| DB pool | sesuai DB capacity | Monitor pending |
| Error response | JSON only | No stack trace |
| Session | disabled/unused for stateless | Audit JSESSIONID |
| Access log | enabled | Include duration/status |
| Correlation ID | required/generated | Propagate downstream |

---

# 19. Hardening `web.xml` Baseline

```xml id="s9u5ci"
<web-app xmlns="https://jakarta.ee/xml/ns/jakartaee"
         xmlns:xsi="http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance"
         xsi:schemaLocation="https://jakarta.ee/xml/ns/jakartaee
                             https://jakarta.ee/xml/ns/jakartaee/web-app_6_1.xsd"
         version="6.1">

    <display-name>case-service</display-name>

        <listener-class>com.company.caseapp.lifecycle.AppLifecycleListener</listener-class>
    </listener>

    <filter>
        <filter-name>correlationIdFilter</filter-name>

    </filter>

    </filter>

    </filter>

    <servlet>

    </error-page>

        <location>/internal/error</location>
    </error-page>
    <error-page>
        <error-code>413</error-code>
        <location>/internal/error</location>
    </error-page>
    <error-page>
        <error-code>415</error-code>
        <location>/internal/error</location>
    </error-page>
    <error-page>
        <error-code>500</error-code>
        <location>/internal/error</location>
    </error-page>
    <error-page>
        <exception-type>java.lang.Throwable</exception-type>
        <location>/internal/error</location>
    </error-page>

    <session-config>
        <session-timeout>30</session-timeout>
        <cookie-config>
            <http-only>true</http-only>
            <secure>true</secure>
        </cookie-config>
    </session-config>

    <servlet>
        <servlet-name>jersey</servlet-name>
        <servlet-class>org.glassfish.jersey.servlet.ServletContainer</servlet-class>
        <init-param>
            <param-name>jakarta.ws.rs.Application</param-name>
            <param-value>com.company.caseapp.config.CaseApplication</param-value>
        </init-param>
        <load-on-startup>1</load-on-startup>
    </servlet>

    <servlet-mapping>
        <servlet-name>jersey</servlet-name>
        <url-pattern>/api/*</url-pattern>
    </servlet-mapping>

</web-app>
```

# 20. Production Smoke Tests

## 20.1 Startup/readiness/liveness

```bash id="i7hdwk"
curl -i http://localhost:8080/api/v1/health/startup
curl -i http://localhost:8080/api/v1/health/readiness
curl -i http://localhost:8080/api/v1/health/liveness
```

Expected:

```text id="bmndp5"
startup: 200 after app initialized
readiness: 200 only when app can serve traffic
liveness: 200 unless app is fatally stuck
```

## 20.2 No session on stateless health

```bash id="hm1abu"
curl -i http://localhost:8080/api/v1/health/readiness | grep -i set-cookie
```

Expected for stateless API:

```text id="jtfj8r"
No Set-Cookie: JSESSIONID
```

## 20.3 Error JSON

```bash id="w1xwi8"
curl -i http://localhost:8080/api/v1/not-found
```

Expected:

```text id="oek1gl"
HTTP/1.1 404
Content-Type: application/json

{"code":"NOT_FOUND",...}
```

## 20.4 Payload limit

```bash id="1ntxxa"
python3 - <<'PY'
import requests
body = {"description": "x" * (3 * 1024 * 1024)}
r = requests.post(
    "http://localhost:8080/api/v1/cases",
    json=body,
    timeout=5,
)
print(r.status_code)
print(r.text[:200])
PY
```

Expected if limit 2MB:

```text id="k9gq5y"
413
```

## 20.5 Graceful shutdown simulation

```bash id="f145ps"
kubectl rollout restart deployment/case-service
kubectl get pods -w
```

Observe:

```text id="s9z4bq"
old pod terminating
readiness false
new pod ready before old pod gone
no spike in 5xx/timeout
no stuck termination beyond grace period
```
