# 21. Production Debug Playbook

## Case A - Pod restart terus

Cek:

```text id="q2m403"
[ ] Startup probe terlalu pendek?
[ ] Liveness probe mengecek DB/downstream?
[ ] App startup lebih lama dari failureThreshold * periodSeconds?
[ ] Config/secret missing?
[ ] OOMKilled?
[ ] Port/path probe salah?
```

Command:

```bash id="qptp2z"
kubectl describe pod <pod>
kubectl logs <pod>
kubectl logs <pod> --previous
kubectl get events --sort-by=.lastTimestamp
```

## Case B - Rolling update menyebabkan 5xx

Cek:

```text id="ol9m8g"
[ ] readiness turun saat shutdown?
[ ] preStop cukup?
[ ] terminationGracePeriodSeconds cukup?
[ ] maxUnavailable terlalu tinggi?
[ ] app close connector terlalu cepat?
[ ] in-flight request lebih lama dari grace?
```

## Case C - 413/431/400 dari gateway/container

Cek:

```text id="vapxxp"
[ ] body lebih besar dari gateway limit?
[ ] header/JWT/cookie terlalu besar?
[ ] container max header size?
[ ] multipart max request size?
[ ] client mengirim chunked tanpa Content-Length?
```

## Case D - CPU rendah tapi timeout

Cek:

```text id="x3npxw"
[ ] busy threads/max threads
[ ] DB pool pending
[ ] downstream HTTP latency
[ ] thread dump
[ ] retry storm
[ ] queue terlalu panjang
```

## Case E - App ready padahal DB down

Cek:

```text id="67pcgv"
[ ] readiness hanya return OK statis?
[ ] DB health check timeout terlalu lama/pendek?
[ ] exception health check ditelan?
[ ] readiness endpoint kena auth filter?
```

---

# 22. Code Review Checklist Final

## 22.1 Kubernetes

```text id="cigicw"
[ ] startupProbe ada jika startup bisa lama.
[ ] readinessProbe ada dan bukan liveness duplicate.
[ ] livenessProbe tidak mengecek dependency eksternal berat.
[ ] terminationGracePeriodSeconds sesuai max request/shutdown.
[ ] preStop ada jika diperlukan untuk draining.
[ ] rollingUpdate maxUnavailable/maxSurge sesuai availability target.
[ ] resources requests/limits diset.
[ ] health endpoint tidak butuh auth/session.
```

## 22.2 Servlet Container

```text id="8xd0s1"
[ ] maxThreads diketahui.
[ ] maxConnections diketahui.
[ ] acceptCount diketahui.
[ ] connectionTimeout/keepAliveTimeout diketahui.
[ ] header size limit diset.
[ ] body/request limit diset di gateway/container/app.
[ ] access log aktif.
[ ] forwarded headers diproses dengan trusted proxy.
```

## 22.3 Application lifecycle

```text id="m7fg50"
[ ] Startup validation fail fast.
[ ] Startup complete flag jelas.
[ ] Draining flag jelas.
[ ] Executor shutdown.
[ ] DB pool closed.
[ ] HTTP client closed jika perlu.
[ ] Camunda/client workers stopped.
[ ] Schedulers stopped.
[ ] ThreadLocal/MDC cleanup.
```

## 22.4 API hardening

```text id="9kdidg"
[ ] Error response JSON konsisten.
[ ] No stack trace to client.
[ ] Correlation ID di response error.
[ ] Content-Type guard.
[ ] Request size guard.
[ ] Multipart/file limit.
[ ] Security headers.
[ ] No raw body/token/cookie logging.
```

## 22.5 Dependency/runtime

```text id="o4wm2d"
[ ] WAR/JAR artifact inspected.
[ ] `javax.*` dan `jakarta.*` tidak campur.
[ ] Servlet API scope sesuai model deployment.
[ ] Jersey versions aligned.
[ ] Dockerfile copy artifact benar.
[ ] Context path final terdokumentasi.
[ ] Readiness path sesuai route final.
```

## 22.6 Observability

```text id="ay4u3z"
[ ] Access log ada.
[ ] App structured log punya correlationId.
[ ] HTTP p95/p99 latency metric.
[ ] Status code metric.
[ ] Busy thread metric.
[ ] DB pool metric.
[ ] Downstream latency/timeout metric.
[ ] Pod restart/probe failure alert.
[ ] Thread dump runbook.
```

---

# 23. Anti-Pattern Final

| Anti-pattern | Dampak | Alternatif |
|---|---|---|
| Readiness dan liveness endpoint sama persis | Restart palsu atau traffic salah | Pisahkan semantics |
| Liveness cek DB/downstream | Dependency outage jadi restart storm | Liveness hanya process/fatal health |
| Tidak ada startup probe untuk app lambat | App dibunuh saat masih init | Startup probe |
| `terminationGracePeriodSeconds` terlalu pendek | Request/cleanup terpotong | Sesuaikan max shutdown |
| Semua request diterima saat overload | Thread starvation | Concurrency limit/backpressure |
| Body/header unlimited | OOM/abuse | Limit gateway/container/app |
| Multipart default unlimited | Disk/memory habis | `maxFileSize`/`maxRequestSize` |
| Error default HTML | API contract rusak | Servlet JSON error handler |
| Callback URL dari internal request URL | URL salah di balik proxy | Public base URL config |
| Monitor CPU saja | IO wait/thread starvation terlewat | Busy threads, DB pool, downstream metrics |

---

# Ringkasan Seri 12

Production hardening untuk Servlet Container bukan hanya tuning satu angka. Ini adalah desain batas operasional:

```text id="m90mjj"
kapan service dianggap siap
kapan service harus menerima traffic
kapan service harus direstart
berapa lama request boleh berjalan
berapa besar request boleh masuk
berapa banyak request boleh diproses
bagaimana service berhenti
bagaimana error dikembalikan
bagaimana incident bisa didebug
```

Invariants akhir:

```text id="h4qhyc"
Startup probe untuk startup lambat.
Readiness untuk traffic eligibility.
Liveness untuk stuck/fatal process only.
Graceful shutdown harus menurunkan readiness dan menutup resource.
Timeout harus berlapis dan konsisten.
Thread/connection/body/header limits harus eksplisit.
Multipart tidak boleh unlimited.
Error API harus JSON dan aman.
Forwarded headers hanya dari trusted proxy.
Access log, metrics, correlation id, dan thread dump runbook wajib ada.
```

---

# Rekomendasi materi selanjutnya

Setelah Servlet Container, materi paling natural berikutnya adalah:

**“HTTP Client & Outbound Integration Hardening in Java Microservices”**

Alasannya: banyak risiko Servlet Container di production sebenarnya dipicu oleh outbound call yang buruk-HTTP client tanpa timeout, retry storm, tidak ada circuit breaker, tidak ada bulkhead, tidak propagate correlation id, dan tidak ada idempotency. Materi ini akan melengkapi seri Servlet Container dari sisi **request keluar**.

Alternatif rekomendasi lain yang juga relevan:

```text id="c3ngsh"
1. API Gateway / Ingress / Reverse Proxy Pattern untuk Java Microservice
2. PostgreSQL + MyBatis Production Hardening
3. Camunda Worker/Delegate Runtime Hardening
4. End-to-End Timeout, Retry, Circuit Breaker, Bulkhead Pattern
5. API Contract Hardening: Error Schema, Versioning, Idempotency, Pagination
```

**Seri 12/12 selesai. Seluruh seri Servlet Container selesai.**

---

**Sources:**

- [Configure Liveness, Readiness and Startup Probes | Kubernetes](https://kubernetes.io/docs/tasks/configure-pod-container/configure-liveness-readiness-startup-probes/)
- [Apache Tomcat 9 Configuration Reference (9.0.120) - The HTTP Connector](https://tomcat.apache.org/tomcat-9.0-doc/config/http.html)

## Response:
7/15/2026, 1:31:05 PM

Saya akan rangkum sebagai cheat sheet praktis, bukan daftar API mentah seluruh Servlet spec. Saya fokus ke yang paling sering dipakai saat onboarding/debugging Jersey/JAX-RS microservice: annotations, core classes/interfaces, method penting, `web.xml`, dan container properties.

# Cheat Sheet - Servlet Container: Annotations, Classes, Methods, Properties

Scope cheat sheet ini untuk **modern Jakarta Servlet**, terutama Servlet 6.x / Jakarta namespace `jakarta.servlet.*`. Untuk stack lama, nama package sering masih `javax.servlet.*`; jangan dicampur dengan `jakarta.*` dalam satu aplikasi karena bisa menyebabkan runtime mismatch.

Jakarta Servlet menyediakan kontrak antara class servlet/filter/listener dan runtime Servlet Container. Package utama adalah `jakarta.servlet`, `jakarta.servlet.http`, dan `jakarta.servlet.annotation`. API HTTP seperti `HttpServletRequest`, `HttpServletResponse`, `HttpSession`, `Cookie`, dan `Part` berada di `jakarta.servlet.http`. ([Apache Tomcat](https://tomcat.apache.org/tomcat-11.0-doc/servletapi/jakarta/servlet/package-summary.html))

---

# 1. Import Packages yang Paling Sering Dipakai

```java
import jakarta.servlet.Filter;
import jakarta.servlet.FilterChain;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletException;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletResponse;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletContext;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletContextListener;
import jakarta.servlet.ServletContextEvent;
import jakarta.servlet.DispatcherType;
import jakarta.servlet.AsyncContext;
import jakarta.servlet.RequestDispatcher;

import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServlet;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpSession;
import jakarta.servlet.http.Cookie;
import jakarta.servlet.http.Part;

import jakarta.servlet.annotation.WebServlet;
import jakarta.servlet.annotation.WebFilter;
import jakarta.servlet.annotation.WebListener;
import jakarta.servlet.annotation.WebInitParam;
import jakarta.servlet.annotation.MultipartConfig;
import jakarta.servlet.annotation.ServletSecurity;
```

---

# 2. Servlet Annotations Cheat Sheet

Package `jakarta.servlet.annotation` berisi annotation seperti `@WebServlet`, `@WebFilter`, `@WebListener`, `@MultipartConfig`, `@ServletSecurity`, `@HttpConstraint`, `@HttpMethodConstraint`, `@WebInitParam`, dan `@HandlesTypes`. Dokumentasi Servlet 6.1 menjelaskan annotation ini untuk deklarasi servlet, filter, listener, multipart, dan security constraint. ([Apache Tomcat](https://tomcat.apache.org/tomcat-11.0-doc/servletapi/jakarta/servlet/annotation/package-summary.html))

| Annotation | Target | Fungsi | Properti penting | Praktis di microservice |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `@WebServlet` | Class servlet | Mendaftarkan servlet tanpa `web.xml` | `name`, `value`, `urlPatterns`, `loadOnStartup`, `initParams`, `asyncSupported` | Cocok untuk servlet custom seperti health/debug/upload, tapi Jersey biasanya lebih sering via `web.xml` |
| `@WebFilter` | Class filter | Mendaftarkan servlet filter | `filterName`, `value`, `urlPatterns`, `servletNames`, `dispatcherTypes`, `initParams`, `asyncSupported` | Bisa untuk correlation id, auth pre-check, security header |
| `@WebListener` | Listener class | Mendaftarkan listener lifecycle/event | `value` | Startup/shutdown, session listener, request listener |
| `@WebInitParam` | Dalam `@WebServlet` / `@WebFilter` | Init parameter | `name`, `value`, `description` | Config kecil untuk servlet/filter |
| `@MultipartConfig` | Servlet class | Menandai servlet menerima `multipart/form-data` | `location`, `fileSizeThreshold`, `maxFileSize`, `maxRequestSize` | File upload, evidence upload, CSV import |
| `@ServletSecurity` | Servlet class | Declarative security untuk servlet | `value`, `httpMethodConstraints` | Jarang cukup untuk business auth; lebih cocok coarse security |
| `@HttpConstraint` | Dalam `@ServletSecurity` | Default security constraint | `value`, `rolesAllowed`, `transportGuarantee` | Default constraint semua HTTP method |
| `@HttpMethodConstraint` | Dalam `@ServletSecurity` | Constraint per method | `value`, `rolesAllowed`, `transportGuarantee` | Misalnya GET public, POST protected |
| `@HandlesTypes` | `ServletContainerInitializer` | Class discovery saat container startup | `value` | Dipakai framework/library, jarang dipakai langsung di app |

Contoh `@WebFilter`:

```java
@WebFilter(
        filterName = "correlationIdFilter",
        urlPatterns = "/api/*",
        dispatcherTypes = DispatcherType.REQUEST
)
public class CorrelationIdFilter implements Filter {
    // ...
}
```

Contoh `@WebServlet`:

```java
@WebServlet(
        name = "healthServlet",
        urlPatterns = "/internal/health",
        loadOnStartup = 1
)
public class HealthServlet extends HttpServlet {
    // ...
}
```

Contoh `@MultipartConfig`:

```java
@WebServlet("/upload")
@MultipartConfig(
        location = "/tmp/case-service-upload",
        fileSizeThreshold = 1024 * 1024,
        maxFileSize = 10L * 1024 * 1024,
        maxRequestSize = 12L * 1024 * 1024
)
public class UploadServlet extends HttpServlet {
}
```

---

# 3. Core Classes & Interfaces - `jakarta.servlet`

Package `jakarta.servlet` berisi kontrak umum Servlet Container, termasuk `Filter`, `FilterChain`, `ServletContext`, `AsyncContext`, `DispatcherType`, `ServletRegistration`, dan `FilterRegistration`. Dokumentasi package Servlet menyebut package ini mendeskripsikan kontrak antara class servlet dan runtime environment container. ([Apache Tomcat](https://tomcat.apache.org/tomcat-11.0-doc/servletapi/jakarta/servlet/package-summary.html))

| Class / Interface | Fungsi | Method penting | Catatan praktis |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Servlet` | Kontrak dasar servlet | `init`, `service`, `destroy`, `getServletConfig`, `getServletInfo` | Jarang implement langsung; biasanya extend `HttpServlet` |
| `GenericServlet` | Base servlet protocol-independent | `init`, `service`, `log`, `getServletContext` | Lebih umum pakai `HttpServlet` |
| `ServletRequest` | Request generic | `getAttribute`, `setAttribute`, `getInputStream`, `getReader`, `getParameter`, `startAsync`, `getDispatcherType` | Parent dari `HttpServletRequest` |
| `ServletResponse` | Response generic | `getWriter`, `getOutputStream`, `setContentType`, `setCharacterEncoding`, `setBufferSize`, `flushBuffer`, `isCommitted` | Parent dari `HttpServletResponse` |
| `Filter` | Boundary sebelum/sesudah resource | `init`, `doFilter`, `destroy` | Correlation ID, CORS, auth pre-check, request guard |
| `GenericFilter` | Base filter modern | `doFilter`, `getFilterConfig`, `getServletContext` | Mengurangi boilerplate filter |
| `FilterChain` | Chain filter berikutnya | `doFilter(request, response)` | Jangan lupa dipanggil kecuali short-circuit |
| `FilterConfig` | Config filter | `getFilterName`, `getInitParameter`, `getServletContext` | Baca init-param filter |
| `ServletConfig` | Config servlet | `getServletName`, `getInitParameter`, `getServletContext` | Baca init-param servlet |
| `ServletContext` | Context web app per JVM | `getContextPath`, `getAttribute`, `setAttribute`, `getInitParameter`, `getResourceAsStream`, `addServlet`, `addFilter`, `addListener`, `log` | Jangan simpan user/request data di sini |
| `ServletContextListener` | Startup/shutdown web app | `contextInitialized`, `contextDestroyed` | Init/close DB pool, executor, client |
| `ServletRequestListener` | Lifecycle request | `requestInitialized`, `requestDestroyed` | Jarang; filter biasanya lebih fleksibel |
| `ServletRequestAttributeListener` | Perubahan request attribute | `attributeAdded`, `attributeRemoved`, `attributeReplaced` | Audit/debug internal |
| `ServletContextAttributeListener` | Perubahan context attribute | `attributeAdded`, `attributeRemoved`, `attributeReplaced` | Jarang di microservice |
| `RequestDispatcher` | Forward/include server-side | `forward`, `include` | Bisa memicu dispatcher `FORWARD` / `INCLUDE` |
| `DispatcherType` | Enum dispatch | `REQUEST`, `FORWARD`, `INCLUDE`, `ERROR`, `ASYNC` | Penting untuk filter mapping |
| `AsyncContext` | Async servlet context | `start`, `dispatch`, `complete`, `setTimeout`, `addListener` | Gunakan hati-hati; wajib timeout/complete |
| `AsyncListener` | Event async | `onComplete`, `onTimeout`, `onError`, `onStartAsync` | Wajib untuk observability async |
| `ServletRegistration.Dynamic` | Programmatic servlet registration | `addMapping`, `setLoadOnStartup`, `setAsyncSupported`, `setInitParameter` | Embedded/container initializer |
| `FilterRegistration.Dynamic` | Programmatic filter registration | `addMappingForUrlPatterns`, `setAsyncSupported`, `setInitParameter` | Embedded/container initializer |
| `ServletContainerInitializer` | Startup hook framework | `onStartup` | Dipakai framework seperti Jersey/CDI integrations |
| `MultipartConfigElement` | Programmatic multipart config | constructor properties | Alternatif `@MultipartConfig` |
| `ServletException` | Exception servlet | constructors | Umum di servlet/filter |
| `UnavailableException` | Servlet unavailable | constructors | Jarang dipakai langsung |

---

# 4. Core Classes - `jakarta.servlet.http`

Package `jakarta.servlet.http` berisi API khusus HTTP: `HttpServlet`, `HttpFilter`, `HttpServletRequest`, `HttpServletResponse`, `HttpSession`, `Cookie`, `Part`, dan wrapper request/response. ([Apache Tomcat](https://tomcat.apache.org/tomcat-11.0-doc/servletapi/jakarta/servlet/http/package-summary.html))

| Class / Interface | Fungsi | Method penting | Catatan praktis |
|---|---|---|---|
| `HttpServlet` | Base servlet HTTP | `doGet`, `doPost`, `doPut`, `doDelete`, `doHead`, `doOptions`, `doTrace`, `service` | Untuk servlet custom; Jersey sendiri pakai servlet implementation |
| `HttpFilter` | Base filter HTTP | `doFilter(HttpServletRequest, HttpServletResponse, FilterChain)` | Lebih nyaman daripada cast manual |
| `HttpServletRequest` | HTTP request | Lihat section khusus | Container membuat object ini dan meneruskannya ke service methods seperti `doGet`/`doPost`. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/servlet/6.1/apidocs/jakarta.servlet/jakarta/servlet/http/httpservletrequest)) |
| `HttpServletResponse` | HTTP response | Lihat section khusus | Set status/header/body/cookie/redirect |
| `HttpServletRequestWrapper` | Wrapper request | override method request | Body caching, header override |
| `HttpServletResponseWrapper` | Wrapper response | override response method | Capture status/body |
| `HttpServletMapping` | Info mapping servlet | `getMatchValue`, `getPattern`, `getServletName`, `getMappingMatch` | Debug mapping |
| `MappingMatch` | Enum mapping type | `CONTEXT_ROOT`, `DEFAULT`, `EXACT`, `EXTENSION`, `PATH` | Debug servlet mapping |
| `HttpSession` | Server-side session | `getId`, `getAttribute`, `setAttribute`, `invalidate`, `setMaxInactiveInterval`, `isNew` | Hindari untuk stateless API |
| `Cookie` | HTTP cookie | `setSecure`, `setHttpOnly`, `setPath`, `setDomain`, `setMaxAge`, `setAttribute`, `getAttributes` | Servlet 6.0+ mendukung arbitrary cookie attributes seperti `SameSite` via `setAttribute`. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/servlet/6.1/apidocs/jakarta.servlet/jakarta/servlet/http/cookie)) |
| `Part` | Multipart part | `getInputStream`, `getSubmittedFileName`, `getSize`, `write`, `delete` | File upload |
| `HttpSessionListener` | Session lifecycle | `sessionCreated`, `sessionDestroyed` | Monitor session accidental |
| `HttpSessionAttributeListener` | Session attribute changes | `attributeAdded`, `attributeRemoved`, `attributeReplaced` | Audit/debug |
| `HttpSessionIdListener` | Session ID changed | `sessionIdChanged` | Session fixation mitigation/debug |
| `HttpSessionBindingListener` | Object bound/unbound session | `valueBound`, `valueUnbound` | Jarang |
| `HttpSessionActivationListener` | Session passivation/activation | `sessionWillPassivate`, `sessionDidActivate` | Cluster/session replication |
| `HttpUpgradeHandler` | HTTP protocol upgrade | `init`, `destroy` | WebSocket/custom protocol internals |
| `WebConnection` | Upgraded connection | `getInputStream`, `getOutputStream` | Dengan `HttpUpgradeHandler` |
| `PushBuilder` | HTTP/2 server push | deprecated | Dokumentasi Servlet 6.1 menandai `PushBuilder` deprecated in favor of 103 Early Hints. ([Apache Tomcat](https://tomcat.apache.org/tomcat-11.0-doc/servletapi/jakarta/servlet/http/package-summary.html)) |

---

# 5. `HttpServletRequest` - Method Penting

`HttpServletRequest` memperluas `ServletRequest` untuk informasi HTTP; container membuat object ini dan memberikannya ke service methods servlet. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/servlet/6.1/apidocs/jakarta.servlet/jakarta/servlet/http/httpservletrequest))

## 5.1 Path / Routing

| Method | Fungsi | Contoh |
|---|---|---|
| `getMethod()` | HTTP method | `GET`, `POST` |
| `getRequestURI()` | URI path yang diterima container | `/case-service/api/v1/cases/123` |
| `getRequestURL()` | Full URL menurut container | `http://internal:8080/...` |
| `getContextPath()` | Context path web app | `/case-service` |
| `getServletPath()` | Path yang match servlet mapping | `/api` |
| `getPathInfo()` | Sisa path setelah servlet path | `/v1/cases/123` |
| `getQueryString()` | Raw query string | `includeHistory=true` |
| `getHttpServletMapping()` | Detail servlet mapping | pattern, match type, servlet name |

Debug 404 minimal:

```java
System.out.printf(
        "method=%s uri=%s contextPath=%s servletPath=%s pathInfo=%s query=%s mapping=%s%n",
        request.getMethod(),
        request.getRequestURI(),
        request.getContextPath(),
        request.getServletPath(),
        request.getPathInfo(),
        request.getQueryString(),
        request.getHttpServletMapping()
);
```

---

## 5.2 Header / Metadata

| Method | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `getHeader(String)` | Ambil satu header |
| `getHeaders(String)` | Ambil semua value header |
| `getHeaderNames()` | Semua nama header |
| `getIntHeader(String)` | Header sebagai int |
| `getDateHeader(String)` | Header tanggal |
| `getContentType()` | Content-Type request |
| `getContentLength()` / `getContentLengthLong()` | Ukuran body jika diketahui |
| `getLocale()` / `getLocales()` | Locale dari request |

Header penting:

```text
Authorization
Cookie
Content-Type
Accept
Origin
Host
User-Agent
X-Correlation-Id
X-Forwarded-For
X-Forwarded-Proto
X-Forwarded-Host
X-Tenant-Id
```

Rule production:

```text
Jangan log Authorization, Cookie, token, raw query sensitif, atau raw body.
```

---

## 5.3 Body / Parameter

| Method | Fungsi | Risiko |
|---|---|---|
| `getInputStream()` | Baca body sebagai bytes | Jangan dibaca di filter tanpa wrapper |
| `getReader()` | Baca body sebagai character stream | Tidak boleh dipakai bersama `getInputStream()` |
| `getParameter(String)` | Query/form parameter | Bisa trigger parsing form body |
| `getParameterMap()` | Semua parameter | Hati-hati untuk body form |
| `getPart(String)` | Ambil multipart part | Butuh multipart config |
| `getParts()` | Semua multipart parts | Hati-hati file besar |

Prinsip:

```text
Untuk JSON body di Jersey, biarkan Jersey MessageBodyReader yang membaca stream.
Filter sebaiknya hanya baca metadata/header, bukan body.
```

---

## 5.4 Attribute / Context

| Method | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `getAttribute(String)` | Ambil request attribute |
| `setAttribute(String, Object)` | Set data internal per request |
| `removeAttribute(String)` | Hapus attribute |
| `getAttributeNames()` | Daftar attribute |

Contoh:

```java
request.setAttribute("correlationId", correlationId);
```

Gunakan request attribute untuk data per-request, bukan `ServletContext`.

---

## 5.5 Security / Identity

| Method | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `getUserPrincipal()` | Principal container-managed auth |
| `isUserInRole(String)` | Role check container-managed auth |
| `getAuthType()` | Tipe auth |
| `authenticate(HttpServletResponse)` | Trigger container auth |
| `login(username, password)` | Programmatic login |
| `logout()` | Logout container-managed |
| `isSecure()` | Apakah request dianggap secure |
| `getScheme()` | `http` / `https` menurut container |

Di balik proxy/TLS termination, `getScheme()` dan `isSecure()` bisa salah jika forwarded headers belum dinormalisasi.

---

## 5.6 Session

| Method | Fungsi | Catatan |
|---|---|---|
| `getSession()` | Ambil atau buat session | Berbahaya untuk stateless API |
| `getSession(false)` | Ambil session jika ada, tanpa membuat | Prefer untuk cek |
| `changeSessionId()` | Rotate session id | Penting setelah login |
| `getRequestedSessionId()` | Session id dari client | Debug |
| `isRequestedSessionIdValid()` | Valid/tidak | Debug |
| `isRequestedSessionIdFromCookie()` | Session dari cookie | Debug |

Rule:

```java
// cek session tanpa membuat session baru
HttpSession session = request.getSession(false);
```

---

## 5.7 Network / Proxy

| Method | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `getRemoteAddr()` | IP client menurut container |
| `getRemoteHost()` | Host client menurut container |
| `getRemotePort()` | Port client |
| `getLocalAddr()` | Local IP |
| `getLocalPort()` | Local port |
| `getServerName()` | Server name menurut request/container |
| `getServerPort()` | Server port |

Jika di balik gateway/ingress:

```text
getRemoteAddr() bisa berisi IP proxy, bukan IP client asli.
Gunakan trusted proxy normalization seperti RemoteIpValve atau equivalent.
```

---

## 5.8 Async / Dispatcher

| Method | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `startAsync()` | Mulai async processing |
| `startAsync(request, response)` | Mulai async dengan wrapped request/response |
| `isAsyncStarted()` | Async sedang berjalan |
| `isAsyncSupported()` | Apakah async didukung |
| `getAsyncContext()` | Ambil async context |
| `getDispatcherType()` | `REQUEST`, `ERROR`, `ASYNC`, dll |

---

# 6. `HttpServletResponse` - Method Penting

| Area | Method | Fungsi |
|---|---|---|
| Status | `setStatus(int)` | Set HTTP status |
| Error | `sendError(int)` / `sendError(int, String)` | Kirim error via container |
| Redirect | `sendRedirect(String)` | HTTP redirect |
| Header | `setHeader`, `addHeader`, `containsHeader` | Response header |
| Date/int header | `setDateHeader`, `addDateHeader`, `setIntHeader`, `addIntHeader` | Header typed |
| Cookie | `addCookie(Cookie)` | Set-Cookie |
| Content | `setContentType(String)` | Contoh `application/json` |
| Encoding | `setCharacterEncoding(String)` | Contoh `UTF-8` |
| Length | `setContentLength`, `setContentLengthLong` | Content-Length |
| Body char | `getWriter()` | Text response |
| Body bytes | `getOutputStream()` | Binary response |
| Buffer | `setBufferSize`, `getBufferSize`, `flushBuffer`, `resetBuffer`, `reset` | Commit/buffer control |
| Commit | `isCommitted()` | Apakah response sudah committed |
| Status read | `getStatus()` | Status current |
| Header read | `getHeader`, `getHeaders`, `getHeaderNames` | Inspect response |

Pattern aman:

```java
if (!response.isCommitted()) {
    response.setStatus(HttpServletResponse.SC_UNAUTHORIZED);
    response.setContentType("application/json");
    response.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
    response.getWriter().write("""
            {"code":"UNAUTHORIZED","message":"Missing Authorization header"}
            """);
}
```

Anti-pattern:

```java
response.getWriter().write("partial");
response.flushBuffer();
response.setStatus(500); // terlambat
```

---

# 7. `Filter` / `FilterChain` Cheat Sheet

| Method | Fungsi | Production rule |
|---|---|---|
| `init(FilterConfig)` | Init filter | Jangan call dependency berat tanpa timeout |
| `doFilter(ServletRequest, ServletResponse, FilterChain)` | Core filtering | `chain.doFilter` tepat sekali untuk normal path |
| `destroy()` | Cleanup | Release resource jika ada |
| `chain.doFilter(request, response)` | Lanjut ke filter berikutnya/resource | Tidak dipanggil hanya saat deliberate short-circuit |

Template filter aman:

```java
public class SafeFilter implements Filter {

    @Override
    public void doFilter(
            ServletRequest request,
            ServletResponse response,
            FilterChain chain
    ) throws IOException, ServletException {

        HttpServletRequest httpRequest = (HttpServletRequest) request;
        HttpServletResponse httpResponse = (HttpServletResponse) response;

        try {
            // before
            chain.doFilter(request, response);
        } finally {
            // cleanup ThreadLocal/MDC
        }
    }
}
```

Dispatcher mapping:

```xml
<filter-mapping>
    <filter-name>correlationIdFilter</filter-name>
    <url-pattern>/api/*</url-pattern>
    <dispatcher>REQUEST</dispatcher>
</filter-mapping>
```

Default praktis:

```text
Correlation/auth/access-log filter utama: REQUEST only.
ERROR/ASYNC hanya kalau memang didesain.
```

---

# 8. `ServletContext` Cheat Sheet

| Method | Fungsi | Catatan |
|---|---|---|
| `getContextPath()` | Context path app | Debug routing |
| `getInitParameter(String)` | Global init-param | Dari `web.xml` |
| `getInitParameterNames()` | Semua init-param | Audit config |
| `getAttribute`, `setAttribute`, `removeAttribute` | App-wide attribute | Jangan simpan user/request data |
| `getResource(String)` | Resource URL dalam webapp | Static/classpath-ish web resource |
| `getResourceAsStream(String)` | Stream resource | Config/resource default |
| `getRequestDispatcher(String)` | Forward/include | Internal server-side dispatch |
| `log(String)` | Log ke container | Startup/shutdown |
| `addServlet(...)` | Programmatic servlet registration | Embedded/initializer |
| `addFilter(...)` | Programmatic filter registration | Embedded/initializer |
| `addListener(...)` | Programmatic listener registration | Embedded/initializer |
| `getServletRegistration(String)` | Inspect servlet registration | Debug |
| `getFilterRegistration(String)` | Inspect filter registration | Debug |
| `getSessionCookieConfig()` | Configure session cookie | Name/path/httpOnly/secure |
| `setSessionTimeout(int)` | Set session timeout minutes | Servlet 4+ |
| `getClassLoader()` | Webapp classloader | Debug classloading |

Rule:

```text
ServletContext = app-wide per web application per JVM.
Request/user/tenant data jangan disimpan di ServletContext.
```

---

# 9. Listener Cheat Sheet

| Listener | Trigger | Method penting | Use case |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ServletContextListener` | App startup/shutdown | `contextInitialized`, `contextDestroyed` | Init/close resource |
| `ServletContextAttributeListener` | Context attribute berubah | `attributeAdded`, `attributeRemoved`, `attributeReplaced` | Debug/audit |
| `ServletRequestListener` | Request start/end | `requestInitialized`, `requestDestroyed` | Rare; filter biasanya lebih cocok |
| `ServletRequestAttributeListener` | Request attribute berubah | `attributeAdded`, `attributeRemoved`, `attributeReplaced` | Debug |
| `HttpSessionListener` | Session created/destroyed | `sessionCreated`, `sessionDestroyed` | Detect accidental sessions |
| `HttpSessionAttributeListener` | Session attribute berubah | `attributeAdded`, `attributeRemoved`, `attributeReplaced` | Audit |
| `HttpSessionIdListener` | Session id changed | `sessionIdChanged` | Login/session fixation |
| `HttpSessionBindingListener` | Object bound/unbound session | `valueBound`, `valueUnbound` | Object-level session lifecycle |
| `HttpSessionActivationListener` | Session passivate/activate | `sessionWillPassivate`, `sessionDidActivate` | Cluster/session replication |

Contoh listener startup/shutdown:

```java
@WebListener
public class AppLifecycleListener implements ServletContextListener {

    @Override
    public void contextInitialized(ServletContextEvent event) {
        event.getServletContext().log("App starting");
    }

    @Override
    public void contextDestroyed(ServletContextEvent event) {
        event.getServletContext().log("App stopping");
    }
}
```

---

# 10. Async Servlet Cheat Sheet

`AsyncContext` menyediakan context untuk asynchronous request handling, dan `AsyncListener` menerima event async seperti complete, timeout, error, dan start async. ([Apache Tomcat](https://tomcat.apache.org/tomcat-11.0-doc/servletapi/jakarta/servlet/package-summary.html))

| Class / Method | Fungsi | Rule |
|---|---|---|
| `request.startAsync()` | Mulai async | Jangan lupa timeout |
| `asyncContext.setTimeout(ms)` | Timeout async | Wajib |
| `asyncContext.start(Runnable)` | Jalankan async task | Worker tetap harus bounded |
| `asyncContext.dispatch(path)` | Dispatch ulang | Bisa masuk dispatcher `ASYNC` |
| `asyncContext.complete()` | Selesaikan response | Wajib di akhirnya |
| `asyncContext.addListener(...)` | Register listener | Observability timeout/error |
| `AsyncListener.onTimeout` | Timeout event | Return timeout response/cleanup |
| `AsyncListener.onError` | Error event | Log/cleanup |
| `AsyncListener.onComplete` | Complete event | Metrics/cleanup |

Template:

```java
AsyncContext async = request.startAsync();
async.setTimeout(5_000);

executor.submit(() -> {
    try {
        // work
    } finally {
        async.complete();
    }
});
```

---

# 11. Cookie Cheat Sheet

`Cookie` merepresentasikan informasi kecil yang dikirim servlet ke browser dan dikembalikan browser pada request berikutnya; cookie umum dipakai untuk session management. Servlet 6.0+ menambahkan `setAttribute`, `getAttribute`, dan `getAttributes`, sehingga atribut seperti `SameSite` dapat diset sebagai cookie attribute. ([Jakarta EE](https://jakarta.ee/specifications/servlet/6.1/apidocs/jakarta.servlet/jakarta/servlet/http/cookie))

| Method | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `new Cookie(name, value)` | Buat cookie |
| `setValue(String)` | Set value |
| `setPath(String)` | Scope path |
| `setDomain(String)` | Scope domain |
| `setMaxAge(int)` | Lifetime seconds; `0` delete; negative session cookie |
| `setSecure(boolean)` | Kirim hanya via secure protocol |
| `setHttpOnly(boolean)` | Tidak exposed ke client-side scripting |
| `setAttribute(String, String)` | Attribute tambahan seperti `SameSite` |
| `getAttributes()` | Semua attributes |
| `response.addCookie(cookie)` | Kirim cookie |
| `request.getCookies()` | Ambil cookie dari request |

Contoh:

```java
Cookie cookie = new Cookie("CASESESSIONID", sessionId);
cookie.setHttpOnly(true);
cookie.setSecure(true);
cookie.setPath("/case-service");
cookie.setMaxAge(3600);
cookie.setAttribute("SameSite", "Lax");

response.addCookie(cookie);
```

Tomcat juga punya `CookieProcessor` yang mengubah `Cookie` menjadi header `Set-Cookie` dan dapat dikonfigurasi di level Context; Tomcat menyediakan opsi SameSite melalui cookie processor. ([Apache Tomcat](https://tomcat.apache.org/tomcat-11.0-doc/config/cookie-processor.html))

---

# 12. Multipart / Upload Cheat Sheet

| Class / Annotation | Properti / Method | Fungsi |
|---|---|---|
| `@MultipartConfig` | `location` | Directory temp file |
| `@MultipartConfig` | `fileSizeThreshold` | Threshold memory → disk |
| `@MultipartConfig` | `maxFileSize` | Max size per file |
| `@MultipartConfig` | `maxRequestSize` | Max total multipart request |
| `Part` | `getName()` | Field name |
| `Part` | `getSubmittedFileName()` | Original filename |
| `Part` | `getSize()` | Size |
| `Part` | `getContentType()` | Content type |
| `Part` | `getInputStream()` | Stream content |
| `Part` | `write(String)` | Save |
| `Part` | `delete()` | Delete temp |

Rule production:

```text
Jangan unlimited.
Jangan load file besar ke memory.
Validasi content type, extension, size.
Gunakan async processing untuk OCR/media/large file.
```

# 13. `web.xml` Cheat Sheet

| Element | Fungsi | Properti umum |
|---|---|---|
| `<display-name>` | Nama web app | text |
| `<context-param>` | Global app param | `<param-name>`, `<param-value>` |
| `<listener>` | Register listener | `<listener-class>` |
| `<filter>` | Register filter | `<filter-name>`, `<filter-class>`, `<init-param>`, `<async-supported>` |
| `<filter-mapping>` | Mapping filter | `<filter-name>`, `<url-pattern>`, `<servlet-name>`, `<dispatcher>` |
| `<servlet>` | Register servlet | `<servlet-name>`, `<servlet-class>`, `<init-param>`, `<load-on-startup>`, `<async-supported>`, `<multipart-config>` |
| `<servlet-mapping>` | Mapping servlet | `<servlet-name>`, `<url-pattern>` |
| `<session-config>` | Session config | `<session-timeout>`, `<cookie-config>` |
| `<cookie-config>` | Session cookie config | `<name>`, `<domain>`, `<path>`, `<comment>`, `<http-only>`, `<secure>`, `<max-age>` |
| `<error-page>` | Error mapping | `<error-code>`, `<exception-type>`, `<location>` |
| `<security-constraint>` | Declarative security | `<web-resource-collection>`, `<auth-constraint>`, `<user-data-constraint>` |
| `<login-config>` | Container auth | `<auth-method>`, `<realm-name>` |
| `<security-role>` | Role declaration | `<role-name>` |
| `<mime-mapping>` | MIME type mapping | `<extension>`, `<mime-type>` |
| `<welcome-file-list>` | Welcome files | `<welcome-file>` |

Jersey common `web.xml`:

```xml
<servlet>
    <servlet-name>jersey</servlet-name>
    <servlet-class>org.glassfish.jersey.servlet.ServletContainer</servlet-class>
    <init-param>
        <param-name>jakarta.ws.rs.Application</param-name>
        <param-value>com.company.caseapp.config.CaseApplication</param-value>
    </init-param>
    <load-on-startup>1</load-on-startup>
</servlet>

<servlet-mapping>
    <servlet-name>jersey</servlet-name>
    <url-pattern>/api/*</url-pattern>
</servlet-mapping>
```

Jersey documentation menjelaskan deployment server-side Jersey dapat berjalan di berbagai server environment, dan cara publish aplikasi bergantung pada apakah berjalan di Java SE environment atau di dalam container. ([Eclipse EE4J](https://eclipse-ee4j.github.io/jersey.github.io/documentation/latest3x/deployment.html?utm_source=chatgpt.com))

# 14. Jersey Servlet Init Params yang Sering Muncul

| Param | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `jakarta.ws.rs.Application` | FQCN class `Application` / `ResourceConfig` |
| `jersey.config.server.provider.packages` | Package scanning resource/provider |
| `jersey.config.server.provider.classnames` | Explicit provider/resource class names |
| `jersey.config.server.provider.scanning.recursive` | Recursive package scan |
| `jersey.config.server.tracing` | Jersey tracing mode, biasanya non-prod |
| `jersey.config.server.tracing.threshold` | Threshold tracing |
| `jersey.config.beanValidation.enableOutputValidationErrorEntity.server` | Bean validation behavior, tergantung Jersey version |
| `jersey.config.server.response.setStatusOverSendError` | Prefer `setStatus` over `sendError`, tergantung Jersey setup |

Pattern yang paling mudah diaudit:

```java
public class CaseApplication extends ResourceConfig {
    public CaseApplication() {
        register(CaseResource.class);
        register(HealthResource.class);
        register(GlobalExceptionMapper.class);
        register(JsonProcessingExceptionMapper.class);
    }
}
```

# 15. Tomcat Connector Properties Cheat Sheet

Ini **Tomcat-specific**, bukan Servlet spec. Nama setting Jetty/Undertow/GlassFish bisa berbeda. Tomcat HTTP Connector memiliki banyak property seperti `connectionTimeout`, `keepAliveTimeout`, `maxConnections`, `maxHttpRequestHeaderSize`, `maxThreads`, `executor`, dan compression-related settings. Tomcat docs menjelaskan connector menerima koneksi pada port tertentu dan meneruskan request ke engine untuk diproses. ([Apache Tomcat](https://tomcat.apache.org/tomcat-11.0-doc/config/http.html))

| Property | Fungsi | Production note |
|---|---|---|
| `port` | Port listen | Biasanya `8080` di container |
| `protocol` | Protocol handler | `HTTP/1.1`, NIO |
| `address` | Bind address | Bisa bind internal IP |
| `connectionTimeout` | Timeout request line/body awal | Jangan infinite |
| `keepAliveTimeout` | Timeout idle keep-alive | Jangan terlalu besar |
| `maxThreads` | Request processing threads | Selaraskan dengan DB/downstream |
| `executor` | Pakai shared executor | Jika dipakai, thread attrs connector diabaikan |
| `acceptCount` | Queue saat max connections tercapai | Terlalu besar = latency queue |
| `maxConnections` | Max connection diproses | Default Tomcat NIO umum 8192, tapi tune sesuai workload |
| `maxHttpRequestHeaderSize` | Batas request line + header | Hati-hati large JWT/cookie |
| `maxHttpResponseHeaderSize` | Batas response header | Hati-hati banyak Set-Cookie |
| `maxHeaderCount` | Max jumlah header | Guard abuse |
| `maxPostSize` | Limit POST body tertentu | Tetap butuh app/gateway body limit |
| `maxSwallowSize` | Body yang diswallow saat upload aborted | Mencegah resource abuse |
| `compression` | Enable compression | Pastikan tidak double dengan gateway |
| `compressionMinSize` | Minimum size compression | Jangan compress payload kecil |
| `compressibleMimeType` | MIME type yang dikompresi | Include `application/json` jika perlu |
| `server` | Override `Server` header | Hardening |
| `serverRemoveAppProvidedValues` | Remove app-provided Server header | Hardening |
| `executorTerminationTimeoutMillis` | Tunggu thread terminate saat connector stop | Graceful shutdown |

Contoh:

```xml
<Connector
    port="8080"
    protocol="HTTP/1.1"
    connectionTimeout="10000"
    keepAliveTimeout="15000"
    maxThreads="200"
    acceptCount="100"
    maxConnections="8192"
    maxHttpRequestHeaderSize="16384"
    maxPostSize="2097152"
    compression="on"
    compressionMinSize="2048"
    compressibleMimeType="text/plain,text/html,text/xml,text/css,application/json,application/javascript" />
```

---

# 16. Session / Cookie Container Properties

## `web.xml`

```xml
<session-config>
    <session-timeout>30</session-timeout>
    <cookie-config>
        <name>CASESESSIONID</name>
        <http-only>true</http-only>
        <secure>true</secure>
        <path>/case-service</path>
    </cookie-config>
</session-config>
```

## Tomcat Context / CookieProcessor

| Property | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `sameSiteCookies` | Set SameSite cookie policy di Tomcat CookieProcessor |
| `className` | CookieProcessor implementation |
| `cookiesWithoutEquals` | Handling cookie invalid/tanpa equals, Tomcat-specific |

Tomcat `CookieProcessor` bertanggung jawab parsing cookie dari request menjadi `jakarta.servlet.http.Cookie` dan mengubah cookie response menjadi header yang dikirim ke client. ([Apache Tomcat](https://tomcat.apache.org/tomcat-11.0-doc/config/cookie-processor.html))

Contoh Tomcat context:

```xml
<Context>
    <CookieProcessor sameSiteCookies="lax" />
</Context>
```

# 17. Security Constraint Cheat Sheet

```xml
<security-constraint>
    <web-resource-collection>
        <web-resource-name>Protected API</web-resource-name>
        <url-pattern>/api/*</url-pattern>
        <http-method>GET</http-method>
        <http-method>POST</http-method>
    </web-resource-collection>

    <auth-constraint>
        <role-name>api-user</role-name>
    </auth-constraint>

    <user-data-constraint>
        <transport-guarantee>CONFIDENTIAL</transport-guarantee>
    </user-data-constraint>
</security-constraint>

<login-config>
    <auth-method>BASIC</auth-method>
    <realm-name>CaseServiceRealm</realm-name>
</login-config>
```

| Element | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| `<url-pattern>` | Path yang diproteksi |
| `<http-method>` | Method yang diproteksi |
| `<auth-constraint>` | Role required |
| `<role-name>` | Role |
| `<transport-guarantee>CONFIDENTIAL</transport-guarantee>` | Require secure transport |
| `<login-config>` | Basic/Form/Client-Cert auth |

Practical note:

```text
Servlet security constraint cocok untuk coarse security.
Business authorization tetap di service/domain layer.
```

# 18. Error Handling Properties

`web.xml`:

```xml
<error-page>
    <error-code>404</error-code>
    <location>/internal/error</location>
</error-page>

<error-page>
    <error-code>500</error-code>
    <location>/internal/error</location>
</error-page>

<error-page>
    <exception-type>java.lang.Throwable</exception-type>
    <location>/internal/error</location>
</error-page>
```

Request attributes saat error dispatch:

| Attribute constant | Isi |
|---|---|
| `RequestDispatcher.ERROR_STATUS_CODE` | HTTP status |
| `RequestDispatcher.ERROR_EXCEPTION` | Throwable |
| `RequestDispatcher.ERROR_EXCEPTION_TYPE` | Exception class |
| `RequestDispatcher.ERROR_MESSAGE` | Error message |
| `RequestDispatcher.ERROR_REQUEST_URI` | Original request URI |
| `RequestDispatcher.ERROR_SERVLET_NAME` | Servlet name |

Pattern:

```java
Object status = request.getAttribute(RequestDispatcher.ERROR_STATUS_CODE);
Object ex = request.getAttribute(RequestDispatcher.ERROR_EXCEPTION);
Object uri = request.getAttribute(RequestDispatcher.ERROR_REQUEST_URI);
```

---

# 19. Dispatcher Types Cheat Sheet

| Dispatcher | Makna | Filter cocok? |
|---|---|---|
| `REQUEST` | Request normal dari client | Default untuk hampir semua filter |
| `ERROR` | Error dispatch container | Hanya error-specific filter |
| `ASYNC` | Async dispatch | Hanya async-aware filter |
| `FORWARD` | Server-side forward | Jarang untuk REST API |
| `INCLUDE` | Server-side include | Jarang untuk REST API |

Filter mapping:

```xml
<filter-mapping>
    <filter-name>accessLogFilter</filter-name>
    <url-pattern>/api/*</url-pattern>
    <dispatcher>REQUEST</dispatcher>
</filter-mapping>
```

Rule:

```text
Filter utama: REQUEST only.
Tambahkan ERROR/ASYNC hanya jika sudah dites.
```

HttpServletResponse.SC_OK                    // 200
HttpServletResponse.SC_CREATED               // 201
HttpServletResponse.SC_ACCEPTED              // 202

public static final String HEADER_AUTHORIZATION = "Authorization";
public static final String HEADER_CONTENT_TYPE = "Content-Type";
public static final String HEADER_ACCEPT = "Accept";
public static final String HEADER_ORIGIN = "Origin";
public static final String HEADER_CORRELATION_ID = "X-Correlation-Id";
public static final String HEADER_FORWARDED_FOR = "X-Forwarded-For";
public static final String HEADER_FORWARDED_PROTO = "X-Forwarded-Proto";
public static final String HEADER_FORWARDED_HOST = "X-Forwarded-Host";

# 21. Production Patterns

## 21.1 Correlation ID Filter

```java
public class CorrelationIdFilter implements Filter {

    public static final String HEADER = "X-Correlation-Id";
    public static final String ATTRIBUTE = "correlationId";

    @Override
    public void doFilter(
            ServletRequest request,
            ServletResponse response,
            FilterChain chain
    ) throws IOException, ServletException {

        HttpServletRequest r = (HttpServletRequest) request;
        HttpServletResponse w = (HttpServletResponse) response;

        String correlationId = r.getHeader(HEADER);
        if (correlationId == null || correlationId.isBlank()) {
            correlationId = java.util.UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        }

        try {
            org.slf4j.MDC.put("correlationId", correlationId);
            r.setAttribute(ATTRIBUTE, correlationId);
            w.setHeader(HEADER, correlationId);

            chain.doFilter(request, response);
        } finally {
            org.slf4j.MDC.remove("correlationId");
        }
    }
}
```

## 21.2 Response Status Wrapper

```java
public class StatusCaptureResponseWrapper extends HttpServletResponseWrapper {

    private int statusCode = SC_OK;

    public StatusCaptureResponseWrapper(HttpServletResponse response) {
        super(response);
    }

    @Override
    public void setStatus(int sc) {
        statusCode = sc;
        super.setStatus(sc);
    }

    @Override
    public void sendError(int sc) throws IOException {
        statusCode = sc;
        super.sendError(sc);
    }

    @Override
    public void sendRedirect(String location) throws IOException {
        statusCode = SC_FOUND;
        super.sendRedirect(location);
    }

    public int getStatusCode() {
        return statusCode;
    }
}
```

## 21.3 JSON Error Servlet

```java
public class JsonErrorServlet extends HttpServlet {

    @Override
    protected void service(
            HttpServletRequest request,
            HttpServletResponse response
    ) throws IOException {

        Object value = request.getAttribute(RequestDispatcher.ERROR_STATUS_CODE);
        int status = value instanceof Integer s ? s : 500;

        response.setStatus(status);
        response.setContentType("application/json");
        response.setCharacterEncoding("UTF-8");
        response.getWriter().write("""
                {"code":"HTTP_ERROR","message":"Request could not be processed"}
                """);
    }
}
```

# 22. Maven Dependency Cheat Sheet

Untuk WAR di external Servlet Container:

```xml
<dependency>
    <groupId>jakarta.servlet</groupId>
    <artifactId>jakarta.servlet-api</artifactId>
    <version>${jakarta.servlet.version}</version>
    <scope>provided</scope>
</dependency>
```

Rule:

```text
WAR external container:
  servlet-api biasanya provided

Embedded container:
  servlet/container runtime ikut dibundle
```

Cek konflik:

```bash
mvn dependency:tree | grep -E "servlet|javax|jakarta|jersey"
```

Red flag:

```text
javax.servlet-api
javax.ws.rs-api
jakarta.servlet-api
jakarta.ws.rs-api
jersey 2.x
jersey 3.x / 4.x
```

muncul campur tanpa alasan.

# 24. Quick Review Checklist

```text
Annotations:
[ ] @WebFilter / web.xml filter order jelas.
[ ] Dispatcher type eksplisit.
[ ] @MultipartConfig punya maxFileSize/maxRequestSize.
[ ] @WebListener lifecycle resource cleanup.

Request:
[ ] Tidak baca body di filter tanpa wrapper.
[ ] Path debug: URI/contextPath/servletPath/pathInfo.
[ ] Header sensitif tidak dilog.
[ ] getSession() tidak dipakai tanpa alasan.

Response:
[ ] Error JSON konsisten.
[ ] Status/header diset sebelum committed.
[ ] Security header ada.
[ ] Cache-Control aman untuk data sensitif.

Session/Cookie:
[ ] Stateless API tidak mengeluarkan JSESSIONID.
[ ] Cookie auth/session Secure + HttpOnly + SameSite.
[ ] Session timeout jelas.

Container:
[ ] maxThreads, maxConnections, acceptCount diketahui.
[ ] connectionTimeout/keepAliveTimeout jelas.
[ ] header/body/multipart limit jelas.
[ ] Access log aktif.
[ ] Forwarded headers dinormalisasi trusted proxy.

Deployment:
[ ] WAR/JAR model jelas.
[ ] servlet-api scope sesuai.
[ ] Tidak campur javax.* dan jakarta.*.
[ ] Jersey registration jelas.
```

---

# 25. Practical Priority untuk Onboarding Repo

Urutan audit tercepat:

```text
1. Cari web.xml / @WebServlet / @WebFilter / @WebListener.
2. Pastikan Jersey ServletContainer mapping.
3. Cek semua Filter dan dispatcher type.
4. Cek getSession(), getInputStream(), getReader(), startAsync().
5. Cek error-page dan ExceptionMapper.
6. Cek max request body/header/multipart limit.
7. Cek dependency tree javax vs jakarta.
8. Inspect WAR: WEB-INF/lib, WEB-INF/classes, web.xml.
9. Cek Tomcat/Container connector config.
10. Cek access log + correlation id.
```

Kalau mau cheat sheet lanjutannya, yang paling nyambung adalah **“Cheat Sheet Servlet Container Production Troubleshooting: symptom → likely layer → commands → fix”**.
